### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/routing/gdprRouter.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

/** Org enables GDPR → WORKFLOW requirements get satisfaction rows + checklist. */
export async function routeGdprWorkflows(orgId: string) {
const workflowReqs = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: { framework: { code: "GDPR" }, kind: "WORKFLOW" },
});
for (const req of workflowReqs) {
await prisma.workflowSatisfaction.upsert({
where: { orgId_requirementId: { orgId, requirementId: req.id } },
update: {},
create: { orgId, requirementId: req.id, module: req.satisfiedByWorkflow ?? "privacy",
status: "NOT_STARTED", metrics: {}, lastEvaluatedAt: new Date() },
});
}
return generateGdprChecklist(orgId);
}

export async function generateGdprChecklist(orgId: string) {
const settings = await prisma.privacySettings.findUnique({ where: { orgId } });
const map = await prisma.dataMapEntry.findMany({ where: { orgId } });
const drills = await prisma.dsarDrill.findFirst({ where: { orgId },
orderBy: { executedAt: "desc" } });
return [
{ order: 1, article: "Art.37", task: "Designate DPO (or record N/A rationale)",
done: !!settings?.dpoEmail },
{ order: 2, article: "Art.30", task: "Build Records of Processing from the data map",
done: map.length > 0 },
{ order: 3, article: "Art.5/6", task: "Record lawful basis for every processing purpose",
done: map.length > 0 && map.every((m) => m.lawfulBasis) },
{ order: 4, article: "Art.13/14", task: "Publish privacy notices + record URLs",
done: ((settings?.noticeUrls as unknown[]) ?? []).length > 0 },
{ order: 5, article: "Art.15–22", task: "Activate DSAR intake + run first drill",
done: !!drills },
{ order: 6, article: "Art.33/34", task: "Wire GDPR 72h into incident workflows + drill",
done: !!settings?.lastBreachDrillAt },
{ order: 7, article: "Art.35", task: "Activate DPIA process; assess high-risk processing",
done: !!settings?.dpiaProcessActive },
{ order: 8, article: "Art.44", task: "Record transfer mechanisms for non-EEA processors",
done: map.every((m) => !m.processorName || !!m.transferMechanism) },
];
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/routing/satisfaction.ts
```typescript
export type SatStatus = "NOT_STARTED" | "PARTIAL" | "SATISFIED" | "DEGRADED" | "NOT_APPLICABLE";

export interface Telemetry {
dsar: { total12m: number; onTimeRate: number; overdueOpen: number;
lastDrillAt: Date | null; extensionPolicy: boolean };
ropa: { entries: number; lawfulBasisCoverage: number; lastPublishedAt: Date | null };
notices: { count: number; allReviewedRecently: boolean };
vendors: { processors: number; dpaCoverage: number };
transfers: { nonEeuProcessors: number; withMechanism: number };
incidents: { gdprSlaConfigured: boolean; lastDrillAt: Date | null };
dpia: { processActive: boolean; records: number; highRiskFlagged: number };
governance: { dpoConfigured: boolean | "NA"; specialCatSafeguarded: boolean };
}

const DSAR_FAMILY = ["Art.15", "Art.16", "Art.17", "Art.18", "Art.19", "Art.20", "Art.21", "Art.22"];
const BREACH_FAMILY = ["Art.33", "Art.34"];
const PRINCIPLES_FAMILY = ["Art.5", "Art.6", "Art.7", "Art.9", "Art.24", "Art.25"];

const EVALUATORS: Record<string, (t: Telemetry) => SatStatus> = {
"Art.12": (t) => {
if (t.dsar.overdueOpen > 0) return "DEGRADED";
if (t.dsar.total12m === 0 && !t.dsar.lastDrillAt) return "NOT_STARTED";
return t.dsar.onTimeRate >= 0.9 ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL";
},
"Art.13": (t) => t.notices.count > 0
? (t.notices.allReviewedRecently ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL") : "NOT_STARTED",
"Art.30": (t) => t.ropa.entries === 0 ? "NOT_STARTED"
: t.ropa.lastPublishedAt && t.ropa.lawfulBasisCoverage === 1 ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL",
"Art.35": (t) => !t.dpia.processActive ? "NOT_STARTED"
: t.dpia.records >= t.dpia.highRiskFlagged ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL",
"Art.37": (t) => t.governance.dpoConfigured === "NA" ? "NOT_APPLICABLE"
: t.governance.dpoConfigured ? "SATISFIED" : "NOT_STARTED",
"Art.44": (t) => t.transfers.nonEeuProcessors === 0 ? "SATISFIED"
: t.transfers.withMechanism === t.transfers.nonEeuProcessors ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL",
};

export function evaluateRequirement(reqCode: string, t: Telemetry): SatStatus {
if (DSAR_FAMILY.includes(reqCode)) {
if (t.dsar.overdueOpen > 0) return "DEGRADED";
if (t.dsar.total12m === 0 && !t.dsar.lastDrillAt) return "NOT_STARTED";
return t.dsar.onTimeRate >= 0.9 ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL";
}
if (BREACH_FAMILY.includes(reqCode)) {
if (!t.incidents.gdprSlaConfigured) return "NOT_STARTED";
const fresh = t.incidents.lastDrillAt &&
(Date.now() - t.incidents.lastDrillAt.getTime()) < 365 * 86_400_000;
return fresh ? "SATISFIED" : "PARTIAL";
}
if (PRINCIPLES_FAMILY.includes(reqCode)) {
if (t.ropa.entries === 0) return "NOT_STARTED";
if (t.ropa.lawfulBasisCoverage < 1) return "PARTIAL";
if (reqCode === "Art.9" && !t.governance.specialCatSafeguarded) return "PARTIAL";
return "SATISFIED";
}
const eval_ = EVALUATORS[reqCode];
return eval_ ? eval_(t) : "PARTIAL";
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/routing/runEvaluation.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { sha256, canonicalJSON, addDays } from "../../utils/canonical";
import { appendEvidence } from "../../evidence/chain";
import { evaluateRequirement, Telemetry } from "./satisfaction";
import { createGapTask } from "../../gaps/detect";

async function gatherTelemetry(orgId: string): Promise<Telemetry> {
const yearAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 86_400_000);
const dsar = await prisma.privacyRequest.findMany({
where: { orgId, createdAt: { gte: yearAgo } } });
const delivered = dsar.filter((r) => r.status === "DELIVERED");
const onTime = delivered.filter((r) =>
new Date(r.updatedAt ?? r.createdAt) <= (r.extensionTo ?? r.dueAt)).length;

const map = await prisma.dataMapEntry.findMany({ where: { orgId } });
const ropa = await prisma.ropaDocument.findFirst({ where: { orgId, status: "PUBLISHED" },
orderBy: { version: "desc" } });
const vendors = await prisma.vendor.findMany({ where: { orgId },
include: { reviews: { orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 1 } } });
const processors = vendors.filter((v) => v.dataAccess !== "NONE");
const dpaCovered = processors.filter((v) => v.reviews[0]?.dpProvided).length;
const settings = await prisma.privacySettings.findUnique({ where: { orgId } });
const drills = await prisma.dsarDrill.findFirst({ where: { orgId },
orderBy: { executedAt: "desc" } });
const dpias = await prisma.dpiaRecord.count({ where: { orgId } });

return {
dsar: {
total12m: dsar.length,
onTimeRate: delivered.length ? onTime / delivered.length : 1,
overdueOpen: dsar.filter((r) => (r.extensionTo ?? r.dueAt) < new Date() &&
!["DELIVERED", "CLOSED_REJECTED", "CLOSED_NO_DATA"].includes(r.status)).length,
lastDrillAt: drills?.executedAt ?? null,
extensionPolicy: settings?.extensionPolicy ?? false,
},
ropa: {
entries: map.length,
lawfulBasisCoverage: map.length
? map.filter((m) => m.lawfulBasis).length / map.length : 0,
lastPublishedAt: ropa?.publishedAt ?? null,
},
notices: { count: ((settings?.noticeUrls as unknown[]) ?? []).length,
allReviewedRecently: true },
vendors: { processors: processors.length,
dpaCoverage: processors.length ? dpaCovered / processors.length : 1 },
transfers: {
nonEeuProcessors: map.filter((m) => m.processorName && !m.transferMechanism).length,
withMechanism: map.filter((m) => m.processorName && m.transferMechanism).length,
},
incidents: { gdprSlaConfigured: true, lastDrillAt: settings?.lastBreachDrillAt ?? null },
dpia: { processActive: settings?.dpiaProcessActive ?? false,
records: dpias, highRiskFlagged: dpias },
governance: {
dpoConfigured: settings?.dpoEmail ? true : false,
specialCatSafeguarded: map.every((m) => m.specialCategories.length === 0) || !!settings?.dpiaProcessActive,
},
};
}

/**
* The evaluation run: telemetry → satisfaction states → hash-chained evidence.
* One evidence row per run enters the tamper-evident chain on the privacy
* program anchor control.
*/
export async function evaluatePrivacyWorkflows(orgId: string, trigger: string) {
const t = await gatherTelemetry(orgId);

const rows = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: { framework: { code: "GDPR" }, kind: "WORKFLOW" },
include: { framework: true },
});

const anchor = await prisma.control.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { orgId_slug: { orgId, slug: "privacy.program-operations" } },
});

const results: { req: { id: string; code: string }; status: string; prev: string }[] = [];
for (const req of rows) {
const existing = await prisma.workflowSatisfaction.findUnique({
where: { orgId_requirementId: { orgId, requirementId: req.id } },
});
if (existing?.status === "NOT_APPLICABLE") continue; // human decision preserved
results.push({ req, status: evaluateRequirement(req.code, t),
prev: existing?.status ?? "NOT_STARTED" });
}

const evidence = await appendEvidence({
orgId, controlId: anchor.id, sourceType: "AUTOMATED",
title: "GDPR workflow requirements — privacy module telemetry",
expiresAt: addDays(new Date(), 90),
payload: { trigger, telemetry: t,
statuses: results.map((r) => [r.req.code, r.status]) },
});

for (const r of results) {
await prisma.workflowSatisfaction.upsert({
where: { orgId_requirementId: { orgId, requirementId: r.req.id } },
update: { status: r.status, metrics: { telemetry: t, trigger },
evidenceId: evidence.id, lastEvaluatedAt: new Date(),
evaluatedBy: trigger },
create: { orgId, requirementId: r.req.id, module: "privacy", status: r.status,
metrics: { telemetry: t }, evidenceId: evidence.id,
lastEvaluatedAt: new Date(), evaluatedBy: trigger },
});
if (r.status === "DEGRADED" || (r.prev === "SATISFIED" && r.status === "PARTIAL")) {
await createGapTask(orgId, `GDPR ${r.req.code} workflow degraded`,
`Satisfaction moved ${r.prev} → ${r.status}. Trigger: ${trigger}`, "HIGH");
}
}
return { evidenceId: evidence.id,
statuses: results.map((r) => [r.req.code, r.status]) };
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/vendors/score.ts
```typescript
export function vendorTier(v: { criticality: string; dataAccess: string }) {
// Access to production data or PHI/PII trumps everything
const M: Record<string, number> = { NONE: 0, LIMITED: 1, EXTENSIVE: 2 };
const C: Record<string, number> = { LOW: 0, MEDIUM: 1, HIGH: 2, CRITICAL: 3 };
const score = (M[v.dataAccess] ?? 0) * 2 + (C[v.criticality] ?? 0);
if (score >= 5) return { tier: "CRITICAL", reviewCadenceMonths: 6, requiresCert: true, requiresDpa: true };
if (score >= 3) return { tier: "HIGH", reviewCadenceMonths: 12, requiresCert: true, requiresDpa: true };
if (score >= 1) return { tier: "MEDIUM", reviewCadenceMonths: 12, requiresCert: false, requiresDpa: true };
return { tier: "LOW", reviewCadenceMonths: 24, requiresCert: false, requiresDpa: false };
}

/** Review posture 0–100: certifications fresh, DPA, review recency. */
export function vendorScore(input: {
tier: string;
certs: { type: string; expiresAt: Date | null }[];
dpaProvided: boolean;
lastReviewedAt: Date | null;
cadenceMonths: number;
}) {
let s = 40;
for (const c of input.certs) {
const fresh = c.expiresAt && c.expiresAt.getTime() > Date.now() + 30 * 86_400_000;
s += fresh ? 20 : c.expiresAt ? 5 : 10; // fresh 20, expiring 5, unverified claim 10
}
s += input.dpaProvided ? 15 : -15;
if (input.lastReviewedAt) {
const monthsOver = (Date.now() - input.lastReviewedAt.getTime()) / (30.44 * 86_400_000)
- input.cadenceMonths;
s += monthsOver <= 0 ? 25 : monthsOver <= 3 ? 10 : -20;
} else s -= 15;
return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(s)));
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/incidents/machine.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

export const IR_STATES = ["OPEN", "TRIAGE", "CONTAINED", "ERADICATED",
"RECOVERED", "POSTMORTEM", "CLOSED"] as const;
export type IrState = (typeof IR_STATES)[number];

const TRANSITIONS: Record<IrState, { to: IrState[]; require: string[] }> = {
OPEN: { to: ["TRIAGE"], require: [] },
TRIAGE: { to: ["CONTAINED", "OPEN"],
require: ["affectedDataCategories"] }, // facts the calculator needs
CONTAINED: { to: ["ERADICATED"], require: [] },
ERADICATED: { to: ["RECOVERED"], require: [] },
RECOVERED: { to: ["POSTMORTEM"], require: [] },
POSTMORTEM: { to: ["CLOSED"], require: [] }, // root cause recorded via postmortem notes
CLOSED: { to: [], require: [] },
};

export class InvalidTransition extends Error {
constructor(from: string, to: string) { super(`Invalid IR transition ${from} → ${to}`); }
}
export class MissingFacts extends Error {
constructor(public missing: string[]) { super(`Facts required: ${missing.join(", ")}`); }
}

export async function transitionIncident(incidentId: string, to: IrState) {
const inc = await prisma.incident.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: incidentId } });
const allowed = TRANSITIONS[inc.status as IrState];
if (!allowed.to.includes(to)) throw new InvalidTransition(inc.status, to);
const missing = allowed.require.filter((f) => {
const v = (inc as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[f];
return v == null || (Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 0);
});
if (missing.length) throw new MissingFacts(missing);

await prisma.incident.update({ where: { id: incidentId }, data: { status: to } });
if (to === "TRIAGE") {
// Clock starts — the API layer calls computeAndScheduleNotifications next
}
return { from: inc.status, to };
}
```

```typescript
export interface IncidentFacts {
detectedAt: Date;
dataCategories: string[]; // ["phi","pii","financial","credentials"]
subjectCountByJurisdiction: Record<string, number>;
encryptedAtRest: boolean;
encryptedInTransit: boolean;
isPublicCompany: boolean;
materialityDetermined: boolean;
isNydfsLicensed?: boolean;
isFinancialInstitution?: boolean;
}

export const euSubjects = (f: IncidentFacts) =>
Object.entries(f.subjectCountByJurisdiction)
.filter(([j]) => j.startsWith("EU")).reduce((s, [, n]) => s + n, 0);

/** Federal/sector overlays — separate from the state matrix. */
export const SECTOR_OVERLAYS: Array<{
authority: string; statute: string; deadlineHours: number;
applies: (f: IncidentFacts) => boolean; v: number;
}> = [
{ authority: "GDPR_DPA", statute: "GDPR Art.33", deadlineHours: 72,
applies: (f) => f.dataCategories.includes("pii") && euSubjects(f) > 0, v: 1.0 },
{ authority: "HHS", statute: "HIPAA 164.408", deadlineHours: 1440, // 60 days
applies: (f) => f.dataCategories.includes("phi"), v: 1.0 },
{ authority: "NYDFS", statute: "23 NYCRR 500.17", deadlineHours: 72,
applies: (f) => f.isNydfsLicensed === true, v: 0.9 },
{ authority: "GLBA_REGULATOR", statute: "Interagency Guidance (FFIEC)", deadlineHours: 720,
applies: (f) => f.isFinancialInstitution === true, v: 0.7 },
{ authority: "SEC_8K", statute: "17 CFR 249.308 Item 1.05", deadlineHours: 0, // 4 BUSINESS days post-determination
applies: (f) => f.isPublicCompany && f.materialityDetermined, v: 1.0 },
];
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/incidents/loadMatrix.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { STATE_MATRIX } from "./stateMatrixData";

const jsonAg = (a: number | null | false) =>
a === false ? "NEVER" : a === null ? "ALWAYS" : JSON.stringify(a);

export async function loadBreachMatrix() {
for (const r of STATE_MATRIX) {
await prisma.stateBreachRule.upsert({
where: { id: r.code },
update: { name: r.name, citation: r.cite, deadlineDays: r.days,
notifyResidents: r.residents ?? true,
agThreshold: jsonAg(r.ag), creditAgencyThreshold: jsonAg(r.credit),
otherAgencies: r.other ?? [], harmGate: r.harm ?? false,
notes: r.notes ?? [], compiledConfidence: r.v,
version: { increment: 1 } }, // reg radar bumps version on amendment
create: { id: r.code, name: r.name, citation: r.cite, deadlineDays: r.days,
notifyResidents: r.residents ?? true,
agThreshold: jsonAg(r.ag), creditAgencyThreshold: jsonAg(r.credit),
otherAgencies: r.other ?? [], harmGate: r.harm ?? false,
notes: r.notes ?? [], compiledConfidence: r.v,
verificationStatus: "AI_COMPILED" },
});
}
console.log(`Loaded ${STATE_MATRIX.length} jurisdiction rules (all AI_COMPILED until expert verification).`);
}

if (require.main === module) {
loadBreachMatrix().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/incidents/notifications.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { addDays, addHours, addBusinessDays } from "../utils/canonical";
import { IncidentFacts, SECTOR_OVERLAYS, euSubjects } from "./stateMatrixData";

export const POLICY_DEFAULT_DAYS = 45;

export interface NotificationObligation {
authority: string;
deadlineAt: Date;
statute: string;
notes?: string;
basis?: "STATUTE" | "INTERNAL_POLICY";
verified?: boolean;
}

type AgThreshold = number | "ALWAYS" | "NEVER";
const parseAg = (j: string | null): AgThreshold => {
if (j === null || j === "ALWAYS") return "ALWAYS";
if (j === "NEVER") return "NEVER";
const n = Number(j);
return Number.isNaN(n) ? "ALWAYS" : n;
};

/** Federal + sector overlays. */
function computeOverlayObligations(f: IncidentFacts): NotificationObligation[] {
const out: NotificationObligation[] = [];
for (const o of SECTOR_OVERLAYS) {
if (!o.applies(f)) continue;
if (o.authority === "SEC_8K") {
out.push({ authority: "SEC_8K",
deadlineAt: addBusinessDays(new Date(), 4), // 4 business days post-determination
statute: o.statute, basis: "STATUTE", verified: o.v >= 0.99,
notes: "4 business days after materiality DETERMINATION (not detection)" });
} else {
out.push({ authority: o.authority,
deadlineAt: o.deadlineHours > 0
? addHours(f.detectedAt, o.deadlineHours)
: addDays(f.detectedAt, 1),
statute: o.statute, basis: "STATUTE", verified: o.v >= 0.99 });
}
}
// HIPAA ≥500: prompt HHS portal + media
if (f.dataCategories.includes("phi")) {
const usTotal = Object.entries(f.subjectCountByJurisdiction)
.filter(([j]) => j.startsWith("US")).reduce((s, [, n]) => s + n, 0);
if (usTotal >= 500) {
out.push({ authority: "HHS_PROMPT", deadlineAt: addDays(f.detectedAt, 60),
statute: "HIPAA 164.408(c)", basis: "STATUTE", verified: true,
notes: `≥500 individuals (${usTotal}) — contemporaneous portal report` });
out.push({ authority: "MEDIA", deadlineAt: addDays(f.detectedAt, 60),
statute: "HIPAA 164.406", basis: "STATUTE", verified: true,
notes: "Media outlets in affected states" });
}
}
return out;
}

/** State obligations — data-driven from StateBreachRule rows. */
export async function computeStateObligations(
f: IncidentFacts, detectedAt: Date,
): Promise<NotificationObligation[]> {
const rules = await prisma.stateBreachRule.findMany();
const out: NotificationObligation[] = [];
const encrypted = f.encryptedAtRest && f.encryptedInTransit;

for (const rule of rules) {
const subjects = f.subjectCountByJurisdiction[rule.id] ?? 0;
if (subjects === 0) continue;

const agT = parseAg(rule.agThreshold as string | null);
const creditT = parseAg(rule.creditAgencyThreshold as string | null);
const days = rule.deadlineDays ?? POLICY_DEFAULT_DAYS;
const draft = rule.verificationStatus !== "EXPERT_VERIFIED";

const flags: string[] = [];
if (draft) flags.push("DRAFT — rule not yet expert-verified; treat as provisional");
if (rule.deadlineDays === null) flags.push("No statutory day count — internal policy default");
if (rule.harmGate) flags.push("CONDITIONAL — notification required only if harm determination made");
if (rule.encryptionSafeHarbor && encrypted)
flags.push("Data encrypted — possible safe harbor; legal decides, never auto-suppressed");
if (!rule.notifyResidents) flags.push("Business notifies AG only — AG notifies residents");

const mk = (authority: string, who: string): NotificationObligation => ({
authority, deadlineAt: addDays(detectedAt, days), statute: rule.citation,
notes: [`${who} · ${rule.name}`, ...flags].join(" · "),
basis: rule.deadlineDays === null ? "INTERNAL_POLICY" : "STATUTE",
verified: rule.verificationStatus === "EXPERT_VERIFIED",
});

if (rule.notifyResidents)
out.push(mk(`RESIDENTS_${rule.id}`, `Individuals (${subjects} residents)`));
if (agT !== "NEVER" && (agT === "ALWAYS" || subjects >= agT)) {
const others = rule.otherAgencies.length ? ` + ${rule.otherAgencies.join(" + ")}` : "";
out.push(mk(`AG_${rule.id}`, `Attorney General${others}`));
}
if (creditT !== "NEVER" && (creditT === "ALWAYS" || subjects >= creditT))
out.push(mk(`CRA_${rule.id}`, "All consumer reporting agencies"));
}
return out;
}

/** The calculator — fed by incident facts gated by the IR state machine. */
export async function computeNotifications(
f: IncidentFacts,
): Promise<NotificationObligation[]> {
const overlays = computeOverlayObligations(f);
const states = await computeStateObligations(f, f.detectedAt);
return [...overlays, ...states].sort(
(a, b) => a.deadlineAt.getTime() - b.deadlineAt.getTime());
}

/** Persist obligations + hand off to SLA timers (kind INCIDENT_NOTIFICATION). */
export async function computeAndScheduleNotifications(incidentId: string) {
const inc = await prisma.incident.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: incidentId } });
const facts: IncidentFacts = {
detectedAt: inc.detectedAt,
dataCategories: inc.affectedDataCategories as string[],
subjectCountByJurisdiction:
(inc.affectedSystems as Record<string, number>) ?? {},
encryptedAtRest: true, encryptedInTransit: true, // updated when facts recorded
isPublicCompany: inc.isPublicCompany, materialityDetermined: true,
};
const obligations = await computeNotifications(facts);
if (obligations.length) {
await prisma.incidentNotification.createMany({
data: obligations.map((o) => ({ incidentId, authority: o.authority,
jurisdiction: o.authority.split("_").pop() ?? "",
statutoryDeadlineAt: o.deadlineAt, status: "PENDING",
notes: o.notes })),
});
}
// SLA timers (WARN_LEGAL @50%, ESCALATE_CISO @100%) are started by the
// API layer via incidentNotificationSlaWorkflow (workers batch).
return obligations;
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/reporting/riskIndex.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { detectGaps, GAP_PRIORITY, Gap } from "../gaps/detect";
import { frameworkReadiness } from "../controls/readiness";
import { clamp } from "../utils/canonical";

/** Frameworks in scope for an org = distinct frameworks on accepted mappings. */
export async function getOrgFrameworks(orgId: string) {
return prisma.framework.findMany({
where: { requirements: { some: { controlMappings: {
some: { status: "ACCEPTED", control: { orgId } } } } } },
});
}

/** +50% severity if the gap touches PHI/PII/privacy-adjacent controls. */
function boosts(g: Gap): number {
const s = g.detail.toLowerCase();
return /phi|pii|privacy|encrypt|hipaa|gdpr/.test(s) ? 1.5 : 1.0;
}

/**
* RiskIndex: 0 (clean) to 100 (critical exposure).
* coverage gap × severity weight × framework weighting × data-sensitivity boost.
*/
export async function computeRiskIndex(orgId: string): Promise<{
index: number; byFramework: Record<string, number>;
topDrivers: { label: string; points: number }[];
}> {
const frameworks = await getOrgFrameworks(orgId);
const gaps = await detectGaps(orgId);
const byFramework: Record<string, number> = {};
const drivers = new Map<string, number>();

for (const fw of frameworks) {
const readiness = await frameworkReadiness(orgId, fw.id);
const fwGaps = gaps.filter((g) => g.frameworkCode === fw.code);
let score = 0;
for (const g of fwGaps) {
const points = GAP_PRIORITY[g.type].severity * 4 * boosts(g);
score += points;
const label = `${fw.code} ${g.detail}`;
drivers.set(label, (drivers.get(label) ?? 0) + points);
}
score += readiness.staleEvidence.length * 3; // latent risk without formal gaps
byFramework[fw.code] = Math.min(100, Math.round(score));
}

// Composite: worst-framework-weighted (boards should see the weakest leg)
const values = Object.values(byFramework);
const index = values.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(100, Math.round(
Math.max(...values) * 0.6 +
(values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length) * 0.4));

const topDrivers = [...drivers.entries()]
.map(([label, points]) => ({ label, points }))
.sort((a, b) => b.points - a.points).slice(0, 5);

return { index, byFramework, topDrivers };
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/reporting/monthly.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { computeRiskIndex } from "./riskIndex";

const prevPeriod = (p: string) => {
const [y, m] = p.split("-").map(Number);
return m === 1 ? `${y - 1}-12` : `${y}-${String(m - 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};

export async function generateMonthlyReport(orgId: string, period: string) {
const [risk, qualityTrend, gapOpenClose, dsarMetrics, incidentMetrics] = await Promise.all([
computeRiskIndex(orgId),
qualityTrendSeries(orgId),
gapOpenCloseSeries(orgId),
dsarMetricsFor(orgId, period),
incidentMetricsFor(orgId, period),
]);

const prev = await prisma.executiveReport.findUnique({
where: { orgId_period: { orgId, period: prevPeriod(period) } } });

// ReadinessBrief agent: structured metrics in, narrative out — every claim
// must reference a metric; trend deltas mandatory; no speculation.
const { runAgent } = await import("@agents/runtime");
const narrative = await runAgent<{ text: string }>("executive_report", {
risk, qualityTrend, gapOpenClose, dsarMetrics, incidentMetrics,
prevIndex: prev?.riskIndex ?? null, period,
});

return prisma.executiveReport.upsert({
where: { orgId_period: { orgId, period } },
update: {},
create: {
orgId, period, riskIndex: risk.index,
metrics: { qualityTrend, gapOpenClose, dsarMetrics, incidentMetrics,
byFramework: risk.byFramework },
narrative: narrative.text,
},
});
}

async function qualityTrendSeries(orgId: string) {
const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ month: Date; avg: number | null }>>`
SELECT date_trunc('month', "computedAt") AS month, AVG(score) AS avg
FROM "EvidenceQuality" eq JOIN "Evidence" e ON e.id = eq."evidenceId"
WHERE e."orgId" = ${orgId} AND eq."computedAt" > now() - interval '6 months'
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`;
return rows.map((r) => ({ month: r.month.toISOString().slice(0, 7),
avgScore: r.avg ? Math.round(Number(r.avg)) : null }));
}

async function gapOpenCloseSeries(orgId: string) {
const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ month: Date; opened: bigint; closed: bigint }>>`
SELECT date_trunc('month', "createdAt") AS month,
COUNT(*) AS opened,
COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'DONE') AS closed
FROM "Task" WHERE "orgId" = ${orgId}
AND "createdAt" > now() - interval '6 months'
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`;
return rows.map((r) => ({ month: r.month.toISOString().slice(0, 7),
opened: Number(r.opened), closed: Number(r.closed) }));
}

async function dsarMetricsFor(orgId: string, period: string) {
const [y, m] = period.split("-").map(Number);
const start = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, 1));
const end = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 1));
const total = await prisma.privacyRequest.count({
where: { orgId, createdAt: { gte: start, lt: end } } });
const delivered = await prisma.privacyRequest.count({
where: { orgId, status: "DELIVERED", createdAt: { gte: start, lt: end } } });
return { total, delivered, onTimeRate: total ? delivered / total : null };
}

async function incidentMetricsFor(orgId: string, period: string) {
const [y, m] = period.split("-").map(Number);
const start = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, 1));
const end = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 1));
const incidents = await prisma.incident.findMany({
where: { orgId, detectedAt: { gte: start, lt: end } },
include: { notifications: true } });
const resolved = incidents.filter((i) => i.resolvedAt);
const mttrHours = resolved.length
? resolved.reduce((s, i) => s + (i.resolvedAt!.getTime() - i.detectedAt.getTime()), 0)
/ resolved.length / 3_600_000
: null;
return { count: incidents.length, mttrHours: mttrHours ? Math.round(mttrHours) : null,
notificationsFiled: incidents.flatMap((i) => i.notifications)
.filter((n) => n.filedAt).length };
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/calculator/soc2.ts
```typescript
import { clamp } from "../utils/canonical";

export interface CalcInput {
headcount: number;
inScopeSystems: number;
cloudProviders: number;
tscScope: "SECURITY_ONLY" | "SECURITY_AVAILABILITY" | "ALL_FIVE";
auditType: "TYPE_I" | "TYPE_II";
auditorTier: "BOUTIQUE" | "MID_TIER" | "BIG_4";
industry: "SAAS" | "FINTECH" | "HEALTHCARE" | "OTHER";
hasPriorAudit: boolean;
employeeLoadedRateUsd: number;
}

/** Published fixed platform fee by headcount band (pricing artifact). */
export function fixedPlatformFee(headcount: number): number {
if (headcount <= 50) return 4990;
if (headcount <= 200) return 11990;
if (headcount <= 1000) return 29990;
return 29990; // 1,000+ → custom (enterprise)
}

// [P10, P50, P90] in USD — market estimates, calibrate quarterly
const AUDITOR_BASE: Record<string, Record<string, [number, number, number]>> = {
TYPE_I: { BOUTIQUE: [8000, 12500, 18000], MID_TIER: [12000, 18000, 26000], BIG_4: [20000, 30000, 45000] },
TYPE_II: { BOUTIQUE: [18000, 26000, 38000], MID_TIER: [28000, 40000, 60000], BIG_4: [45000, 75000, 120000] },
};

const round = (n: number) => Math.round(n / 100) * 100;

export function calculate(input: CalcInput) {
// ── Complexity multiplier ──
let cx = 1.0;
cx += clamp(input.inScopeSystems, 1, 30) * 0.015;
cx += (input.cloudProviders - 1) * 0.08;
cx += { SECURITY_ONLY: 0, SECURITY_AVAILABILITY: 0.1, ALL_FIVE: 0.25 }[input.tscScope];
if (input.industry === "HEALTHCARE") cx += 0.15;
if (input.industry === "FINTECH") cx += 0.1;
cx = Math.min(cx, 1.8);

// ── Auditor fee ──
const [p10, p50, p90] = AUDITOR_BASE[input.auditType][input.auditorTier];
const auditor = { p10: round(p10 * cx), p50: round(p50 * cx), p90: round(p90 * cx) };

// ── Internal labor ──
const baseHours = input.auditType === "TYPE_II" ? 320 : 200;
const hours = baseHours * cx * (input.hasPriorAudit ? 0.55 : 1);
const internal = {
hours: Math.round(hours),
cost: Math.round(hours * input.employeeLoadedRateUsd),
};

// ── Remediation ──
const remediation = input.hasPriorAudit
? { p10: 2000, p50: 6000, p90: 15000 }
: { p10: 5000, p50: 12000, p90: 30000 };

// ── Platform (fixed fee — the differentiator) ──
const platform = fixedPlatformFee(input.headcount);

const total = {
p10: auditor.p10 + internal.cost + remediation.p10 + platform * 12,
p50: auditor.p50 + internal.cost + remediation.p50 + platform * 12,
p90: auditor.p90 + internal.cost + remediation.p90 + platform * 12,
};

return {
total,
breakdown: { auditor, internal, remediation, platformAnnual: platform * 12 },
firstYearVsSteadyState: {
firstYear: total,
year2Plus: {
p10: round(total.p10 * 0.6), p50: round(total.p50 * 0.6), p90: round(total.p90 * 0.6),
},
},
};
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/kms.ts
```typescript
import { KMSClient, GenerateDataKeyCommand, DecryptCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-kms";
import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import type { IntegrationType } from "@prisma/client";

const kms = new KMSClient({});
const MAIN_KEY = process.env.AWS_KMS_MAIN_KEY_ID!;

const b64 = (b: Buffer) => b.toString("base64");

interface Sealed { iv: string; ct: string; tag: string; dekCt: string }

/** Envelope seal with the platform MAIN key (privacy request details etc.). */
export async function sealText(plaintext: string): Promise<string> {
const dk = await kms.send(new GenerateDataKeyCommand({ KeyId: MAIN_KEY, KeySpec: "AES_256" }));
const iv = randomBytes(12);
const cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", dk.Plaintext!, iv);
const ct = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
const sealed: Sealed = {
iv: b64(iv), ct: b64(ct), tag: b64(cipher.getAuthTag()),
dekCt: b64(Buffer.from(dk.CiphertextBlob!)),
};
dk.Plaintext?.fill(0);
return JSON.stringify(sealed);
}

export async function unsealText(sealed: string): Promise<string> {
const s = JSON.parse(sealed) as Sealed;
const dk = await kms.send(new DecryptCommand({
CiphertextBlob: Buffer.from(s.dekCt, "base64"), KeyId: MAIN_KEY }));
const decipher = createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", dk.Plaintext!, Buffer.from(s.iv, "base64"));
decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(s.tag, "base64"));
const pt = Buffer.concat([decipher.update(Buffer.from(s.ct, "base64")), decipher.final()]);
dk.Plaintext?.fill(0);
return pt.toString("utf8");
}

/** Connect an integration: seal creds, store. */
export async function storeCredentials(
orgId: string, type: IntegrationType, creds: unknown, config: object = {},
): Promise<string> {
const sealed = await sealText(JSON.stringify(creds));
const integration = await prisma.integration.create({
data: { orgId, type, config: config as object,
encryptedCredentials: sealed, status: "CONNECTED" },
});
return integration.id;
}

/** The function every SkillContext.getOrgCredentials calls. */
export async function decryptCredentials<T = unknown>(
orgId: string, type: IntegrationType | string,
): Promise<T> {
const integration = await prisma.integration.findFirst({
where: { orgId, type: type as IntegrationType, status: { not: "DISCONNECTED" } },
orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
});
if (!integration) throw new Error(`No ${type} integration connected for org`);
try {
return JSON.parse(await unsealText(integration.encryptedCredentials)) as T;
} catch (err) {
await prisma.integration.update({ where: { id: integration.id },
data: { status: "ERROR",
syncError: `Credential decrypt failed: ${String(err).slice(0, 200)}` } });
throw new Error(`${type} credentials unreadable — re-connect the integration`);
}
}
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/skills/types.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { decryptCredentials } from "@core";

export interface SkillContext {
orgId: string;
getOrgCredentials<T = any>(integrationType: string): Promise<T>;
}

export interface Skill {
id: string;
description: string;
inputSchema: z.ZodSchema;
execute(ctx: SkillContext, input: any): Promise<unknown>;
}

const registry = new Map<string, Skill>();

export const skillRegistry = {
register: (s: Skill) => registry.set(s.id, s),
async invoke<T = unknown>(id: string, orgId: string, input: unknown): Promise<T> {
const s = registry.get(id);
if (!s) throw new Error(`Unknown skill: ${id}`);
const ctx: SkillContext = {
orgId,
getOrgCredentials: <T>(t: string) => decryptCredentials<T>(orgId, t),
};
return s.execute(ctx, s.inputSchema.parse(input ?? {})) as Promise<T>;
},
list: () => [...registry.values()],
};

export type { Skill, SkillContext };
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/skills/okta.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { Skill, skillRegistry } from "./types";

interface OktaCreds { domain: string; apiToken: string }

async function oktaFetch<T>(c: OktaCreds, path: string): Promise<{ body: T; next: string | null }> {
const res = await fetch(`https://${c.domain}${path}`, {
headers: { Authorization: `SSWS ${c.apiToken}`, Accept: "application/json" },
});
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Okta ${res.status} on ${path}: ${(await res.text()).slice(0, 300)}`);
const next = res.headers.get("link")?.split(",").find((l) => l.includes('rel="next"'))
?.match(/<([^>]+)>/)?.[1] ?? null;
return { body: (await res.json()) as T, next };
}

const listUsers: Skill = {
id: "okta.listUsers",
description: "List Okta users with status and last login",
inputSchema: z.object({}).passthrough(),
async execute(ctx) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<OktaCreds>("OKTA");
const out: { id: string; login: string; email: string; status: string }[] = [];
let path = `/api/v1/users?limit=200&filter=${encodeURIComponent('status eq "ACTIVE"')}`;
while (path) {
const { body, next } = await oktaFetch<any[]>(c, path);
for (const u of body)
out.push({ id: u.id, login: u.profile.login,
email: (u.profile.email ?? "").toLowerCase(), status: u.status });
path = next ? next.replace(`https://${c.domain}`, "") : null;
}
return out;
},
};

const ADMIN_ROLE_TYPES = new Set([
"SUPER_ADMIN", "ORG_ADMIN", "APP_ADMIN", "READ_ONLY_ADMIN",
"HELP_DESK_ADMIN", "MOBILE_ADMIN", "USER_ADMIN", "API_ACCESS_MANAGEMENT_ADMIN",
]);

const listAdmins: Skill = {
id: "okta.listAdmins",
description: "List Okta users holding admin roles and their MFA enrollment",
inputSchema: z.object({}).passthrough(),
async execute(ctx) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<OktaCreds>("OKTA");
const users = (await skillRegistry.invoke<{ id: string; login: string; email: string; status: string }[]>(
"okta.listUsers", ctx.orgId, {})).filter((u) => u.status === "ACTIVE");

// Bounded concurrency (10) to respect Okta rate limits
const results: any[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < users.length; i += 10) {
results.push(...await Promise.all(users.slice(i, i + 10).map(async (u) => {
const [{ body: roles }, { body: factors }] = await Promise.all([
oktaFetch<any[]>(c, `/api/v1/users/${u.id}/roles`),
oktaFetch<any[]>(c, `/api/v1/users/${u.id}/factors`),
]);
const adminRoles = roles.filter((r) => ADMIN_ROLE_TYPES.has(r.type)).map((r) => r.type);
if (adminRoles.length === 0) return null;
return { id: u.id, login: u.login, email: u.email,
adminRoles, mfaEnrolled: factors.length > 0 };
})));
}
return results.filter(Boolean);
},
};

const listAppAssignments: Skill = {
id: "okta.listAppAssignments",
description: "List application-to-user assignments across Okta apps (bounded to 2000 rows)",
inputSchema: z.object({}).passthrough(),
async execute(ctx) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<OktaCreds>("OKTA");
const out: { appId: string; appName: string; userId: string; login: string }[] = [];
let path = "/api/v1/apps?limit=100";
while (path && out.length < 2000) {
const { body: apps, next } = await oktaFetch<any[]>(c, path);
for (const app of apps) {
let upath = `/api/v1/apps/${app.id}/users?limit=200`;
while (upath && out.length < 2000) {
const { body: users, next: unext } = await oktaFetch<any[]>(c, upath);
for (const u of users)
out.push({ appId: app.id, appName: app.label,
userId: u.id, login: u.credentials?.userName ?? "" });
upath = unext ? unext.replace(`https://${c.domain}`, "") : null;
}
}
path = next ? next.replace(`https://${c.domain}`, "") : null;
}
return out;
},
};

/** DSAR extension skill — invoked by core/privacy fetchSubjectData. */
const subjectData: Skill = {
id: "okta.subjectData",
description: "DSAR: all Okta records for a subject identified by email (profile, groups, app links)",
inputSchema: z.object({ email: z.string().email() }),
async execute(ctx, { email }) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<OktaCreds>("OKTA");
const { body: users } = await oktaFetch<any[]>(
c, `/api/v1/users?q=${encodeURIComponent(email.toLowerCase())}&limit=25`);
const match = users.find((u) =>
(u.profile?.login ?? "").toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase() ||
(u.profile?.email ?? "").toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase());
if (!match) return { items: [] };
const [{ body: groups }, { body: appLinks }] = await Promise.all([
oktaFetch<any[]>(c, `/api/v1/users/${match.id}/groups`),
oktaFetch<any[]>(c, `/api/v1/users/${match.id}/appLinks`),
]);
return { items: [{
source: "okta", id: match.id, login: match.profile?.login,
email: match.profile?.email, status: match.status,
created: match.created, lastLogin: match.lastLogin,
groups: groups.map((g: any) => g.profile?.name).filter(Boolean),
assignedApps: appLinks.map((a: any) => a.label),
}] };
},
};

skillRegistry.register(listAdmins);
skillRegistry.register(listAppAssignments);
skillRegistry.register(subjectData);
```

```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { EC2Client, paginateDescribeVolumes } from "@aws-sdk/client-ec2";
import { STSClient, AssumeRoleCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-sts";
import { Skill, skillRegistry } from "./types";

interface AwsCreds { roleArn: string; externalId?: string; regions: string[] }

// NOTE: no aws.subjectData skill is registered by design — AWS accounts hold
// no subject-indexed PII reachable via a single API. A DSAR drill on an
// AWS-mapped system surfaces as ERROR → gap task → documented manual path.

const listEbsVolumes: Skill = {
id: "aws.listEbsVolumes",
description: "List EBS volumes across configured regions with encryption status",
inputSchema: z.object({ regions: z.array(z.string()).optional() }),
async execute(ctx, input: { regions?: string[] }) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<AwsCreds>("AWS");
const regions = input.regions ?? c.regions;

const assumed = await new STSClient({}).send(new AssumeRoleCommand({
RoleArn: c.roleArn, RoleSessionName: "compliance-check", ExternalId: c.externalId,
}));
const out: any[] = [];
for (const region of regions) {
const ec2 = new EC2Client({
region,
credentials: {
accessKeyId: assumed.Credentials!.AccessKeyId,
secretAccessKey: assumed.Credentials!.SecretAccessKey,
sessionToken: assumed.Credentials!.SessionToken,
},
});
for await (const page of paginateDescribeVolumes({ client: ec2 }, {})) {
for (const v of page.Volumes ?? []) {
out.push({
volumeId: v.VolumeId, region, encrypted: v.Encrypted === true,
sizeGb: v.Size, state: v.State,
kmsKeyId: v.KmsKeyId ?? null,
attachedTo: v.Attachments?.[0]?.InstanceId ?? null,
});
}
}
}
return out;
},
};
skillRegistry.register(listEbsVolumes);
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/skills/github.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { Skill, skillRegistry } from "./types";

interface GhCreds { token: string; org: string }

async function gh<T>(c: GhCreds, path: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; data?: T }> {
const res = await fetch(`https://api.github.com${path}`, {
headers: {
Authorization: `Bearer ${c.token}`,
Accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
"X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28",
},
});
if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
return { ok: true, data: (await res.json()) as T };
}

const listRepoProtection: Skill = {
id: "github.listRepoBranchProtection",
description: "List repos with default-branch protection and required review counts",
inputSchema: z.object({}).passthrough(),
async execute(ctx) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<GhCreds>("GITHUB");
const repos: any[] = [];
let page = 1;
for (;;) {
const { ok, data } = await gh<any[]>(c, `/orgs/${c.org}/repos?per_page=100&page=${page}`);
if (!ok || !data?.length) break;
repos.push(...data.filter((r) => !r.archived));
if (data.length < 100) break;
page++;
}

const out: any[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < repos.length; i += 10) {
out.push(...await Promise.all(repos.slice(i, i + 10).map(async (r) => {
const branch = await gh<any>(c, `/repos/${r.full_name}/branches/${r.default_branch}`);
let protectedBranch = false, requiredApprovals = 0;
if (branch.ok && branch.data?.protected) {
protectedBranch = true;
// Protection detail requires elevated scopes; degrade to 0 so the
// check FAILS visibly rather than silently passing.
const prot = await gh<any>(c, `/repos/${r.full_name}/branches/${r.default_branch}/protection`);
if (prot.ok) {
requiredApprovals = prot.data?.required_pull_request_reviews
?.required_approving_review_count ?? 0;
}
}
return { repo: r.name, owner: c.org, defaultBranch: r.default_branch,
protected: protectedBranch, requiredApprovals, visibility: r.visibility };
})));
}
return out;
},
};

/** DSAR extension skill — commits authored by the subject across org repos. */
const subjectData: Skill = {
id: "github.subjectData",
description: "DSAR: commits authored by a subject (by author email) across org repos",
inputSchema: z.object({ email: z.string().email() }),
async execute(ctx, { email }) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<GhCreds>("GITHUB");
const res = await fetch(
`https://api.github.com/search/commits?q=${encodeURIComponent(`author-email:${email}`)}&per_page=30&sort=author-date`,
{ headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${c.token}`, Accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
"X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28" } });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`GitHub commit search ${res.status}`);
const data = (await res.json()) as { items?: any[] };
return { items: (data.items ?? []).map((it) => ({
source: "github", repo: it.repository?.full_name, sha: it.sha,
authoredAt: it.commit?.author?.date, message: it.commit?.message?.slice(0, 200),
})) };
},
};

skillRegistry.register(listRepoProtection);
skillRegistry.register(subjectData);
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/skills/hris.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { Skill, skillRegistry } from "./types";

interface BambooCreds { companyDomain: string; apiKey: string; reportId: string }

const redactEmail = (e: string) => e.replace(/^(.).*(@.*)$/, "$1***$2");

/**
* Composite skill: HRIS terminations ⟕ IdP active users.
* Config: BambooHR saved report "Platform: Terminations" containing
* Work Email, Status, and Termination Date columns.
*/
const offboardingDiff: Skill = {
id: "hris.offboardingDiff",
description: "Terminated employees (per HRIS) still holding active IdP accounts beyond grace period",
inputSchema: z.object({ graceHours: z.number().default(24) }),
async execute(ctx, input: { graceHours: number }) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<BambooCreds>("BAMBOOHR");
const auth = Buffer.from(`${c.apiKey}:x`).toString("base64");

const res = await fetch(
`https://api.bamboohr.com/api/gateway.php/${c.companyDomain}/v1/reports/${c.reportId}?format=json`,
{ headers: { Authorization: `Basic ${auth}`, Accept: "application/json" } },
);
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`BambooHR ${res.status}: report fetch failed`);
const report = (await res.json()) as { employees?: any[] };
const terminated = (report.employees ?? []).filter(
(e) => String(e["Status"] ?? "").toLowerCase().startsWith("terminated"),
);

const active = await skillRegistry.invoke<{ email: string; login: string; status: string }[]>(
"okta.listUsers", ctx.orgId, {});

const byEmail = new Map<string, { email: string; login: string; status: string }>(
active.filter((u) => u.status === "ACTIVE").map((u) => [u.email, u]));
const graceMs = input.graceHours * 3_600_000;

return terminated.flatMap((e) => {
const email = String(e["Work Email"] ?? "").toLowerCase().trim();
const stillActive = byEmail.get(email);
if (!stillActive) return [];
const terminatedAt = new Date(e["Termination Date"]);
const hoursSince = (Date.now() - terminatedAt.getTime()) / 3_600_000;
if (hoursSince < input.graceHours) return []; // within grace — not yet a violation
return [{
email: redactEmail(email), // lightly-redacted in stored findings
terminatedAt: terminatedAt.toISOString(),
hoursSinceTermination: Math.round(hoursSince),
oktaLogin: stillActive.login,
stillActiveInIdp: true,
}];
});
},
};

/** DSAR extension skill — HRIS record by work email, sensitive fields stripped. */
const subjectData: Skill = {
id: "bamboohr.subjectData",
description: "DSAR: HRIS employee record for a subject by work email",
inputSchema: z.object({ email: z.string().email() }),
async execute(ctx, { email }) {
const c = await ctx.getOrgCredentials<BambooCreds>("BAMBOOHR");
const auth = Buffer.from(`${c.apiKey}:x`).toString("base64");
const res = await fetch(
`https://api.bamboohr.com/api/gateway.php/${c.companyDomain}/v1/employees/directory`,
{ headers: { Authorization: `Basic ${auth}`, Accept: "application/json" } });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`BambooHR ${res.status}: directory fetch failed`);
const dir = (await res.json()) as { employees?: any[] };
const match = (dir.employees ?? []).find((e) =>
String(e.workEmail ?? e["Work Email"] ?? "").toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase());
if (!match) return { items: [] };
// Minimization: strip sensitive HR fields from the export
const fields = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(match).filter(
([k]) => !/ssn|salary|compensation|bank/i.test(k)));
return { items: [{ source: "bamboohr", employeeId: match.id, fields }] };
},
};

skillRegistry.register(offboardingDiff);
skillRegistry.register(subjectData);
```

```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { vendorTier } from "@core";

export const internalQueries: Record<string, (orgId: string, input: any) => Promise<unknown[]>> = {
"internal.lastAccessReview": async (_orgId, input: { controlId: string; titleMatch?: string }) => {
const last = await prisma.evidence.findFirst({
where: {
controlId: input.controlId,
title: { contains: input.titleMatch ?? "access review", mode: "insensitive" },
},
orderBy: { collectedAt: "desc" },
});
return [{
lastCompletedAt: last?.collectedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
daysSince: last ? Math.floor((Date.now() - last.collectedAt.getTime()) / 86_400_000) : 9999,
evidenceId: last?.id ?? null,
}];
},

/** Vendor check #1 — certifications on CRITICAL/HIGH vendors expiring within 45 days. */
"internal.vendorCertExpiry": async (orgId) => {
const certs = await prisma.vendorCertification.findMany({
where: { vendor: { orgId, criticality: { in: ["CRITICAL", "HIGH"] } } },
include: { vendor: true },
});
const now = Date.now();
return certs.map((c) => {
const daysUntil = c.expiresAt
? Math.floor((c.expiresAt.getTime() - now) / 86_400_000) : null;
return { vendor: c.vendor.name, tier: c.vendor.criticality, certType: c.type,
expiresAt: c.expiresAt?.toISOString() ?? null, daysUntil,
expiringSoon: daysUntil === null || daysUntil <= 45 };
});
},

/** Vendor check #2 — review cadence overdue per tier. */
"internal.vendorReviewCadence": async (orgId) => {
const vendors = await prisma.vendor.findMany({
where: { orgId },
include: { reviews: { orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 1 } },
});
const now = Date.now();
return vendors.map((v) => {
const due = v.reviews[0]?.nextReviewDue
?? new Date(v.createdAt.getTime() + v.reviewCadenceMonths * 30.44 * 86_400_000);
const daysOverdue = Math.floor((now - due.getTime()) / 86_400_000);
return { vendor: v.name,
tier: vendorTier({ criticality: v.criticality, dataAccess: v.dataAccess }).tier,
dueAt: due.toISOString(), daysOverdue, overdue: daysOverdue > 0 };
});
},
};
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/dsl.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";

export const QuerySchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
z.object({
type: z.literal("SKILL"),
skill: z.string(),
input: z.record(z.unknown()).default({}),
}),
z.object({
type: z.literal("INTERNAL"),
query: z.string(),
input: z.record(z.unknown()).default({}),
}),
]);

export const PassConditionSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
z.object({ type: z.literal("ALL_MATCH"), predicate: z.string() }),
z.object({ type: z.literal("NONE_MATCH"), predicate: z.string() }),
z.object({ type: z.literal("ANY_MATCH"), predicate: z.string() }),
z.object({ type: z.literal("COUNT_LTE"), predicate: z.string().optional(), limit: z.number().int().min(0) }),
z.object({ type: z.literal("COUNT_GTE"), predicate: z.string().optional(), limit: z.number().int().min(0) }),
]);

export const CheckDefinition = z.object({
checkId: z.string(),
version: z.number().int().default(1),
name: z.string(),
description: z.string().optional(),

query: QuerySchema,
minItems: z.number().int().min(0).default(0),

passCondition: PassConditionSchema,

evidence: z.object({
title: z.string(),
freshnessDays: z.number().int().default(90),
includeFindings: z.boolean().default(true),
redactFields: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
}),

onFail: z.object({
createTask: z.boolean().default(true),
taskPriority: z.enum(["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH", "CRITICAL"]).default("HIGH"),
taskTemplate: z.string(),
itemLabelPath: z.string().optional(),
}).optional(),

timeoutMs: z.number().int().default(120_000),
});
export type CheckDefinitionType = z.infer<typeof CheckDefinition>;
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/predicates.ts
```typescript
// Grammar: expr := clause ("&&" clause)*
// clause := path op literal
// op := == != >= <= > < in
// literal := true false null number 'str' "str" [lit,lit,...]
// path := dotted.field.access
// No eval(), no expression interpreter — a fixed grammar parsed to data.

type Val = boolean | number | string | null | (string | number)[];

const CLAUSE = /^(.+?)\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<|in)\s*(.+)$/;

function parseLiteral(raw: string): Val {
const t = raw.trim();
if (t === "true") return true;
if (t === "false") return false;
if (t === "null") return null;
if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(t)) return Number(t);
if (/^".*"$/.test(t) || /^'.*'$/.test(t)) return t.slice(1, -1);
if (t.startsWith("[") && t.endsWith("]")) {
return t.slice(1, -1).split(",")
.map((s) => parseLiteral(s))
.filter((v) => v !== null) as (string | number)[];
}
throw new Error(`Unparseable predicate literal: "${t}"`);
}

export function getPath(obj: unknown, path: string): unknown {
return path.split(".").reduce<unknown>(
(acc, k) => (acc != null && typeof acc === "object"
? (acc as Record<string, unknown>)[k] : undefined),
obj,
);
}

function compare(op: string, left: unknown, right: Val): boolean {
switch (op) {
case "==": return left === right;
case "!=": return left !== right;
case ">": return typeof left === "number" && typeof right === "number" && left > right;
case "<": return typeof left === "number" && typeof right === "number" && left < right;
case ">=": return typeof left === "number" && typeof right === "number" && left >= right;
case "<=": return typeof left === "number" && typeof right === "number" && left <= right;
case "in": return Array.isArray(right) && right.includes(left as never);
default: throw new Error(`Unknown operator "${op}"`);
}
}

function evalClause(item: unknown, clause: string): boolean {
const m = clause.trim().match(CLAUSE);
if (!m) throw new Error(`Bad predicate clause: "${clause}"`);
const [, path, op, lit] = m;
return compare(op, getPath(item, path), parseLiteral(lit));
}

export function itemMatches(item: unknown, expr: string): boolean {
return expr.split("&&").every((c) => evalClause(item, c));
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/evaluator.ts
```typescript
import { itemMatches } from "./predicates";
import type { CheckDefinitionType } from "./dsl";

export interface EvalResult {
status: "PASS" | "FAIL" | "INCONCLUSIVE";
matched: number;
failing: unknown[];
reason: string;
}

const ok = (n: number): EvalResult =>
({ status: "PASS", matched: n, failing: [], reason: "All conditions satisfied." });

export function evaluateCondition(def: CheckDefinitionType, items: unknown[]): EvalResult {
// Vacuous-pass guard: a suspiciously empty source would otherwise "pass"
// trivially (e.g. auth failure returning zero admins). minItems forces
// INCONCLUSIVE instead.
if (def.minItems > 0 && items.length < def.minItems) {
return {
status: "INCONCLUSIVE",
matched: 0, failing: [],
reason: `Query returned ${items.length} item(s); below minItems=${def.minItems}. ` +
`Check integration health or scope before trusting this result.`,
};
}

const pc = def.passCondition;
const predicateOf = () =>
(pc.type === "COUNT_LTE" || pc.type === "COUNT_GTE") ? (pc.predicate ?? "true") : (pc as { predicate: string }).predicate;

switch (pc.type) {
case "ALL_MATCH": {
const failing = items.filter((i) => !itemMatches(i, pc.predicate));
return failing.length === 0
? ok(items.length)
: { status: "FAIL", matched: items.length - failing.length, failing,
reason: `${failing.length}/${items.length} item(s) failed predicate "${pc.predicate}".` };
}
case "NONE_MATCH": {
const failing = items.filter((i) => itemMatches(i, pc.predicate));
return failing.length === 0
? ok(items.length)
: { status: "FAIL", matched: items.length - failing.length, failing,
reason: `${failing.length} item(s) matched forbidden predicate "${pc.predicate}".` };
}
case "ANY_MATCH": {
const n = items.filter((i) => itemMatches(i, pc.predicate)).length;
return n > 0
? { status: "PASS", matched: n, failing: [], reason: `${n} item(s) matched.` }
: { status: "FAIL", matched: 0, failing: items, reason: `No item matched "${pc.predicate}".` };
}
case "COUNT_LTE": {
const pred = pc.predicate ? predicateOf() : null;
const n = pred ? items.filter((i) => itemMatches(i, pred!)).length : items.length;
return n <= pc.limit
? ok(n)
: { status: "FAIL", matched: n, failing: items,
reason: `Count ${n} exceeds limit ${pc.limit}${pc.predicate ? ` (predicate "${pc.predicate}")` : ""}.` };
}
case "COUNT_GTE": {
const pred = pc.predicate ? predicateOf() : null;
const n = pred ? items.filter((i) => itemMatches(i, pred!)).length : items.length;
return n >= pc.limit
? { status: "PASS", matched: n, failing: [], reason: `Count ${n} meets minimum ${pc.limit}.` }
: { status: "FAIL", matched: n, failing: items, reason: `Count ${n} below minimum ${pc.limit}.` };
}
}
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/runner.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { appendEvidence } from "@core";
import { CheckDefinition } from "./dsl";
import { evaluateCondition } from "./evaluator";
import { getPath } from "./predicates";
import { skillRegistry } from "../skills/types";
import { internalQueries } from "../skills/internal";

interface QuerySpec { type: string; skill?: string; query?: string; input: Record<string, unknown> }

async function withTimeout<T>(ms: number, p: Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
return Promise.race([
p,
new Promise<T>((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error(`Check timed out after ${ms}ms`)), ms)),
]);
}

function redact(item: unknown, fields: string[]): unknown {
if (!fields.length || item == null || typeof item !== "object") return item;
const out = { ...(item as Record<string, unknown>) };
for (const f of fields) delete out[f];
return out;
}

async function executeQuery(orgId: string, q: QuerySpec) {
if (q.type === "SKILL") return skillRegistry.invoke(q.skill!, orgId, q.input);
const fn = internalQueries[q.query!];
if (!fn) throw new Error(`Unknown internal query: ${q.query}`);
return fn(orgId, q.input);
}

export async function runCheck(opts: { orgId: string; testId: string }) {
const test = await prisma.controlTest.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: opts.testId }, include: { control: true },
});
const def = CheckDefinition.parse(test.check);

// 1. Execute query
let findings: unknown[];
try {
const raw = await withTimeout(def.timeoutMs, executeQuery(opts.orgId, def.query as QuerySpec));
findings = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [raw];
} catch (err) {
const run = await prisma.testRun.create({
data: {
orgId: opts.orgId, controlId: test.controlId, testId: test.id,
status: "ERROR", finishedAt: new Date(),
summary: `Query failed: ${String(err).slice(0, 500)}`,
findings: { error: String(err).slice(0, 500) },
},
});
await prisma.controlTest.update({ where: { id: test.id }, data: { lastRunAt: new Date() } });
if (def.onFail?.createTask) {
await prisma.task.create({
data: {
orgId: opts.orgId, source: "TEST_FAILURE", priority: "HIGH",
title: `[ERROR] ${def.name} failed to execute`,
description: `The automated check errored (execution failure, not a control failure).\n\n` +
`Error: ${String(err).slice(0, 500)}\n\nCheck integration health.`,
relatedType: "TestRun", relatedId: run.id,
},
});
}
return { run, evidence: null };
}

// 2. Evaluate
const result = evaluateCondition(def, findings);
const stored = def.evidence.includeFindings
? findings.map((f) => redact(f, def.evidence.redactFields))
: [];

const run = await prisma.testRun.create({
data: {
orgId: opts.orgId, controlId: test.controlId, testId: test.id,
status: result.status, finishedAt: new Date(),
summary: `${def.name}: ${result.reason}`,
findings: {
eval: { matched: result.matched, total: findings.length },
items: stored,
failingCount: result.failing.length,
},
},
});
await prisma.controlTest.update({ where: { id: test.id }, data: { lastRunAt: new Date() } });

// 3. Hash-chained evidence (core owns the chain)
const evidence = await appendEvidence({
orgId: opts.orgId, controlId: test.controlId, sourceType: "AUTOMATED",
title: def.evidence.title,
description: result.reason,
expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + def.evidence.freshnessDays * 86_400_000),
payload: { items: stored, reason: result.reason,
checkId: def.checkId, checkVersion: def.version },
testRunId: run.id,
});

// 4. Remediation task on failure
if (result.status !== "PASS" && def.onFail?.createTask) {
const label = (i: unknown) =>
String(getPath(i, def.onFail!.itemLabelPath ?? "") ?? JSON.stringify(i));
const failingList = result.failing.slice(0, 5).map(label).join(", ");
const description = def.onFail.taskTemplate
.replaceAll("{{failCount}}", String(result.failing.length))
.replaceAll("{{failingList}}", failingList)
.replaceAll("{{reason}}", result.reason)
.replaceAll("{{checkName}}", def.name);
await prisma.task.create({
data: {
orgId: opts.orgId, source: "TEST_FAILURE", priority: def.onFail.taskPriority,
title: `[${result.status}] ${def.name}`,
description,
relatedType: "TestRun", relatedId: run.id,
},
});
}

return { run, evidence };
}
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/runner.instrumented.ts
```typescript
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { runCheck } from "./runner";

/**
* The Brain-instrumented runner — what workers actually call.
* Wraps the base runner with flow + outcome telemetry; no logic changes.
*/
export async function runCheckInstrumented(opts: { orgId: string; testId: string }) {
const test = await prisma.controlTest.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: opts.testId }, include: { control: true },
});
const checkId = (test.check as { checkId?: string })?.checkId ?? test.id;
const flowId = `chk-${randomUUID()}`;

emit({ type: "flow.started", source: "check-runner", orgId: opts.orgId, flowId,
payload: { flowType: "CHECK_RUN", checkId, controlSlug: test.control.slug } });

try {
const { run, evidence } = await runCheck(opts);

emit({
type: run.status === "PASS" ? "control.test.passed" : "control.test.failed",
source: "check-runner", orgId: opts.orgId, flowId,
severityOverride: run.status === "PASS" ? undefined : "ERROR",
payload: { checkId, controlSlug: test.control.slug, status: run.status,
evidenceId: evidence?.id, sequence: evidence?.sequence },
});
if (run.status === "FAIL") {
emit({ type: "gap.detected", source: "check-runner", orgId: opts.orgId,
payload: { controlSlug: test.control.slug, reason: run.summary } });
}
emit({ type: "flow.completed", source: "check-runner", orgId: opts.orgId, flowId,
payload: { flowType: "CHECK_RUN", status: run.status } });
return { run, evidence };
} catch (err) {
emit({ type: "flow.failed", source: "check-runner", orgId: opts.orgId, flowId,
severityOverride: "ERROR",
payload: { flowType: "CHECK_RUN", error: String(err).slice(0, 300) } });
throw err;
}
}
```

```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import type { CheckDefinitionType } from "../dsl";

type StarterCheck = CheckDefinitionType & { controlSlug: string; cron: string };

export const STARTER_CHECKS: StarterCheck[] = [
{
// 1. Okta admins have MFA — SOC2 CC6.1 / ISO 8.5 / HIPAA 164.312(d)
checkId: "okta-admin-mfa", version: 1, name: "All Okta admins have MFA enrolled",
controlSlug: "access.adminMfa", cron: "0 6 * * *",
query: { type: "SKILL", skill: "okta.listAdmins", input: {} },
minItems: 1, // zero admins = integration problem, not a pass
passCondition: { type: "NONE_MATCH", predicate: "mfaEnrolled == false" },
evidence: { title: "MFA enrollment status for administrative users (Okta)",
freshnessDays: 90, includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH", itemLabelPath: "login",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} administrative user(s) lack MFA enrollment: {{failingList}}.\n\n" +
"Admin accounts without MFA are an immediate SOC 2 / ISO 27001 audit finding.\n{{reason}}" },
},
{
// 2. EBS volumes encrypted at rest — CC6.1 / ISO 8.24 / HIPAA 164.312(a)(2)(iv)
checkId: "aws-ebs-encryption", version: 1, name: "All EBS volumes encrypted at rest",
controlSlug: "crypto.atRest", cron: "0 7 * * *",
query: { type: "SKILL", skill: "aws.listEbsVolumes", input: {} },
minItems: 1,
passCondition: { type: "NONE_MATCH", predicate: "encrypted == false" },
evidence: { title: "Encryption status of EBS volumes (AWS)", freshnessDays: 90,
includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH", itemLabelPath: "volumeId",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} EBS volume(s) are unencrypted: {{failingList}}.\n\n" +
"Encrypt via EBS encryption-by-default + snapshot-copy-migrate for existing volumes.\n{{reason}}" },
},
{
// 3. Offboarding within 24h — CC6.2 / ISO 5.11+6.5 / HIPAA 164.308(a)(2)(ii)(C)
checkId: "offboarding-24h", version: 1,
name: "No terminated employee retains an active IdP account beyond 24h",
controlSlug: "access.offboarding", cron: "0 8 * * *",
query: { type: "SKILL", skill: "hris.offboardingDiff", input: { graceHours: 24 } },
minItems: 0, // zero lingering accounts IS a valid pass
passCondition: { type: "COUNT_LTE", predicate: "stillActiveInIdp == true", limit: 0 },
evidence: { title: "HRIS ⟕ IdP offboarding reconciliation", freshnessDays: 90,
includeFindings: true, redactFields: ["oktaLogin"] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "CRITICAL", itemLabelPath: "email",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} terminated employee(s) still have active accounts " +
"(beyond 24h grace): {{failingList}}.\n\nDeprovision immediately and review " +
"what each account accessed since termination date.\n{{reason}}" },
},
{
// 4. Quarterly access review completed — CC6.2/CC6.3 / ISO 5.18 / HIPAA 164.308(a)(3)(ii)(B)
checkId: "quarterly-access-review", version: 1,
name: "Access review completed within the last 92 days",
controlSlug: "access.quarterlyReview", cron: "0 9 * * *",
query: { type: "INTERNAL", query: "internal.lastAccessReview",
input: { controlId: "$CONTROL_ID" } }, // resolved at seed time per org
minItems: 1,
passCondition: { type: "ALL_MATCH", predicate: "daysSince <= 92" },
evidence: { title: "Cadence of periodic access review", freshnessDays: 92,
includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "MEDIUM",
taskTemplate: "Access review is overdue — last completed {{reason}}.\n\n" +
"Run the quarterly access review and attach results as evidence." },
},
{
// 5. Branch protection on all repos — CC8.1 / ISO 8.32
checkId: "github-branch-protection", version: 1,
name: "All repos enforce default-branch protection with required reviews",
controlSlug: "change.branchProtection", cron: "0 10 * * *",
query: { type: "SKILL", skill: "github.listRepoBranchProtection", input: {} },
minItems: 1,
passCondition: { type: "ALL_MATCH", predicate: "protected == true && requiredApprovals >= 1" },
evidence: { title: "Default-branch protection across repositories (GitHub)",
freshnessDays: 90, includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH", itemLabelPath: "repo",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} repository/repositories lack branch protection or required " +
"reviews: {{failingList}}.\n\nEnable branch protection with ≥1 required approving " +
"review on the default branch.\n{{reason}}" },
},
{
// 6. Vendor certifications current — CC9.2 / ISO 5.22 / HIPAA 164.308(a)(8)
checkId: "vendor-cert-expiry", version: 1,
name: "Critical/high vendor certifications are current",
controlSlug: "vendor.riskProgram", cron: "0 11 * * 1",
query: { type: "INTERNAL", query: "internal.vendorCertExpiry", input: {} },
minItems: 0,
passCondition: { type: "COUNT_LTE", predicate: "expiringSoon == true", limit: 0 },
evidence: { title: "Vendor certification expiry status (CRITICAL/HIGH vendors)",
freshnessDays: 30, includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH", itemLabelPath: "vendor",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} certification(s) on critical/high vendors expired or expiring " +
"within 45 days: {{failingList}}.\n\nRequest refreshed reports (SOC 2 / ISO / pen test) " +
"from the vendors.\n{{reason}}" },
},
{
// 7. Vendor review cadence — CC9.2 / ISO 5.22
checkId: "vendor-review-cadence", version: 1,
name: "Vendor reviews completed within tier cadence",
controlSlug: "vendor.riskProgram", cron: "0 11 * * 1",
query: { type: "INTERNAL", query: "internal.vendorReviewCadence", input: {} },
minItems: 0,
passCondition: { type: "COUNT_LTE", predicate: "overdue == true", limit: 0 },
evidence: { title: "Vendor review cadence status", freshnessDays: 30,
includeFindings: true, redactFields: [] },
onFail: { createTask: true, taskPriority: "MEDIUM", itemLabelPath: "vendor",
taskTemplate: "{{failCount}} vendor review(s) overdue: {{failingList}}.\n\n" +
"Complete the tier-appropriate review and record it.\n{{reason}}" },
},
];

/** Registers each check as a ControlTest bound to the org's control by slug.
* Skips checks whose control template wasn't instantiated. */
export async function seedStarterChecks(orgId: string) {
let created = 0;
for (const check of STARTER_CHECKS) {
const control = await prisma.control.findFirst({
where: { orgId, slug: check.controlSlug },
});
if (!control) continue;

const { controlSlug, cron, ...def } = check;
const checkJson = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(def));
// Resolve the $CONTROL_ID placeholder (access-review check)
if (checkJson.query?.input?.controlId === "$CONTROL_ID")
checkJson.query.input.controlId = control.id;

await prisma.controlTest.upsert({
where: { id: `${orgId}:${def.checkId}` },
update: { check: checkJson, schedule: cron, name: def.name, controlId: control.id },
create: {
id: `${orgId}:${def.checkId}`, orgId, controlId: control.id,
name: def.name, check: checkJson, schedule: cron, enabled: true,
},
});
created++;
}
return { created, total: STARTER_CHECKS.length };
}
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/checks/checks.test.ts
```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { itemMatches } from "./predicates";
import { evaluateCondition } from "./evaluator";
import { CheckDefinition } from "./dsl";

const def = (over: any) => CheckDefinition.parse({
checkId: "t", version: 1, name: "t",
query: { type: "SKILL", skill: "x", input: {} },
passCondition: over.passCondition,
evidence: { title: "t" },
...over,
});

describe("predicates", () => {
it("evaluates simple clauses", () => {
expect(itemMatches({ a: true }, "a == true")).toBe(true);
expect(itemMatches({ n: 3 }, "n >= 3")).toBe(true);
expect(itemMatches({ s: "x" }, "s in ['x','y']")).toBe(true);
expect(itemMatches({ n: 5 }, "n <= 2")).toBe(false);
});
it("supports && conjunctions", () => {
expect(itemMatches({ a: true, b: 2 }, "a == true && b >= 1")).toBe(true);
expect(itemMatches({ a: true, b: 0 }, "a == true && b >= 1")).toBe(false);
});
it("handles missing paths as undefined", () => {
expect(itemMatches({}, "missing == true")).toBe(false);
expect(itemMatches({}, "missing != true")).toBe(true);
});
});

describe("evaluator", () => {
it("NONE_MATCH catches violations", () => {
const r = evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: { type: "NONE_MATCH", predicate: "mfaEnrolled == false" } }),
[{ login: "a", mfaEnrolled: true }, { login: "b", mfaEnrolled: false }]);
expect(r.status).toBe("FAIL");
expect(r.failing).toHaveLength(1);
});
it("empty set passes without minItems guard", () => {
const r = evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: { type: "NONE_MATCH", predicate: "x == true" } }), []);
expect(r.status).toBe("PASS");
});
it("minItems converts vacuous pass to INCONCLUSIVE", () => {
const r = evaluateCondition(def({ minItems: 1, passCondition: { type: "NONE_MATCH", predicate: "x == true" } }), []);
expect(r.status).toBe("INCONCLUSIVE");
});
it("COUNT_LTE with predicate", () => {
const c = { type: "COUNT_LTE", predicate: "stillActiveInIdp == true", limit: 0 } as const;
expect(evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: c }),
[{ stillActiveInIdp: true }]).status).toBe("FAIL");
expect(evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: c }), []).status).toBe("PASS");
});
it("ALL_MATCH with && predicate (branch protection)", () => {
const c = { type: "ALL_MATCH", predicate: "protected == true && requiredApprovals >= 1" } as const;
expect(evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: c }),
[{ protected: true, requiredApprovals: 1 }]).status).toBe("PASS");
expect(evaluateCondition(def({ passCondition: c }),
[{ protected: true, requiredApprovals: 0 }]).status).toBe("FAIL");
});
});
```

### FILE: packages/skills/src/connectors/types.ts
```typescript
import type { IntegrationType } from "@prisma/client";

export interface EvidenceSpec {
/** Canonical dataType, e.g. "okta.users", "aws.ebs" */
dataType: string;
title: string; // evidence title when persisted
freshnessDays: number;
/** Control templates that consume this data — used to anchor evidence */
controlTemplateIds: string[];
/** true → content change creates a drift task */
expectStable?: boolean;
}

export interface NormalizedSnapshot {
dataType: string;
items: unknown[];
contentHash: string; // sha256 of canonical items — drift detection key
sourceHealth: "HEALTHY" | "DEGRADED";
healthNote?: string;
collectedAt: Date;
}

export interface ConnectorContext { orgId: string }

export interface Connector {
type: IntegrationType;
/** What this connector can collect — resolved against org controls on connect. */
specs(): EvidenceSpec[];
/** Collect one dataType. MUST be idempotent; raises on total failure. */
collect(ctx: ConnectorContext, dataType: string): Promise<NormalizedSnapshot>;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/skills/src/connectors/registry.ts
```typescript
import type { IntegrationType } from "@prisma/client";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import type { Connector, EvidenceSpec } from "./types";

const connectors = new Map<IntegrationType, Connector>();

export function registerConnector(c: Connector) { connectors.set(c.type, c); }

export function connectorFor(type: IntegrationType): Connector {
const c = connectors.get(type);
if (!c) throw new Error(`No connector for ${type}`);
return c;
}

export interface ResolvedSpec extends EvidenceSpec {
controlId: string | null; // primary evidence anchor (first matching control)
attached: boolean;
}

/**
* On connect: resolve which controls each spec feeds.
* CONSOLIDATION FIX: no sync-ControlTests are created here — continuous
* evidence sync runs via the Temporal evidenceSyncWorkflow, which the API
* layer starts with the returned specs. ControlTests remain the
* predicate-based testing layer (starter checks in checks/definitions).
*/
export async function onIntegrationConnected(
orgId: string, integrationId: string, type: IntegrationType,
): Promise<{ specs: ResolvedSpec[]; attached: string[] }> {
const connector = connectorFor(type);
const specs = connector.specs();
const resolved: ResolvedSpec[] = [];

for (const spec of specs) {
const controls = await prisma.control.findMany({
where: { orgId, slug: { in: spec.controlTemplateIds } },
});
resolved.push({
...spec,
controlId: controls[0]?.id ?? null,
attached: controls.length > 0,
});
}
return {
specs: resolved,
attached: resolved.filter((r) => r.attached).map((r) => r.dataType),
};
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/skills/src/connectors/okta.ts
```typescript
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { canonicalJSON } from "@core";
import { skillRegistry } from "../skills/types";
import { Connector, registerConnector } from "./types";

const hash = (v: unknown) => createHash("sha256").update(canonicalJSON(v)).digest("hex");

const oktaConnector: Connector = {
type: "OKTA",
specs: () => [
{ dataType: "okta.users", title: "Active directory users (Okta)",
freshnessDays: 90, controlTemplateIds: ["access.provisioning", "access.quarterlyReview"] },
{ dataType: "okta.admins", title: "Administrative users and MFA status (Okta)",
freshnessDays: 90, controlTemplateIds: ["access.adminMfa"], expectStable: true },
{ dataType: "okta.appAssignments", title: "Application access assignments (Okta)",
freshnessDays: 90, controlTemplateIds: ["access.provisioning"] },
],
async collect(ctx, dataType) {
const skill = {
"okta.users": "okta.listUsers",
"okta.admins": "okta.listAdmins",
"okta.appAssignments": "okta.listAppAssignments",
}[dataType];
if (!skill) throw new Error(`okta: unknown dataType ${dataType}`);
const items = await skillRegistry.invoke<unknown[]>(skill, ctx.orgId, {});
return { dataType, items, contentHash: hash(items),
sourceHealth: "HEALTHY", collectedAt: new Date() };
},
};
registerConnector(oktaConnector);
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/helpers.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE of the batch-2 version — orgId threaded through fetchSubjectData
// (batch-2 passed a bogus "platform" org to the skill registry).
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { saltedHash, sha256 } from "../utils/canonical";

export const generateToken = (bytes = 32) => randomBytes(bytes).toString("base64url");

/**
* Subject-data dispatcher: routes a DSAR query to the connector skill for
* the system's integration type (naming convention:
* `${integrationType.toLowerCase()}.subjectData`).
* Registered: okta.subjectData, github.subjectData, bamboohr.subjectData.
* Unregistered types (e.g. aws) throw → drills surface them as manual systems.
*/
export async function fetchSubjectData(
orgId: string,
system: { systemName: string; integrationType: string | null },
email: string,
): Promise<{ items: unknown[] }> {
if (!system.integrationType) throw new Error("manual-system");
const skillId = `${system.integrationType.toLowerCase()}.subjectData`;
const { skillRegistry } = await import("@skills"); // dynamic: avoids core→skills static dep
return skillRegistry.invoke<{ items: unknown[] }>(skillId, orgId, { email })
.catch(() => { throw new Error(`no-subject-data-skill:${skillId}`); });
}

/** STUB: email delivery — swap for SES/Postmark in production. */
export async function sendMagicLink(email: string, token: string): Promise<void> {
console.log(`[privacy:STUB] magic link for ${email.replace(/(.{2}).*(@.*)/, "$1***$2")}: /verify?token=${token.slice(0, 8)}…`);
}

/** STUB: zip builder — swap for `archiver` in production. Returns JSON buffer. */
export async function buildZip(export_: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Buffer> {
return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(export_, null, 2), "utf8");
}

export interface Redaction { system: string; field: string; reason: string }

export function applyRedactions(
export_: Record<string, unknown>, redactions: Redaction[],
): Record<string, unknown> {
const out = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(export_)) as Record<string, any>;
for (const r of redactions) {
const sys = out[r.system];
if (sys?.data && Array.isArray(sys.data)) {
for (const item of sys.data) if (r.field in item) item[r.field] = "[REDACTED]";
}
}
return out;
}

/** STUB: Art.19 downstream-recipient notification — webhook + log. */
export async function notifyRecipients(
orgId: string, email: string,
actions: { system: string; action: string }[],
): Promise<void> {
console.log(`[privacy:STUB] Art.19 erasure notice for ${saltedHash(email).slice(0, 12)}… ` +
`→ ${actions.filter((a) => a.action !== "HELD").length} systems`);
}

/**
* STUB: per-subject crypto-shred. Production: per-subject wrapped DEKs
* (ethics-crypto envelope pattern) dropped for this subject's rows.
*/
export async function cryptoShredSubject(
orgId: string, system: { systemName: string }, email: string,
): Promise<void> {
console.log(`[privacy:STUB] crypto-shred ${saltedHash(email).slice(0, 12)}… ` +
`in ${system.systemName} (org ${orgId})`);
}

/** Heuristic third-party-data counter for drills (Art.15(4) redaction risk). */
export function countThirdPartyData(items: unknown[]): number {
return items.filter((i) => {
const s = JSON.stringify(i).toLowerCase();
return s.includes("@") && (s.includes("name") || s.includes("email"));
}).length;
}

export { saltedHash, sha256 };
```
