### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/compile.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE of the batch-2 version — orgId threaded to fetchSubjectData.
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { runAgent } from "@agents/runtime";
import { fetchSubjectData, buildZip, applyRedactions, Redaction } from "./helpers";

/** Article 15 access compilation: gather → legal review → redact → package. */
export async function compileAccessExport(orgId: string, requestId: string) {
const request = await prisma.privacyRequest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: requestId } });
const map = await prisma.dataMapEntry.findMany({ where: { orgId } });
const export_: Record<string, unknown> = {};
const errors: string[] = [];

for (const system of map) {
try {
const snapshot = await fetchSubjectData(orgId, system, request.email);
if (snapshot.items.length) {
export_[system.systemName] = {
dataCategories: system.dataCategories,
purposes: system.purposes,
data: snapshot.items,
};
}
} catch (e) {
errors.push(system.systemName); // unreachable systems surface as drill gaps
}
}

// Legal review flags: third-party personal data (Art.15(4)), trade secrets,
// active legal holds — agent proposes redactions; human confirms in REVIEW.
const review = await runAgent<{ redactions: Redaction[]; ready: boolean }>(
"dsar_review", { systems: Object.keys(export_), request: { type: request.type } });

const buf = await buildZip(applyRedactions(export_, review.redactions ?? []));
// STUB: production uploads to S3 with the org privacy KMS key + 14d TTL.
const key = `dsar/${orgId}/${requestId}.json`;
console.log(`[privacy:STUB] compiled export ${key} (${buf.length} bytes, ` +
`${Object.keys(export_).length} systems, ${errors.length} unreachable)`);

await prisma.privacyRequest.update({ where: { id: requestId },
data: { status: "REVIEW" } });
await prisma.privacyRequestEvent.create({
data: { requestId, kind: "COMPILED", details: JSON.stringify(
{ systems: Object.keys(export_), errors, redactions: review.redactions?.length ?? 0 }) } });
return { key, systems: Object.keys(export_).length, errors,
redactions: review.redactions ?? [] };
}
```

### FILE: packages/core/src/privacy/drills.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE of the batch-2 version — orgId threaded to fetchSubjectData.
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { fetchSubjectData, countThirdPartyData } from "./helpers";
import { createGapTask } from "../gaps/detect";

/**
* Quarterly synthetic DSAR exercise = real Art.15 evidence.
* Exercises the ACCESS path only — compile is read-only by construction,
* so a drill can never mutate production systems.
*/
export async function runDsarDrill(orgId: string) {
const start = Date.now();
const syntheticEmail = `dsar-drill-${Date.now()}@drill.internal`;
const map = await prisma.dataMapEntry.findMany({ where: { orgId } });

let systemsReached = 0, redactions = 0;
const results: Record<string, "REACHED" | "EMPTY" | "ERROR"> = {};
for (const system of map) {
try {
const snap = await fetchSubjectData(orgId, system, syntheticEmail);
results[system.systemName] = snap.items.length ? "REACHED" : "EMPTY";
if (snap.items.length) { systemsReached++; redactions += countThirdPartyData(snap.items); }
} catch { results[system.systemName] = "ERROR"; }
}

const systemsTotal = Object.values(results).filter((r) => r !== "EMPTY").length;
const outcome = Object.values(results).includes("ERROR")
? "PARTIAL" : systemsTotal === 0 ? "FAIL" : "PASS";

const drill = await prisma.dsarDrill.create({
data: { orgId, systemsReached, systemsTotal, compileMs: Date.now() - start,
redactionsApplied: redactions, outcome },
});

// ERROR systems become remediation tasks — they'd fail a real DSAR too
for (const [name, r] of Object.entries(results)) {
if (r === "ERROR")
await createGapTask(orgId, `DSAR drill: ${name} unreachable`,
"The quarterly access-request drill could not query this system. " +
"A real Art.15 request would fail. Fix the connector or document the manual path.",
"HIGH");
}
return drill;
}
```

```typescript
import OpenAI from "openai";

/**
* Shared embedding client (consolidation fix: one implementation consumed by
* knowledge chunking AND questionnaire retrieval). Default profile =
* text-embedding-3-small @ 1536 dims, matching the pgvector columns.
* Multilingual/on-prem profiles live in the questionnaire package (batch 5).
*/
const openai = new OpenAI();
export const EMBED_DIMS = 1536;

export async function embedTexts(texts: string[]): Promise<number[][]> {
if (texts.length === 0) return [];
const res = await openai.embeddings.create({
model: "text-embedding-3-small", input: texts,
});
return res.data.map((d) => d.embedding);
}
```

### FILE: packages/agents/src/runtime.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { readFileSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import Anthropic from "@anthropic-ai/sdk";
import OpenAI from "openai";

// ── LLM ABSTRACTION ─────────────────────────────────────────────
interface CompletionRequest {
system: string;
messages: { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }[];
maxTokens: number;
temperature: number;
jsonMode: boolean;
}
interface CompletionResult {
text: string; promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number;
model: string; provider: "anthropic" | "openai";
}

const anthropic = new Anthropic(); // ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
const openai = new OpenAI(); // OPENAI_API_KEY

const PRICING: Record<string, { in: number; out: number }> = {
"claude-sonnet-4-20250514": { in: 3 / 1e6, out: 15 / 1e6 },
"claude-3-5-haiku-20241022": { in: 0.8 / 1e6, out: 4 / 1e6 },
"gpt-4o": { in: 2.5 / 1e6, out: 10 / 1e6 },
};

async function complete(req: CompletionRequest): Promise<CompletionResult> {
const primary = process.env.LLM_PRIMARY ?? "anthropic";
try {
return primary === "anthropic" ? await anthropicComplete(req) : await openaiComplete(req);
} catch (err) {
if (process.env.LLM_FALLBACK === "off") throw err;
return primary === "anthropic" ? openaiComplete(req) : anthropicComplete(req);
}
}

async function anthropicComplete(req: CompletionRequest): Promise<CompletionResult> {
const model = req.maxTokens > 2000 ? "claude-sonnet-4-20250514" : "claude-3-5-haiku-20241022";
const res = await anthropic.messages.create({
model, max_tokens: req.maxTokens, temperature: req.temperature, system: req.system,
messages: req.messages.map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),
});
const text = res.content.filter((b) => b.type === "text").map((b) => b.text).join("");
return { text, model, provider: "anthropic",
promptTokens: res.usage.input_tokens, completionTokens: res.usage.output_tokens };
}

async function openaiComplete(req: CompletionRequest): Promise<CompletionResult> {
const res = await openai.chat.completions.create({
model: "gpt-4o", max_tokens: req.maxTokens, temperature: req.temperature,
...(req.jsonMode ? { response_format: { type: "json_object" as const } } : {}),
messages: [{ role: "system", content: req.system }, ...req.messages],
});
return { text: res.choices[0]?.message?.content ?? "",
model: "gpt-4o", provider: "openai",
promptTokens: res.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0,
completionTokens: res.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0 };
}

function costUsd(model: string, pt: number, ct: number): number {
const p = PRICING[model] ?? { in: 3 / 1e6, out: 15 / 1e6 };
return pt * p.in + ct * p.out;
}

// ── AGENT REGISTRY ──────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface AgentDef {
name: string;
promptKey: string; // PromptTemplate.key + prompts/<key>.md
fallbackSystem: string; // code default if DB and file are absent
model: "sonnet" | "haiku";
maxTokens: number;
budget: { maxUsdPerRun: number };
redact?: RegExp[]; // guardrail: strip from output
}

export const AGENTS: Record<string, AgentDef> = {
policy_drafter: { name: "policy_drafter", promptKey: "policy_drafter",
fallbackSystem: "You are a senior compliance policy writer. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 4000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.25 } },
policy_critic: { name: "policy_critic", promptKey: "policy_critic",
fallbackSystem: "You are a compliance auditor reviewing a draft policy. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 2000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.15 } },
policy_change_classifier: { name: "policy_change_classifier", promptKey: "policy_change_classifier",
fallbackSystem: "Classify a policy change. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "haiku", maxTokens: 1500, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.05 } },
question_segmenter: { name: "question_segmenter", promptKey: "question_segmenter",
fallbackSystem: "Split questionnaire questions into atomic units. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "haiku", maxTokens: 1000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.03 } },
questionnaire_composer: { name: "questionnaire_composer", promptKey: "questionnaire_composer",
fallbackSystem: "Answer ONLY from provided context. Cite sources. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 1500, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.12 } },
change_extractor: { name: "change_extractor", promptKey: "change_extractor",
fallbackSystem: "Extract regulatory changes conservatively. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "haiku", maxTokens: 2000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.05 } },
impact_analyst: { name: "impact_analyst", promptKey: "impact_analyst",
fallbackSystem: "Grade regulatory impact per framework requirement. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 2500, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.15 } },
matrix_amendment_extractor: { name: "matrix_amendment_extractor", promptKey: "matrix_amendment_extractor",
fallbackSystem: "Amend a state breach rule from enacted bill text. Quote statutory language. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 3000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.2 } },
bill_topic_classifier: { name: "bill_topic_classifier", promptKey: "bill_topic_classifier",
fallbackSystem: "Classify bill relevance to breach/privacy law. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "haiku", maxTokens: 800, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.02 } },
ops_diagnostician: { name: "ops_diagnostician", promptKey: "ops_diagnostician",
fallbackSystem: "Investigate platform health using event-lake tools. Cite event IDs. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 2500, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.15 } },
dsar_review: { name: "dsar_review", promptKey: "dsar_review",
fallbackSystem: "Flag third-party data and legal-hold issues in a DSAR export. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "haiku", maxTokens: 2000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.05 } },
executive_report: { name: "executive_report", promptKey: "executive_report",
fallbackSystem: "Write an executive compliance narrative from metrics. Every claim references a metric. Output valid JSON only.",
model: "sonnet", maxTokens: 3000, budget: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.2 } },
};

// ── PROMPT RESOLUTION: DB version → .md file → inline fallback ──
const PROMPT_DIR = join(__dirname, "prompts");

async function resolvePrompt(key: string, fallback: string): Promise<{ system: string; version: number }> {
const row = await prisma.promptTemplate.findFirst({
where: { key, status: "ACTIVE" }, orderBy: { version: "desc" },
});
if (row) return { system: row.systemPrompt, version: row.version };
const file = join(PROMPT_DIR, `${key}.md`);
if (existsSync(file)) return { system: readFileSync(file, "utf8"), version: 0 };
return { system: fallback, version: 0 };
}

/** Renders {{mustache}} templates from a flat context object. */
function render(template: string, ctx: Record<string, unknown>): string {
return template.replace(/\{\{(\w+)\}\}/g, (_, k) =>
k in ctx ? String(ctx[k]) : `{{${k}}}`);
}

// ── THE ENTRY POINT every AI feature calls ──────────────────────
export async function runAgent<T = unknown>(
name: string,
input: unknown,
opts?: { orgId?: string },
): Promise<T> {
const def = AGENTS[name];
if (!def) throw new Error(`Unknown agent: ${name}`);

const { system, version } = await resolvePrompt(def.promptKey, def.fallbackSystem);
const userContent = typeof input === "string" ? input : JSON.stringify(input, null, 2);

const job = await prisma.generationJob.create({
data: {
orgId: opts?.orgId ?? "platform", promptKey: def.promptKey, promptVersion: version,
model: def.model, inputContext: input as object, status: "RUNNING",
},
});

const t0 = Date.now();
try {
let result = await complete({
system, messages: [{ role: "user", content: userContent }],
maxTokens: def.maxTokens, temperature: def.model === "haiku" ? 0.1 : 0.3,
jsonMode: true,
});
let parsed = tryParseJson(result.text);

// Repair retry on parse failure (one shot only)
if (parsed === null) {
const repaired = await complete({
system,
messages: [
{ role: "user", content: userContent },
{ role: "assistant", content: result.text },
{ role: "user", content: "Your previous output was not valid JSON. Output ONLY valid JSON matching the schema, no prose, no markdown fences." },
],
maxTokens: def.maxTokens, temperature: 0, jsonMode: true,
});
parsed = tryParseJson(repaired.text);
result = { ...repaired,
promptTokens: result.promptTokens + repaired.promptTokens,
completionTokens: result.completionTokens + repaired.completionTokens };
}
if (parsed === null) throw new Error("Agent failed to produce valid JSON after repair retry");

// Guardrails (FIX: batch-1 version discarded the redacted string)
let output = parsed;
if (def.redact?.length) {
let json = JSON.stringify(parsed);
for (const re of def.redact) json = json.replace(re, "[REDACTED]");
output = JSON.parse(json);
}

const cost = costUsd(result.model, result.promptTokens, result.completionTokens);
if (cost > def.budget.maxUsdPerRun)
console.warn(`[agents] ${name} exceeded budget: $${cost.toFixed(4)} > $${def.budget.maxUsdPerRun}`);

await prisma.generationJob.update({
where: { id: job.id },
data: { output, status: "SUCCEEDED", completedAt: new Date(),
promptTokens: result.promptTokens, completionTokens: result.completionTokens,
costUsd: cost, latencyMs: Date.now() - t0, model: result.model },
});

// Brain telemetry (lazy import — agents must not hard-depend on brain boot)
const { emit } = await import("@brain/emit");
emit({ type: "llm.generation.completed", source: "agent", orgId: opts?.orgId,
payload: { agent: name, costUsd: cost, latencyMs: Date.now() - t0 } });

return output as T;
} catch (err) {
await prisma.generationJob.update({
where: { id: job.id },
data: { status: "FAILED", error: String(err).slice(0, 500), completedAt: new Date() },
});
const { emit } = await import("@brain/emit");
emit({ type: "llm.generation.failed", source: "agent", orgId: opts?.orgId,
severityOverride: "ERROR", payload: { agent: name, error: String(err).slice(0, 200) } });
throw err;
}
}

function tryParseJson(text: string): unknown | null {
const cleaned = text.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/m, "").replace(/```\s*$/m, "").trim();
try { return JSON.parse(cleaned); } catch { return null; }
}

export { render };
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/agents/src/prompts/index.ts
```typescript
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { AGENTS } from "../runtime";

const PROMPT_DIR = __dirname;

/** All .md prompt files in this directory, keyed by filename (prompt key). */
export function loadPromptFiles(): Record<string, string> {
const out: Record<string, string> = {};
if (!existsSync(PROMPT_DIR)) return out;
for (const f of readdirSync(PROMPT_DIR)) {
if (f.endsWith(".md")) out[f.replace(/\.md$/, "")] = readFileSync(join(PROMPT_DIR, f), "utf8");
}
return out;
}

/** Seed rows for db:seed:prompts — file content where present, fallback otherwise. */
export const PROMPT_SEED_FILES = (() => {
const files = loadPromptFiles();
return Object.values(AGENTS).map((a) => ({
key: a.promptKey, version: 1, name: a.name,
systemPrompt: files[a.promptKey] ?? a.fallbackSystem,
userTemplate: "{{input}}",
modelConfig: { model: a.model, maxTokens: a.maxTokens },
status: "ACTIVE",
}));
})();
```

### FILE: packages/agents/src/prompts/policy_drafter.md
```text
You are a senior compliance policy writer drafting a policy for
{{org.name}}, a {{org.industry}} company with {{org.employeeCount}} employees.

CONTEXT:
- Framework requirements this policy must satisfy:
{{#each requirements}}
[{{this.framework}} {{this.code}}] {{this.paraphrased_description}}
{{/each}}
- Existing controls that implement these requirements:
{{#each controls}}
- {{this.name}}: {{this.description}}
{{/each}}
- Company profile: {{org.profile_notes}}

RULES:
1. Write in plain English, ~10th-grade reading level. No legalese.
2. Structure: Purpose | Scope | Policy Statements | Roles & Responsibilities
| Exceptions & Approval | Enforcement | Review Cadence.
3. Every policy statement must be TESTABLE — an auditor must be able to
verify it from evidence. Prefer "Access reviews occur at least quarterly"
over "Access is reviewed periodically."
4. Where requirements conflict, adopt the STRICTEST rule and note in a
comment which requirement drove it.
5. Use [BRACKETED_PLACEHOLDERS] for org-specific values you don't know.
6. Never reproduce framework text verbatim — paraphrase obligations only.
7. Roles must map to RACI: state who performs, who approves, who audits.

OUTPUT: Valid JSON only, matching this schema:
{
"title": string,
"contentMd": string,
"sections": [{ "heading": string, "body": string,
"requirement_refs": ["CC6.1", "A.5.18"] }],
"exceptions": string,
"review_cadence_months": number,
"approval_chain": ["{{role}}"],
"open_questions": [string] // flags for the human reviewer
}
```

### FILE: packages/agents/src/prompts/question_segmenter.md
```text
Split security-questionnaire questions into atomic, independently-answerable
units. Split when a question asks about two distinct controls, scopes, or
timeframes ("Do you encrypt at rest and in transit?"). Do NOT split when
clauses are one coherent ask ("Describe your encryption key rotation process,
including algorithms").

EXAMPLES:
- "Do you perform background checks on employees and contractors?"
→ ["Do you perform background checks on employees?",
"Do you perform background checks on contractors?"]
- "How long do you retain audit logs?"
→ ["How long do you retain audit logs?"]
- "Is MFA required? If so, for which roles and via which factors?"
→ ["Is MFA required, and for which roles?",
"Which MFA factors are supported?"]
```

### FILE: packages/agents/src/prompts/questionnaire_composer.md

```text
Answer a security questionnaire question using ONLY the provided context
(policies, evidence, library answers, controls).

RULES:
1. Every factual claim must cite a source from the context, formatted
[1], [2]… matching the numbered sources provided.
2. If the context does not address the question, answer
"Not addressed in current documentation" — never invent details.
3. Match the specificity of the evidence: cite numbers, dates, and cadences
exactly as they appear. Do not round or generalize upward.
4. Flag uncertainty: if sources conflict or are stale, lower confidence and
note the conflict in caveats.
5. Keep answers under 120 words unless the question demands detail.

OUTPUT JSON:
{
"answer": string,
"citations": [{ "index": number, "type": "policy"|"evidence"|"library"|"control",
"id": string, "title": string, "excerpt": string }],
"confidence": 0.0-1.0,
"caveats": [string]
}
```

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/utils/text.ts
```typescript
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { sha256 } from "@core";
import { embedTexts } from "@agents";

/** Fetch a URL and strip HTML to readable text (no headless browser —
* registry documents are server-rendered). */
export async function fetchAndClean(url: string): Promise<string> {
const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { "user-agent": "ComplianceRadar/1.0" } });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`fetch ${res.status} ${url}`);
return htmlToText(await res.text());
}

export function htmlToText(html: string): string {
return html
.replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, "")
.replace(/<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, "")
.replace(/<nav[\s\S]*?<\/nav>/gi, "")
.replace(/<footer[\s\S]*?<\/footer>/gi, "")
.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, " ")
.replace(/&nbsp;/g, " ").replace(/&amp;/g, "&").replace(/&lt;/g, "<")
.replace(/&gt;/g, ">").replace(/&quot;/g, '"').replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
.replace(/[ \t]+/g, " ")
.replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
.trim();
}

/** Fixed-size chunks with overlap — pgvector rows. */
export function chunkText(text: string, size = 1200, overlap = 150): string[] {
const chunks: string[] = [];
let i = 0;
while (i < text.length) {
chunks.push(text.slice(i, i + size));
i += size - overlap;
if (chunks.length >= 200) break; // document cap — radar docs, not corpora
}
return chunks;
}

/** Chunk + embed + persist DocChunk rows (vector via raw SQL — Prisma
* can't write pgvector columns). Idempotent per (documentId, chunkIndex). */
export async function chunkAndEmbed(documentId: string, text: string): Promise<number> {
const chunks = chunkText(text);
if (chunks.length === 0) return 0;
const vectors = await embedTexts(chunks);
for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
await prisma.$executeRaw`
INSERT INTO "DocChunk" ("id", "documentId", "chunkIndex", "text", "embedding")
VALUES (${randomUUID()}, ${documentId}, ${i}, ${chunks[i]},
${`[${vectors[i].join(",")}]`}::vector)
ON CONFLICT ("documentId", "chunkIndex")
DO UPDATE SET "text" = EXCLUDED."text", "embedding" = EXCLUDED."embedding"`;
}
return chunks.length;
}

export { sha256 };
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/sources/federalRegister.ts
```typescript
const FR_API = "https://www.federalregister.gov/api/v1/documents.json";

export interface FetchedDoc {
title: string; url: string; externalRef: string | null;
publishedAt: Date | null; docType: string;
}

const AGENCY_FILTERS: Record<string, string[]> = {
SEC: ["securities-and-exchange-commission"],
HHS: ["department-of-health-and-human-services"],
FTC: ["federal-trade-commission"],
OCC: ["office-of-the-comptroller-of-the-currency"],
};

function mapFRType(t: string): string {
if (/Rule/i.test(t)) return "RULE";
if (/Proposed/i.test(t)) return "GUIDANCE";
if (/Notice/i.test(t)) return "NEWS";
return "NEWS";
}

export async function fetchFederalRegister(
source: { name: string; authority: string }, since: Date,
): Promise<FetchedDoc[]> {
const agencies = AGENCY_FILTERS[source.authority] ?? [];
const url = new URL(FR_API);
url.searchParams.set("conditions[term]", "data security OR privacy OR cybersecurity");
url.searchParams.set("per_page", "100");
url.searchParams.set("order", "newest");
url.searchParams.set("fields[]", "title,document_number,publication_date,type,body_html_url");
if (agencies.length) url.searchParams.set("conditions[agencies][]", agencies.join(","));

const res = await fetch(url);
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Federal Register ${res.status}`);
const docs = ((await res.json()) as { results: any[] }).results ?? [];
return docs
.filter((d) => !d.publication_date || new Date(d.publication_date) >= since)
.map((d) => ({
title: d.title as string,
url: (d.body_html_url ?? `https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/${d.document_number}`) as string,
externalRef: (d.document_number ?? null) as string | null,
publishedAt: d.publication_date ? new Date(d.publication_date) : null,
docType: mapFRType(d.type ?? ""),
}));
}

/** Minimal RSS fetcher (FINRA notices, state trackers). */
export async function fetchRss(
source: { url: string }, since: Date,
): Promise<FetchedDoc[]> {
const res = await fetch(source.url, { headers: { "user-agent": "ComplianceRadar/1.0" } });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`RSS ${res.status} ${source.url}`);
const xml = await res.text();
const items = [...xml.matchAll(/<item>([\s\S]*?)<\/item>/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
const out: FetchedDoc[] = [];
for (const item of items) {
const title = item.match(/<title>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?([\s\S]*?)(?:\]\]>)?<\/title>/)?.[1]?.trim();
const link = item.match(/<link>([\s\S]*?)<\/link>/)?.[1]?.trim();
const pub = item.match(/<pubDate>([\s\S]*?)<\/pubDate>/)?.[1];
const publishedAt = pub ? new Date(pub) : new Date();
if (!title || Number.isNaN(publishedAt.getTime()) || publishedAt < since) continue;
out.push({ title, url: link ?? source.url, externalRef: link ?? null,
publishedAt, docType: "NEWS" });
}
return out;
}

/** The source catalog — seeded by scripts/db:seed:sources. */
export const REG_SOURCES = [
{ name: "Federal Register — SEC", authority: "SEC", jurisdiction: "US-Federal",
category: "Financial", parserType: "FEDERAL_REGISTER_API", cron: "0 12 * * 1-5",
url: "https://www.federalregister.gov" },
{ name: "Federal Register — HHS", authority: "HHS", jurisdiction: "US-Federal",
category: "Healthcare", parserType: "FEDERAL_REGISTER_API", cron: "0 12 * * 1-5",
url: "https://www.federalregister.gov" },
{ name: "Federal Register — FTC", authority: "FTC", jurisdiction: "US-Federal",
category: "Privacy", parserType: "FEDERAL_REGISTER_API", cron: "0 12 * * 1-5",
url: "https://www.federalregister.gov" },
{ name: "Federal Register — OCC", authority: "OCC", jurisdiction: "US-Federal",
category: "Financial", parserType: "FEDERAL_REGISTER_API", cron: "0 12 * * 1-5",
url: "https://www.federalregister.gov" },
{ name: "FINRA Regulatory Notices", authority: "FINRA", jurisdiction: "US-Federal",
category: "Financial", parserType: "RSS", cron: "0 12 * * 1-5",
url: "https://www.finra.org/rules-guidance/notices/rss" },
// EUR-Lex OJ feed: parser stub — tracked in docs/ASSEMBLY.md as BUILD.
];
```

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/sources/seed.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { REG_SOURCES } from "./federalRegister";

async function main() {
for (const s of REG_SOURCES) {
const existing = await prisma.regSource.findFirst({ where: { name: s.name } });
if (!existing) {
await prisma.regSource.create({
data: { name: s.name, url: s.url, authority: s.authority,
jurisdiction: s.jurisdiction, category: s.category,
checkFrequencyCron: s.cron, parserType: s.parserType },
});
}
}
console.log(`Seeded ${REG_SOURCES.length} regulatory sources.`);
}
main().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());
```

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/sources/diffGate.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { sha256, fetchAndClean, chunkAndEmbed } from "../utils/text";
import type { FetchedDoc } from "./federalRegister";

export interface StoredDoc { stored: { id: string; status: string }; content: string; changed: boolean }

/**
* Diff gate: fetch content, hash-compare against the stored document,
* re-chunk+embed only when content changed. Returns null when nothing moved.
*/
export async function processSourceDocument(sourceId: string, doc: FetchedDoc): Promise<StoredDoc | null> {
const content = await fetchAndClean(doc.url);
const contentHash = sha256(content);

const existing = await prisma.sourceDocument.findFirst({
where: { sourceId, externalRef: doc.externalRef },
});
if (existing?.contentHash === contentHash) return null; // unchanged — skip

// STUB: production stores raw content in S3; key recorded for the future.
const rawStorageKey = `raw/${sourceId}/${doc.externalRef ?? Date.now()}.txt`;

const stored = existing
? await prisma.sourceDocument.update({
where: { id: existing.id }, data: { contentHash, status: "CHANGED" } })
: await prisma.sourceDocument.create({
data: { sourceId, title: doc.title, url: doc.url, externalRef: doc.externalRef,
publishedAt: doc.publishedAt, contentHash, rawStorageKey,
docType: doc.docType as any, status: "NEW" } });

await chunkAndEmbed(stored.id, content);
return { stored, content, changed: true };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/sources/scan.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { runAgent } from "@agents";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { DAY } from "@core";
import { fetchFederalRegister, fetchRss } from "./federalRegister";
import { processSourceDocument } from "./diffGate";
import { analyzeImpact } from "../impact";
import type { FetchedDoc } from "./federalRegister";

async function fetchForSource(
source: { name: string; authority: string; url: string; parserType: string },
since: Date,
): Promise<FetchedDoc[]> {
switch (source.parserType) {
case "FEDERAL_REGISTER_API": return fetchFederalRegister(source, since);
case "RSS": return fetchRss(source, since);
default: throw new Error(`parser ${source.parserType} not implemented (EUR-Lex: tracked stub)`);
}
}

/**
* The reg-radar scan: fetch → diff-gate → extract → RegChange → impact.
* Runs on a Temporal schedule (workers batch). Nothing here applies a
* change — the expert review console gates application.
*/
export async function scanAllSources(): Promise<{ scanned: number; changes: number }> {
const sources = await prisma.regSource.findMany({ where: { enabled: true } });
const since = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * DAY);
let scanned = 0, changes = 0;

for (const source of sources) {
try {
const docs = await fetchForSource(source, since);
for (const doc of docs) {
scanned++;
const gate = await processSourceDocument(source.id, doc);
if (!gate) continue;

const extraction = await runAgent<{
changeType: string | null; jurisdictions?: string[]; summary: string;
effectiveAt?: string | null; obligations?: unknown[]; confidence?: number;
}>("change_extractor", {
title: doc.title,
publishedAt: doc.publishedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
body: gate.content.slice(0, 8000),
});
if (!extraction.changeType) continue;

const change = await prisma.regChange.create({
data: {
sourceDocumentId: gate.stored.id,
changeType: extraction.changeType as any,
jurisdiction: extraction.jurisdictions?.[0] ?? source.jurisdiction,
summary: extraction.summary,
effectiveAt: extraction.effectiveAt ? new Date(extraction.effectiveAt) : null,
extracted: extraction as object,
},
});
changes++;
emit({ type: "regchange.detected", source: "review",
payload: { regChangeId: change.id, summary: extraction.summary } });
await analyzeImpact(change.id);
}
await prisma.regSource.update({ where: { id: source.id },
data: { lastCheckedAt: new Date() } });
} catch (err) {
console.error(`[radar] source ${source.name} failed: ${String(err).slice(0, 200)}`);
}
}
return { scanned, changes };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/impact.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { runAgent } from "@agents";

const STOP = new Set(["data", "security", "the", "and", "for", "with", "that", "must", "shall"]);

function extractKeywords(summary: string, obligations: { what?: string }[]): string[] {
const text = `${summary} ${obligations.map((o) => o.what ?? "").join(" ")}`.toLowerCase();
const words = text.match(/[a-z]{4,}/g) ?? [];
return [...new Set(words.filter((w) => !STOP.has(w)))].slice(0, 12);
}

/**
* Grade a detected change against candidate requirements. Candidate retrieval
* is keyword-based over the (small) framework content set; the vector index
* upgrades this when the questionnaire retrieval batch lands. Expert review
* gates everything downstream.
*/
export async function analyzeImpact(regChangeId: string) {
const change = await prisma.regChange.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: regChangeId } });
const obligations = ((change.extracted as Record<string, unknown>)?.obligations as { what?: string }[]) ?? [];
const keywords = extractKeywords(change.summary, obligations);
if (keywords.length === 0) return [];

const candidates = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: {
OR: keywords.flatMap((k) => [
{ title: { contains: k, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
{ description: { contains: k, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
]),
},
include: { framework: true },
take: 25,
});
if (candidates.length === 0) return [];

const graded = await runAgent<{
impacts: { requirementId: string; impactLevel: "CRITICAL" | "MAJOR" | "MINOR" | "INFO";
rationale: string; suggestedAction: string | null }[];
}>("impact_analyst", {
change: { summary: change.summary, obligations },
candidates: candidates.map((c) => ({
id: c.id, framework: c.framework.code, code: c.code,
title: c.title, description: c.description,
})),
});

const out = [];
for (const g of graded.impacts ?? []) {
const cand = candidates.find((c) => c.id === g.requirementId);
if (!cand) continue; // agent hallucinated an ID — drop
out.push(await prisma.changeImpact.create({
data: {
regChangeId, frameworkId: cand.frameworkId,
requirementId: cand.id, impactLevel: g.impactLevel,
rationale: g.rationale, suggestedAction: g.suggestedAction,
},
}));
}
return out;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/fanout.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";

/**
* Expert-approved reg change → fan out tasks to every affected org.
* Called by the review console on APPROVE (regChange source).
*/
export async function applyVerifiedChange(regChangeId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string) {
const change = await prisma.regChange.update({
where: { id: regChangeId },
data: { status: "APPLIED", reviewedByMembershipId: reviewerMembershipId },
include: { impacts: { include: { requirement: true } } },
});

const orgsNotified = new Set<string>();
for (const impact of change.impacts) {
if (impact.impactLevel !== "MAJOR" && impact.impactLevel !== "CRITICAL") continue;
if (!impact.requirementId) continue;

const orgControls = await prisma.controlMapping.findMany({
where: { requirementId: impact.requirementId, status: "ACCEPTED" },
select: { control: { select: { orgId: true, id: true, name: true } } },
});

// one task per org (dedupe when one control maps multiple times)
const byOrg = new Map<string, { orgId: string; id: string; name: string }>();
for (const m of orgControls) byOrg.set(m.control.orgId, m.control);

for (const control of byOrg.values()) {
orgsNotified.add(control.orgId);
await prisma.task.create({
data: {
orgId: control.orgId, source: "REG_CHANGE",
priority: impact.impactLevel === "CRITICAL" ? "CRITICAL" : "HIGH",
title: `Regulatory change affects ${control.name}`,
description: `${change.summary}\n\nEffective: ${change.effectiveAt?.toISOString().slice(0, 10) ?? "TBD"}\n` +
`Suggested action: ${impact.suggestedAction ?? "review control"}`,
relatedType: "RegChange", relatedId: regChangeId,
},
});
}
}

emit({ type: "regchange.applied", source: "review",
payload: { regChangeId, orgsNotified: orgsNotified.size } });
return { orgsNotified: orgsNotified.size };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/legislative/sourcePack.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

/** Privacy statutes beyond breach-notification worth watching — new
* comprehensive privacy acts routinely amend breach definitions/deadlines. */
const PRIVACY_WATCH: Record<string, string[]> = {
"US-CA": ["1798.100", "1798.81.5"],
"US-VA": ["18.2-186.6", "59.1-575"],
"US-CO": ["6-1-1301"],
"US-CT": ["42-515"],
"US-UT": ["13-61-101"],
"US-TX": ["521.002", "541.001"],
"US-OR": ["646A.600"],
"US-MT": ["30-14-1701"],
"US-IA": ["715D.1"],
"US-IN": ["24-15-1"],
"US-WA": ["19.373"],
};

/** Biennial/full-session-cap states — calendar is config, expert-editable. */
const SESSION_OVERRIDES: Record<string, { cycle: string; months: number[] }> = {
"US-TX": { cycle: "BIENNIAL_ODD", months: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5] },
"US-ND": { cycle: "BIENNIAL_ODD", months: [1, 2, 3, 4] },
"US-MT": { cycle: "BIENNIAL_ODD", months: [1, 2, 3, 4] },
"US-NV": { cycle: "BIENNIAL_ODD", months: [2, 3, 4, 5, 6] },
"US-OR": { cycle: "ANNUAL", months: [2, 3, 4, 5, 6] },
};

/** Official APIs used as full-text enrichment (LegiScan stays primary). */
const ENRICHMENT: Record<string, string> = {
"US-NY": "https://legislation.nysenate.gov/api/3",
"US-CA": "https://leginfo.legislature.ca.gov",
"US-TX": "https://capitol.texas.gov",
"US-IL": "https://ilga.gov/legislation",
"US-WA": "https://leg.wa.gov",
};

const SECTION_RE = /§+\s*([0-9A-Za-z.\-]+)/g;
const extractSections = (citation: string) =>
[...citation.matchAll(SECTION_RE)].map((m) => m[1]);

/**
* The state universe comes from the breach matrix itself — adding a
* jurisdiction to the matrix automatically registers its legislature feed.
*/
export async function loadLegislativeSources() {
const rules = await prisma.stateBreachRule.findMany();
for (const rule of rules) {
const watchSections = [
...extractSections(rule.citation),
...(PRIVACY_WATCH[rule.id] ?? []),
];
const cal = SESSION_OVERRIDES[rule.id] ?? { cycle: "ANNUAL", months: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6] };

await prisma.legislativeSource.upsert({
where: { stateCode: rule.id },
update: { watchSections },
create: {
id: `LEGISCAN:${rule.id}`,
stateCode: rule.id,
strategy: "LEGISCAN_API",
sessionCycle: cal.cycle,
sessionMonths: cal.months,
watchSections,
enrichmentUrl: ENRICHMENT[rule.id] ?? null,
},
});
}
console.log(`Loaded legislative sources for ${rules.length} jurisdictions.`);
}

if (require.main === module) {
loadLegislativeSources().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/legislative/fetcher.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { sha256, canonicalJSON } from "@core";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { classifyRelevance } from "./classify";
import { proposeMatrixAmendment } from "./extract";

const LEGISCAN = "https://api.legiscan.com";

async function legi(op: string, params: Record<string, string>) {
const url = new URL(LEGISCAN);
url.searchParams.set("key", process.env.LEGISCAN_API_KEY ?? "");
url.searchParams.set("op", op);
for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) url.searchParams.set(k, v);
const res = await fetch(url);
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`LegiScan ${res.status} op=${op}`);
return res.json() as any;
}

/** Session-aware: active months poll daily, off-session weekly. */
export function isPollDue(source: { sessionMonths: unknown; lastPolledAt: Date | null }): boolean {
const months = (source.sessionMonths as number[]) ?? [];
const inSession = months.includes(new Date().getMonth() + 1);
if (inSession) return true;
const daysSince = (Date.now() - (source.lastPolledAt?.getTime() ?? 0)) / 86_400_000;
return daysSince >= 7;
}

/** LegiScan progress-event → our stage. SIGNED/ADOPTED = ENACTED. */
const STAGE_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
Introduced: "INTRODUCED", Amended: "AMENDED",
"Passed House": "PASSED_ONE_CHAMBER", "Passed Senate": "PASSED_ONE_CHAMBER",
"Passed both chambers": "PASSED_BOTH",
Signed: "ENACTED", Adopted: "ENACTED",
Vetoed: "VETOED", "Veto overridden": "ENACTED",
Failed: "DIED",
};
const STAGE_ORDER = ["DIED", "VETOED", "ENACTED", "PASSED_BOTH",
"PASSED_ONE_CHAMBER", "AMENDED", "INTRODUCED"];

async function updateLifecycle(trackedBillId: string, detail: any): Promise<Date | null> {
const tracked = await prisma.trackedBill.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: trackedBillId } });
const history = (tracked.stageHistory as { stage: string; at: string; action: string }[]) ?? [];
let enactedAt: Date | null = tracked.enactedAt ?? null;
for (const step of detail.progress ?? []) {
const stage = STAGE_MAP[step.event];
if (!stage) continue;
if (!history.some((h) => h.stage === stage && h.at === step.date)) {
history.push({ stage, at: step.date, action: step.event });
if (stage === "ENACTED") enactedAt = new Date(step.date);
}
}
const best = STAGE_ORDER.find((s) => history.some((h) => h.stage === s)) ?? "INTRODUCED";
await prisma.trackedBill.update({
where: { id: trackedBillId },
data: { stage: best as any, stageHistory: history,
enactedAt, lastActionAt: new Date() },
});
return enactedAt;
}

export async function pollSource(source: { id: string; stateCode: string; watchSections: unknown }) {
emit({ type: "flow.started", source: "workers",
payload: { flowType: "REG_SCAN", stateCode: source.stateCode } });
try {
const stateAbbr = source.stateCode.split("-")[1]; // "US-CA" → "CA"

// 1. Master list — the session's full bill inventory with change hashes
const master = await legi("getMasterList", { state: stateAbbr });
const entries = Object.values(master.masterlist ?? {}) as any[];
const masterHash = sha256(canonicalJSON(
entries.filter((b) => b.bill_id !== 0)
.map((b) => `${b.bill_id}:${b.status}:${b.last_action}`)));
if (masterHash === (await prisma.legislativeSource.findUnique({
where: { id: source.id } }))?.lastMasterHash) {
return { changed: 0 }; // nothing moved
}

// 2. For each bill whose status/action changed → fetch detail
let changed = 0;
for (const entry of entries) {
if (entry.bill_id === 0) continue; // LegiScan metadata placeholder row
const tracked = await prisma.trackedBill.upsert({
where: { externalRef: String(entry.bill_id) },
update: { title: entry.title, lastActionAt: new Date() },
create: {
sourceId: source.id, stateCode: source.stateCode,
billNumber: `${entry.bill_number ?? ""} ${entry.chamber ?? ""}`.trim(),
session: String(master.session?.session_id ?? "current"),
externalRef: String(entry.bill_id), title: entry.title ?? "",
relevanceScore: 0, relevancePath: "KEYWORD",
},
});

const bill = await legi("getBill", { id: String(entry.bill_id) });
const detail = bill.bill ?? {};
await updateLifecycle(tracked.id, detail);
const score = await classifyRelevance(tracked, detail, source);

if (score < 0.5) {
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: tracked.id },
data: { status: "DISMISSED", relevanceScore: score } });
continue;
}
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: tracked.id },
data: { relevanceScore: score } });

if (tracked.stage === "ENACTED" || detail.status === "Signed") {
await proposeMatrixAmendment(tracked, detail, source);
}
changed++;
}

await prisma.legislativeSource.update({
where: { id: source.id },
data: { lastPolledAt: new Date(), lastMasterHash: masterHash },
});
emit({ type: "flow.completed", source: "workers",
payload: { flowType: "REG_SCAN", stateCode: source.stateCode, changed } });
return { changed };
} catch (err) {
emit({ type: "flow.failed", source: "workers", severityOverride: "ERROR",
payload: { flowType: "REG_SCAN", stateCode: source.stateCode,
error: String(err).slice(0, 300) } });
throw err;
}
}

/** Poll every due source (Temporal calls this daily). */
export async function pollDueSources() {
const sources = await prisma.legislativeSource.findMany({ where: { enabled: true } });
const results: { state: string; changed: number }[] = [];
for (const s of sources) {
if (!isPollDue(s)) continue;
try {
const r = await pollSource(s);
results.push({ state: s.stateCode, changed: r.changed });
} catch (e) {
console.error(`[legislative] ${s.stateCode}: ${String(e).slice(0, 200)}`);
}
}
return results;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/legislative/classify.ts
```typescript
import { runAgent } from "@agents";

const KEYWORDS = [
"data breach", "breach notification", "breach of security",
"personal information", "personally identifiable", "consumer privacy",
"cybersecurity incident", "social security number",
"consumer reporting agency", "encrypted", "confidential information",
];

/** Relevance classification — three paths, cheapest first. */
export async function classifyRelevance(
bill: { title: string; stateCode: string },
detail: any,
source: { watchSections: unknown },
): Promise<number> {
const text = `${detail.title ?? bill.title} ${detail.description ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();

// Path 1 — amends a watched statute section? Auto-relevant. Strongest signal.
const watch = (source.watchSections as string[]) ?? [];
const fullText = detail.texts?.[0]?.doc ?? "";
const citesWatched = watch.some((s) =>
text.includes(s) || fullText.includes(s));

// Path 2 — keyword gate
const hits = KEYWORDS.filter((k) => text.includes(k));

// Path 3 — topic match on full text, only when 1 and 2 fire (cost control)
let score = citesWatched ? 0.6 : 0;
score += Math.min(0.3, hits.length * 0.1);

if (score >= 0.5) {
const topic = await runAgent<{ relevant: boolean; confidence: number }>(
"bill_topic_classifier", {
title: detail.title ?? bill.title,
summary: detail.description ?? "",
fullTextExcerpt: fullText.slice(0, 4000),
});
score = topic.relevant ? Math.max(score, topic.confidence) : 0;
}
return score;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/legislative/extract.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { runAgent } from "@agents";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import type { StateBreachRule } from "@prisma/client";

/** Whitelist of rule fields exposed to the extractor prompt. */
function sanitizeRule(rule: StateBreachRule) {
return {
deadlineDays: rule.deadlineDays, notifyResidents: rule.notifyResidents,
agThreshold: rule.agThreshold, creditAgencyThreshold: rule.creditAgencyThreshold,
otherAgencies: rule.otherAgencies, harmGate: rule.harmGate,
encryptionSafeHarbor: rule.encryptionSafeHarbor, citation: rule.citation,
};
}

function mergeChanges(rule: StateBreachRule, changes: { field: string; to: unknown }[]) {
const proposed: Record<string, unknown> = { ...sanitizeRule(rule) };
for (const c of changes) if (c.field in proposed) proposed[c.field] = c.to;
return proposed;
}

/**
* Enacted bill → structured rule-diff proposal. The proposal enters the
* expert review queue (the console scans MatrixAmendmentProposal where
* status=PENDING_REVIEW — no direct enqueue dependency needed here).
*/
export async function proposeMatrixAmendment(
bill: { id: string; stateCode: string; enactedAt: Date | null; externalRef: string; url?: string | null },
detail: any,
source: { stateCode: string; enrichmentUrl?: string | null },
) {
const currentRule = await prisma.stateBreachRule.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: source.stateCode } });

const billText = (detail.texts?.[0]?.doc ?? detail.description ?? "") as string;
if (!billText) {
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: bill.id }, data: { status: "DISMISSED" } });
return null;
}

const extraction = await runAgent<{
changes: { field: string; from: unknown; to: unknown; quote?: string; confidence?: number }[];
effectiveAt?: string | null; uncertainties?: string[]; overallConfidence: number;
}>("matrix_amendment_extractor", {
currentRule: sanitizeRule(currentRule),
billText: billText.slice(0, 24_000),
state: source.stateCode,
enactedAt: bill.enactedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
});

if (!extraction.changes?.length) {
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: bill.id }, data: { status: "DISMISSED" } });
return null; // enacted, but touches nothing we track
}

const proposal = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.create({
data: {
billId: bill.id, stateRuleId: currentRule.id,
proposed: mergeChanges(currentRule, extraction.changes) as object,
diff: extraction.changes as object,
effectiveAt: extraction.effectiveAt ? new Date(extraction.effectiveAt) : null,
extractorConfidence: extraction.overallConfidence ?? 0,
},
});
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: bill.id }, data: { status: "PROPOSED" } });
emit({ type: "regchange.detected", source: "review",
payload: { proposalId: proposal.id, state: source.stateCode,
changes: extraction.changes.length } });
return proposal;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/knowledge/src/legislative/apply.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { computeStateObligations } from "@core";
import type { IncidentFacts } from "@core";

function summarizeDiff(diff: { field: string; from: unknown; to: unknown }[]): string {
return diff.map((d) => `${d.field}: ${String(d.from)} → ${String(d.to)}`).join("; ");
}

/** Records an applied amendment as a schema-valid RegChange chain. */
async function recordAppliedAmendment(proposal: {
stateRuleId: string; diff: unknown; effectiveAt: Date | null; extractorConfidence: number;
}, bill: { externalRef: string; title: string; url: string | null; stateCode: string }) {
const regSource = await prisma.regSource.upsert({
where: { id: `legis:${bill.stateCode}` },
update: {},
create: {
id: `legis:${bill.stateCode}`, name: `Legislature — ${bill.stateCode}`,
url: bill.url ?? "", authority: "STATE", jurisdiction: bill.stateCode,
category: "Privacy", checkFrequencyCron: "0 6 * * *", parserType: "RSS",
},
}).catch(async () => { // upsert by id on a cuid-defaulted model — fall back to find/create
const found = await prisma.regSource.findFirst({ where: { name: `Legislature — ${bill.stateCode}` } });
return found ?? prisma.regSource.create({
data: { name: `Legislature — ${bill.stateCode}`, url: bill.url ?? "",
authority: "STATE", jurisdiction: bill.stateCode, category: "Privacy",
checkFrequencyCron: "0 6 * * *", parserType: "RSS" } });
});

const doc = await prisma.sourceDocument.findFirst({
where: { sourceId: regSource.id, externalRef: bill.externalRef } })
?? await prisma.sourceDocument.create({
data: { sourceId: regSource.id, title: bill.title, url: bill.url ?? "",
externalRef: bill.externalRef, contentHash: "legislative-amendment",
rawStorageKey: "legislative", docType: "AMENDMENT" } });

return prisma.regChange.create({
data: {
sourceDocumentId: doc.id, changeType: "AMENDMENT",
jurisdiction: bill.stateCode,
summary: `${bill.stateCode} breach rule amended: ${summarizeDiff(proposal.diff as any[])}`,
effectiveAt: proposal.effectiveAt, status: "APPLIED",
extracted: { proposal: true, confidence: proposal.extractorConfidence },
},
});
}

/**
* Expert-approved matrix proposal → rule update + open-incident recompute.
* Future-dated proposals are marked APPLIED with appliedRuleVersion null;
* applyDueProposals() (daily sweep) flips the rule at the effective date.
*/
export async function applyMatrixProposal(proposalId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string, note?: string) {
const proposal = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: proposalId }, include: { bill: true, stateRule: true },
});
if (proposal.status !== "PENDING_REVIEW") throw new Error("Proposal not reviewable");

const effective = proposal.effectiveAt ?? new Date();

await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
if (effective <= new Date()) {
await tx.stateBreachRule.update({
where: { id: proposal.stateRuleId },
data: { ...(proposal.proposed as object), version: { increment: 1 },
verificationStatus: "EXPERT_VERIFIED", // the approving reviewer IS the verification
lastVerifiedAt: new Date() },
});
}
await tx.matrixAmendmentProposal.update({
where: { id: proposal.id },
data: { status: "APPLIED", reviewedByMembershipId: reviewerMembershipId,
appliedRuleVersion: effective <= new Date() ? proposal.stateRule.version + 1 : null,
reviewerNote: note },
});
await tx.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: proposal.billId }, data: { status: "APPLIED" } });
});

const regChange = await recordAppliedAmendment(proposal, proposal.bill);

// ★ Open incidents in this jurisdiction get recomputed deadlines immediately
const affected = effective <= new Date()
? await recomputeOpenIncidentObligations(proposal.stateRuleId) : { incidents: 0, notifications: 0 };

emit({ type: "regchange.applied", source: "review",
payload: { regChangeId: regChange.id, state: proposal.stateRuleId,
diff: proposal.diff, openIncidentsUpdated: affected.incidents,
scheduledFor: effective > new Date() ? effective.toISOString() : null } });
return { scheduledFor: effective > new Date() ? effective : null, ...affected };
}

/** Daily sweep: apply proposals whose effective date has now passed. */
export async function applyDueProposals() {
const due = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.findMany({
where: { status: "APPLIED", appliedRuleVersion: null,
effectiveAt: { lte: new Date() } },
include: { stateRule: true },
});
let applied = 0;
for (const p of due) {
await prisma.stateBreachRule.update({
where: { id: p.stateRuleId },
data: { ...(p.proposed as object), version: { increment: 1 },
verificationStatus: "EXPERT_VERIFIED", lastVerifiedAt: new Date() },
});
await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.update({
where: { id: p.id }, data: { appliedRuleVersion: p.stateRule.version + 1 } });
await recomputeOpenIncidentObligations(p.stateRuleId);
applied++;
}
return { applied };
}

function loadIncidentFacts(inc: {
detectedAt: Date; affectedDataCategories: unknown; affectedSystems: unknown;
isPublicCompany: boolean;
}): IncidentFacts {
return {
detectedAt: inc.detectedAt,
dataCategories: (inc.affectedDataCategories as string[]) ?? [],
subjectCountByJurisdiction: (inc.affectedSystems as Record<string, number>) ?? {},
encryptedAtRest: true, encryptedInTransit: true, // updated when facts are recorded on the incident
isPublicCompany: inc.isPublicCompany, materialityDetermined: true,
};
}

/**
* A matrix amendment landing mid-incident recomputes every open obligation
* in that jurisdiction — deadlines shift under active clocks.
*/
export async function recomputeOpenIncidentObligations(stateRuleId: string) {
const open = await prisma.incidentNotification.findMany({
where: {
status: "PENDING",
authority: { contains: stateRuleId }, // AG_US-CA, RESIDENTS_US-CA, CRA_US-CA…
incident: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "TRIAGE", "CONTAINED", "ERADICATED", "RECOVERED"] } },
},
include: { incident: true },
});

let rescheduled = 0;
const incidents = new Set<string>();
for (const n of open) {
incidents.add(n.incidentId);
const facts = loadIncidentFacts(n.incident);
const fresh = await computeStateObligations(facts, facts.detectedAt);
const match = fresh.find((o) => o.authority === n.authority);
if (match && match.deadlineAt.getTime() !== n.statutoryDeadlineAt?.getTime()) {
await prisma.incidentNotification.update({
where: { id: n.id },
data: { statutoryDeadlineAt: match.deadlineAt, status: "PENDING",
notes: `DEADLINE RECOMPUTED — law amended (was ${n.statutoryDeadlineAt?.toISOString()})` },
});
// SLA timer reschedule: brain event lets the workers layer re-signal
// the Temporal handle (deterministic workflowId per notification).
emit({ type: "sla.timer.fired", source: "sla", orgId: n.incident.orgId,
payload: { incidentId: n.incidentId, notificationId: n.id,
rescheduledTo: match.deadlineAt.toISOString() } });
await prisma.task.create({
data: { orgId: n.incident.orgId, source: "REG_CHANGE", priority: "CRITICAL",
title: `Notification deadline changed for ${n.authority}`,
description: `New deadline: ${match.deadlineAt.toISOString()} ` +
`(was ${n.statutoryDeadlineAt?.toISOString()}). Statute amended mid-incident.`,
relatedType: "Incident", relatedId: n.incidentId },
});
rescheduled++;
}
}
return { incidents: incidents.size, notifications: rescheduled };
}
```

### FILE: packages/db/prisma/vector.sql
```sql
-- Answer-library vector side-table. The canonical schema's
-- AnswerLibrary.embeddingId points at rows here (org-scoped, RLS applies).
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "AnswerEmbedding" (
"id" TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
"orgId" TEXT NOT NULL,
"answerId" TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
"embedding" vector(1536) NOT NULL,
"model" TEXT NOT NULL,
"createdAt" TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "answer_embedding_hnsw" ON "AnswerEmbedding"
USING hnsw ("embedding" vector_cosine_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "answer_embedding_org" ON "AnswerEmbedding" ("orgId");

ALTER TABLE "AnswerEmbedding" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "AnswerEmbedding" FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS org_isolation ON "AnswerEmbedding";
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON "AnswerEmbedding" FOR ALL
USING ("orgId" = current_setting('app.current_org', true))
WITH CHECK ("orgId" = current_setting('app.current_org', true));

-- Vector columns for schema-declared Unsupported fields (idempotent):
ALTER TABLE "DocChunk" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "embedding" vector(1536);
ALTER TABLE "NegativeExample" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "embedding" vector(1536);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "docchunk_hnsw" ON "DocChunk"
USING hnsw ("embedding" vector_cosine_ops);
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/package.json
