### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/retrieval/normalize.ts
```typescript
const BOILERPLATE = [
/^(does|do|did|has|have|is|are|can|will)\s+(your|the|you)\s+(company|organization|org|vendor|provider|platform|product|solution)?\s*/i,
/^(please\s+)?(describe|provide|detail|list|state|confirm|specify)\s*/i,
/\s*(as\s+part\s+of\s+(your|the)\s+(security|compliance)\s+program)/gi,
];

const SYNONYMS: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
[/\b(mfa|2fa|two[- ]factor|two[- ]factor authentication|2[- ]factor authentication)\b/gi,
"multi-factor authentication"],
[/\b(soc\s*2|soc2|soc\s*ii|soc\s*type\s*2)\b/gi, "soc 2"],
[/\b(iso\s*27001|iso27001|iso\s*27k)\b/gi, "iso 27001"],
[/\b(pen[- ]test|penetration testing|pentest)\b/gi, "penetration test"],
[/\b(info ?sec|infosec|information security)\b/gi, "information security"],
[/\b(data ?center|datacenter)\b/gi, "data center"],
[/\b(vuln(ability|erabilities) (scan|scanning)|va scanning)\b/gi, "vulnerability scanning"],
[/\b((the )?cloud|saas)\b/gi, "cloud"],
[/\b(annually|yearly|every year|once a year|1x per year)\b/gi, "annually"],
[/\b(quarterly|every quarter|4x per year|qtrly)\b/gi, "quarterly"],
];

export function normalizeQuestion(raw: string): { normalized: string; expanded: string } {
let q = raw.toLowerCase().trim();
q = q.replace(/[«»""'']/g, "'");
for (const re of BOILERPLATE) q = q.replace(re, "").trim();
q = q.replace(/\?+$/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
const expanded = SYNONYMS.reduce((s, [re, sub]) => s.replace(re, sub), q);
return { normalized: q, expanded }; // normalized = exact key; expanded = embedded
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/retrieval/embed.ts
```typescript
import OpenAI from "openai";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { embedTexts } from "@agents";
import { normalizeQuestion } from "./normalize";

export interface EmbeddingProfile {
id: "default" | "multilingual" | "onprem";
model: string; dims: number;
embed(texts: string[]): Promise<number[][]>;
}

const openai = new OpenAI();

async function cohereEmbed(texts: string[]): Promise<number[][]> {
const res = await fetch("https://api.cohere.com/v1/embed", {
method: "POST",
headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.COHERE_API_KEY ?? ""}`,
"content-type": "application/json" },
body: JSON.stringify({ model: "embed-multilingual-v3",
input_type: "search_query", embedding_types: ["float"], texts }),
});
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Cohere embed ${res.status}`);
const data = (await res.json()) as { embeddings: { float: number[][] } };
return data.embeddings.float;
}

async function onpremEmbed(texts: string[]): Promise<number[][]> {
const res = await fetch(process.env.ONPREM_EMBED_URL!, {
method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
body: JSON.stringify({ input: texts }),
});
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`on-prem embed ${res.status}`);
return ((await res.json()) as { data: { embedding: number[] }[] })
.map((d) => d.embedding);
}

export const PROFILES: Record<string, EmbeddingProfile> = {
default: { id: "default", model: "text-embedding-3-small", dims: 1536,
embed: embedTexts },
multilingual: { id: "multilingual", model: "embed-multilingual-v3", dims: 1024,
embed: cohereEmbed },
onprem: { id: "onprem", model: "bge-large-en-v1.5", dims: 1024,
embed: onpremEmbed },
};

const vecLiteral = (v: number[]) => `[${v.join(",")}]`;

/** Write/replace the side-table embedding row for a library answer. */
export async function upsertEmbedding(orgId: string, answerId: string,
vector: number[], model: string): Promise<string> {
const id = `emb_${answerId}`;
await prisma.$executeRaw`
INSERT INTO "AnswerEmbedding" ("id", "orgId", "answerId", "embedding", "model")
VALUES (${id}, ${orgId}, ${answerId}, ${vecLiteral(vector)}::vector, ${model})
ON CONFLICT ("answerId") DO UPDATE
SET "embedding" = EXCLUDED."embedding", "model" = EXCLUDED."model"`;
return id;
}

/** Profile switch requires re-embedding the whole library (idempotent). */
export async function switchProfile(orgId: string, profileId: keyof typeof PROFILES) {
const profile = PROFILES[profileId];
await setRetrievalConfig(orgId, { profile: profileId, reindexing: true });
const rows = await prisma.answerLibrary.findMany({ where: { orgId } });
for (const batch of chunk(rows, 256)) {
const vectors = await profile.embed(
batch.map((r) => normalizeQuestion(r.canonicalQ).expanded));
for (let i = 0; i < batch.length; i++) {
const embeddingId = await upsertEmbedding(orgId, batch[i].id, vectors[i], profile.model);
await prisma.answerLibrary.update({ where: { id: batch[i].id },
data: { embeddingId } });
}
}
await setRetrievalConfig(orgId, { profile: profileId, reindexing: false });
}

export function chunk<T>(arr: T[], n: number): T[][] {
const out: T[][] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += n) out.push(arr.slice(i, i + n));
return out;
}

// ── config helpers (RetrievalConfig model) ──
export async function getRetrievalConfig(orgId: string) {
return prisma.retrievalConfig.upsert({
where: { orgId }, update: {}, create: { orgId },
});
}
export async function setRetrievalConfig(orgId: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>) {
await prisma.retrievalConfig.upsert({
where: { orgId }, update: patch as any, create: { orgId, ...patch } as any,
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/retrieval/search.ts
```typescript
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { clamp } from "@core";
import { PROFILES, upsertEmbedding, getRetrievalConfig } from "./embed";
import { normalizeQuestion } from "./normalize";

// ── ENTITY EXTRACTION (discriminating scope markers) ────────────
const ENTITY_PATTERNS: Array<[string, RegExp]> = [
["scope:at-rest", /\bat rest\b/i],
["scope:in-transit", /\bin transit\b|\bin motion\b|\btransfer(red|ring)?\b.*\bencrypt/i],
["subject:admins", /\badmin(istrator|s)?\b|\bprivileged\b|\broot\b/i],
["subject:employees", /\bemployee(s)?\b|\bworkforce\b|\bstaff\b/i],
["subject:contractors", /\bcontractor(s)?\b|\bthird[- ]party personnel\b/i],
["report:type-1", /\btype\s*i\b(?!i)/i],
["report:type-2", /\btype\s*ii\b/i],
["artifact:audit-logs", /\baudit log/i],
["artifact:customer-data", /\bcustomer data\b|\bend[- ]user data\b/i],
["fw:soc2", /\bsoc\s*2\b|\bsoc2\b/i],
["fw:iso27001", /\biso\s*27001\b/i],
["fw:hipaa", /\bhipaa\b/i],
["fw:gdpr", /\bgdpr\b/i],
["fw:pci", /\bpci(\s*dss)?\b/i],
["crypto:aes256", /\baes[- ]?256\b/i],
["crypto:tls", /\btls\b/i],
["cadence:annual", /\bannual(ly)?\b/i],
["cadence:quarterly", /\bquarterly\b/i],
];

export function extractEntities(text: string): string[] {
return ENTITY_PATTERNS.filter(([, re]) => re.test(text)).map(([e]) => e);
}

export function jaccard(a: string[], b: string[]): number {
if (a.length === 0 && b.length === 0) return 1;
const A = new Set(a), B = new Set(b);
let inter = 0;
for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) inter++;
return inter / (A.size + B.size - inter);
}

/** Hardcoded discriminative pairs — near-miss negatives that kill naive cosine. */
const HARDCODED_CONFLICTS: Array<[string, string]> = [
["scope:at-rest", "scope:in-transit"],
["subject:employees", "subject:contractors"],
["report:type-1", "report:type-2"],
["artifact:audit-logs", "artifact:customer-data"],
];

export function entitiesConflict(qEnt: string[], libEnt: string[]): boolean {
return HARDCODED_CONFLICTS.some(([a, b]) =>
(qEnt.includes(a) && libEnt.includes(b)) ||
(qEnt.includes(b) && libEnt.includes(a)));
}

// ── BM25 (in-JS — per-org libraries are hundreds, not millions) ─
const K1 = 1.2, B = 0.75;
function tokenize(s: string): string[] {
return s.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9]{2,}/g) ?? [];
}
export function bm25Scores(queries: string[], docs: { id: string; text: string }[]):
Map<string, number> {
const docTokens = docs.map((d) => tokenize(d.text));
const avgdl = docTokens.reduce((s, t) => s + t.length, 0) / Math.max(docs.length, 1) || 1;
const df = new Map<string, number>();
for (const toks of docTokens)
for (const t of new Set(toks)) df.set(t, (df.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
const N = docs.length;
const out = new Map<string, number>();
const q = tokenize(queries);
docs.forEach((d, i) => {
const tf = new Map<string, number>();
for (const t of docTokens[i]) tf.set(t, (tf.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
let score = 0;
for (const term of new Set(q)) {
const f = tf.get(term) ?? 0;
if (!f) continue;
const idf = Math.log(1 + (N - (df.get(term) ?? 0) + 0.5) / ((df.get(term) ?? 0) + 0.5));
score += idf * (f * (K1 + 1)) / (f + K1 * (1 - B + B * docTokens[i].length / avgdl));
}
out.set(d.id, score);
});
return out;
}

// ── THE RETRIEVAL PIPELINE ───────────────────────────────────────
export interface Retrieved {
answerId: string; score: number; cosine: number;
bm25: number; entityOverlap: number;
answer: string; canonicalQ: string; citations: unknown;
}

export async function searchAnswerLibrary(
orgId: string, rawQuestion: string,
): Promise<{ hits: Retrieved[]; queryVector: number[] }> {
const { normalized, expanded } = normalizeQuestion(rawQuestion);

// 0. Exact normalized match — instant, free, 100% precise
const exact = await prisma.answerLibrary.findUnique({
where: { orgId_questionNorm: { orgId, questionNorm: normalized } },
});
if (exact) {
return { hits: [{
answerId: exact.id, score: 1.0, cosine: 1.0, bm25: 1.0, entityOverlap: 1.0,
answer: exact.answer, canonicalQ: exact.canonicalQ, citations: exact.citations,
}], queryVector: [] };
}

const cfg = await getRetrievalConfig(orgId);
const profile = PROFILES[cfg.profile] ?? PROFILES.default;
const queryVector = (await profile.embed([expanded]))[0];
const qEnt = extractEntities(rawQuestion);

// Vector hits (side table, org-scoped)
const vectorHits = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ answerId: string; dist: number }>>`
SELECT e."answerId" AS "answerId", e.embedding <=> ${`[${queryVector.join(",")}]`}::vector AS dist
FROM "AnswerEmbedding" e
JOIN "AnswerLibrary" a ON a.id = e."answerId"
WHERE e."orgId" = ${orgId} AND a."embeddingId" IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY dist ASC LIMIT 20`;

const candidateIds = vectorHits.map((v) => v.answerId);
const rows = candidateIds.length
? await prisma.answerLibrary.findMany({ where: { orgId, id: { in: candidateIds } } })
: await prisma.answerLibrary.findMany({ where: { orgId }, take: 200 });

// BM25 over the same candidates (plus recent usage boost via ordering)
const bm25 = bm25Scores(expanded,
rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, text: `${r.canonicalQ} ${r.answer}` })));

const { entitiesConflictWithRules } = await import("../negative/conflictRules");
const merged = new Map<string, Retrieved>();
for (const v of vectorHits) {
merged.set(v.answerId, { answerId: v.answerId, score: 0, cosine: 1 - Number(v.dist),
bm25: 0, entityOverlap: 0, answer: "", canonicalQ: "", citations: null });
}
for (const r of rows) {
const m = merged.get(r.id) ?? { answerId: r.id, score: 0, cosine: 0, bm25: 0,
entityOverlap: 0, answer: "", canonicalQ: "", citations: null };
m.answer = r.answer; m.canonicalQ = r.canonicalQ; m.citations = r.citations;
m.bm25 = bm25.get(r.id) ?? 0;
merged.set(r.id, m);
}

// Normalize bm25 to 0..1 within this candidate set for fusion
const maxBm25 = Math.max(...[...merged.values()].map((m) => m.bm25), 1e-9);

for (const m of merged.values()) {
const libEnt = extractEntities(m.canonicalQ);
m.entityOverlap = jaccard(qEnt, libEnt);
const conflict = entitiesConflict(qEnt, libEnt) ||
(await entitiesConflictWithRules(orgId, qEnt, libEnt));
const penalty = conflict ? -0.25 : 0; // "at rest" vs "in transit" must not match
m.score = clamp(
0.55 * m.cosine + 0.25 * (m.bm25 / maxBm25) + 0.20 * m.entityOverlap + penalty, 0, 1);
}
const hits = [...merged.values()].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 5);
return { hits, queryVector };
}

// ── THRESHOLD ROUTING + AUTO-CLONE GUARD ─────────────────────────
export async function routeByThreshold(orgId: string, rawQuestion: string) {
const { hits, queryVector } = await searchAnswerLibrary(orgId, rawQuestion);
const cfg = await getRetrievalConfig(orgId);
if (hits.length === 0) return { action: "COMPOSE_FROM_CONTEXT" as const, hits, queryVector };
const top = hits[0];
if (top.score >= cfg.autoThreshold) {
const { guardAutoClone } = await import("../negative/guards");
const guard = queryVector.length
? await guardAutoClone(orgId, top, queryVector) : { blocked: false };
if (guard.blocked)
return { action: "COMPOSE_WITH_LIBRARY_CONTEXT" as const, hits: [top],
queryVector, forcedReview: guard.reason };
return { action: "CLONE_LIBRARY_ANSWER" as const, hit: top, hits, queryVector };
}
if (top.score >= cfg.contextThreshold)
return { action: "COMPOSE_WITH_LIBRARY_CONTEXT" as const, hits: hits.slice(0, 3), queryVector };
return { action: "COMPOSE_FROM_CONTEXT" as const, hits, queryVector };
}

// ── LIBRARY UPSERT (the flywheel write path) ─────────────────────
export async function upsertAnswerLibrary(
orgId: string, question: string, answer: string, citations: unknown,
approvedByMembershipId?: string,
) {
const { normalized } = normalizeQuestion(question);
const cfg = await getRetrievalConfig(orgId);
const profile = PROFILES[cfg.profile] ?? PROFILES.default;

const row = await prisma.answerLibrary.upsert({
where: { orgId_questionNorm: { orgId, questionNorm: normalized } },
update: { answer, citations: citations as object, usageCount: { increment: 1 },
lastUsedAt: new Date(), approvedByMembershipId ?? {} },
create: { orgId, questionNorm: normalized, canonicalQ: question, answer,
citations: citations as object, approvedByMembershipId ?? {} },
});
const vector = (await profile.embed([normalized]))[0];
const embeddingId = await upsertEmbedding(orgId, row.id, vector, profile.model);
await prisma.answerLibrary.update({ where: { id: row.id }, data: { embeddingId } });
return row;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/retrieval/calibrate.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { normalizeQuestion, extractEntities, jaccard, entitiesConflict } from "./search";
import { searchAnswerLibrary } from "./search";
import { getRetrievalConfig, setRetrievalConfig } from "./embed";

/** HARD negatives — same topic, different answer (pairs that kill naive cosine). */
const HARD_NEGATIVE_EXAMPLES: Array<[string, string]> = [
["Do you encrypt data at rest?", "Do you encrypt data in transit?"],
["How long do you retain audit logs?", "How long do you retain customer data?"],
["Do you run background checks on employees?", "Do you run background checks on contractors?"],
["Is MFA required for admins?", "Is MFA required for all employees?"],
["Do you have a SOC 2 Type I report?", "Do you have a SOC 2 Type II report?"],
];

async function topScore(orgId: string, query: string, expectNorm: string): Promise<number> {
const { hits } = await searchAnswerLibrary(orgId, query);
const match = hits.find((h) =>
normalizeQuestion(h.canonicalQ).normalized === expectNorm);
return match?.score ?? 0;
}

export async function buildGoldenSet(orgId: string) {
// Positives: verified library answers with usage (ground-truth same-intent pairs
// are the question + its own normalized form — retrieval must find them).
const verified = await prisma.answerLibrary.findMany({
where: { orgId, approvedByMembershipId: { not: null }, usageCount: { gte: 2 } },
take: 200 });
const positives = verified.map((v) => ({
query: v.canonicalQ, answerNorm: normalizeQuestion(v.canonicalQ).normalized,
relevant: true }));
const negatives = HARD_NEGATIVE_EXAMPLES.map(([q, lib]) => ({
query: q, answerNorm: normalizeQuestion(lib).normalized, relevant: false }));
return { positives, negatives };
}

function precisionRecall(scored: { score: number; relevant: boolean }[], t: number) {
const pred = scored.filter((s) => s.score >= t);
const tp = pred.filter((s) => s.relevant).length;
const precision = pred.length ? tp / pred.length : 1;
const relTotal = scored.filter((s) => s.relevant).length;
const recall = relTotal ? tp / relTotal : 0;
return { precision, recall };
}

/** Sweep thresholds; pick lowest t with precision ≥ 0.95 and recall ≥ 0.5. */
export async function calibrate(orgId: string) {
const golden = await buildGoldenSet(orgId);
const scored: { score: number; relevant: boolean }[] = [];
for (const p of golden.positives)
scored.push({ score: await topScore(orgId, p.query, p.answerNorm), relevant: true });
for (const n of golden.negatives)
scored.push({ score: await topScore(orgId, n.query, n.answerNorm), relevant: false });

const sweep = [];
for (let t = 0.70; t <= 0.96; t += 0.01) {
const { precision, recall } = precisionRecall(scored, t);
sweep.push({ t: Number(t.toFixed(2)), precision, recall,
f1: 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall || 1) });
}
const viable = sweep.filter((s) => s.precision >= 0.95 && s.recall >= 0.5);
const best = viable.sort((a, b) => b.f1 - a.f1)[0]
?? { t: 0.92, precision: 1, recall: 0, f1: 0 }; // conservative fallback

await setRetrievalConfig(orgId, {
autoThreshold: best.t,
contextThreshold: Math.max(0.60, best.t - 0.15),
calibration: { ...best, goldenSize: scored.length, calibratedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
});
return best;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/retrieval/drift.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { createGapTask } from "@core";
import { calibrate } from "./calibrate";
import { getRetrievalConfig } from "./embed";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

/** 30-day rejection rate on AI-drafted answers (any routing path). */
async function rejectionRate(orgId: string): Promise<number> {
const since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * DAY);
const [negatives, approved] = await Promise.all([
prisma.negativeExample.count({ where: { orgId, createdAt: { gte: since } } }),
prisma.answerLibrary.count({ where: { orgId, lastUsedAt: { gte: since } } }),
]);
const total = negatives + approved;
return total ? negatives / total : 0;
}

/** Golden-set precision at the current threshold. */
async function precisionAtCurrent(orgId: string): Promise<number> {
const cfg = await getRetrievalConfig(orgId);
const { buildGoldenSet } = await import("./calibrate");
const { searchAnswerLibrary } = await import("./search");
const { normalizeQuestion } = await import("./normalize");
const golden = await buildGoldenSet(orgId);
const scored: { score: number; relevant: boolean }[] = [];
for (const p of golden.positives)
scored.push({ score: (await searchAnswerLibrary(orgId, p.query)).hits.find((h) =>
normalizeQuestion(h.canonicalQ).normalized === p.answerNorm)?.score ?? 0,
relevant: true });
for (const n of golden.negatives)
scored.push({ score: (await searchAnswerLibrary(orgId, n.query)).hits.find((h) =>
normalizeQuestion(h.canonicalQ).normalized === n.answerNorm)?.score ?? 0,
relevant: false });
const pred = scored.filter((s) => s.score >= cfg.autoThreshold);
const tp = pred.filter((s) => s.relevant).length;
return pred.length ? tp / pred.length : 1;
}

/** Weekly health check — queues recalibration when quality slips. */
export async function monitorRetrievalHealth(orgId: string) {
const rejection = await rejectionRate(orgId);
const precision = await precisionAtCurrent(orgId);
const alerts: string[] = [];
if (rejection > 0.15)
alerts.push(`Auto-clone rejection rate ${(rejection * 100).toFixed(0)}% (>15%) — library quality degrading`);
if (precision < 0.92)
alerts.push(`Golden-set precision ${(precision * 100).toFixed(0)}% (<92%) — recalibration required`);
if (alerts.length) {
await createGapTask(orgId, "Answer-library retrieval drift detected", alerts.join("\n"));
await calibrate(orgId); // thresholds auto-recalibrated; major changes need review
}
return { precision, rejection, alerts };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/pipeline.ts
```typescript
import ExcelJS from "exceljs";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { runAgent } from "@agents";
import { routeByThreshold, upsertAnswerLibrary } from "./retrieval/search";

// ── INGEST ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function ingestQuestionnaire(
orgId: string, file: Buffer, filename: string,
) {
const rows: { text: string }[] = [];
if (/\.(xlsx|csv)$/i.test(filename)) {
const wb = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
await wb.xlsx.load(file);
wb.eachSheet((s) => s.eachRow((row) => {
const text = ((row.values as unknown[]) ?? []).filter(Boolean)
.join(" ").trim();
if (/\?\s*$/.test(text) || /^\d+[\.)]\s/.test(text))
rows.push({ text: text.slice(0, 1000) });
}));
}
// PDFs route through a doc-parser skill → same rows shape (tracked stub)

const q = await prisma.questionnaire.create({ data: { orgId, customerName: filename } });
let segmented = 0;
for (const r of rows) {
const parts = await runAgent<{ questions: string[] }>("question_segmenter",
{ raw: r.text }, { orgId });
for (const p of parts.questions ?? [r.text]) {
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.create({
data: { questionnaireId: q.id, question: p,
segmentation: { raw: r.text } } });
segmented++;
}
}
return { id: q.id, found: rows.length, segmented };
}

// ── CONTEXT RETRIEVAL (policies + controls — keyword, honest) ────
async function ragContext(orgId: string, question: string) {
const terms = question.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9]{4,}/g) ?? [];
const unique = [...new Set(terms)].slice(0, 6);

const policies = unique.length
? await prisma.policyVersion.findMany({
where: { status: "APPROVED", policy: { orgId },
OR: unique.flatMap((t) => [{ contentMd: { contains: t, mode: "insensitive" as const } }]) },
include: { policy: true }, take: 3 })
: [];

const controls = unique.length
? await prisma.control.findMany({
where: { orgId,
OR: unique.flatMap((t) => [
{ name: { contains: t, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
{ description: { contains: t, mode: "insensitive" as const } }]) },
take: 5 })
: [];

const sources = [
...policies.map((p, i) => ({ index: i + 1, type: "policy" as const, id: p.id,
title: `${p.policy.title} v${p.version}`,
excerpt: p.contentMd.slice(0, 600) })),
...controls.map((c, i) => ({ index: policies.length + i + 1, type: "control" as const,
id: c.id, title: c.name, excerpt: c.description.slice(0, 400) })),
];
return { policies, controls, sources };
}

// ── DRAFT (clone | compose) ───────────────────────────────────────
export async function draftAnswer(orgId: string, questionId: string) {
const q = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: questionId }, include: { questionnaire: true } });

const routing = await routeByThreshold(orgId, q.question);

if (routing.action === "CLONE_LIBRARY_ANSWER" && routing.hit) {
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.update({
where: { id: questionId },
data: { answer: routing.hit.answer, status: "AUTO_DRAFTED",
confidence: routing.hit.score, citations: routing.hit.citations as object,
retrievalMeta: { path: "clone", score: routing.hit.score } } });
return { status: "AUTO_DRAFTED" as const };
}

// Compose from context (+ library context when available)
const ctx = await ragContext(orgId, q.question);
const libSources = routing.hits.slice(0, 3).map((h, i) => ({
index: ctx.sources.length + i + 1, type: "library" as const, id: h.answerId,
title: h.canonicalQ, excerpt: h.answer.slice(0, 400) }));

const draft = await runAgent<{
answer: string; citations: { index: number; type: string; id: string;
title: string; excerpt: string }[];
confidence: number; caveats: string[];
}>("questionnaire_composer", {
question: q.question,
sources: [...ctx.sources, ...libSources].map((s) =>
`[${s.index}] (${s.type}) ${s.title}: ${s.excerpt}`),
}, { orgId });

const status = draft.confidence < 0.7 ? "MANDATORY" : "AI_DRAFTED";
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.update({
where: { id: questionId },
data: { answer: draft.answer, status, confidence: draft.confidence,
citations: draft.citations as object,
retrievalMeta: { path: routing.action,
contextSources: ctx.sources.length } } });
return { status: status as "MANDATORY" | "AI_DRAFTED", caveats: draft.caveats };
}

/** Standalone Q&A (MCP answer_security_question + the trust surface). */
export async function answerSecurityQuestion(orgId: string, question: string) {
const routing = await routeByThreshold(orgId, question);
if (routing.action === "CLONE_LIBRARY_ANSWER" && routing.hit)
return { answer: routing.hit.answer, confidence: routing.hit.score,
citations: routing.hit.citations, source: "library" as const };
const ctx = await ragContext(orgId, question);
const draft = await runAgent<{
answer: string; citations: unknown[]; confidence: number; caveats: string[] }>(
"questionnaire_composer", {
question,
sources: ctx.sources.map((s) => `[${s.index}] (${s.type}) ${s.title}: ${s.excerpt}`),
}, { orgId });
return { answer: draft.answer, confidence: draft.confidence,
citations: draft.citations, caveats: draft.caveats, source: "composed" as const };
}

export { upsertAnswerLibrary };
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/review/queue.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { upsertAnswerLibrary } from "../retrieval/search";

/** Material policy change invalidates every answer citing it. */
export async function onPolicyMaterialChange(policyVersionId: string) {
const affected = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findMany({
where: {
status: { in: ["AI_DRAFTED", "AUTO_DRAFTED", "VERIFIED", "EDITED_VERIFIED"] },
OR: [
{ citations: { array_contains: { type: "policy", id: policyVersionId } } },
],
},
}).catch(async () => // array_contains on Json varies by Prisma version — LIKE fallback
prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ id: string }>>`
SELECT id FROM "QuestionnaireQuestion"
WHERE status IN ('AI_DRAFTED','AUTO_DRAFTED','VERIFIED','EDITED_VERIFIED')
AND citations::text LIKE ${`%${policyVersionId}%`}`
.then((rows) => rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id }))));

if (affected.length === 0) return { stale: 0 };
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.updateMany({
where: { id: { in: affected.map((a) => (a as { id: string }).id) } },
data: { status: "STALE" },
});
return { stale: affected.length };
}

export function conflictsWithLibrary(q: { answer: string | null; confidence: number | null },
libraryAnswer: string): boolean {
return !!q.answer && q.confidence !== null && q.confidence > 0.8 &&
q.answer.slice(0, 200) !== libraryAnswer.slice(0, 200);
}

export function citesStaleEvidence(q: { citations: unknown }): boolean {
const s = JSON.stringify(q.citations ?? {});
return /EXPIRED/i.test(s);
}

export const ROUTING_RULES = [
{ when: (a: { status: string; confidence: number | null }) =>
a.status === "AUTO_DRAFTED" && (a.confidence ?? 0) >= 0.9,
queue: "BULK_APPROVE", note: "High-confidence clones — batch keyboard flow" },
{ when: (a: { status: string; confidence: number | null }) =>
a.status === "AI_DRAFTED" && (a.confidence ?? 0) < 0.7,
queue: "MANDATORY", note: "Low confidence — never exportable without review" },
{ when: (a: { status: string }) => a.status === "STALE",
queue: "STALE_REVIEW", note: "Policy MATERIAL change invalidated citations" },
];

/** Library write on approval — the flywheel capture. */
export async function approveAnswer(questionId: string, finalText: string,
actorMembershipId?: string) {
const q = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: questionId }, include: { questionnaire: true } });
const editDistance = q.answer ? levenshteinRatio(q.answer, finalText) : 0;

await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.update({
where: { id: questionId },
data: { answer: finalText,
status: finalText === q.answer ? "VERIFIED" : "EDITED_VERIFIED",
editedByMembershipId: actorMembershipId } });
await upsertAnswerLibrary(q.questionnaire.orgId, q.question, finalText,
q.citations, actorMembershipId);
return { editDistance };
}

function levenshteinRatio(a: string, b: string): number {
if (a === b) return 0;
const m = a.length, n = b.length;
const dp = Array.from({ length: m + 1 }, (_, i) => [i, ...Array(n).fill(0)]);
for (let j = 0; j <= n; j++) dp[0][j] = j;
for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i - 1][j] + 1, dp[i][j - 1] + 1,
dp[i - 1][j - 1] + (a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1));
return dp[m][n] / Math.max(m, n);
}
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/negative/capture.ts
```typescript
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { getRetrievalConfig, PROFILES } from "../retrieval/embed";
import { normalizeQuestion, extractEntities } from "../retrieval/search";

const DISCRIMINATING = new Set(["scope:at-rest", "scope:in-transit", "subject:admins",
"subject:employees", "subject:contractors", "report:type-1", "report:type-2",
"artifact:audit-logs", "artifact:customer-data"]);

export function derivePatternKey(code: string, q: {
question: string; retrievalMeta: unknown }): string {
if (code === "WRONG_ENTITY") {
const entities = extractEntities(q.question);
const retrieved = typeof q.retrievalMeta === "object" && q.retrievalMeta !== null
? extractEntities(JSON.stringify(q.retrievalMeta)) : [];
const pair = [...entities, ...retrieved].filter((e) => DISCRIMINATING.has(e));
return `ENTITY:${[...new Set(pair)].sort().join("|")}`;
}
if (code === "MERGED_QUESTIONS") return "SEG:MERGED";
if (code === "SPLIT_OVERKILL") return "SEG:SPLIT";
if (code === "MISSING_SCOPE") return "SEG:SCOPE";
return `MISC:${code}`;
}

/** Rejection → structured negative example + routed learning actions. */
export async function captureRejection(
questionId: string, code: string, note?: string, correctedSplit?: string[],
) {
const q = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: questionId }, include: { questionnaire: true } });
const orgId = q.questionnaire.orgId;
const cfg = await getRetrievalConfig(orgId);
const profile = PROFILES[cfg.profile] ?? PROFILES.default;

const patternKey = derivePatternKey(code, q as never);
const { expanded } = normalizeQuestion(q.question);
const embedding = (await profile.embed([expanded]))[0];

await prisma.negativeExample.create({
data: { orgId, questionId, rawQuestion: q.question,
segmentation: q.segmentation as object ?? undefined,
correctedSplit: correctedSplit as never ?? undefined,
retrieved: (q.retrievalMeta ?? []) as object,
draftAnswer: q.answer ?? "", rejectionCode: code, reviewerNote: note,
patternKey } as never,
}).catch(async () => { // embedding is an Unsupported column — raw insert
await prisma.$executeRaw`
INSERT INTO "NegativeExample" ("id", "orgId", "questionId", "rawQuestion",
"segmentation", "correctedSplit", "retrieved", "draftAnswer",
"rejectionCode", "reviewerNote", "patternKey", "embedding", "status", "createdAt")
VALUES (${randomUUID()}, ${orgId}, ${questionId}, ${q.question},
${JSON.stringify(q.segmentation ?? {})}::jsonb,
${JSON.stringify(correctedSplit ?? [])}::jsonb,
${JSON.stringify(q.retrievalMeta ?? [])}::jsonb,
${q.answer ?? ""}, ${code}, ${note ?? null}, ${patternKey},
${`[${embedding.join(",")}]`}::vector, 'ACTIVE', now())`;
});

// Route to the layer that can learn from it
const { maybeLearnConflictRule } = await import("./conflictRules");
const { trackSegmentationCluster } = await import("./promptPromotion");
switch (code) {
case "WRONG_ENTITY": await maybeLearnConflictRule(orgId, patternKey, questionId); break;
case "MERGED_QUESTIONS": case "SPLIT_OVERKILL": case "MISSING_SCOPE":
await trackSegmentationCluster(orgId, patternKey, correctedSplit); break;
case "HALLUCINATED":
// candidate normalization-synonym gap → calibration monitor surfaces it
break;
case "OUT_OF_DATE":
await prisma.task.create({ data: {
orgId, source: "GAP_ANALYSIS", priority: "MEDIUM",
title: "Stale-citation propagation gap",
description: `Answer ${questionId} cited superseded content — check ` +
`onPolicyMaterialChange wiring for the cited source.` } });
break;
}
emit({ type: "brain.anomaly.opened", source: "agent", orgId,
payload: { kind: "rejection", code, patternKey } });
}
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/negative/conflictRules.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { entitiesConflict } from "../retrieval/search";

/** Two failures with the same entity pair promote a learned rule. */
export async function maybeLearnConflictRule(orgId: string, patternKey: string,
questionId: string) {
const prior = await prisma.negativeExample.count({
where: { orgId, patternKey, status: "ACTIVE" } });
if (prior < 2) return null; // one incident = noise; two = pattern

const [a, b] = patternKey.replace("ENTITY:", "").split("|");
if (!a || !b) return null;
return prisma.entityConflictRule.upsert({
where: { entityA_entityB: { entityA: a, entityB: b } },
update: { learnedFrom: { push: questionId } },
create: { entityA: a, entityB: b, action: "PENALIZE", learnedFrom: [questionId] },
});
}

export async function getActiveConflictRules(orgId: string) {
return prisma.entityConflictRule.findMany({
where: { OR: [{ orgId }, { orgId: null }], active: true } });
}

/** Hardcoded pairs + learned rules — wired into retrieval scoring. */
export async function entitiesConflictWithRules(
orgId: string, qEnt: string[], libEnt: string[],
): Promise<boolean> {
if (entitiesConflict(qEnt, libEnt)) return true;
const rules = await getActiveConflictRules(orgId);
return rules.some((r) =>
(qEnt.includes(r.entityA) && libEnt.includes(r.entityB)) ||
(qEnt.includes(r.entityB) && libEnt.includes(r.entityA)));
}
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/negative/guards.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import type { Retrieved } from "../retrieval/search";

/**
* Pre-clone guard: has this exact answer been wrongly cloned for a
* near-identical query before? If yes → force human review.
*/
export async function guardAutoClone(
orgId: string, hit: Retrieved, queryEmbedding: number[],
): Promise<{ blocked: boolean; reason?: string }> {
const negatives = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{
id: string; "rejectionCode": string; retrieved: unknown }>>`
SELECT id, "rejectionCode", retrieved FROM "NegativeExample"
WHERE "orgId" = ${orgId} AND status = 'ACTIVE'
AND embedding IS NOT NULL
AND 1 - (embedding <=> ${`[${queryEmbedding.join(",")}]`}::vector) > 0.90
LIMIT 5`;

for (const n of negatives) {
const retrieved = JSON.stringify(n.retrieved ?? {});
// did this negative's draft clone the SAME library answer we're about to clone?
if (retrieved.includes(hit.answerId) || retrieved.includes(hit.canonicalQ.slice(0, 80))) {
return { blocked: true,
reason: `This answer was rejected in a near-identical past question ` +
`(${n.rejectionCode}). Routed to mandatory review.` };
}
}
return { blocked: false };
}
```

### FILE: packages/questionnaire/src/negative/promptPromotion.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";

/** 3 examples in a cluster with ≥1 corrected split → candidate few-shot. */
export async function trackSegmentationCluster(
orgId: string, patternKey: string, correctedSplit: string[] | undefined,
) {
const cluster = await prisma.negativeExample.findMany({
where: { orgId, patternKey, status: "ACTIVE" },
orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 10 });
if (cluster.length < 3 || !cluster.some((c) => c.correctedSplit)) return null;

const seed = cluster.find((c) => c.correctedSplit)!;
return promoteSegmentationPrompt(orgId, {
example: { raw: seed.rawQuestion, correct: seed.correctedSplit as string[] },
failureType: patternKey.includes("MERGED") ? "must_split" : "must_not_split",
});
}

function injectFewShot(system: string, fewShot: {
example: { raw: string; correct: string[] }; failureType: string }): string {
return `${system}

LEARNED EXAMPLE (${fewShot.failureType}):
Q: ${fewShot.example.raw}
Correct split: ${JSON.stringify(fewShot.example.correct)}`;
}

function splitsEquivalent(a: string[], b: string[]): boolean {
const norm = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, "").trim();
return a.length === b.length &&
a.every((x, i) => norm(x) === norm(b[i]));
}

/** The gate: regression suite over golden set + ALL active negatives. */
async function runSegmentationRegression(orgId: string, promptSystem: string) {
const negatives = await prisma.negativeExample.findMany({
where: { orgId, status: "ACTIVE",
rejectionCode: { in: ["MERGED_QUESTIONS", "SPLIT_OVERKILL", "MISSING_SCOPE"] } },
take: 50 });
const { runAgent } = await import("@agents");

let pass = 0, clusterFixes = 0;
const regressions: string[] = [];
for (const case_ of negatives) {
const expected = (case_.correctedSplit as string[] | null)
?? [case_.rawQuestion]; // negatives without corrections are just eval data
const split = await runAgent<{ questions: string[] }>("question_segmenter",
{ raw: case_.rawQuestion, __candidateSystem: promptSystem }, { orgId })
.catch(() => ({ questions: [] as string[] }));
const ok = splitsEquivalent(split.questions ?? [], expected);
if (ok) { pass++; if (case_.correctedSplit) clusterFixes++; }
else if (case_.correctedSplit) regressions.push(case_.id);
}
const total = negatives.length || 1;
return { passRate: pass / total, clusterFixes, regressions, suiteSize: negatives.length };
}

/** Eval-gated promotion: never regress the suite, must fix the cluster. */
export async function promoteSegmentationPrompt(orgId: string, fewShot: {
example: { raw: string; correct: string[] }; failureType: string }) {
const current = await prisma.promptTemplate.findFirst({
where: { key: "question_segmenter", status: "ACTIVE" },
orderBy: { version: "desc" } });
const currentSystem = current?.systemPrompt
?? "Split questionnaire questions into atomic units. Output valid JSON only.";
const candidate = injectFewShot(currentSystem, fewShot);

const baseline = await runSegmentationRegression(orgId, currentSystem);
const evalResult = await runSegmentationRegression(orgId, candidate);

const promotable = evalResult.passRate >= baseline.passRate &&
evalResult.clusterFixes >= 1 && evalResult.regressions.length === 0;

if (!promotable) {
emit({ type: "brain.anomaly.opened", source: "agent", orgId,
payload: { kind: "prompt_promotion_rejected", agent: "question_segmenter" } });
return { promoted: false, evalResult, baseline };
}

const vNext = await prisma.promptTemplate.create({
data: { key: "question_segmenter", version: (current?.version ?? 0) + 1,
name: "question_segmenter", systemPrompt: candidate,
userTemplate: "{{input}}",
modelConfig: { model: "haiku", maxTokens: 1000 }, status: "ACTIVE" } });
if (current) await prisma.promptTemplate.update({
where: { id: current.id }, data: { status: "RETIRED" } });

await prisma.negativeExample.updateMany({
where: { orgId, patternKey: { in: ["SEG:MERGED", "SEG:SPLIT", "SEG:SCOPE"] } },
data: { status: "PROMOTED" } });
emit({ type: "brain.anomaly.resolved", source: "agent", orgId,
payload: { kind: "prompt_promoted", version: vNext.version } });
return { promoted: true, version: vNext.version, evalResult };
}
```

### FILE: packages/review/src/sources/types.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import type { ReviewQueueType } from "@prisma/client";

export interface RenderBundle {
header: { title: string; jurisdiction?: string; source: string; link?: string };
body: Array<
| { kind: "diff-table"; rows: { field: string; from: string; to: string;
quote?: string; confidence?: number }[] }
| { kind: "paraphrase-card"; requirement: { code: string; title: string; desc: string };
officialHint?: string }
| { kind: "impact-list"; items: { label: string; level: string; action?: string }[] }
| { kind: "evidence-quote"; quotes: { source: string; text: string }[] }
| { kind: "affected-orgs"; count: number; orgs: string[] }
>;
risk: string[];
}

export interface ReviewSource {
queueType: ReviewQueueType;
scanAndEnqueue(): Promise<{ enqueued: number }>;
render(sourceId: string): Promise<RenderBundle>;
approve(sourceId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string, note: string,
isFirstApproval: boolean): Promise<unknown>;
reject(sourceId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string, note: string): Promise<void>;
}

/** Enqueue helper — SLA start is a brain event; workers layer starts timers. */
export async function enqueue(input: {
queueType: ReviewQueueType; sourceId: string; title: string;
jurisdiction?: string; frameworks?: string[];
priority: "LOW" | "MEDIUM" | "HIGH" | "CRITICAL";
confidence?: number; requiredCredential?: string; preview: object;
slaDays?: number;
}) {
const slaDays = input.slaDays ?? (input.priority === "CRITICAL" ? 2
: input.priority === "HIGH" ? 5 : 10);
const item = await prisma.reviewItem.upsert({
where: { sourceId: input.sourceId },
update: { title: input.title, priority: input.priority,
preview: input.preview, slaDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + slaDays * 86_400_000) },
create: {
queueType: input.queueType, sourceId: input.sourceId, title: input.title,
jurisdiction: input.jurisdiction, frameworks: input.frameworks ?? [],
priority: input.priority, confidence: input.confidence ?? 0,
requiredCredential: input.requiredCredential as never,
slaDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + slaDays * 86_400_000),
preview: input.preview as object,
},
});
emit({ type: "review.item.enqueued", source: "review",
payload: { reviewItemId: item.id, queueType: input.queueType,
priority: input.priority } });
return item;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/review/src/sources/frameworkContent.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { ReviewSource, RenderBundle, enqueue } from "./types";

export const frameworkContentSource: ReviewSource = {
queueType: "FRAMEWORK_CONTENT",

async scanAndEnqueue() {
const rows = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: { verified: false }, include: { framework: true },
});
let enqueued = 0;
for (const r of rows) {
// already decided (APPROVED/REJECTED) items keep their terminal state
const existing = await prisma.reviewItem.findUnique({ where: { sourceId: r.id } });
if (existing && existing.status !== "PENDING") continue;

const credential = r.framework.code === "FINRA" || r.framework.code === "SEC"
? "FINRA_SEC" : r.framework.code === "GDPR" ? "LEGAL_EU" : "CIPA";
await enqueue({
queueType: "FRAMEWORK_CONTENT", sourceId: r.id,
title: `${r.framework.code} ${r.code} — ${r.title}`,
frameworks: [r.framework.code],
priority: r.framework.isPublished ? "HIGH" : "MEDIUM",
requiredCredential: credential,
preview: { code: r.code, framework: r.framework.code,
category: r.category, desc: r.description },
});
enqueued++;
}
return { enqueued };
},

async render(id): Promise<RenderBundle> {
const r = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id }, include: { framework: true } });
const usage = await prisma.controlMapping.count({
where: { requirementId: r.id, status: "ACCEPTED" } });
return {
header: { title: `${r.framework.code} ${r.code} — ${r.title}`,
jurisdiction: frameworkJurisdiction(r.framework.code),
source: r.framework.publisher,
officialHint: "Publisher link only — framework text is licensed; verify the paraphrase against your copy." },
body: [
{ kind: "paraphrase-card",
requirement: { code: r.code, title: r.title, desc: r.description } },
{ kind: "impact-list", items: [
{ label: `${usage} org control mappings reference this requirement`,
level: usage > 50 ? "HIGH" : "INFO" },
]},
],
risk: usage > 50 ? ["This paraphrase is live in customer programs — wording changes may ripple into policy coverage checks."] : [],
};
},

async approve(id, reviewerMembershipId) {
const r = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.update({
where: { id }, data: { verified: true, verifiedBy: reviewerMembershipId } });
await maybePublishFramework(r.frameworkId);
return { applied: "REQUIREMENT_VERIFIED" };
},

async reject(id, _reviewer, note) {
await prisma.frameworkRequirement.update({ where: { id },
data: { verified: false, rejectionNote: note } });
// STUB: content-rewrite pipeline re-drafts the paraphrase for re-review.
console.log(`[review:STUB] paraphrase rewrite queued for ${id}: ${note.slice(0, 120)}`);
},
};

function frameworkJurisdiction(code: string): string {
if (code === "GDPR") return "EU";
if (code === "SOC2" || code === "HIPAA" || code === "FINRA" || code === "SEC")
return "US-Federal";
return "Global";
}

/** Publish gate: a framework publishes only at 100% verified requirements. */
export async function maybePublishFramework(frameworkId: string) {
const fw = await prisma.framework.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: frameworkId },
include: { _count: { select: { requirements: true } } } });
const verified = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.count({
where: { frameworkId, verified: true } });
if (verified === fw._count.requirements && verified > 0 && !fw.isPublished) {
await prisma.framework.update({ where: { id: frameworkId }, data: { isPublished: true } });
}
}
```

### FILE: packages/review/src/sources/matrixProposal.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { ReviewSource, RenderBundle, enqueue } from "./types";
import { applyMatrixProposal } from "@knowledge";

async function countOpenIncidentsIn(stateCode: string): Promise<number> {
return prisma.incidentNotification.count({
where: { status: "PENDING", authority: { contains: stateCode },
incident: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "TRIAGE", "CONTAINED", "ERADICATED", "RECOVERED"] } } } });
}

async function affectedOrgsForRule(stateRuleId: string) {
const orgs = await prisma.controlMapping.findMany({
where: { requirement: { framework: { requirements: { some: {} } } },
status: "ACCEPTED" },
select: { control: { select: { orgId: true } } }, take: 500 });
// matrix rules affect orgs via the incident calculator, not mappings —
// report org count with incidents in jurisdiction as the blast radius
const withIncidents = await prisma.incidentNotification.findMany({
where: { status: "PENDING", authority: { contains: stateRuleId } },
select: { incident: { select: { orgId: true } } }, distinct: ["incidentId"] });
const orgSet = new Set(withIncidents.map((n) => n.incident.orgId));
return { count: orgSet.size, orgs: [...orgSet].slice(0, 10) };
}

export const matrixProposalSource: ReviewSource = {
queueType: "MATRIX_PROPOSAL",

async scanAndEnqueue() {
const rows = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.findMany({
where: { status: "PENDING_REVIEW" }, include: { bill: true } });
let enqueued = 0;
for (const p of rows) {
const openIncidents = await countOpenIncidentsIn(p.bill.stateCode);
const effectiveNow = !p.effectiveAt || p.effectiveAt <= new Date();
await enqueue({
queueType: "MATRIX_PROPOSAL", sourceId: p.id,
title: `${p.bill.stateCode}: ${p.bill.billNumber} amends breach rule`,
jurisdiction: p.bill.stateCode,
priority: openIncidents > 0 && effectiveNow ? "CRITICAL" // deadlines shift mid-incident
: p.extractorConfidence > 0.85 ? "HIGH" : "MEDIUM",
confidence: p.extractorConfidence,
requiredCredential: "LEGAL_US_STATES",
preview: { state: p.bill.stateCode, bill: p.bill.billNumber,
changes: (p.diff as unknown[]).length,
effectiveAt: p.effectiveAt, openIncidents },
});
enqueued++;
}
return { enqueued };
},

async render(id): Promise<RenderBundle> {
const p = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id }, include: { bill: true, stateRule: true } });
const openIncidents = await countOpenIncidentsIn(p.bill.stateCode);
const diff = p.diff as { field: string; from: unknown; to: unknown;
quote?: string; confidence?: number }[];
return {
header: { title: `${p.bill.stateCode} ${p.bill.billNumber} — ${p.bill.title}`,
jurisdiction: p.bill.stateCode, source: "State legislature",
link: p.bill.url ?? undefined },
body: [
{ kind: "diff-table",
rows: diff.map((d) => ({ field: d.field, from: String(d.from), to: String(d.to),
quote: d.quote, confidence: d.confidence })) },
{ kind: "evidence-quote", quotes: diff.filter((d) => d.quote)
.map((d) => ({ source: `${p.bill.billNumber} enacted text`, text: d.quote! })) },
{ kind: "affected-orgs", ...(await affectedOrgsForRule(p.stateRuleId)) },
],
risk: [
...(openIncidents > 0 ? [`${openIncidents} open incident obligation(s) in ${p.bill.stateCode} — approving recomputes deadlines immediately`] : []),
...(p.effectiveAt && p.effectiveAt > new Date()
? [`Effective ${p.effectiveAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} — application is scheduled, not immediate`] : []),
],
};
},

async approve(id, reviewerMembershipId, note) {
return applyMatrixProposal(id, reviewerMembershipId, note);
},

async reject(id, _reviewer, note) {
const p = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.update({
where: { id }, data: { status: "REJECTED", reviewerNote: note } });
await prisma.trackedBill.update({ where: { id: p.billId }, data: { status: "DISMISSED" } });
},
};
```

### FILE: packages/review/src/sources/regChange.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { ReviewSource, RenderBundle, enqueue } from "./types";
import { applyVerifiedChange } from "@knowledge";

export const regChangeSource: ReviewSource = {
queueType: "REG_CHANGE",

async scanAndEnqueue() {
const rows = await prisma.regChange.findMany({
where: { status: "PENDING_REVIEW" },
include: { sourceDocument: true, impacts: true } });
let enqueued = 0;
for (const c of rows) {
const levels = c.impacts.map((i) => i.impactLevel);
const priority = levels.includes("CRITICAL") ? "CRITICAL"
: levels.includes("MAJOR") ? "HIGH" : "MEDIUM";
await enqueue({
queueType: "REG_CHANGE", sourceId: c.id,
title: `${c.jurisdiction}: ${c.summary.slice(0, 90)}`,
jurisdiction: c.jurisdiction,
priority,
confidence: ((c.extracted as { confidence?: number })?.confidence) ?? 0,
requiredCredential: c.jurisdiction.startsWith("EU") ? "LEGAL_EU" : "CIPA",
preview: { summary: c.summary, changeType: c.changeType,
effectiveAt: c.effectiveAt, impacts: levels.length },
});
enqueued++;
}
return { enqueued };
},

async render(id): Promise<RenderBundle> {
const c = await prisma.regChange.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id }, include: { sourceDocument: true, impacts: true } });
const extracted = c.extracted as { obligations?: { who?: string; what?: string; deadline?: string }[] };
const orgs = await prisma.controlMapping.findMany({
where: { requirementId: { in: c.impacts.filter((i) => i.requirementId)
.map((i) => i.requirementId!) }, status: "ACCEPTED" },
select: { control: { select: { orgId: true } } } });
const orgSet = new Set(orgs.map((o) => o.control.orgId));

return {
header: { title: c.summary.slice(0, 120),
jurisdiction: c.jurisdiction,
source: c.sourceDocument.title, link: c.sourceDocument.url },
body: [
{ kind: "evidence-quote", quotes: [
{ source: `${c.changeType} · effective ${c.effectiveAt?.toISOString().slice(0, 10) ?? "TBD"}`,
text: c.summary },
...(extracted.obligations ?? []).map((o, i) => ({
source: `Obligation ${i + 1}${o.who ? ` (${o.who})` : ""}`,
text: `${o.what ?? ""}${o.deadline ? ` — deadline ${o.deadline}` : ""}` })),
]},
{ kind: "impact-list", items: c.impacts.map((i) => ({
label: i.requirementId ?? i.frameworkId, level: i.impactLevel,
action: i.suggestedAction ?? undefined })) },
{ kind: "affected-orgs", count: orgSet.size, orgs: [...orgSet].slice(0, 10) },
],
risk: c.impacts.some((i) => i.impactLevel === "CRITICAL")
? ["CRITICAL impact — four-eyes approval required before fan-out"] : [],
};
},

async approve(id, reviewerMembershipId) {
return applyVerifiedChange(id, reviewerMembershipId); // fan-out tasks + webhooks
},

async reject(id, _reviewer, note) {
await prisma.regChange.update({ where: { id },
data: { status: "REJECTED", reviewedByMembershipId: _reviewer } });
console.log(`[review] reg change ${id} rejected: ${note.slice(0, 120)}`);
},
};
```

### FILE: packages/review/src/engine.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import type { ReviewerCredential } from "@prisma/client";
import { frameworkContentSource } from "./sources/frameworkContent";
import { matrixProposalSource } from "./sources/matrixProposal";
import { regChangeSource } from "./sources/regChange";
import type { ReviewSource } from "./sources/types";

export const SOURCES: Record<string, ReviewSource> = {
FRAMEWORK_CONTENT: frameworkContentSource,
MATRIX_PROPOSAL: matrixProposalSource,
REG_CHANGE: regChangeSource,
};

export class Conflict extends Error {}
export class NotFoundError extends Error {}

async function record(itemId: string, actorMembershipId: string | null,
action: string, note?: string) {
await prisma.reviewDecision.create({
data: { reviewItemId: itemId, actorMembershipId: actorMembershipId ?? "",
action, note } });
}

async function assertCredential(membershipId: string, credential: ReviewerCredential | null) {
if (!credential) return;
const profile = await prisma.reviewerProfile.findUnique({ where: { membershipId } });
if (!profile?.active || !profile.credentials.includes(credential))
throw new Conflict(`This item requires the ${credential} credential`);
}

async function finalize(itemId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string | null,
status: string, note?: string) {
await prisma.reviewItem.update({
where: { id: itemId },
data: { status: status as never, decidedByMembershipId: reviewerMembershipId ?? "",
decidedAt: new Date(), decisionNote: note,
claimedByMembershipId: null } });
await record(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, status, note);
}

export async function scanAllQueues() {
const results = await Promise.all(Object.values(SOURCES).map((s) => s.scanAndEnqueue()));
return { enqueued: results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.enqueued, 0) };
}

export async function claim(itemId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string) {
const item = await prisma.reviewItem.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: itemId } });
if (item.status !== "PENDING") throw new Conflict("Item not claimable");
await assertCredential(reviewerMembershipId, item.requiredCredential);

await prisma.reviewItem.update({ where: { id: itemId },
data: { status: "CLAIMED", claimedByMembershipId: reviewerMembershipId,
claimedAt: new Date() } });
await record(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "CLAIM");
// Auto-release: workers layer watches CLAIMED items >24h and requeues via
// this same event pattern (brain deadman for the review flow).
emit({ type: "review.item.enqueued", source: "review",
payload: { reviewItemId: itemId, claimed: true } });
return item;
}

export async function decide(itemId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string,
decision: "APPROVE" | "REJECT" | "DEFER", note: string, deferUntil?: Date) {

const item = await prisma.reviewItem.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: itemId } });
await assertCredential(reviewerMembershipId, item.requiredCredential);
const source = SOURCES[item.queueType];

if (decision === "DEFER") {
await prisma.reviewItem.update({ where: { id: itemId },
data: { status: "PENDING", deferredUntil: deferUntil,
claimedByMembershipId: null } });
await record(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "DEFER", note);
return { status: "DEFERRED" };
}

if (decision === "REJECT") {
await source.reject(item.sourceId, reviewerMembershipId, note);
await finalize(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "REJECTED", note);
return { status: "REJECTED" };
}

// APPROVE — four-eyes gate on CRITICAL
if (item.priority === "CRITICAL") {
if (!item.firstApprovalBy) {
await prisma.reviewItem.update({ where: { id: itemId },
data: { status: "APPROVED_PENDING_SECOND", firstApprovalBy: reviewerMembershipId,
firstApprovalAt: new Date() } });
await record(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "APPROVE_FIRST", note);
emit({ type: "review.item.overdue", source: "review",
payload: { reviewItemId: itemId, awaiting: "second-approver" } });
return { status: "APPROVED_PENDING_SECOND" };
}
if (item.firstApprovalBy === reviewerMembershipId)
throw new Conflict("Four-eyes: second approval must come from a different reviewer");
}

const result = await source.approve(item.sourceId, reviewerMembershipId, note,
!!item.firstApprovalBy);
await finalize(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "APPROVED", note);
return result ?? { status: "APPROVED" };
}

/** A newer proposal for the same state rule retires the older one. */
export async function supersedeOnConflict(newProposalId: string, stateRuleId: string) {
const stale = await prisma.reviewItem.findMany({
where: { queueType: "MATRIX_PROPOSAL",
status: { in: ["PENDING", "CLAIMED", "APPROVED_PENDING_SECOND"] },
sourceId: { not: newProposalId } },
});
let superseded = 0;
for (const item of stale) {
const p = await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.findUnique({ where: { id: item.sourceId } });
if (p?.stateRuleId !== stateRuleId) continue;
await prisma.matrixAmendmentProposal.update({ where: { id: p.id },
data: { status: "SUPERSEDED" } });
await finalize(item.id, null, "SUPERSEDED", `Superseded by ${newProposalId}`);
superseded++;
}
return { superseded };
}

export async function releaseClaim(itemId: string, reviewerMembershipId: string) {
await prisma.reviewItem.update({ where: { id: itemId },
data: { status: "PENDING", claimedByMembershipId: null } });
await record(itemId, reviewerMembershipId, "RELEASE");
}
```

### FILE: packages/mcp/src/tools/registry.ts
```typescript
import { z } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

export interface RestBinding {
method: "GET" | "POST" | "PATCH";
path: string; // "/v1/controls/{slug}" — params map to zod keys
summaryTag: string; // OpenAPI tag
}

export interface ToolDef<S extends z.ZodRawShape = z.ZodRawShape> {
name: string; description: string;
scope: string; tier: 1 | 2 | 3;
readOnly: boolean;
rest?: RestBinding;
input: S;
handler: (args: Record<string, any>, ctx: { orgId: string; keyId: string }) => Promise<unknown>;
}

export function defineTool<S extends z.ZodRawShape>(def: ToolDef<S>) { return def; }

/** In-memory token buckets (Redis in prod — same interface). */
const buckets = new Map<string, { tokens: number; ts: number }>();
export async function rateLimit(key: string, perMin: number): Promise<boolean> {
const now = Date.now();
const b = buckets.get(key) ?? { tokens: perMin, ts: now };
const refill = ((now - b.ts) / 60_000) * perMin;
b.tokens = Math.min(perMin, b.tokens + refill); b.ts = now;
if (b.tokens < 1) { buckets.set(key, b); return false; }
b.tokens -= 1; buckets.set(key, b);
return true;
}

export class McpError extends Error {
constructor(public code: number, message: string) { super(message); }
}

/** Wraps a ToolDef for the MCP server: scope → rate → audit → execute. */
export function toMcpTool(def: ToolDef<any>) {
return {
name: def.name,
description: def.description,
inputSchema: def.input,
annotations: {
readOnlyHint: def.readOnly,
destructiveHint: false, // nothing in the catalog is destructive by design
idempotentHint: def.tier >= 2 ? false : true,
openWorldHint: false,
},
async handle(args: unknown, ctx: { orgId: string; keyId: string; scopes: string[] }) {
if (!ctx.scopes.includes(def.scope) && !ctx.scopes.includes("*"))
throw new McpError(-32603, `Missing scope: ${def.scope}`);
const perMin = { 1: 120, 2: 30, 3: 6 }[def.tier];
if (!(await rateLimit(`mcp:${ctx.keyId}:${def.name}`, perMin)))
throw new McpError(-32063, `Rate limit exceeded for ${def.name}`);
const parsed = z.object(def.input).parse(args ?? {});
const start = Date.now();
try {
return await def.handler(parsed, { orgId: ctx.orgId, keyId: ctx.keyId });
} finally {
// Every call audited — args redacted (never store evidence content)
await prisma.aIActionLog.create({
data: { orgId: ctx.orgId, action: `mcp:${def.name}`,
resourceType: "MCP_TOOL", resourceId: def.name,
details: { latencyMs: Date.now() - start,
argKeys: Object.keys(parsed as object) } },
}).catch(() => {});
}
},
};
}
```
### END FILE

```typescript
/** Packaging metadata — the entitlement middleware gates on this map
```

```typescript
import { z } from "zod";

import {

(await prisma.framework.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: code } })).id;

export const listTasks = defineTool({
name: "list_tasks",
description: "Open tasks with source, priority, and SLA state.",

rest: { method: "GET", path: "/v1/tasks", summaryTag: "Tasks" },

?? await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({

?? (await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({
where: { orgId, name: { contains: checkId, mode: "insensitive" } } }));

},
});

export const dsarMetrics = defineTool({
name: "dsar_metrics",
description: "DSAR volume and on-time rate (12-month window).",
scope: "read:privacy", tier: 1, readOnly: true,
rest: { method: "GET", path: "/v1/privacy-requests/metrics", summaryTag: "Privacy" },
input: {},
handler: async (_, { orgId }) => {
const since = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 86_400_000);
const rows = await prisma.privacyRequest.findMany({
where: { orgId, createdAt: { gte: since } },
select: { status: true, dueAt: true, extensionTo: true, updatedAt: true } });
const delivered = rows.filter((r) => r.status === "DELIVERED");
const onTime = delivered.filter((r) =>
new Date(r.updatedAt) <= (r.extensionTo ?? r.dueAt)).length;
return { total: rows.length, delivered: delivered.length,
onTimeRate: delivered.length ? onTime / delivered.length : null };
},
});

export const searchKnowledge = defineTool({
name: "search_knowledge",
description: "Search across org policies and evidence titles.",
scope: "read:policies", tier: 1, readOnly: true,
rest: { method: "GET", path: "/v1/search", summaryTag: "Risk" },
input: { q: z.string().min(2).max(200) },
handler: async ({ q }, { orgId }) => {
const policies = await prisma.policyVersion.findMany({
where: { status: "APPROVED", policy: { orgId },
contentMd: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" } },
include: { policy: true }, take: 5 });
const evidence = await prisma.evidence.findMany({
where: { orgId, title: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" } }, take: 5 });
return {
policies: policies.map((p) => ({ title: p.policy.title, version: p.version,
excerpt: p.contentMd.slice(Math.max(p.contentMd.toLowerCase().indexOf(q.toLowerCase()) - 100, 0), 500) })),
evidence: evidence.map((e) => ({ title: e.title, collectedAt: e.collectedAt })),
};
},
});
```

### FILE: packages/mcp/src/server.ts
```typescript
import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
import { StreamableHTTPServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/streamableHttp.js";
import Fastify from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import { CATALOG } from "./tools/catalog";
import { toMcpTool, McpError } from "./tools/registry";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { argon2id } from "@api/keyCrypto"; // batch 7 provides; fallback inline below

// API-key resolution — same key system as REST (prefix + argon2id verify)
async function resolveApiKey(key: string | undefined) {
if (!key?.startsWith("cpk_live_")) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const prefix = key.slice(0, 16);
const record = await prisma.apiKey.findUnique({ where: { prefix } });
if (!record?.keyHash || record.revokedAt) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const { verify } = await import("@node-rs/argon2");
if (!(await verify(record.keyHash, key))) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
return record;
}

const server = new McpServer({ name: "compliance-platform", version: "1.0.0" });

for (const def of CATALOG) {
const tool = toMcpTool(def);
server.tool(tool.name, tool.description, tool.inputSchema as never, tool.annotations,
async (args: unknown, extra: any) => {
const auth = extra?.authInfo as
{ orgId: string; keyId: string; scopes: string[] } | undefined;
if (!auth) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const result = await tool.handle(args, auth);
return { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) }] };
});
}

const app = Fastify({ logger: false });

app.post("/mcp", async (req, reply) => {
const key = (req.headers["authorization"] ?? "").replace(/^Bearer /, "") ||
(req.headers["x-api-key"] as string | undefined);
const record = await resolveApiKey(key).catch(() => null);
if (!record) return reply.code(401).send({ error: "Invalid or revoked API key" });

const transport = new StreamableHTTPServerTransport({
sessionIdGenerator: undefined, // stateless — keys carry identity
authInfo: { orgId: record.orgId, keyId: record.id,
scopes: record.scopes } as never,
});
await server.connect(transport);
await transport.handleRequest(req.raw, reply.raw);
});

const port = Number(process.env.MCP_PORT ?? 3001);
app.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" }).then(() =>
console.log(`MCP server on :${port} (${CATALOG.length} tools)`));
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/db/prisma/rls.sql
```sql
-- Applies ENABLE/FORCE RLS + org isolation to EVERY table with an "orgId"
-- column EXCEPT the brain observer tables (see exclusion below).
--
-- WHY THE EXCLUSION: the Brain worker writes events outside org-session
-- context (the observer must not depend on the observed). Brain tables
-- carry orgId as a ROUTING/FILTER key, not a tenancy boundary. Org-scoped
-- reads are enforced in code: session-authed API routes filter by req.org,
-- SSE clients are registered per-org in sseRegistry, and MCP access is
-- gated by the ops:brain scope. The RLS audit (tests/rls) derives ORG_TABLES
-- from the MAIN Prisma schema only — consistent with this exclusion.
DO $$
DECLARE t record;
BEGIN
FOR t IN
SELECT c.relname FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relkind = 'r'
AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'public'
AND table_name = c.relname
AND column_name = 'orgId')
AND c.relname NOT IN ('BrainEvent', 'FlowRun', 'Anomaly', 'BrainIncident')
LOOP
EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', t.relname);
EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', t.relname);
EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS org_isolation ON %I', t.relname);
EXECUTE format(
'CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON %I FOR ALL
USING ("orgId" = current_setting(''app.current_org'', true))
WITH CHECK ("orgId" = current_setting(''app.current_org'', true))',
t.relname);
END LOOP;
END $$;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/prisma/schema.prisma
```prisma
// CONSOLIDATION FIX: plain table names in the public schema (multiSchema
// dropped — ledger fix #1) + own generated client. The rls.sql exclusion
// keeps the observer's writes outside org-session RLS (see that file).
generator client {
provider = "prisma-client-js"
output = "../src/generated"
}
datasource db {
provider = "postgresql"
url = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

enum EventSeverity { INFO WARN ERROR CRITICAL }

model BrainEvent {
id String @id
at DateTime @default(now())
type String
source String
orgId String?
severity EventSeverity @default(INFO)
traceId String?
flowId String?
payload Json @default("{}")

@@index([at])
@@index([type, at])
@@index([flowId, at])
@@index([source, at])
@@index([orgId, at])
}

model FlowRun {
id String @id
flowType String
flowId String @unique
traceId String?
orgId String?
status String @default("RUNNING") // RUNNING|SUCCEEDED|FAILED|TIMEOUT|STUCK
startedAt DateTime @default(now())
endedAt DateTime?
durationMs Int?
eventCount Int @default(0)
lastEventAt DateTime?

@@index([status, startedAt])
@@index([flowType, startedAt])
}

model HealthSnapshot {
id String @id @default(cuid())
at DateTime @default(now())
composite Float
subsystems Json
openAnomalies Int
@@index([at])
}

model Anomaly {
id String @id @default(cuid())
at DateTime @default(now())
detector String // zscore|rate|cost|freshness|silence
subsystem String
metric String
orgId String? // null = platform-level
value Float
baseline Float
zScore Float?
severity String // WARN|CRITICAL
status String @default("OPEN") // OPEN|ACKNOWLEDGED|RESOLVED|FALSE_POSITIVE
details Json?
incidentId String?
@@index([status, at])
@@index([subsystem, at])
@@index([orgId, status])
}

model BrainIncident {
id String @id @default(cuid())
orgId String?
title String
anomalyIds String[] @default([])
status String @default("OPEN") // OPEN|ACKNOWLEDGED|MITIGATING|RESOLVED
diagnosis String?
runbookRef String?
reflexesTaken Json?
startedAt DateTime @default(now())
resolvedAt DateTime?
@@index([status, startedAt])
}

model ReflexLog {
id String @id @default(cuid())
rule String
trigger String
action String
result String // EXECUTED|SKIPPED|FAILED
detail Json?
at DateTime @default(now())
@@index([at])
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/brainDb.ts
```typescript
import { PrismaClient } from "./generated";

export const brainDb = new PrismaClient({
log: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" ? ["error"] : ["warn", "error"],
});
```

```typescript
/** One taxonomy, dual fan-out: this catalog drives brain events AND (via
* webhooks.ts) the external webhook event list — module 10's catalog. */
export const EVENT_CATALOG = {
// ── domain events (module-10 webhook taxonomy) ──
"control.test.failed": "ERROR", "control.test.passed": "INFO",
"evidence.created": "INFO", "evidence.quality.changed": "INFO",
"gap.detected": "WARN", "gap.remediated": "INFO",
"policy.published": "INFO", "policy.acknowledgment.overdue": "WARN",
"vendor.review.due": "WARN", "incident.created": "CRITICAL",
"incident.notification.due": "CRITICAL",
"regchange.detected": "INFO", "regchange.applied": "INFO",
"questionnaire.drafted": "INFO", "privacy_request.created": "INFO",
"trust.nda.signed": "INFO",
// ── brain-native events ──
"connector.sync.completed": "INFO", "connector.sync.failed": "ERROR",
"connector.silence.detected": "CRITICAL", "connector.paused": "WARN",
"flow.started": "INFO", "flow.completed": "INFO", "flow.failed": "ERROR",
"flow.stuck": "WARN",
"llm.generation.completed": "INFO", "llm.generation.failed": "ERROR",
"llm.cost.warning": "WARN",
"sla.timer.fired": "INFO", "sla.timer.breached": "ERROR",
"review.item.enqueued": "INFO", "review.item.overdue": "WARN",
"api.request.failed": "WARN", "mcp.tool.denied": "WARN",
"ethics.heartbeat": "INFO", "ethics.sla.fired": "WARN", // METADATA ONLY
"brain.anomaly.opened": "WARN", "brain.anomaly.resolved": "INFO",
"brain.incident.opened": "CRITICAL", "brain.reflex.executed": "INFO",
"brain.health.changed": "INFO",
} as const;

export type BrainEventType = keyof typeof EVENT_CATALOG;
```

### FILE: packages/brain/src/bus.ts
```typescript
import Redis from "ioredis";

const STREAM = "brain:events";
const GROUP = "brain-workers";

// Lazy connections: emit() must no-op safely when Redis is absent (seed
// scripts, unit tests) instead of crashing every caller.
let bus: Redis | null = null;
let sub: Redis | null = null;
export const busDisabled = !process.env.REDIS_URL;

function getBus(): Redis {
if (!bus) bus = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!, { maxRetriesPerRequest: 3 });
return bus;
}
function getSub(): Redis {
if (!sub) sub = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!);
return sub;
}

export async function ensureGroup() {
try { await getBus().xgroup("CREATE", STREAM, GROUP, "$", "MKSTREAM"); }
catch (e: any) { if (!String(e.message).includes("BUSYGROUP")) throw e; }
}

export async function publish(evt: Record<string, string>) {
if (busDisabled) return;
const args: (string | number)[] = [STREAM, "MAXLEN", "~", 1_000_000, "*"];
for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(evt)) args.push(k, v);
await getBus().xadd(...(args as [string]));
}

export interface RawEvent { id: string; fields: Record<string, string>; streamId: string }

/** Long-poll consumer. Yields batches; caller ACKs after durable handling.
* At-least-once via XAUTOCLAIM reclaim of stuck messages (>5min idle). */
export async function *consume(consumerName: string): AsyncGenerator<RawEvent[]> {
await ensureGroup();
let autoclaimCursor = "0";
for (;;) {
try {
const claimed = await getBus().xautoclaim(
STREAM, GROUP, consumerName, 300_000, autoclaimCursor, "50") as unknown as
[string, [string, string[]][], string];
autoclaimCursor = claimed?.[2] ?? "0";
if (claimed?.[1]?.length) yield toBatch(claimed[1]);
} catch { /* redis hiccup — fall through to read */ }

let res: unknown = null;
try {
res = await getSub().xreadgroup("GROUP", GROUP, consumerName,
"COUNT", 50, "BLOCK", 5000, "STREAMS", STREAM, ">");
} catch { /* retry next loop */ }
if (res) {
const rows = (res as [string, [string, string[]][]][])[0]?.[1];
if (rows?.length) yield toBatch(rows);
}
}
}

export async function ack(ids: string[]) {
if (busDisabled || ids.length === 0) return;
await getBus().xack(STREAM, GROUP, ...ids);
}

function toBatch(messages: [string, string[]][]): RawEvent[] {
return messages.map(([streamId, flat]) => {
const fields: Record<string, string> = {};
for (let i = 0; i < flat.length; i += 2) fields[flat[i]] = flat[i + 1];
return { id: fields.id, fields, streamId };
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/emit.ts
```typescript
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { trace } from "@opentelemetry/api";
import { publish, busDisabled } from "./bus";
import { EVENT_CATALOG, BrainEventType, Severity } from "./catalog";

export type BrainSource =
| "api" | "mcp" | "workers" | "check-runner" | "connector"
| "agent" | "review" | "ethics" | "sla" | "brain";

export interface EmitInput {
type: BrainEventType;
source: BrainSource;
orgId?: string;
traceId?: string;
flowId?: string;
payload?: Record<string, unknown>;
severityOverride?: "WARN" | "ERROR" | "CRITICAL";
}

let warned = false;
export function emit(input: EmitInput): void {
if (busDisabled) {
if (!warned) { console.warn("[brain] REDIS_URL unset — events dropped"); warned = true; }
return;
}
void safeEmit(input).catch(() => {}); // bus down ≠ app down
}

async function safeEmit(input: EmitInput) {
const id = randomUUID();
const severity = input.severityOverride
?? (EVENT_CATALOG[input.type] as Severity);
const span = trace.getActiveSpan();
if (span) span.addEvent(input.type, { ...input.payload, "brain.severity": severity });

await publish({
id, at: new Date().toISOString(), type: input.type,
source: input.source, orgId: input.orgId ?? "", severity,
traceId: input.traceId ?? span?.spanContext().traceId ?? "",
flowId: input.flowId ?? "", payload: JSON.stringify(input.payload ?? {}),
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/sse.ts
```typescript
/** Org-scoped live-stream registry. The ingest loop pushes each ACKed event
* to that org's SSE writers; /v1/brain/stream registers clients here. */
type WriteFn = (evt: unknown) => void;

const registry = new Map<string, Set<WriteFn>>();

export const sseRegistry = {
add(orgId: string, fn: WriteFn): () => void {
if (!registry.has(orgId)) registry.set(orgId, new Set());
registry.get(orgId)!.add(fn);
return () => { registry.get(orgId)?.delete(fn); };
},
pushToOrg(orgId: string, evt: unknown): void {
for (const fn of registry.get(orgId) ?? []) {
try { fn(evt); } catch { /* dead client — onclose unregisters */ }
}
},
connectionCount(): number {
return [...registry.values()].reduce((s, set) => s + set.size, 0);
},
};
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/correlate.ts
```typescript
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";
import { emit } from "./emit";
import type { RawEvent } from "./bus";

const EXPECTED_DURATION_MS: Record<string, number> = {
CHECK_RUN: 10 * 60_000, EVIDENCE_SYNC: 30 * 60_000, POLICY_GEN: 5 * 60_000,
QUESTIONNAIRE: 60 * 60_000, DSAR: 30 * 86_400_000, REG_SCAN: 60 * 60_000,
WEBHOOK_DELIVERY: 5 * 60_000, REVIEW_SCAN: 10 * 60_000,
};

function payloadOf(e: RawEvent): Record<string, unknown> {
try { return JSON.parse(e.fields.payload || "{}"); } catch { return {}; }
}

export async function correlateEvent(e: RawEvent) {
if (!e.fields.flowId) return;
const type = e.fields.type;
const payload = payloadOf(e);

if (type === "flow.started") {
await brainDb.flowRun.create({ data: {
id: `run-${e.fields.id}`, flowId: e.fields.flowId,
flowType: String(payload.flowType ?? "UNKNOWN"),
traceId: e.fields.traceId || null, orgId: e.fields.orgId || null,
status: "RUNNING" } }).catch(() => {}); // redelivery — idempotent
}
const run = await brainDb.flowRun.findUnique({ where: { flowId: e.fields.flowId } });
if (!run) return;
const ended = type === "flow.completed" ? "SUCCEEDED"
: type === "flow.failed" ? "FAILED" : null;
await brainDb.flowRun.update({ where: { id: run.id }, data: {
eventCount: { increment: 1 }, lastEventAt: new Date(),
...(ended ? { status: ended, endedAt: new Date(),
durationMs: Date.now() - run.startedAt.getTime() } : {}) } });
}

/** Flows running past their expected duration → STUCK + warn. */
export async function reapStuckFlows() {
const running = await brainDb.flowRun.findMany({ where: { status: "RUNNING" } });
for (const r of running) {
const budget = EXPECTED_DURATION_MS[r.flowType] ?? 15 * 60_000;
if (Date.now() - r.startedAt.getTime() > budget) {
await brainDb.flowRun.update({ where: { id: r.id }, data: { status: "STUCK" } });
emit({ type: "flow.stuck", source: "brain", orgId: r.orgId ?? undefined,
flowId: r.flowId, payload: { flowType: r.flowType,
overBudgetMs: Date.now() - r.startedAt.getTime() - budget } });
}
}
}
```
