### FILE: packages/brain/src/health.ts
```typescript
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";

const SUBSYSTEMS = ["api", "mcp", "connectors", "check_runner", "llm",
"temporal", "sla_timers", "review_queue", "ethics_service"] as const;

const SOURCE_OF: Record<(typeof SUBSYSTEMS)[number], string> = {
api: "api", mcp: "mcp", connectors: "connector", check_runner: "check-runner",
llm: "agent", temporal: "workers", sla_timers: "sla", review_queue: "review",
ethics_service: "ethics",
};

/** Sources that should never go quiet for long — silence here is a signal. */
const EXPECTED_ACTIVE = new Set(["connector", "check-runner", "workers", "api"]);

const avg = (xs: number[]) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (xs.length || 1);

export async function computeAndStoreHealth() {
const subsystems: Record<string, number> = {};

// Error-rate scoring over the last hour, per source
const rates = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{ source: string; errs: bigint; total: bigint }>>`
SELECT source,
COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE severity IN ('ERROR','CRITICAL')) AS errs,
COUNT(*) AS total
FROM "BrainEvent" WHERE at > now() - interval '1 hour'
GROUP BY source`;

for (const r of rates) {
const errRate = Number(r.errs) / Math.max(Number(r.total), 1);
const score = Math.max(0, 1 - errRate * 5); // 20% errors → 0
const subsystem = (Object.keys(SOURCE_OF) as (typeof SUBSYSTEMS)[number][])
.find((k) => SOURCE_OF[k] === r.source);
if (subsystem) subsystems[subsystem] = score;
}

// Silence scoring: an EXPECTED_ACTIVE source with zero events in 6h scores low.
for (const s of SUBSYSTEMS) {
if (subsystems[s] === undefined) subsystems[s] = 1;
const src = SOURCE_OF[s];
const seen = await brainDb.brainEvent.findFirst({
where: { source: src }, orderBy: { at: "desc" }, select: { at: true } });
if (!seen) continue; // not wired yet — don't penalize
if (EXPECTED_ACTIVE.has(src) &&
Date.now() - seen.at.getTime() > 6 * 3_600_000)
subsystems[s] = Math.min(subsystems[s], 0.3); // silent subsystem ≠ healthy
}

const openAnomalies = await brainDb.anomaly.count({ where: { status: "OPEN" } });
const values = Object.values(subsystems);
const composite = Math.round(
(Math.min(...values) * 0.6 + avg(values) * 0.4) * 100) / 100; // weakest-leg weighted

await brainDb.healthSnapshot.create({ data: { composite, subsystems, openAnomalies } });
return { composite, subsystems, openAnomalies };
}

export async function latestHealth() {
const snap = await brainDb.healthSnapshot.findFirst({
orderBy: { at: "desc" } });
return snap ?? { composite: 1, subsystems: {}, openAnomalies: 0, at: new Date() };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/anomaly.ts
```typescript
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";

export interface AnomalyDraft {
detector: string; subsystem: string; metric: string; orgId?: string | null;
value: number; baseline: number; zScore?: number;
severity: "WARN" | "CRITICAL"; details?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

const DAY = 86_400_000;

export async function runDetectors(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const out: AnomalyDraft[] = [];
out.push(...await silenceDetector());
out.push(...await errorRateDetector());
out.push(...await costDetector());
out.push(...await freshnessDetector());
out.push(...await stuckFlowDetector());
return dedupeAgainstOpen(out);
}

/** No events from a source for >3× its 7-day median gap. */
async function silenceDetector(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const sources = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{ source: string; last: Date; p50h: number }>>`
WITH gaps AS (
SELECT source, at - lag(at) OVER (PARTITION BY source ORDER BY at) AS gap
FROM "BrainEvent" WHERE at > now() - interval '7 days')
SELECT source, max(gap) AS last,
percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM gap) / 3600) AS p50h
FROM gaps WHERE gap IS NOT NULL GROUP BY source
HAVING percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM gap) / 3600) > 0`;
return sources
.filter((s) => (Date.now() - s.last.getTime()) / 3_600_000 > s.p50h * 3)
.map((s) => ({
detector: "silence", subsystem: s.source, metric: "hours_since_last_event",
value: Math.round(((Date.now() - s.last.getTime()) / 3_600_000) * 10) / 10,
baseline: Math.round(s.p50h * 10) / 10,
severity: "CRITICAL" as const, details: { lastAt: s.last.toISOString() },
}));
}

/** Per-source hourly error share vs its own 7-day baseline (z ≥ 2). */
async function errorRateDetector(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const rows = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{
source: string; hr: Date; errs: bigint; total: bigint }>>`
SELECT source, date_trunc('hour', at) AS hr,
COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE severity IN ('ERROR','CRITICAL')) AS errs, COUNT(*) AS total
FROM "BrainEvent" WHERE at > now() - interval '8 days'
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1, 2`;
const bySource = new Map<string, number[]>();
const current = new Map<string, { errs: number; total: number }>();
const hourAgo = Date.now() - 3_600_000;
for (const r of rows) {
const share = Number(r.errs) / Math.max(Number(r.total), 1);
const src = r.source;
if (r.hr.getTime() >= hourAgo) {
const c = current.get(src) ?? { errs: 0, total: 0 };
c.errs += Number(r.errs); c.total += Number(r.total); current.set(src, c);
} else bySource.set(src, [...(bySource.get(src) ?? []), share]);
}
const out: AnomalyDraft[] = [];
for (const [src, c] of current) {
if (c.total < 10) continue; // thin hour — noise
const baseline = bySource.get(src) ?? [];
if (baseline.length < 24) continue; // need a real baseline
const x = c.errs / c.total;
const μ = baseline.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / baseline.length;
const σ = Math.sqrt(baseline.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - μ) ** 2, 0) / baseline.length) || 1e-9;
const z = (x - μ) / σ;
if (z >= 2 && x > 0.05)
out.push({ detector: "rate", subsystem: src, metric: "hourly_error_share",
value: Math.round(x * 1000) / 1000, baseline: Math.round(μ * 1000) / 1000,
zScore: Math.round(z * 10) / 10, severity: "WARN" });
}
return out;
}

/** LLM $/hour vs 7-day hourly baseline (z ≥ 2) — from the event lake only. */
async function costDetector(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const rows = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{ hr: Date; cost: number | null }>>`
SELECT date_trunc('hour', at) AS hr,
SUM((payload->>'costUsd')::float) AS cost
FROM "BrainEvent" WHERE type = 'llm.generation.completed'
AND at > now() - interval '8 days'
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`;
const hourAgo = Date.now() - 3_600_000;
const baseline = rows.filter((r) => r.hr.getTime() < hourAgo)
.map((r) => r.cost ?? 0);
const current = rows.filter((r) => r.hr.getTime() >= hourAgo)
.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.cost ?? 0), 0);
if (baseline.length < 24 || current <= 0) return [];
const μ = baseline.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / baseline.length;
const σ = Math.sqrt(baseline.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - μ) ** 2, 0) / baseline.length) || 1e-9;
const z = (current - μ) / σ;
if (z < 2) return [];
return [{ detector: "cost", subsystem: "llm", metric: "dollars_per_hour",
value: Math.round(current * 100) / 100, baseline: Math.round(μ * 100) / 100,
zScore: Math.round(z * 10) / 10, severity: "WARN",
details: { hint: "Check agent budgets / runaway generation loops" } }];
}

/** Controls whose last test event is >90d old at ACTIVE orgs — evidence
* going stale, derived purely from the lake. */
async function freshnessDetector(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const rows = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{
orgId: string; controlSlug: string; last: Date; orgActive: Date }>>`
WITH tests AS (
SELECT "orgId", payload->>'controlSlug' AS slug, max(at) AS last
FROM "BrainEvent"
WHERE type IN ('control.test.passed','control.test.failed') AND "orgId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY 1, 2),
orgs AS (
SELECT "orgId", max(at) AS "orgActive" FROM "BrainEvent"
WHERE "orgId" IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1)
SELECT t."orgId", t.slug AS "controlSlug", t.last, o."orgActive"
FROM tests t JOIN orgs o ON o."orgId" = t."orgId"
WHERE t.last < now() - interval '90 days'
AND o."orgActive" > now() - interval '7 days'`;
return rows.map((r) => ({
detector: "freshness", subsystem: "check_runner", orgId: r.orgId,
metric: "days_since_last_test",
value: Math.floor((Date.now() - r.last.getTime()) / DAY),
baseline: 90, severity: "WARN" as const,
details: { controlSlug: r.controlSlug },
}));
}

/** STUCK flow count today vs 7-day daily baseline (z ≥ 2). */
async function stuckFlowDetector(): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const rows = await brainDb.$queryRaw<Array<{ day: Date; n: bigint }>>`
SELECT date_trunc('day', "startedAt") AS day, COUNT(*) AS n
FROM "FlowRun" WHERE status = 'STUCK' AND "startedAt" > now() - interval '8 days'
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`;
const today = rows.find((r) =>
r.day.getTime() >= Date.now() - DAY)?.n ?? 0n;
const baseline = rows.filter((r) => r.day.getTime() < Date.now() - DAY)
.map((r) => Number(r.n));
if (baseline.length < 3) return [];
const μ = baseline.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / baseline.length;
const σ = Math.sqrt(baseline.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - μ) ** 2, 0) / baseline.length) || 1e-9;
const z = (Number(today) - μ) / σ;
if (z < 2 || Number(today) === 0) return [];
return [{ detector: "zscore", subsystem: "temporal", metric: "stuck_flows_today",
value: Number(today), baseline: Math.round(μ * 10) / 10,
zScore: Math.round(z * 10) / 10, severity: "WARN" }];
}

/** Don't re-open what's already OPEN (same detector+subsystem+metric, 24h). */
async function dedupeAgainstOpen(drafts: AnomalyDraft[]): Promise<AnomalyDraft[]> {
const open = await brainDb.anomaly.findMany({
where: { status: "OPEN", at: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - DAY) } },
select: { detector: true, subsystem: true, metric: true } });
const keys = new Set(open.map((a) => `${a.detector}|${a.subsystem}|${a.metric}`));
return drafts.filter((d) => !keys.has(`${d.detector}|${d.subsystem}|${d.metric}`));
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/reflexes.ts
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";
import { emit } from "./emit";
import type { AnomalyDraft } from "./anomaly";
import { diagnoseIncident } from "./agents/diagnostician";

/** Every automated action lands here, immutably. */
async function reflex(rule: string, trigger: string, action: string,
detail?: Record<string, unknown>) {
const result = await runReflex(rule, trigger, action, detail);
await brainDb.reflexLog.create({ data: { rule, trigger, action, result,
detail: (detail ?? {}) as object } });
emit({ type: "brain.reflex.executed", source: "brain",
payload: { rule, action, result } });
}

async function runReflex(_rule: string, _trigger: string, action: string,
_detail?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<string> {
try {
switch (action) {
case "pause_connector": return await pauseStaleConnectors();
case "page": return await page();
default: return "SKIPPED";
}
} catch (e) { return `FAILED: ${String(e).slice(0, 200)}`; }
}

/**
* REFLEX RULES — operational state ONLY. Never compliance data (controls,
* evidence, policies, framework content). Standing rule of the architecture.
*/
export async function processAnomalies(drafts: AnomalyDraft[]) {
const created: string[] = [];
for (const d of drafts) {
const a = await brainDb.anomaly.create({ data: {
detector: d.detector, subsystem: d.subsystem, metric: d.metric,
orgId: d.orgId ?? null, value: d.value, baseline: d.baseline,
zScore: d.zScore, severity: d.severity, details: (d.details ?? {}) as object } });
created.push(a.id);
emit({ type: "brain.anomaly.opened", source: "brain", orgId: d.orgId ?? undefined,
severityOverride: d.severity as "WARN" | "CRITICAL",
payload: { anomalyId: a.id, ...d } });

// Silence on connectors → pause ONLY the stale ones + task a human
if (d.detector === "silence" && d.subsystem === "connector")
await reflex("silence_pause", a.id, "pause_connector", d.details);

if (d.severity === "CRITICAL") {
const incident = await brainDb.brainIncident.create({ data: {
orgId: d.orgId ?? null,
title: `${d.subsystem}: ${d.metric} anomaly (${d.value} vs baseline ${d.baseline})`,
anomalyIds: [a.id] } });
await brainDb.anomaly.update({ where: { id: a.id },
data: { incidentId: incident.id } });
emit({ type: "brain.incident.opened", source: "brain", orgId: d.orgId ?? undefined,
severityOverride: "CRITICAL", payload: { incidentId: incident.id } });
await reflex("critical_page", incident.id, "page",
{ incidentId: incident.id, title: incident.title });
void diagnoseIncident(incident.id).catch(() => {}); // background diagnosis
}
}
return { created };
}

/** Pauses integrations whose last sync is stale >24h (the dead ones —
* not every connector). */
async function pauseStaleConnectors(): Promise<string> {
const r = await prisma.integration.updateMany({
where: { status: "CONNECTED",
lastSyncAt: { lt: new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 3_600_000) } },
data: { status: "ERROR", syncError: "brain reflex: connector silence — auto-paused" } });
emit({ type: "connector.paused", source: "brain",
payload: { paused: r.count, reason: "silence" } });
return `EXECUTED (${r.count} paused)`;
}

/** PagerDuty Events API v2 (or any webhook receiver). */
async function page(): Promise<string> {
const url = process.env.PAGERDUTY_WEBHOOK;
if (!url) { console.warn("[brain:reflex] CRITICAL anomaly — no PAGERDUTY_WEBHOOK configured, logging only"); return "SKIPPED (no pager configured)"; }
const res = await fetch(url, { method: "POST",
headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
body: JSON.stringify({ event_action: "trigger",
payload: { summary: "Compliance platform brain: CRITICAL anomaly",
source: "brain", severity: "critical" } }) });
return res.ok ? "EXECUTED" : `FAILED (${res.status})`;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/webhooks.ts
```typescript
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import type { RawEvent } from "./bus";

/**
* External fan-out (module 10): HMAC-signed, at-least-once. The brain makes
* ONE synchronous attempt per delivery here; the retry/backoff/dead-letter
* loop is the workers' webhookRetryWorkflow (batch 8) operating on
* WebhookDelivery rows. Ethics case content never crosses — metadata events
* only, enforced by the catalog.
*/
export async function fanOutWebhooks(e: RawEvent) {
const type = e.fields.type;
const endpoints = await prisma.webhookEndpoint.findMany({
where: { active: true, events: { has: type } } }).catch(() => []);
if (endpoints.length === 0) return;

for (const ep of endpoints) {
const delivery = await prisma.webhookDelivery.create({
data: { endpointId: ep.id, eventType: type,
payload: { id: e.fields.id, type, at: e.fields.at,
orgId: e.fields.orgId || null,
data: safeParse(e.fields.payload) } } });

const body = JSON.stringify({
id: delivery.id, type, created: e.fields.at,
data: safeParse(e.fields.payload) });
const signature = createHmac("sha256", ep.secret)
.update(`${delivery.id}.${body}`).digest("hex");

let ok = false, error: string | null = null;
try {
const res = await fetch(ep.url, { method: "POST", body,
headers: { "content-type": "application/json",
"X-Compliance-Event": type,
"X-Compliance-Delivery": delivery.id,
"X-Compliance-Signature": `sha256=${signature}` } });
ok = res.ok;
if (!ok) error = `HTTP ${res.status}`;
} catch (err) { error = String(err).slice(0, 200); }

await prisma.webhookDelivery.update({ where: { id: delivery.id },
data: ok ? { status: "DELIVERED", deliveredAt: new Date(), attempts: 1 }
: { status: "PENDING", attempts: 1, lastError: error } });
}
}

function safeParse(s: string): unknown {
try { return JSON.parse(s || "{}"); } catch { return {}; }
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/brain/src/agents/diagnostician.ts
```typescript
import { runAgent } from "@agents";
import { brainDb } from "../brainDb";
import { latestHealth } from "../health";

interface LakeEvent { id: string; at: Date; type: string; source: string;
severity: string; flowId: string | null; payload: unknown }

async function queryEvents(orgId: string | null, hours: number, limit = 200): Promise<LakeEvent[]> {
return brainDb.brainEvent.findMany({
where: { ...(orgId ? { orgId } : {}), at: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - hours * 3_600_000) } },
orderBy: { at: "desc" }, take: limit,
select: { id: true, at: true, type: true, source: true, severity: true,
flowId: true, payload: true } });
}

async function flowTimeline(flowId: string) {
const run = await brainDb.flowRun.findUnique({ where: { flowId } });
const events = await brainDb.brainEvent.findMany({
where: { flowId }, orderBy: { at: "asc" }, take: 100,
select: { id: true, at: true, type: true, severity: true, payload: true } });
return { run, events };
}

/**
* ask_brain — code-driven retrieval + agent synthesis. The agent receives
* pre-fetched lake context (not live tools) so every hypothesis cites event
* IDs that were actually retrieved. orgId=null = platform scope.
*/
export async function askBrain(orgId: string | null, question: string,
opts: { flowId?: string; timeRangeHours?: number } = {}) {
const hours = opts.timeRangeHours ?? 24;
const [events, anomalies, health] = await Promise.all([
queryEvents(orgId, hours),
brainDb.anomaly.findMany({
where: { ...(orgId ? { orgId } : {}), status: "OPEN" },
orderBy: { at: "desc" }, take: 20 }),
latestHealth(),
]);
const timeline = opts.flowId ? await flowTimeline(opts.flowId) : null;

const result = await runAgent<{
answer: string;
root_causes: { hypothesis: string; confidence: number;
supporting_events: string[]; disconfirming_check: string }[];
recommended_actions: string[];
data_gaps: string[];
}>("ops_diagnostician", {
question, scopeHours: hours,
events: events.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, at: e.at.toISOString(), type: e.type,
source: e.source, severity: e.severity, flowId: e.flowId,
payload: e.payload })),
anomalies: anomalies.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, detector: a.detector,
subsystem: a.subsystem, metric: a.metric, value: a.value,
baseline: a.baseline, at: a.at.toISOString() })),
health, flowTimeline: timeline,
}, { orgId: orgId ?? undefined });
return result;
}

/** Background diagnosis for a reflex-opened incident. */
export async function diagnoseIncident(incidentId: string) {
const incident = await brainDb.brainIncident.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: incidentId } });
const anomalies = await brainDb.anomaly.findMany({
where: { id: { in: incident.anomalyIds } } });
const events = await queryEvents(incident.orgId, 24, 300);

const diagnosis = await runAgent<{ answer: string;
root_causes: { hypothesis: string; confidence: number;
supporting_events: string[]; disconfirming_check: string }[];
recommended_actions: string[]; data_gaps: string[] }>("ops_diagnostician", {
question: `Diagnose brain incident: ${incident.title}`,
anomalies: anomalies.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, detector: a.detector,
subsystem: a.subsystem, metric: a.metric, value: a.value,
baseline: a.baseline })),
events: events.slice(0, 150).map((e) => ({ id: e.id, at: e.at.toISOString(),
type: e.type, source: e.source, severity: e.severity })),
health: await latestHealth(),
}, { orgId: incident.orgId ?? undefined });

await brainDb.brainIncident.update({ where: { id: incidentId },
data: { diagnosis: JSON.stringify(diagnosis) } });
return diagnosis;
}
```

```typescript
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";
import { latestHealth } from "./health";
import { sseRegistry } from "./sse";

/** Public status-page payload (no org data — platform aggregates only). */
export async function publicStatus() {
const [health, openAnomalies, openIncidents, dayEvents] = await Promise.all([
latestHealth(),
brainDb.anomaly.count({ where: { status: "OPEN" } }),
brainDb.brainIncident.count({ where: { status: "OPEN" } }),
brainDb.brainEvent.count({ where: { at: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - 86_400_000) } } }),
]);
return {
status: health.composite >= 0.9 ? "ok" : health.composite >= 0.7 ? "degraded" : "critical",
composite: health.composite,
subsystems: health.subsystems,
openAnomalies, openIncidents,
events24h: dayEvents,
liveConnections: sseRegistry.connectionCount(),
at: new Date().toISOString(),
};
}
```

### FILE: packages/brain/src/worker.ts
```typescript
/**
* THE BRAIN — plain Node, deliberately OUTSIDE Temporal (rule #1: the
* observer must survive the observed dying). An external uptime monitor
* watches /healthz — the deadman for the brain itself.
*
* Loops: ingest (stream → lake → correlate → SSE → webhooks) and
* periodic (stuck flows → health → detectors → reflexes).
*/
import Fastify from "fastify";
import { brainDb } from "./brainDb";
import { consume, ack, busDisabled } from "./bus";
import { correlateEvent, reapStuckFlows } from "./correlate";
import { computeAndStoreHealth } from "./health";
import { runDetectors } from "./anomaly";
import { processAnomalies } from "./reflexes";
import { fanOutWebhooks } from "./webhooks";
import { sseRegistry } from "./sse";
import { publicStatus } from "./status";

const CONSUMER = `brain-${process.pid}`;
const ingestState = { lastAt: new Date(), lag: 0, processed: 0 };

async function ingestLoop() {
if (busDisabled) { console.warn("[brain] no REDIS_URL — ingest disabled"); return; }
for await (const batch of consume(CONSUMER)) {
await brainDb.brainEvent.createMany({
data: batch.map((e) => ({
id: e.id, type: e.fields.type, source: e.fields.source,
orgId: e.fields.orgId || null, severity: e.fields.severity as never,
traceId: e.fields.traceId || null, flowId: e.fields.flowId || null,
payload: safeParse(e.fields.payload),
})),
skipDuplicates: true, // at-least-once redelivery is expected
}).catch(() => {});
for (const e of batch) {
await correlateEvent(e);
await fanOutWebhooks(e);
if (e.fields.orgId) sseRegistry.pushToOrg(e.fields.orgId, {
id: e.id, type: e.fields.type, severity: e.fields.severity,
at: e.fields.at, payload: safeParse(e.fields.payload),
});
}
await ack(batch.map((e) => e.streamId));
ingestState.lastAt = new Date();
ingestState.processed += batch.length;
ingestState.lag = Date.now() - ingestState.lastAt.getTime();
}
}

async function periodicLoop() {
for (;;) {
const t0 = Date.now();
try {
await reapStuckFlows();
await computeAndStoreHealth();
const anomalies = await runDetectors();
if (anomalies.length) await processAnomalies(anomalies);
} catch (e) { console.error(`[brain] periodic: ${String(e).slice(0, 300)}`); }
await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, Math.max(60_000 - (Date.now() - t0), 5000)));
}
}

function safeParse(s: string): Record<string, unknown> {
try { return JSON.parse(s || "{}"); } catch { return {}; }
}

// ── heartbeat + public status server ──
const hb = Fastify();
hb.get("/healthz", async () => ({ ok: true, pid: process.pid,
lastIngestAt: ingestState.lastAt.toISOString(),
processed: ingestState.processed, liveSse: sseRegistry.connectionCount() }));
hb.get("/status.json", async () => publicStatus());

const port = Number(process.env.BRAIN_PORT ?? 3010);
hb.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" }).then(() =>
console.log(`[brain] health/status on :${port} (consumer ${CONSUMER})`));

ingestLoop();
periodicLoop();

for (const sig of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"] as const) {
process.on(sig, async () => {
await brainDb.$disconnect().catch(() => {});
process.exit(0);
});
}
```

### FILE: packages/mcp/src/server.ts
```typescript
import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
import { StreamableHTTPServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/streamableHttp.js";
import Fastify from "fastify";
import { CATALOG } from "./tools/catalog";
import { BRAIN_TOOLS } from "./tools/brainTools";
import { toMcpTool, McpError } from "./tools/registry";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

const ALL_TOOLS = [...CATALOG, ...BRAIN_TOOLS];

// API-key resolution — same key system as REST (prefix + argon2id verify)
async function resolveApiKey(key: string | undefined) {
if (!key?.startsWith("cpk_live_")) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const prefix = key.slice(0, 16);
const record = await prisma.apiKey.findUnique({ where: { prefix } });
if (!record?.keyHash || record.revokedAt) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const { verify } = await import("@node-rs/argon2");
if (!(await verify(record.keyHash, key))) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
return record;
}

const server = new McpServer({ name: "compliance-platform", version: "1.0.0" });

for (const def of ALL_TOOLS) {
const tool = toMcpTool(def);
server.tool(tool.name, tool.description, tool.inputSchema as never, tool.annotations,
async (args: unknown, extra: any) => {
const auth = extra?.authInfo as
{ orgId: string; keyId: string; scopes: string[] } | undefined;
if (!auth) throw new McpError(-32001, "Unauthorized");
const result = await tool.handle(args, auth);
return { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) }] };
});
}

const app = Fastify({ logger: false });

app.post("/mcp", async (req, reply) => {
const key = (req.headers["authorization"] ?? "").replace(/^Bearer /, "") ||
(req.headers["x-api-key"] as string | undefined);
const record = await resolveApiKey(key).catch(() => null);
if (!record) return reply.code(401).send({ error: "Invalid or revoked API key" });

const transport = new StreamableHTTPServerTransport({
sessionIdGenerator: undefined, // stateless — keys carry identity
authInfo: { orgId: record.orgId, keyId: record.id,
scopes: record.scopes } as never,
});
await server.connect(transport);
await transport.handleRequest(req.raw, reply.raw);
});

const port = Number(process.env.MCP_PORT ?? 3001);
app.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" }).then(() =>
console.log(`MCP server on :${port} (${ALL_TOOLS.length} tools)`));
```

### FILE: packages/ethics-crypto/src/envelope.ts
```typescript
import { KMSClient, GenerateDataKeyCommand, DecryptCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-kms";
import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes, createHash } from "node:crypto";

export interface Envelope {
v: 1;
alg: "aes-256-gcm";
keyId: string;
iv: string; // b64(12)
ct: string; // b64 ciphertext
tag: string; // b64(16) GCM auth tag
dekCt: string; // b64 KMS-wrapped DEK — THE ONLY recoverable handle.
// Deleting this string makes the ciphertext permanently
// unreadable, even though the KMS key still exists.
ctSha256: string; // hash of ciphertext — retained in tombstone for proof
}

const b64 = (b: Buffer) => b.toString("base64");
const sha256 = (b: Buffer) => createHash("sha256").update(b).digest("hex");

export async function seal(kms: KMSClient, keyId: string, plaintext: string): Promise<Envelope> {
const dk = await kms.send(new GenerateDataKeyCommand({ KeyId: keyId, KeySpec: "AES_256" }));
const iv = randomBytes(12);
const cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", dk.Plaintext!, iv);
const ct = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
const env: Envelope = {
v: 1, alg: "aes-256-gcm", keyId,
iv: b64(iv), ct: b64(ct), tag: b64(cipher.getAuthTag()),
dekCt: b64(Buffer.from(dk.CiphertextBlob!)),
ctSha256: sha256(ct),
};
dk.Plaintext?.fill(0); // best-effort DEK scrub from memory
return env;
}

export async function unseal(kms: KMSClient, env: Envelope): Promise<string> {
const dk = await kms.send(new DecryptCommand({
CiphertextBlob: Buffer.from(env.dekCt, "base64"), KeyId: env.keyId,
}));
const decipher = createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm",
dk.Plaintext!, Buffer.from(env.iv, "base64"));
decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(env.tag, "base64"));
const pt = Buffer.concat([
decipher.update(Buffer.from(env.ct, "base64")), decipher.final(),
]);
dk.Plaintext?.fill(0);
return pt.toString("utf8");
}

/** Integrity check: does the stored ciphertext still match its recorded hash? */
export function ciphertextIntact(env: Envelope): boolean {
return sha256(Buffer.from(env.ct, "base64")) === env.ctSha256;
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/ethics-crypto/src/retention.ts
```typescript
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const addDays = (d: Date, n: number) => new Date(d.getTime() + n * DAY);

export interface RetentionConfig {
statutoryRetentionDays: number; // jurisdiction-dependent; default 7y
backupRetentionDays: number; // = TF var.backup_retention_days
marginDays: number; // default 7
}

export const DEFAULT_RETENTION: RetentionConfig = {
statutoryRetentionDays: 7 * 365,
backupRetentionDays: 35,
marginDays: 7,
};

/**
* Crypto-shred date. BOTH conditions must hold:
* 1. statutory retention elapsed (case closed + retention), AND
* 2. every backup containing the wrapped DEK has aged out — otherwise a
* pre-shred restore resurrects the "shredded" content.
*/
export function computeShredDate(cfg: RetentionConfig, caseClosedAt: Date): Date {
const statutoryOk = addDays(caseClosedAt, cfg.statutoryRetentionDays);
const backupOk = addDays(new Date(), cfg.backupRetentionDays); // DEK may exist in TODAY's backups
const shred = new Date(Math.max(statutoryOk.getTime(), backupOk.getTime()));
return addDays(shred, cfg.marginDays);
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: packages/ethics-crypto/src/shred.ts
```typescript
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { Envelope, ciphertextIntact } from "./envelope";
import { RetentionConfig, DEFAULT_RETENTION, computeShredDate } from "./retention";

/**
* Crypto-shredding: drop the wrapped DEK (dekCt) from stored envelopes so the
* ciphertext becomes permanently unreadable. The ciphertext hash (ctSha256) is
* RETAINED as a tombstone — proof the content existed and what it hashed to,
* without any ability to recover it.
*/

/** One shreddable unit — a sealed JSON envelope string as stored in the DB. */
export interface Shreddable {
id: string;
encryptedContent: string;
}

export interface ShredOutcome {
id: string;
action: "SHREDDED" | "INTEGRITY_FAIL" | "ALREADY_SHREDDED" | "NOT_ENVELOPE";
tombstone?: { ctSha256: string; shreddedAt: string };
newContent?: string; // the replacement to persist (null for non-shredded)
}

export function shredEnvelopeRecord(item: Shreddable): ShredOutcome {
let env: Envelope;
try { env = JSON.parse(item.encryptedContent) as Envelope; }
catch { return { id: item.id, action: "NOT_ENVELOPE" }; }

if (!env.dekCt) {
return { id: item.id, action: "ALREADY_SHREDDED",
tombstone: { ctSha256: env.ctSha256,
shreddedAt: "unknown (pre-existing tombstone)" } };
}

// Integrity gate: never shred content whose ciphertext doesn't match its
// recorded hash — that's tampering, and tampering is an incident, not a shred.
if (!ciphertextIntact(env))
return { id: item.id, action: "INTEGRITY_FAIL",
tombstone: { ctSha256: env.ctSha256, shreddedAt: "BLOCKED" } };

const tombstone = { ctSha256: env.ctSha256, shreddedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
const shredded: Envelope = { ...env, dekCt: "" }; // DEK handle destroyed
return { id: item.id, action: "SHREDDED", tombstone,
newContent: JSON.stringify(shredded) };
}

/** Structural type the ethics service implements over its Prisma client. */
export interface ShredderDb {
/** Closed cases with their sealed content + messages, due for shredding. */
fetchDue(now: Date, cfg: RetentionConfig): Promise<Array<{
caseId: string; closedAt: Date;
reports: Shreddable[]; messages: Shreddable[];
}>>;
/** Persist shredded content / tombstones. */
persistReports(outcomes: ShredOutcome[]): Promise<void>;
persistMessages(outcomes: ShredOutcome[]): Promise<void>;
/** Audit entry for the shred run itself. */
logRun(summary: { cases: number; shredded: number; blocked: number }): Promise<void>;
}

export async function runShredder(
db: ShredderDb, cfg: RetentionConfig = DEFAULT_RETENTION,
): Promise<{ cases: number; shredded: number; blocked: number }> {
const due = await db.fetchDue(new Date(), cfg);
let shredded = 0, blocked = 0;
const reportOutcomes: ShredOutcome[] = [];
const messageOutcomes: ShredOutcome[] = [];

for (const c of due) {
if (computeShredDate(cfg, c.closedAt) > new Date()) continue; // belt+suspenders
for (const r of c.reports) {
const out = shredEnvelopeRecord(r);
if (out.action === "SHREDDED") shredded++;
if (out.action === "INTEGRITY_FAIL") blocked++;
reportOutcomes.push(out);
}
for (const m of c.messages) {
const out = shredEnvelopeRecord(m);
if (out.action === "SHREDDED") shredded++;
if (out.action === "INTEGRITY_FAIL") blocked++;
messageOutcomes.push(out);
}
}

await db.persistReports(reportOutcomes);
await db.persistMessages(messageOutcomes);
await db.logRun({ cases: due.length, shredded, blocked });
return { cases: due.length, shredded, blocked };
}

// CLI entry for the scheduled ECS task / cron:
// tsx packages/ethics-crypto/src/shred.ts --check (dry-run note below)
if (require.main === module) {
console.log("[shredder] DB wiring lives in apps/ethics (isolated service).");
console.log("[shredder] Run via: tsx apps/ethics/src/shred.ts (batch 8).");
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/keyCrypto.ts
```typescript
import { hash, verify } from "@node-rs/argon2";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

export const argon2id = { hash, verify };

/** Creates a cpk_live_ key. Returns the FULL key ONCE + the stored record. */
export async function createApiKey(orgId: string, name: string,
scopes: string[], rateLimitPerMin = 120) {
const secret = randomBytes(24).toString("base64url");
const fullKey = `cpk_live_${secret}`;
const prefix = fullKey.slice(0, 16); // visible half — lookup handle
const record = await prisma.apiKey.create({
data: { orgId, name, prefix, keyHash: await hash(fullKey),
scopes, rateLimitPerMin } });
return { record, fullKey };
}

export async function resolveApiKey(key: string | undefined) {
if (!key?.startsWith("cpk_live_")) return null;
const prefix = key.slice(0, 16);
const record = await prisma.apiKey.findUnique({ where: { prefix } });
if (!record?.keyHash || record.revokedAt) return null;
if (!(await verify(record.keyHash, key))) return null;
return record;
}

export function hasScope(scopes: string[], required: string): boolean {
return scopes.includes("*") || scopes.includes(required);
}

export function problemDetail(status: number, title: string, detail?: string,
extra?: Record<string, unknown>) {
return { type: `https://compliance.example/problems/${status}`,
title, status, detail, ...extra };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/middleware/session.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from "fastify";
import { SignJWT, jwtVerify } from "jose";

const SECRET = new TextEncoder().encode(process.env.SESSION_SECRET ?? "dev-secret-change-me");
const COOKIE = "cmp_session";
const TTL_HOURS = 8;

export interface SessionOrg { id: string; slug: string; name: string }
export interface SessionUser { id: string; name: string; email: string; role: string }

declare module "fastify" {
interface FastifyRequest { user?: SessionUser; org?: SessionOrg }
}

export async function createSession(reply: FastifyReply, user: SessionUser, org: SessionOrg) {
const token = await new SignJWT({ user, org }).setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256" })
.setExpirationTime(`${TTL_HOURS}h`).sign(SECRET);
reply.setCookie(COOKIE, token, {
httpOnly: true, secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
sameSite: "lax", path: "/", maxAge: TTL_HOURS * 3600,
});
}

export async function verifySession(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
const token = req.cookies?.[COOKIE];
if (!token) return reply.code(401).send(problem401());
try {
const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, SECRET);
req.user = payload.user as SessionUser;
req.org = payload.org as SessionOrg;
} catch {
return reply.code(401).send(problem401());
}
}

export const requireRole = (...roles: string[]) =>
async (req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {
if (!req.user || !roles.includes(req.user.role))
return reply.code(403).send({ type: "…/403", title: "Forbidden", status: 403,
detail: `Requires role: ${roles.join(" or ")}` });
};

const problem401 = () => ({ type: "…/401", title: "Not authenticated", status: 401 });
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/middleware/apiKey.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from "fastify";
import { resolveApiKey, hasScope, problemDetail } from "../keyCrypto";
import { rateLimit } from "@mcp"; // in-memory bucket (Redis in prod — same interface)

/** ctx set for downstream handlers */
declare module "fastify" {
interface FastifyRequest { apiKey?: { orgId: string; id: string; scopes: string[] } }
}

export function apiKeyAuth(requiredScope: string) {
return async (req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {
const key = (req.headers["x-api-key"] as string | undefined)
?? (req.headers["authorization"] ?? "").replace(/^Bearer /, "");
const record = await resolveApiKey(key);
if (!record)
return reply.code(401).send(problemDetail(401, "Invalid or revoked API key"));

if (!hasScope(record.scopes, requiredScope)) {
void import("@brain/emit").then(({ emit }) => emit({
type: "mcp.tool.denied", source: "mcp", orgId: record.orgId,
payload: { scope: requiredScope, keyId: record.id } }));
return reply.code(403).send(problemDetail(403, "Missing scope",
`This key lacks the required scope: ${requiredScope}`,
{ "x-required-scope": requiredScope }));
}

if (!(await rateLimit(`key:${record.id}`, record.rateLimitPerMin)))
return reply.code(429).send(problemDetail(429, "Rate limited",
`Limit: ${record.rateLimitPerMin}/min`));

req.apiKey = { orgId: record.orgId, id: record.id, scopes: record.scopes };
void prismaTouch(record.id);
};
}

async function prismaTouch(id: string) {
const { prisma } = await import("@db/client");
await prisma.apiKey.update({ where: { id }, data: { lastUsedAt: new Date() } }).catch(() => {});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/middleware/entitlement.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { MODULE_OF_TOOL } from "@mcp";
import { problemDetail } from "../keyCrypto";

const cache = new Map<string, { ent: { tier: string; modules: Record<string,
{ enabled: boolean; limit?: number }> }; at: number }>();
const TTL = 60_000;

async function getEntitlement(orgId: string) {
const hit = cache.get(orgId);
if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < TTL) return hit.ent;
let ent = await prisma.entitlement.findUnique({ where: { orgId } });
if (!ent) { // default: full trial experience for 14 days
ent = await prisma.entitlement.create({ data: {
orgId, tier: "ENTERPRISE", addons: [], band: "trial",
modules: trialModules(), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 86_400_000) } });
}
const parsed = { tier: ent.tier,
modules: (ent.modules as Record<string, { enabled: boolean; limit?: number }>) ?? {} };
cache.set(orgId, { ent: parsed, at: Date.now() });
return parsed;
}

function trialModules(): Record<string, { enabled: boolean; limit?: number }> {
// 14-day Enterprise trial — everything on (pricing artifact: trial design)
return { "7": { enabled: true, limit: 500 }, "11": { enabled: true, limit: 100 },
"12": { enabled: true, limit: 50 }, "13": { enabled: true }, "14": { enabled: true },
"15": { enabled: true } };
}

const METER_WINDOW = 30 * 86_400_000; // monthly meters
async function meterUsage(orgId: string, meter: string): Promise<number> {
const since = new Date(Date.now() - METER_WINDOW);
switch (meter) {
case "questionnaire_answers": {
const n = await prisma.answerLibrary.count({ where: { orgId, lastUsedAt: { gte: since } } });
return n;
}
case "vendors":
return prisma.vendor.count({ where: { orgId } });
default: return 0;
}
}

/** Runs BEFORE scope checks — tier/meter gating per the pricing matrix. */
export function checkEntitlement(toolName: string) {
return async (req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {
const orgId = req.apiKey?.orgId ?? req.org?.id;
if (!orgId) return; // session path on ungated tools — no apiKey ctx
const mod = MODULE_OF_TOOL[toolName];
if (!mod || mod.module === null) return; // core loop: never gated

const ent = await getEntitlement(orgId);
const rule = ent.modules[String(mod.module)];
if (!rule?.enabled) {
return reply.code(402).send(problemDetail(402,
`${mod.label} requires ${mod.minTier ?? "GROWTH"}`,
`Current tier: ${ent.tier}. Upgrade to unlock this module.`,
{ module: mod.module, label: mod.label, requiredTier: mod.minTier }));
}
if (mod.meter) {
const usage = await meterUsage(orgId, mod.meter);
if (rule.limit !== undefined && usage >= rule.limit) {
return reply.code(402).send(problemDetail(402, "Monthly quota reached",
`${mod.label}: ${usage}/${rule.limit} this period.`,
{ meter: mod.meter, usage, limit: rule.limit }));
}
}
};
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/auditor.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { verify } from "@node-rs/argon2";
import { verifyControlChain } from "@core";

declare module "fastify" {
interface FastifyRequest { auditor?: {
id: string; orgId: string; name: string; firm: string;
scopeFrameworks: string[]; watermarkToken: string } }
}

/** Bearer token → AuditorAccess; enforces expiry + scope + status. */
export async function auditorAuth(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
const token = (req.headers["authorization"] ?? "").replace(/^Bearer /, "");
const hash = (await prisma.auditorAccess.findMany({
where: { status: "ACTIVE", expiresAt: { gt: new Date() } } })).find(
(a) => a.tokenHash && verify(a.tokenHash, token).catch(() => false));
// NOTE: argon verify is async — iterate serially with await
let access = null;
for (const a of await prisma.auditorAccess.findMany({
where: { status: "ACTIVE", expiresAt: { gt: new Date() } }, take: 500 })) {
if (await verify(a.tokenHash, token).catch(() => false)) { access = a; break; }
}
if (!hash || !access) return reply.code(401).send({ title: "Invalid auditor token", status: 401 });

req.auditor = {
id: access.id, orgId: access.orgId, name: access.name, firm: access.firm,
scopeFrameworks: access.scopeFrameworks,
watermarkToken: `wm_${access.id.slice(-8)}`,
};
return;
}

async function loadEvidenceInScope(auditor: NonNullable<FastifyRequest["auditor"]>,
evidenceId: string) {
const e = await prisma.evidence.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: evidenceId },
include: { control: true, testRun: true, quality: true } });
if (e.orgId !== auditor.orgId) throw { statusCode: 404 };
// scope check: evidence control must map to a scoped framework
const inScope = await prisma.controlMapping.findFirst({
where: { controlId: e.controlId, status: "ACCEPTED",
requirement: { framework: { code: { in: auditor.scopeFrameworks } } } } });
if (!inScope) throw { statusCode: 403, message: "Evidence outside audit scope" };
return e;
}

/** Watermark burned into every rendered view. */
function issueWatermark(auditor: NonNullable<FastifyRequest["auditor"]>): string {
return `${auditor.name} · ${auditor.firm} · ${auditor.watermarkToken} · ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`;
}

async function logEvidenceView(evidenceId: string, auditor: NonNullable<FastifyRequest["auditor"]>) {
await prisma.evidenceView.create({ data: {
evidenceId, viewerKind: "AUDITOR_ACCESS", viewerId: auditor.id,
watermarkToken: auditor.watermarkToken } });
}

export async function auditorRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
app.get("/v1/auditor/frameworks/:code/coverage", { preHandler: auditorAuth },
async (req) => {
const { code } = req.params as { code: string };
if (!req.auditor!.scopeFrameworks.includes(code))
return { error: "Framework outside audit scope" };
const requirements = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: { framework: { code } },
include: { controlMappings: {
where: { status: "ACCEPTED", control: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId } },
include: { control: { include: {
evidences: { orderBy: { sequence: "desc" }, take: 1, include: { quality: true } } } } } } },
orderBy: { code: "asc" } });
return {
framework: code,
requirements: requirements.map((r) => {
const implemented = r.controlMappings.filter(
(m) => m.control.implementationStatus === "IMPLEMENTED");
const latest = implemented[0]?.control.evidences[0];
const quality = latest?.quality;
const satisfied = implemented.length > 0 && latest &&
(!latest.expiresAt || latest.expiresAt > new Date()) &&
(!quality || quality.score >= 70);
return { requirement: r.code, title: r.title,
status: satisfied ? "SATISFIED" : implemented.length ? "PARTIAL" : "GAP",
evidenceId: latest?.id ?? null,
quality: quality ? { score: quality.score, grade: quality.grade } : null };
}),
};
});

app.get("/v1/auditor/evidence/:id", { preHandler: auditorAuth },
async (req, reply) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
try {
const e = await loadEvidenceInScope(req.auditor!, id);
await logEvidenceView(id, req.auditor!);
const chain = await verifyControlChain(e.controlId);
return reply.type("text/html").send(
renderEvidence(e, chain, issueWatermark(req.auditor!)));
} catch (err: any) {
return reply.code(err.statusCode ?? 404).send({ title: "Not found", status: 404 });
}
});

app.get("/v1/auditor/controls/:slug/chain-proof", { preHandler: auditorAuth },
async (req) => {
const { slug } = req.params as { slug: string };
const c = await prisma.control.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { orgId_slug: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId, slug } } });
return verifyControlChain(c.id);
});

// ── auditor request/response loop ──
app.get("/v1/auditor/requests", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) =>
prisma.auditRequest.findMany({
where: { auditCycle: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId } },
include: { evidenceLinks: { include: { evidence: {
select: { id: true, title: true, collectedAt: true } } } } },
orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" } }));

app.post("/v1/auditor/requests", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
const { title, description, auditCycleId } = req.body as {
title: string; description?: string; auditCycleId: string };
return prisma.auditRequest.create({ data: {
auditCycleId, title, description } });
});
}

function renderEvidence(e: any, chain: any, watermark: string): string {
const findings = e.testRun?.findings as { items?: unknown[] } | null;
return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<title>Evidence ${e.id}</title>
<style>
body { font: 13px/1.6 Inter, system-ui, sans-serif; color: #18181b; margin: 2rem; }
.wm { position: fixed; inset: 0; pointer-events: none;
background: repeating-linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 0 220px,
rgba(0,0,0,0.06) 220px 230px); z-index: 9; }
.wm::after { content: "${watermark}"; position: fixed; bottom: 12px; right: 16px;
font: 10px monospace; color: rgba(0,0,0,0.45); }
h1 { font-size: 16px; } .meta { color: #71717a; font-size: 12px; }
.hash { font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px; background: #f4f4f5;
padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; }
.chain { border: 1px solid #e4e4e7; border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px; margin-top: 12px; }
.findings { margin-top: 12px; border: 1px solid #e4e4e7; border-radius: 8px;
padding: 12px; white-space: pre-wrap; font-size: 12px; max-height: 400px; overflow: auto; }
</style></head><body>
<div class="wm"></div>
<h1>${e.title}</h1>
<p class="meta">Collected ${e.collectedAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} ·
${e.sourceType} · control ${e.control?.slug ?? ""}</p>
<p><span class="hash">content ${e.contentHash.slice(0, 12)}…</span>
<span class="hash">chain ${e.chainHash.slice(0, 12)}…</span>
${e.quality ? `<span class="hash">quality ${e.quality.score}/100 (${e.quality.grade})</span>` : ""}</p>
<div class="chain">Chain verification: <strong>${chain.valid ? "VALID" : `BROKEN at #${chain.brokenAtSequence}`}</strong>
— ${chain.links ?? 0} links · head <span class="hash">${chain.head?.slice(0, 16)}…</span></div>
<div class="findings">${findings?.items
? JSON.stringify(findings.items.slice(0, 25), null, 2)
: (e.testRun?.summary ?? "No findings recorded.")}</div>
<p class="meta">Every view of this page is logged with the watermark token above.
Independent verification: recompute sha256(prevChainHash ‖ contentHash) per link.</p>
</body></html>`;
}

export { auditorAuth, loadEvidenceInScope, renderEvidence, issueWatermark, logEvidenceView };
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/auditorGdpr.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { verifyControlChain } from "@core";
import { auditorAuth, loadEvidenceInScope, logEvidenceView,
issueWatermark, renderEvidence } from "./auditor";

const K = 5; // k-anonymity floor for DSAR aggregates

export async function auditorGdprRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {

// 1. GDPR article overview — satisfaction + trend anchor
app.get("/v1/auditor/frameworks/GDPR/workflows", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
if (!req.auditor!.scopeFrameworks.includes("GDPR"))
return { error: "GDPR outside audit scope" };
const rows = await prisma.frameworkRequirement.findMany({
where: { framework: { code: "GDPR" }, kind: "WORKFLOW" },
orderBy: { code: "asc" },
include: { workflowSatisfactions: { where: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId } } } });
return {
framework: "GDPR",
programAnchor: "privacy.program-operations",
articles: rows.map((r) => {
const ws = r.workflowSatisfactions[0];
return { article: r.code, title: r.title,
status: ws?.status ?? "NOT_ROUTED",
since: ws?.lastEvaluatedAt, evidenceId: ws?.evidenceId };
}),
summary: {
satisfied: rows.filter((r) => r.workflowSatisfactions[0]?.status === "SATISFIED"
|| r.workflowSatisfactions[0]?.status === "NOT_APPLICABLE").length,
total: rows.length },
};
});

// 2. Per-article bundle
app.get("/v1/auditor/workflows/:articleCode", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
const { articleCode } = req.params as { articleCode: string };
const ws = await prisma.workflowSatisfaction.findFirst({
where: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId,
requirement: { code: articleCode, framework: { code: "GDPR" } } },
include: { requirement: true } });
if (!ws) return { error: "Article not routed for this org" };
const evidence = ws.evidenceId
? await prisma.evidence.findUnique({ where: { id: ws.evidenceId } }) : null;
return {
article: ws.requirement.code, title: ws.requirement.title,
obligation: ws.requirement.description,
status: ws.status, notApplicableRationale: ws.naRationale,
metrics: (ws.metrics as { telemetry?: unknown })?.telemetry ?? ws.metrics,
evidence: evidence ? {
id: evidence.id, sequence: evidence.sequence,
collectedAt: evidence.collectedAt,
contentHash: evidence.contentHash.slice(0, 16),
chainHash: evidence.chainHash.slice(0, 16) } : null,
};
});

// 3. Telemetry evidence render (watermarked + logged, same as controls)
app.get("/v1/auditor/workflows/evidence/:evidenceId", { preHandler: auditorAuth },
async (req, reply) => {
const { evidenceId } = req.params as { evidenceId: string };
try {
const e = await loadEvidenceInScope(req.auditor!, evidenceId);
await logEvidenceView(evidenceId, req.auditor!);
const chain = await verifyControlChain(e.controlId);
return reply.type("text/html").send(
renderEvidence(e, chain, issueWatermark(req.auditor!)));
} catch (err: any) {
return reply.code(err.statusCode ?? 404).send({ title: "Not found", status: 404 });
}
});

// 4. Independent chain proof on the privacy anchor
app.get("/v1/auditor/controls/privacy-program-operations/chain-proof",
{ preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
const c = await prisma.control.findUnique({
where: { orgId_slug: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId,
slug: "privacy.program-operations" } } });
if (!c) return { error: "Privacy program control not instantiated" };
return verifyControlChain(c.id);
});

// 5. Drill history — quarterly synthetic DSAR exercises
app.get("/v1/auditor/privacy/drills", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) =>
prisma.dsarDrill.findMany({ where: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId },
orderBy: { executedAt: "desc" }, take: 12 }));

// 6. Published RoPA versions
app.get("/v1/auditor/ropa", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
const docs = await prisma.ropaDocument.findMany({
where: { orgId: req.auditor!.orgId, status: "PUBLISHED" },
orderBy: { version: "desc" }, take: 3 });
return { current: docs[0] ?? null,
history: docs.map((d) => ({ version: d.version, publishedAt: d.publishedAt })) };
});

// 7. DSAR aggregates — k-anonymized (an auditor needs the on-time RATE,
// not the ability to reconstruct individual requests)
app.get("/v1/auditor/dsar-metrics", { preHandler: auditorAuth }, async (req) => {
const monthly = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ month: Date; type: string;
n: bigint; onTime: bigint }>>`
SELECT date_trunc('month', "createdAt") AS month, type,
COUNT(*) AS n,
COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'DELIVERED'
AND "updatedAt" <= COALESCE("extensionTo", "dueAt")) AS "onTime"
FROM "PrivacyRequest" WHERE "orgId" = ${req.auditor!.orgId}
AND "createdAt" > now() - interval '18 months'
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1`;
return monthly.map((r) => {
const n = Number(r.n);
return {
month: r.month.toISOString().slice(0, 7), type: r.type,
count: n >= K ? n : null,
onTimeRate: n >= K ? Number(r.onTime) / n : null,
suppressedLowVolume: n < K,
};
});
});
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/trust.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { saltedHash } from "@core";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";

const FREE_EMAIL_DOMAINS = new Set(["gmail.com", "yahoo.com", "outlook.com",
"hotmail.com", "icloud.com", "aol.com", "protonmail.com", "mail.com"]);
const DISPOSABLE_DOMAINS = new Set(["mailinator.com", "tempmail.com",
"guerrillamail.com", "10minutemail.com", "yopmail.com", "throwawaymail.com"]);

export function evaluateNdaRequest(orgId: string, r: { domain: string; email: string; company: string }) {
const domain = r.domain.toLowerCase();
if (DISPOSABLE_DOMAINS.has(domain))
return { status: "BLOCKED", reason: "Disposable email domain" };
// orgSettings.competitorBlocklist — config per org; empty default
if (FREE_EMAIL_DOMAINS.has(domain))
return { status: "REVIEW", reason: "Free email provider" };
if (r.company.trim().length > 1)
return { status: "AUTO_APPROVED", reason: "Business domain" };
return { status: "REVIEW", reason: "Company name required" };
}

function renderNdaForSignature(grant: { name: string; company: string; email: string },
template: { contentMd: string; version: number },
trustSlug: string): string {
const merged = template.contentMd
.replaceAll("{{counterparty}}", grant.company)
.replaceAll("{{signer}}", grant.name)
.replaceAll("{{date}}", new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<title>Mutual NDA — ${grant.company}</title>
<style> body { font: 14px/1.7 Georgia, serif; max-width: 680px; margin: 2rem auto; padding: 0 1rem; }
.sign { margin-top: 2rem; border-top: 1px solid #ccc; padding-top: 1rem; }
input { font: 16px Georgia, serif; padding: 8px; width: 320px; }
button { font: 14px system-ui; padding: 10px 24px; } </style></head>
<body>${merged.replace(/\n/g, "<br>")}
<div class="sign"><p>Type your full legal name to sign:</p>
<form method="post" action="/v1/trust/${trustSlug}/grants/${(grant as any).id}/sign">
<input name="signature" placeholder="Full legal name" required>
<button type="submit">I agree · Sign &amp; get documents</button></form></div>
</body></html>`;
}

/** STUB-MARKED: production presigns S3 Object-Locked docs via
* getSignedUrl(S3GetObjectCommand, { expiresIn: 900 }). This returns a
* logged, TTL-bound internal URL against the evidence/doc route. */
async function presignedDocUrls(grant: { id: string; trustCenterId: string },
ttlMinutes = 15) {
const docs = await prisma.trustDocument.findMany({
where: { trustCenterId: grant.trustCenterId, isPublic: false } });
const exp = Date.now() + ttlMinutes * 60_000;
return docs.map((d) => ({
name: d.name,
url: `/v1/trust/docs/${d.id}?grant=${grant.id}&exp=${exp}`,
expiresInSeconds: ttlMinutes * 60 }));
}

export async function trustRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {

app.get("/v1/trust/:slug", async (req) => {
const tc = await prisma.trustCenter.findFirst({
where: { slug: req.params as { slug: string }.slug, published: true },
include: { documents: true } });
if (!tc) return { error: "Not found" };
return { slug: tc.slug, sections: tc.sections,
documents: tc.documents.map((d) => ({ name: d.name,
public: d.isPublic, id: d.id })) };
});

app.post("/v1/trust/:slug/access", async (req, reply) => {
const { slug } = req.params as { slug: string };
const { name, email, company } = req.body as {
name: string; email: string; company: string };
const tc = await prisma.trustCenter.findFirst({
where: { slug, published: true } });
if (!tc) return reply.code(404).send({ title: "Not found", status: 404 });

const domain = email.split("@")[1]?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
const decision = evaluateNdaRequest(tc.orgId, { domain, email, company });

const grant = await prisma.trustAccessGrant.create({ data: {
trustCenterId: tc.id, name, email, company, domain,
status: decision.status, reason: decision.reason } });

if (decision.status === "AUTO_APPROVED")
return reply.type("text/html").send(await ndaFlow(grant, tc.slug));
if (decision.status === "REVIEW")
return { status: "REVIEW", message:
"Your request is being reviewed — typically minutes, not days." };
return reply.code(403).send({ title: "Request blocked", status: 403 });
});

app.post("/v1/trust/:slug/grants/:grantId/sign", async (req, reply) => {
const { slug, grantId } = req.params as { slug: string; grantId: string };
const { signature } = req.body as { signature?: string };
const grant = await prisma.trustAccessGrant.findUnique({
where: { id: grantId } });
if (!grant || grant.status !== "AUTO_APPROVED")
return reply.code(404).send({ title: "Grant not signable", status: 404 });
if (!signature || signature.trim().length < 3)
return reply.code(400).send({ title: "Signature required", status: 400 });

await prisma.trustAccessGrant.update({ where: { id: grant.id }, data: {
signature, signatureIp: saltedHash(req.ip ?? "unknown"), // minimization
signedAt: new Date(), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 86_400_000) } });

emit({ type: "trust.nda.signed", source: "api", orgId: undefined,
payload: { trustCenterId: grant.trustCenterId } });
return { documents: await presignedDocUrls(grant) };
});

// gated doc access — TTL checked + view logged (STUB presign above)
app.get("/v1/trust/docs/:docId", async (req, reply) => {
const { docId } = req.params as { docId: string };
const { grant: grantId, exp } = req.query as { grant?: string; exp?: string };
if (!grantId || !exp || Number(exp) < Date.now())
return reply.code(403).send({ title: "Link expired", status: 403 });
const grant = await prisma.trustAccessGrant.findUnique({ where: { id: grantId } });
if (!grant?.signedAt) return reply.code(403).send({ title: "Not signed", status: 403 });
const doc = await prisma.trustDocument.findUnique({ where: { id: docId } });
if (!doc || doc.trustCenterId !== grant.trustCenterId)
return reply.code(404).send({ title: "Not found", status: 404 });
return { name: doc.name, storageKey: doc.storageKey, note:
"Production: streamed from S3 via presigned URL; view logged." };
});

async function ndaFlow(grant: { id: string; name: string; company: string; email: string },
trustSlug: string): Promise<string> {
const tc = await prisma.trustCenter.findFirst({ where: { slug: trustSlug } });
const template = await prisma.ndaTemplate.findFirst({
where: { orgId: tc!.orgId, active: true } }) ?? {
contentMd: `# Mutual Non-Disclosure Agreement\n\nThis Mutual NDA is entered into by {{counterparty}} and the disclosing party on {{date}}.\n\nBoth parties agree to keep confidential information confidential for three (3) years from disclosure. Signed: {{signer}}.`,
version: 1 };
return renderNdaForSignature(grant, template, trustSlug);
}
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/review.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { verifySession, requireRole } from "../middleware/session";
import { SOURCES, claim, decide, releaseClaim, scanAllQueues } from "@review";

export async function reviewRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {

app.get("/v1/review/queue", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async () => prisma.reviewItem.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["PENDING", "CLAIMED", "APPROVED_PENDING_SECOND"] },
OR: [{ deferredUntil: null }, { deferredUntil: { lt: new Date() } }] },
orderBy: [{ priority: "desc" }, { slaDueAt: "asc" }] }));

app.post("/v1/review/scan", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async () => scanAllQueues());

app.get("/v1/review/:id", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async (req, reply) => {
const item = await prisma.reviewItem.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: req.params as string } });
const source = SOURCES[item.queueType];
if (!source) return reply.code(404).send({ title: "Unknown queue", status: 404 });
const bundle = await source.render(item.sourceId);
return { item, bundle };
});

app.post("/v1/review/:id/claim", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async (req, reply) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const membership = await prisma.membership.findFirst({
where: { userId: req.user!.id,
org: { slug: "platform" } } });
if (!membership) return reply.code(403).send({ title: "Not a platform reviewer", status: 403 });
try { return await claim(id, membership.id); }
catch (e: any) { return reply.code(409).send({ title: e.message, status: 409 }); }
});

app.post("/v1/review/:id/decide", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async (req, reply) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const { decision, note, deferUntil } = req.body as {
decision: "APPROVE" | "REJECT" | "DEFER"; note: string; deferUntil?: string };
const membership = await prisma.membership.findFirst({
where: { userId: req.user!.id, org: { slug: "platform" } } });
if (!membership) return reply.code(403).send({ title: "Not a platform reviewer", status: 403 });
try {
return await decide(id, membership.id, decision, note ?? "",
deferUntil ? new Date(deferUntil) : undefined);
} catch (e: any) {
return reply.code(e.message?.includes("credential") ? 403 : 409)
.send({ title: e.message, status: 409 });
}
});

app.post("/v1/review/:id/release", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async (req) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const membership = await prisma.membership.findFirst({
where: { userId: req.user!.id, org: { slug: "platform" } } });
return releaseClaim(id, membership!.id);
});

app.get("/v1/review/stats", { preHandler: [verifySession, requireRole("ADMIN")] },
async () => {
const [byStatus, breached] = await Promise.all([
prisma.reviewItem.groupBy({ by: ["status"], _count: true }),
prisma.reviewItem.count({ where: { status: { in: ["PENDING", "CLAIMED"] },
slaDueAt: { lt: new Date() } } })]);
return { byStatus, slaBreaches: breached };
});
}
```

```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { verifySession } from "../middleware/session";

export async function brainRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {

const { latestHealth } = await import("@brain");
return latestHealth();
});

app.get("/v1/brain/health/history", { preHandler: verifySession }, async () =>
brainDb.healthSnapshot.findMany({ orderBy: { at: "desc" }, take: 288 })); // 24h @5min

app.get("/v1/brain/anomalies", { preHandler: verifySession }, async (req) => {
const { status } = req.query as { status?: string };
return brainDb.anomaly.findMany({
where: { status: (status ?? "OPEN") as never,
OR: [{ orgId: req.org!.id }, { orgId: null }] },
orderBy: { at: "desc" }, take: 50 });
});

app.get("/v1/brain/flows/:flowId", { preHandler: verifySession }, async (req) => {
const { flowId } = req.params as { flowId: string };
const run = await brainDb.flowRun.findUnique({ where: { flowId } });
const events = await brainDb.brainEvent.findMany({
where: { flowId, OR: [{ orgId: req.org!.id }, { orgId: null }] },
orderBy: { at: "asc" }, take: 100 });
return { run, events };
});

app.get("/v1/brain/incidents", { preHandler: verifySession }, async (req) =>
brainDb.brainIncident.findMany({
where: { OR: [{ orgId: req.org!.id }, { orgId: null }] },
orderBy: { startedAt: "desc" }, take: 20 }));

app.post("/v1/brain/ask", { preHandler: verifySession }, async (req) => {
const { question, flowId, timeRangeHours } = req.body as {
question: string; flowId?: string; timeRangeHours?: number };
return askBrain(req.org!.id, question, { flowId, timeRangeHours });
});

// public status page — NO auth (deadman + status.json consumers)
app.get("/v1/brain/status.json", async () => publicStatus());
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/brainStream.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { verifySession } from "../middleware/session";
import { sseRegistry } from "@brain";

/** Org-scoped live event feed — the dashboard + control page consume this. */
export async function brainStreamRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
app.get("/v1/brain/stream", { preHandler: verifySession }, (req, reply) => {
reply.raw.writeHead(200, {
"content-type": "text/event-stream",
"cache-control": "no-cache, no-transform",
connection: "keep-alive",
"x-accel-buffering": "no", // disable nginx/caddy buffering
});
const unregister = sseRegistry.add(req.org!.id, (evt) => {
reply.raw.write(`event: message\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(evt)}\n\n`);
});
const ping = setInterval(() => reply.raw.write(": ping\n\n"), 25_000);
req.raw.on("close", () => { clearInterval(ping); unregister(); });
});
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/catalogRest.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { CATALOG } from "@mcp";
import { BRAIN_TOOLS } from "@mcp/tools/brainTools";
import { apiKeyAuth } from "../middleware/apiKey";
import { checkEntitlement } from "../middleware/entitlement";

const ALL_TOOLS = [...CATALOG, ...BRAIN_TOOLS];

/** One catalog, two surfaces: REST mounts the same ToolDefs the MCP server
* serves — single handler, single scope declaration, zero drift. */
export async function mountCatalogRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
for (const tool of ALL_TOOLS) {
if (!tool.rest) continue;
const { method, path } = tool.rest;
const fastifyPath = path.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, ":$1");

app.route({
method, url: fastifyPath,
preHandler: [checkEntitlement(tool.name), apiKeyAuth(tool.scope)],
handler: async (req, reply) => {
const args = { ...req.params, ...req.query, ...(req.body ?? {}) };
try {
const result = await tool.handler(args, {
orgId: req.apiKey!.orgId, keyId: req.apiKey!.id });
reply.code(method === "POST" ? 201 : 200).send(result ?? { ok: true });
} catch (e: any) {
reply.code(e.statusCode ?? 500).send({
title: e.message?.slice(0, 200) ?? "Handler error", status: 500 });
}
},
});
}
// Spec endpoint — always current, regenerated at boot
app.get("/v1/openapi.json", async () => {
const { generateOpenApi } = await import("../openapi/generate");
return generateOpenApi();
});
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/ethics-events.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

/**
* The ethics service is network-isolated; it POSTS metadata-only events here
* (HMAC-authenticated, no case content ever crosses the boundary). The API
* translates them into Temporal SLA-workflow starts (the workers client
* lives here, not in the ethics service).
*/
export async function ethicsEventsRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
app.post("/internal/ethics-events", async (req, reply) => {
const secret = process.env.ETHICS_EVENT_HMAC_SECRET;
if (!secret) return reply.code(503).send({ error: "bridge not configured" });

const sig = req.headers["x-ethics-signature"] as string | undefined;
const raw = JSON.stringify(req.body ?? {});
const expected = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(raw).digest("hex");
if (!sig || !timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(expected),
Buffer.from(sig.replace(/^sha256=/, ""))))
return reply.code(401).send({ error: "bad signature" });

const { event, orgId, caseId, at } = req.body as {
event: string; orgId: string; caseId: string; at: string };

const { emit } = await import("@brain/emit");
emit({
type: event === "CASE_CREATED" ? "ethics.sla.fired" : "ethics.heartbeat",
source: "ethics", orgId,
payload: { caseId, event }, // METADATA ONLY — never content
});

// Temporal SLA starts/stops are wired in workers (batch 8): the ethics
// SLA kinds exist there; this bridge just fans the events out.
return { ok: true };
});
}
```

### FILE: apps/api/src/handlers/controls.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma, rlsClient } from "@db/client";
import { controlTestState, controlQualityScore } from "@core";
import { runCheckInstrumented } from "@skills";
import { computeNotifications } from "@core";

export async function controlHandlers(app: FastifyInstance) {

// ── the ControlDetail shape apps/web types.ts declares ──
app.get("/v1/controls/:slug", async (req) => {
const { slug } = req.params as { slug: string };
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const control = await tx.control.findUnique({
where: { orgId_slug: { orgId: req.org!.id, slug } },
include: {
owner: { include: { user: true } },
controlMappings: {
where: { status: "ACCEPTED" },
include: { requirement: { include: { framework: true } } },
},
tests: { orderBy: { lastRunAt: "desc" } },
evidences: { orderBy: { sequence: "desc" }, take: 50,
include: { quality: true, testRun: true } },
},
});
if (!control) throw { statusCode: 404, message: "Control not found" };

const runs = await tx.testRun.findMany({
where: { controlId: control.id },
orderBy: { startedAt: "desc" }, take: 30,
});
const quality = await controlQualityScore(control.id);
const grade = quality >= 90 ? "A" : quality >= 75 ? "B"
: quality >= 60 ? "C" : quality >= 40 ? "D" : "F";

return {
id: control.id, slug: control.slug, name: control.name,
description: control.description,
implementationStatus: control.implementationStatus,
owner: control.owner ? { name: control.owner.user.name } : null,
quality: control.evidences.length ? { score: quality, grade } : null,
testState: controlTestState(runs, 90),
requirements: control.controlMappings.map((m) => ({
framework: m.requirement.framework.code, code: m.requirement.code,
title: m.requirement.title,
confidence: Number(m.rationale?.match(/confidence ([\d.]+)/)?.[1] ?? 0.9),
verified: m.status === "ACCEPTED",
})),
evidences: control.evidences.map((e) => ({
id: e.id, sequence: e.sequence, collectedAt: e.collectedAt.toISOString(),
title: e.title,
status: (e.testRun?.status ?? "INCONCLUSIVE") as
"PASS" | "FAIL" | "ERROR" | "INCONCLUSIVE",
contentHash: e.contentHash, chainHash: e.chainHash,
testRun: e.testRun ? { id: e.testRun.id, summary: e.testRun.summary,
findings: e.testRun.findings } : null,
})),
tests: control.tests.map((t) => ({
id: t.id, checkId: (t.check as { checkId?: string }).checkId ?? t.id,
name: t.name, schedule: t.schedule, enabled: t.enabled,
lastRunAt: t.lastRunAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
})),
history: runs.slice(0, 20).map((r) => ({
at: r.startedAt.toISOString(), label: `Test ${r.status}`,
detail: r.summary ?? undefined,
})),
};
});
});

// ── dashboard aggregates ──
app.get("/v1/quality/distribution", async (req) =>
rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const rows = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ grade: string; n: bigint }>>`
SELECT eq.grade AS grade, COUNT(*) AS n
FROM "EvidenceQuality" eq
JOIN "Evidence" e ON e.id = eq."evidenceId"
WHERE e."orgId" = ${req.org!.id}
GROUP BY 1`;
return Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.grade, Number(r.n)]));
}));

app.get("/v1/tasks", async (req) => {
const url = new URL(req.url, "http://x");
if (url.searchParams.get("summary") !== "1") {
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => tx.task.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "BLOCKED"] } },
orderBy: [{ priority: "desc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }], take: 100 }));
}
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const tasks = await tx.task.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "BLOCKED"] } },
select: { priority: true, dueAt: true } });
const byPriority: Record<string, number> = {};
for (const t of tasks) byPriority[t.priority] = (byPriority[t.priority] ?? 0) + 1;
return { open: tasks.length,
overdue: tasks.filter((t) => t.dueAt && t.dueAt < new Date()).length,
byPriority };
});
});

// ── run-check-now for the UI (returns the brain flowId for live tracking) ──
app.post("/v1/checks/:checkId/run", async (req) => {
const { checkId } = req.params as { checkId: string };
const test = await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({
where: { orgId: req.org!.id, id: { startsWith: `${req.org!.id}:${checkId}` } } })
?? await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({
where: { orgId: req.org!.id, id: checkId } });
if (!test) throw { statusCode: 404, message: "Check not found" };
const result = await runCheckInstrumented({ orgId: req.org!.id, testId: test.id });
return { runId: result.run.id, status: result.run.status,
evidenceId: result.evidence?.id ?? null };
});

// ── incident facts → obligations (UI live-recompute) ──
app.post("/v1/incidents/:id/obligations", async (req) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const inc = await prisma.incident.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { id, orgId: req.org!.id } });
const facts = {
detectedAt: inc.detectedAt,
dataCategories: inc.affectedDataCategories as string[],
subjectCountByJurisdiction: (inc.affectedSystems as Record<string, number>) ?? {},
encryptedAtRest: true, encryptedInTransit: true,
isPublicCompany: inc.isPublicCompany, materialityDetermined: true,
};
return computeNotifications(facts);
});
}
```
