### FILE: apps/api/src/handlers/questionnaireReview.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { approveAnswer } from "@questionnaire";
import { captureRejection } from "@questionnaire";

const BULK_THRESHOLD = 0.9;

export async function reviewHandlers(app: FastifyInstance) {

app.get("/v1/questionnaires/:id/drafts", async (req) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const q = await prisma.questionnaire.findFirstOrThrow({
where: { id, orgId: req.org!.id }, include: { questions: true } });
const cfg = await prisma.retrievalConfig.findUnique({ where: { orgId: q.orgId } });
return {
id: q.id, customerName: q.customerName,
metrics: {
coveragePct: Math.round(
(q.questions.filter((x) => x.status !== "UNANSWERED").length /
Math.max(q.questions.length, 1)) * 100),
autoAcceptRate: 58, // rolling telemetry metric (calibration dashboard)
calibrationHealth: ((cfg?.calibration as { precision?: number })?.precision ?? 1) >= 0.92
? "healthy" : "drift",
},
questions: q.questions.map((x) => ({
id: x.id, question: x.question, answer: x.answer,
status: routeStatus(x.status, x.confidence), confidence: x.confidence,
citations: x.citations ?? [], staleDiff: null,
})),
};
});

app.post("/v1/questionnaires/:id/questions/:qid/approve", async (req) => {
const { id, qid } = req.params as { id: string; qid: string };
const { answer } = (req.body ?? {}) as { answer?: string };
const q = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findFirstOrThrow({
where: { id: qid, questionnaire: { id, orgId: req.org!.id } },
include: { questionnaire: true } });
const result = await approveAnswer(qid, answer ?? q.answer ?? "", req.user?.id);
return { status: "VERIFIED", editDistance: result.editDistance };
});

app.post("/v1/questionnaires/:id/questions/:qid/reject", async (req) => {
const { id, qid } = req.params as { id: string; qid: string };
const { code, note } = req.body as { code: string; note?: string };
const q = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findFirstOrThrow({
where: { id: qid, questionnaire: { id, orgId: req.org!.id } } });
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.update({ where: { id: qid },
data: { status: "REJECTED" } });
// THE LEARNING LOOP — rejection captured with its taxonomy code
await captureRejection(qid, code, note);
return { status: "REJECTED" };
});

app.post("/v1/questionnaires/:id/bulk-approve", async (req) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const { ids } = req.body as { ids: string[] };
// Server re-verifies confidence — never trust the client's list
const eligible = await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.findMany({
where: { id: { in: ids }, questionnaire: { id, orgId: req.org!.id },
status: "AUTO_DRAFTED", confidence: { gte: BULK_THRESHOLD } },
include: { questionnaire: true } });
for (const q of eligible) {
await prisma.questionnaireQuestion.update({
where: { id: q.id }, data: { status: "VERIFIED" } });
const { upsertAnswerLibrary } = await import("@questionnaire");
await upsertAnswerLibrary(q.questionnaire.orgId, q.question,
q.answer ?? "", q.citations, req.user?.id);
}
return { approved: eligible.length };
});
}

function routeStatus(dbStatus: string, confidence: number | null): string {
if (dbStatus === "STALE") return "STALE";
if (dbStatus === "AUTO_DRAFTED") return "AUTO_DRAFTED";
if (dbStatus === "VERIFIED" || dbStatus === "EDITED_VERIFIED") return "IN_REVIEW";
if ((confidence ?? 0) < 0.7) return "MANDATORY";
return "IN_REVIEW";
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/openapi/generate.ts
```typescript
import { zodToJsonSchema } from "zod-to-json-schema";
import { CATALOG } from "@mcp";
import { BRAIN_TOOLS } from "@mcp/tools/brainTools";
import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const RATE_BY_TIER: Record<number, string> = { 1: "120/min", 2: "30/min", 3: "6/min" };
const ALL_TOOLS = [...CATALOG, ...BRAIN_TOOLS];

function extractPathParams(path: string): string[] {
return [...path.matchAll(/\{(\w+)\}/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
}

export function generateOpenApi(): object {
const paths: Record<string, any> = {};

for (const tool of ALL_TOOLS) {
if (!tool.rest) continue;
const { method, path, summaryTag } = tool.rest;
const jsonSchema = zodToJsonSchema(
(await0 as any, tool.input) as never, { target: "openApi3" }) as {
properties?: Record<string, unknown> };

const pathParams = extractPathParams(path);
const paramSchemas = pickKeys((jsonSchema.properties ?? {}) as Record<string, any>, pathParams);
const bodySchema = omitKeys((jsonSchema.properties ?? {}) as Record<string, any>, pathParams);

paths[path] ??= {};
paths[path][method.toLowerCase()] = {
operationId: tool.name,
summary: tool.description.slice(0, 120),
tags: [summaryTag],
security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
"x-required-scope": tool.scope,
"x-rate-limit": RATE_BY_TIER[tool.tier],
"x-tier": tool.tier,
parameters: pathParams.map((p) => ({
name: p, in: "path", required: true, schema: paramSchemas[p] })),
...(Object.keys(bodySchema).length && method === "POST"
? { requestBody: { required: true, content: { "application/json":
{ schema: { type: "object", properties: bodySchema } } } } } : {}),
responses: {
...(tool.readOnly ? { 200: success() } : { 201: success() }),
400: problem("Validation error"),
401: problem("Invalid or revoked API key"),
402: problem("Tier or quota gate", undefined),
403: problem("Missing scope", `x-required-scope: ${tool.scope}`),
404: problem("Resource not found"),
429: { description: "Rate limited",
headers: { "Retry-After": { schema: { type: "integer" } } },
content: { "application/problem+json": { schema:
{ $ref: "#/components/schemas/Problem" } } } },
},
};
}

return {
openapi: "3.1.0",
info: { title: "Compliance Platform API", version: "1.0.0",
description: "Generated from the tool catalog — the same handlers serve MCP. " +
"Scopes are identical across surfaces." },
servers: [{ url: process.env.API_PUBLIC_URL ?? "https://api.example.com" }],
security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
paths,
components: {
securitySchemes: { ApiKeyAuth: { type: "apiKey",
in: "header", name: "x-api-key" } },
schemas: { Problem: { type: "object", properties: {
type: { type: "string" }, title: { type: "string" },
status: { type: "integer" }, detail: { type: "string" },
"x-required-scope": { type: "string" } },
description: "RFC 9457 problem details" } },
},
};

function success() { return { description: "Success" }; }
function problem(title: string, detail?: string) {
return { description: title, content: { "application/problem+json": {
schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Problem" },
example: { title, status: 400, detail } } } };
}
}
const await0 = undefined; // placeholder for tool.input pass-through below

function pickKeys(o: Record<string, any>, keys: string[]) {
return Object.fromEntries(keys.filter((k) => k in o).map((k) => [k, o[k]]));
}
function omitKeys(o: Record<string, any>, keys: string[]) {
return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(o).filter(([k]) => !keys.includes(k)));
}

// CLI: write spec + fail on drift (CI consumes this via pnpm sdk:generate)
if (require.main === module) {
const spec = generateOpenApi();
const out = join(__dirname, "../../../sdks/openapi.json");
mkdirSync(join(out, ".."), { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2));
console.log(`wrote ${out} (${Object.keys((spec as any).paths).length} paths)`);
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/index.ts
```typescript
import Fastify from "fastify";
import cookie from "@fastify/cookie";
import cors from "@fastify/cors";
import { verifySession } from "./middleware/session";
import { mountCatalogRoutes } from "./routes/catalogRest";
import { brainRoutes } from "./routes/brain";
import { brainStreamRoutes } from "./routes/brainStream";
import { auditorRoutes, auditorGdprRoutes } from "./routes/auditor";
import { trustRoutes } from "./routes/trust";
import { reviewRoutes } from "./routes/review";
import { ethicsEventsRoutes } from "./routes/ethics-events";
import { controlHandlers } from "./handlers/controls";
import { reviewHandlers } from "./handlers/questionnaireReview";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import "@skills"; // warm skill + connector registries

const app = Fastify({ logger: { level: "warn" }, bodyLimit: 12 * 1024 * 1024 });

await app.register(cookie);
await app.register(cors, {
origin: (origin, cb) =>
cb(null, !origin || origin.includes(process.env.APP_DOMAIN ?? "localhost:3000")),
credentials: true,
});

// ── health (no auth — deploy.sh smoke + brain deadman) ──
app.get("/healthz", async () => ({ ok: true, service: "api", at: new Date().toISOString() }));

// ── session-authenticated app surface ───────────────────────────
app.addHook("preHandler", async (req, reply) => {
if (req.url.startsWith("/v1/auditor") || req.url.startsWith("/v1/trust") ||
req.url.startsWith("/internal/") || req.url === "/healthz" ||
req.url.startsWith("/v1/brain/status.json")) return; // own auth chains
await verifySession(req, reply);
});

// screen backends (session-scoped, RLS-enforced)
await app.register(controlHandlers);
await app.register(reviewHandlers);

// catalog REST (entitlement → scope → rate, per route)
await app.register(mountCatalogRoutes);

// brain surface (session) + public status
await app.register(brainRoutes);
await app.register(brainStreamRoutes);

// portals & internal bridges
await app.register(auditorRoutes);
await app.register(auditorGdprRoutes);
await app.register(trustRoutes);
await app.register(reviewRoutes);
await app.register(ethicsEventsRoutes);

// request-failure telemetry → brain
app.addHook("onError", async (req, reply, err) => {
emit({ type: "api.request.failed", source: req.url.startsWith("/mcp") ? "mcp" : "api",
orgId: req.org?.id, severityOverride: "WARN",
payload: { url: req.url, status: reply.statusCode,
error: String(err.message).slice(0, 200) } });
});

const port = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);
await app.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" });
console.log(`API on :${port}`);

for (const sig of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"] as const) {
process.on(sig, async () => { await app.close(); process.exit(0); });
}
```

````
**Batch 7 complete — 18 files.** The API surface is fully assembled: session + API-key + entitlement (tier/meter, RFC-9457 with `x-required-scope`) auth chains, the auditor portal with watermarked evidence rendering and the k-anonymized GDPR view, Trust Center with NDA auto-approval + signature capture, the review console routes, brain REST+SSE, the catalog REST mount (one handler, two surfaces), the ethics HMAC bridge, the screen handlers the web app calls, and the OpenAPI generator writing `sdks/openapi.json` for drift CI.

**Reply "next" → Batch 8: `apps/workers` + `apps/ethics`** — Temporal workflows (evidence sync, check runner, SLA timers incl. ethics kinds, remediation loop, negative-learning weekly, legislative radar, webhook retry), activities, schedule bootstrap with cron jitter, worker entry + ethics service (report/inbox routes, isolated schema boot, crypto-shred job) (~15 files).

**Batch 8 of 10 — `apps/workers` + `apps/ethics` + API Temporal wiring (24 files).** Consolidation fixes applied: (1) **remediation loop is now an hourly verification sweep** (not per-task signal workflows) — DONE-but-unverified gap/test tasks get re-checked, failures auto-reopen with reason; avoids API↔Temporal signal coupling and the verification record *is* the fresh TestRun/quality rows; (2) **`runEscalation` ships default milestone actions** — previously it threw when `EscalationPolicy` rows were missing; DB stages now override sane per-kind defaults; (3) **`notify` implemented** — role-target resolution + unacknowledged-only filter for policy reminders; delivery is a marked STUB (console) with the SES swap point; (4) **the API gets a Temporal client module** — the ethics-events bridge override starts/stops deterministic ethics SLA workflows, and the controls handler override persists incident obligations and starts per-obligation clocks; (5) **cron jitter from the load-test round is real code now** (deterministic hash offset in `schedules.ts`); (6) **webhook retry closes the module-10 loop** — 5-min sweep, attempts-based backoff, DEAD at 10; (7) **ethics DB gains `OrgRef`** (slug→orgId routing + per-org ethics KMS key) — the isolated service can't join the main DB; investigator auth is env-token v1 with opaque `actorRef` (documented simplification); (8) sweeps close promised behaviors: CLAIMED review auto-release >24h, DSAR overdue escalation, weekly retrieval health, monthly exec reports.

Save as `batch8.txt` → `python3 split.py batch8.txt`.
````

### FILE: apps/workers/src/activities/index.ts
```typescript
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { appendEvidence, createGapTask, controlQualityScore,
QUALITY_GATE, generateMonthlyReport } from "@core";
import { runCheckInstrumented } from "@skills";
import { scanAllSources, pollDueSources, applyDueProposals } from "@knowledge";
import { scanAllQueues } from "@review";
import { monitorRetrievalHealth } from "@questionnaire";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

// ── EVIDENCE SYNC (module 2 / Artifact C) ────────────────────────
export async function fetchSnapshot(args: { orgId: string; integrationId: string; dataType: string }) {
const integration = await prisma.integration.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: args.integrationId } });
const { connectorFor } = await import("@skills");
const snap = await connectorFor(integration.type)
.collect({ orgId: args.orgId }, args.dataType);
return { items: snap.items, contentHash: snap.contentHash,
sourceHealth: snap.sourceHealth };
}

export async function getLastEvidenceHash(args: { orgId: string; dataType: string }) {
const last = await prisma.integrationSnapshot.findFirst({
where: { orgId: args.orgId, dataType: args.dataType },
orderBy: { collectedAt: "desc" } });
return last?.contentHash ?? null;
}

/** Persists only if content changed; appends into the control hash chain. */
export async function persistEvidence(args: {
orgId: string; integrationId: string; controlId: string;
dataType: string; title: string; freshnessDays: number;
items: unknown[]; contentHash: string;
}) {
const last = await getLastEvidenceHash({ orgId: args.orgId, dataType: args.dataType });
if (last === args.contentHash) return { created: false };
const evidence = await appendEvidence({
orgId: args.orgId, controlId: args.controlId, sourceType: "AUTOMATED",
title: args.title, expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + args.freshnessDays * DAY),
payload: { dataType: args.dataType, items: args.items },
integrationId: args.integrationId,
});
await prisma.integrationSnapshot.create({
data: { orgId: args.orgId, integrationId: args.integrationId,
dataType: args.dataType, contentHash: args.contentHash,
evidenceId: evidence.id } });
emit({ type: "evidence.created", source: "connector", orgId: args.orgId,
payload: { dataType: args.dataType, evidenceId: evidence.id,
sequence: evidence.sequence } });
return { created: true, evidenceId: evidence.id };
}

export async function createDriftTask(args: { orgId: string; dataType: string; controlId: string }) {
return prisma.task.create({ data: {
orgId: args.orgId, source: "GAP_ANALYSIS", priority: "MEDIUM",
title: `Drift detected: ${args.dataType}`,
description: `Scheduled evidence sync detected unexpected change in "${args.dataType}". ` +
`Review the change and confirm the control still operates as designed.`,
relatedType: "Control", relatedId: args.controlId } });
}

// ── CHECK RUNNER (module 3) ──────────────────────────────────────
/** maxAttempts=1 enforced by the workflow's proxy — runCheck mutates DB. */
export async function runCheckActivity(args: { orgId: string; testId: string }) {
const { run, evidence } = await runCheckInstrumented(args);
return { runId: run.id, status: run.status as string,
evidenceId: evidence?.id ?? null, sequence: evidence?.sequence ?? null };
}

export async function recordCheckErrorActivity(args: {
orgId: string; testId: string; message: string }) {
const test = await prisma.controlTest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: args.testId } });
await prisma.testRun.create({ data: {
orgId: args.orgId, controlId: test.controlId, testId: test.id,
status: "ERROR", finishedAt: new Date(),
summary: `Workflow-level failure: ${args.message.slice(0, 400)}` } });
}

// ── REMEDIATION VERIFICATION (module 6, sweep form) ─────────────
export async function findUnverifiedDoneTasks() {
return prisma.task.findMany({
where: { status: "DONE", source: { in: ["TEST_FAILURE", "GAP_ANALYSIS"] },
completedAt: { gte: new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 3_600_000) } },
take: 100 });
}

export async function rerunCheckForTask(args: { orgId: string; taskId: string }) {
const task = await prisma.task.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: args.taskId } });
if (task.relatedType !== "TestRun")
return { checkable: false as const };
const run = await prisma.testRun.findUnique({ where: { id: task.relatedId! } });
if (!run) return { checkable: false as const };
const result = await runCheckActivity({ orgId: args.orgId, testId: run.testId });
return { checkable: true as const, status: result.status };
}

export async function reopenTask(args: { taskId: string; reason: string }) {
await prisma.task.update({ where: { id: args.taskId },
data: { status: "OPEN", completedAt: null,
description: `${(await prisma.task.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: args.taskId } })).description}\n\n---\nAUTO-REOPENED: ${args.reason}` } });
emit({ type: "brain.anomaly.opened", source: "workers",
payload: { kind: "task_reopened", taskId: args.taskId } });
}

// ── ESCALATION + NOTIFY (Artifact C + defaults fix) ─────────────
/** Default milestone actions — DB EscalationStage rows override these. */
const DEFAULT_ACTIONS: Record<string, { notifyRoles: string[]; instructions: string;
createTask?: boolean; taskPriority?: string }> = {
REMIND_EMPLOYEE: { notifyRoles: ["EMPLOYEE"], instructions: "Policy acknowledgment overdue — complete now." },
ESCALATE_MANAGER: { notifyRoles: ["COMPLIANCE_MANAGER"], instructions: "Employee policy acknowledgment overdue — follow up." },
ESCALATE_ADMIN_TASK: { notifyRoles: ["ADMIN"], instructions: "Policy acknowledgment persistently overdue.", createTask: true, taskPriority: "MEDIUM" },
WARN_OWNER: { notifyRoles: ["COMPLIANCE_MANAGER"], instructions: "Vendor review approaching/over deadline." },
ESCALATE_COMPLIANCE_MANAGER: { notifyRoles: ["COMPLIANCE_MANAGER", "ADMIN"], instructions: "Vendor review overdue — escalate.", createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH" },
TASK_CRITICAL: { notifyRoles: ["ADMIN"], instructions: "Vendor review far overdue.", createTask: true, taskPriority: "CRITICAL" },
WARN_LEGAL: { notifyRoles: ["COMPLIANCE_MANAGER", "ADMIN"], instructions: "Regulatory notification deadline at 50% — prepare filing." },
ESCALATE_CISO: { notifyRoles: ["ADMIN", "OWNER"], instructions: "Regulatory notification deadline REACHED — file now.", createTask: true, taskPriority: "CRITICAL" },
ESCALATE_ETHICS_COMMITTEE: { notifyRoles: ["OWNER", "COMPLIANCE_MANAGER"], instructions: "Ethics case SLA milestone reached. Case ID only — content stays in the ethics service.", createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH" },
};

export async function runEscalation(args: {
kind: string; orgId: string; subjectId: string; action: string;
milestonePct?: number; meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
}) {
const stage = await prisma.escalationStage.findFirst({
where: { policy: { kind: args.kind }, action: args.action } });
const conf = stage
? { notifyRoles: stage.notifyRoles, instructions: stage.instructions,
createTask: stage.createTask, taskPriority: stage.taskPriority ?? "HIGH" }
: DEFAULT_ACTIONS[args.action]
?? { notifyRoles: ["ADMIN"], instructions: args.action, createTask: true, taskPriority: "HIGH" };

await notify({ orgId: args.orgId, roleTargets: conf.notifyRoles,
subject: `SLA escalation [${args.kind}/${args.action}]`,
body: `Subject: ${args.subjectId}\nStage: ${args.milestonePct ?? 100}% of deadline elapsed.\n` +
`Action required: ${conf.instructions}` });

if (conf.createTask && args.orgId) {
await prisma.task.create({ data: {
orgId: args.orgId, source: "GAP_ANALYSIS",
priority: (conf.taskPriority ?? "HIGH") as never,
title: `SLA breach: ${args.kind} (${args.action})`,
description: `SLA milestone reached for ${args.subjectId}. ${conf.instructions}`,
relatedType: "SlaSubject", relatedId: args.subjectId } });
}
}

/** Role-targeted notify — STUB delivery (console); swap for SES/Postmark. */
export async function notify(args: {
orgId?: string; roleTargets: string[]; subject: string; body: string;
filterFn?: string; policyVersionId?: string;
}) {
if (!args.orgId) { console.log(`[notify:STUB] ${args.subject} (no org)`); return; }
let members = await prisma.membership.findMany({
where: { orgId: args.orgId, role: { in: args.roleTargets as never } },
include: { user: { select: { email: true } } } });
if (args.filterFn === "unacknowledgedOnly" && args.policyVersionId) {
const acked = new Set((await prisma.policyAcknowledgment.findMany({
where: { policyVersionId: args.policyVersionId },
select: { membershipId: true } })).map((a) => a.membershipId));
members = members.filter((m) => !acked.has(m.id));
}
console.log(`[notify:STUB] "${args.subject}" → ${members.length} recipient(s): ` +
members.map((m) => m.user.email.replace(/(.{2}).*(@.*)/, "$1***$2")).join(", "));
}

export async function sendPolicyReminder(args: { orgId: string; policyVersionId: string }) {
const pending = await prisma.policyAcknowledgment.count({
where: { policyVersionId: args.policyVersionId } }) === 0
? await prisma.membership.count({ where: { orgId: args.orgId } })
: await countPending(args.policyVersionId);
if (pending > 0)
await notify({ orgId: args.orgId, roleTargets: ["EMPLOYEE"],
subject: "Policy acknowledgment overdue",
body: "You have a pending policy acknowledgment. Please complete it now.",
filterFn: "unacknowledgedOnly", policyVersionId: args.policyVersionId });
return { pending };
}
async function countPending(policyVersionId: string) {
// memberships minus acks — approximated by notify's internal filter
return 1; // STUB: replaced by real count in the SES integration
}

// ── SLA SWEEP (privacy overdue + review auto-release) ───────────
export async function escalateOverduePrivacyRequests() {
const overdue = await prisma.privacyRequest.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["VERIFIED", "COMPILING", "REVIEW"] },
OR: [{ extensionTo: { lt: new Date() } },
{ AND: [{ extensionTo: null }, { dueAt: { lt: new Date() } }] }] } });
let escalated = 0;
for (const r of overdue) {
const existing = await prisma.task.findFirst({
where: { relatedType: "PrivacyRequest", relatedId: r.id } });
if (existing) continue; // one escalation per request
await prisma.task.create({ data: {
orgId: r.orgId, source: "GAP_ANALYSIS", priority: "CRITICAL",
title: `DSAR overdue (${r.type})`,
description: `Request ${r.id} past its Art.12(3) deadline (incl. extension if granted). ` +
`Deliver or document the rejection basis immediately.`,
relatedType: "PrivacyRequest", relatedId: r.id } });
await notify({ orgId: r.orgId, roleTargets: ["COMPLIANCE_MANAGER", "ADMIN"],
subject: "DSAR deadline breached", body: `Request ${r.id} is past due.` });
escalated++;
}
return { overdue: overdue.length, escalated };
}

/** CLAIMED review items >24h unactioned → back to PENDING (auto-release). */
export async function requeueStaleReviewClaims() {
const stale = await prisma.$executeRaw`
UPDATE "ReviewItem" SET status = 'PENDING', "claimedByMembershipId" = NULL
WHERE status = 'CLAIMED' AND "claimedAt" < now() - interval '24 hours'`;
return { requeued: stale };
}

// ── LEARNING + REPORTING + RADAR SWEEPS ─────────────────────────
export async function runRetrievalHealthForOrg(orgId: string) {
return monitorRetrievalHealth(orgId);
}

export async function orgsWithLearning() {
const rows = await prisma.negativeExample.groupBy({ by: ["orgId"] });
return rows.map((r) => r.orgId).filter(Boolean);
}

export async function orgsForMonthlyReport() {
const rows = await prisma.control.groupBy({ by: ["orgId"] });
return rows.map((r) => r.orgId);
}

export async function generateReportForOrg(orgId: string, period: string) {
return generateMonthlyReport(orgId, period);
}

export async function scanRegSources() { return scanAllSources(); }
export async function pollLegislative() { return pollDueSources(); }
export async function applyDueMatrix() { return applyDueProposals(); }
export async function scanReviewQueues() { return scanAllQueues(); }

// ── WEBHOOK RETRY (module 10 close-out) ──────────────────────────
export async function findRetryableDeliveries() {
// attempts-based backoff: eligible once createdAt is older than 2^attempts
// minutes (capped at 60); DEAD at attempt 10.
return prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ id: string }>>`
SELECT id FROM "WebhookDelivery"
WHERE status = 'PENDING' AND attempts < 10
AND "createdAt" < now() - (least(power(2, attempts), 60) || ' minutes')::interval
LIMIT 25`;
}

export async function attemptWebhookDelivery(deliveryId: string) {
const d = await prisma.webhookDelivery.findUniqueOrThrow({
where: { id: deliveryId }, include: { endpoint: true } });
if (!d.endpoint.active) return { skipped: true };

const body = JSON.stringify({ id: d.id, type: d.eventType,
created: d.createdAt.toISOString(), data: d.payload });
const signature = createHmac("sha256", d.endpoint.secret)
.update(`${d.id}.${body}`).digest("hex");

let ok = false, error: string | null = null;
try {
const res = await fetch(d.endpoint.url, { method: "POST", body,
headers: { "content-type": "application/json",
"X-Compliance-Event": d.eventType,
"X-Compliance-Delivery": d.id,
"X-Compliance-Signature": `sha256=${signature}` } });
ok = res.ok; if (!ok) error = `HTTP ${res.status}`;
} catch (e) { error = String(e).slice(0, 200); }

const attempts = d.attempts + 1;
await prisma.webhookDelivery.update({ where: { id: d.id }, data: ok
? { status: "DELIVERED", deliveredAt: new Date(), attempts, lastError: null }
: { attempts, lastError: error,
status: attempts >= 10 ? "DEAD" : "PENDING" } });
return { delivered: ok, attempts, dead: !ok && attempts >= 10 };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/workers/src/workflows/sla.ts
```typescript
import { proxyActivities, defineSignal, defineQuery, setHandler,
condition, startChildWorkflow } from "@temporalio/workflow";
import type * as activities from "../activities";

const act = proxyActivities<typeof activities>({
startToCloseTimeout: "1 minute",
retry: { maximumAttempts: 5, initialInterval: "30 seconds" },
});

export type SlaKind =
| "ETHICS_ACK" | "ETHICS_FEEDBACK" | "POLICY_ACK" | "VENDOR_REVIEW"
| "INCIDENT_NOTIFICATION" | "DSAR_30D";

export const slaCompleted = defineSignal<[string?]>("completed");
export const slaStatus = defineQuery<string>("status");

export interface SlaInput {
kind: SlaKind; orgId: string; subjectId: string;
startAt: string; deadlineAt: string;
milestones?: { atPct: number; action: string }[];
}

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const addDaysIso = (iso: string, days: number) =>
new Date(Date.parse(iso) + days * DAY).toISOString();

function defaultMilestones(kind: SlaKind) {
switch (kind) {
case "ETHICS_ACK": case "ETHICS_FEEDBACK":
return [{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_ETHICS_COMMITTEE" }];
case "POLICY_ACK":
return [{ atPct: 100, action: "REMIND_EMPLOYEE" },
{ atPct: 150, action: "ESCALATE_MANAGER" },
{ atPct: 200, action: "ESCALATE_ADMIN_TASK" }];
case "VENDOR_REVIEW":
return [{ atPct: 80, action: "WARN_OWNER" },
{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_COMPLIANCE_MANAGER" },
{ atPct: 150, action: "TASK_CRITICAL" }];
case "INCIDENT_NOTIFICATION":
return [{ atPct: 50, action: "WARN_LEGAL" },
{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_CISO" }];
case "DSAR_30D":
return [{ atPct: 80, action: "WARN_OWNER" },
{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_COMPLIANCE_MANAGER" }];
}
}

/**
* Generic SLA timer. Escalates at milestone percentages of the window.
* All time math derives from input constants (deterministic on replay).
* Resolves early when the `completed` signal fires.
*/
export async function slaTimerWorkflow(input: SlaInput): Promise<{
outcome: "COMPLETED" | "BREACHED" | "BREACHED_FINAL"; escalations: number }> {
let done = false;
let escalations = 0;
setHandler(slaCompleted, () => { done = true; });
setHandler(slaStatus, () => (done ? "COMPLETED" : "OPEN"));

const t0 = Date.parse(input.startAt);
const t1 = Date.parse(input.deadlineAt);
const window = t1 - t0;

const milestones = [...(input.milestones ?? defaultMilestones(input.kind))]
.sort((a, b) => a.atPct - b.atPct);

let waited = 0; // ms consumed by sleeps so far

for (const m of milestones) {
const targetOffset = window * (m.atPct / 100);
const sleepMs = Math.max(0, targetOffset - waited);
const woke = await condition(() => done, sleepMs);
waited = Math.max(waited, targetOffset);
if (woke) return { outcome: "COMPLETED", escalations };

await act.runEscalation({
kind: input.kind, orgId: input.orgId, subjectId: input.subjectId,
action: m.action, milestonePct: m.atPct });
escalations++;
}

// Final grace window, then close as breached.
const woke = await condition(() => done, 30 * DAY);
return woke ? { outcome: "COMPLETED", escalations }
: { outcome: "BREACHED_FINAL", escalations };
}

/** EU Whistleblower Directive 2019/1937: acknowledge ≤7 days (Art. 9). */
export async function ethicsAckSlaWorkflow(input: {
orgId: string; caseId: string; submittedAt: string }) {
const h = await startChildWorkflow(slaTimerWorkflow, {
kind: "ETHICS_ACK", orgId: input.orgId, subjectId: input.caseId,
startAt: input.submittedAt, deadlineAt: addDaysIso(input.submittedAt, 7),
}, { workflowId: `sla-ethics-ack-${input.caseId}` });
return h.result();
}

/** Directive Art. 9(1)(f): feedback ≤3 months from acknowledgment. */
export async function ethicsFeedbackSlaWorkflow(input: {
orgId: string; caseId: string; acknowledgedAt: string }) {
const h = await startChildWorkflow(slaTimerWorkflow, {
kind: "ETHICS_FEEDBACK", orgId: input.orgId, subjectId: input.caseId,
startAt: input.acknowledgedAt, deadlineAt: addDaysIso(input.acknowledgedAt, 91),
}, { workflowId: `sla-ethics-feedback-${input.caseId}` });
return h.result();
}

/** One timer per statutory deadline (HIPAA 60d, GDPR 72h, state AGs…). */
export async function incidentNotificationSlaWorkflow(input: {
orgId: string; incidentId: string; notificationId: string; authority: string;
detectedAt: string; deadlineAt: string }) {
const h = await startChildWorkflow(slaTimerWorkflow, {
kind: "INCIDENT_NOTIFICATION", orgId: input.orgId,
subjectId: `${input.incidentId}:${input.authority}`,
startAt: input.detectedAt, deadlineAt: input.deadlineAt,
milestones: [{ atPct: 50, action: "WARN_LEGAL" },
{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_CISO" }],
}, { workflowId: `sla-incident-${input.notificationId}` });
return h.result();
}

/** Policy acknowledgment: sleep to due date → 14 daily reminders → escalate. */
export async function policyAcknowledgmentSlaWorkflow(input: {
orgId: string; policyVersionId: string; dueAt: string; startedAt: string }) {
let done = false;
setHandler(slaCompleted, () => { done = true; });

const daysUntilDue = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(
(Date.parse(input.dueAt) - Date.parse(input.startedAt)) / DAY));
for (let i = 0; i < daysUntilDue; i++) {
if (await condition(() => done, DAY)) return { outcome: "COMPLETED" as const };
}
for (let day = 0; day < 14; day++) {
await act.sendPolicyReminder({ orgId: input.orgId, policyVersionId: input.policyVersionId });
if (await condition(() => done, DAY)) return { outcome: "COMPLETED" as const };
}
await act.runEscalation({ kind: "POLICY_ACK", orgId: input.orgId,
subjectId: input.policyVersionId, action: "ESCALATE_ADMIN_TASK",
milestonePct: 100 });
return { outcome: "BREACHED" as const };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/workers/src/workflows/evidenceSync.ts
```typescript
import { proxyActivities, defineSignal, defineQuery, setHandler, condition } from "@temporalio/workflow";
import type * as activities from "../activities";

const act = proxyActivities<typeof activities>({
startToCloseTimeout: "10 minutes",
retry: { maximumAttempts: 3, initialInterval: "60 seconds", backoffCoefficient: 2 },
});

export const pauseSync = defineSignal("pauseSync");
export const syncStatus = defineQuery<string>("status");

export interface SnapshotSpec {
dataType: string; controlId: string; title: string;
freshnessDays: number; expectStable?: boolean;
}

export async function evidenceSyncWorkflow(input: {
orgId: string; integrationId: string; snapshots: SnapshotSpec[];
}): Promise<{ persisted: string[]; skipped: string[]; drift: string[] }> {
let paused = false;
setHandler(pauseSync, () => { paused = true; });
setHandler(syncStatus, () => (paused ? "PAUSED" : "RUNNING"));

const persisted: string[] = [], skipped: string[] = [], drift: string[] = [];

for (const spec of input.snapshots) {
if (paused) break; // respect pause between snapshots

const prevHash = await act.getLastEvidenceHash({
orgId: input.orgId, dataType: spec.dataType });
const { items, contentHash } = await act.fetchSnapshot({
orgId: input.orgId, integrationId: input.integrationId, dataType: spec.dataType });

const { created } = await act.persistEvidence({
orgId: input.orgId, integrationId: input.integrationId,
controlId: spec.controlId, dataType: spec.dataType,
title: spec.title, freshnessDays: spec.freshnessDays,
items, contentHash });

(created ? persisted : skipped).push(spec.dataType);

// Drift: content changed where stability was expected
if (created && spec.expectStable && prevHash !== null) {
await act.createDriftTask({
orgId: input.orgId, dataType: spec.dataType, controlId: spec.controlId });
drift.push(spec.dataType);
}
}
return { persisted, skipped, drift };
}
```

```typescript
import { proxyActivities } from "@temporalio/workflow";

// runCheckActivity mutates DB (TestRun + evidence chain) — never auto-retry

});
```

### FILE: apps/workers/src/workflows/sweeps.ts
```typescript
import { proxyActivities } from "@temporalio/workflow";
import type * as activities from "../activities";

const act = proxyActivities<typeof activities>({
startToCloseTimeout: "10 minutes",
retry: { maximumAttempts: 3, initialInterval: "30 seconds" },
});
const oneShot = proxyActivities<typeof activities>({
startToCloseTimeout: "10 minutes",
retry: { maximumAttempts: 1 },
});

/**
* HOURLY — remediation verification sweep (module 6, poll form).
* DONE gap/test tasks from the last 24h get re-verified: failing checks
* auto-reopen the task with the reason. Verification evidence = the fresh
* TestRun/quality rows themselves. Idempotent: re-running a passing check
* is harmless; reopened tasks drop out of the DONE window naturally.
*/
export async function remediationVerifyWorkflow() {
const tasks = await act.findUnverifiedDoneTasks();
let reopened = 0, verified = 0, skipped = 0;
for (const t of tasks) {
const result = await oneShot.rerunCheckForTask({ orgId: t.orgId, taskId: t.id });
if (!result.checkable) { skipped++; continue; }
if (result.status === "PASS") { verified++; continue; }
await act.reopenTask({ taskId: t.id,
reason: `verification re-test ${result.status} — fix and mark done again` });
reopened++;
}
return { scanned: tasks.length, verified, reopened, skipped };
}

/** HOURLY — DSAR overdue escalation + review-claim auto-release. */
export async function slaSweepWorkflow() {
const [dsar, review] = await Promise.all([
act.escalateOverduePrivacyRequests(),
act.requeueStaleReviewClaims(),
]);
return { dsar, review };
}

/** WEEKLY — negative-example learning loop health (module 7/D). */
export async function negativeLearningSweepWorkflow() {
const orgs = await act.orgsWithLearning();
const reports = [];
for (const orgId of orgs)
reports.push({ orgId, ...(await act.runRetrievalHealthForOrg(orgId)) });
return { orgs: orgs.length, reports };
}

/** MONTHLY — executive risk reports (module 15). */
export async function execReportSweepWorkflow() {
const period = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7); // activity-side time is fine
const orgs = await act.orgsForMonthlyReport();
let generated = 0;
for (const orgId of orgs) {
await act.generateReportForOrg(orgId, period);
generated++;
}
return { period, orgs: orgs.length, generated };
}
```

### FILE: apps/workers/src/workflows/webhookRetry.ts
```typescript
import { proxyActivities } from "@temporalio/workflow";
import type * as activities from "../activities";

const act = proxyActivities<typeof activities>({
startToCloseTimeout: "1 minute",
retry: { maximumAttempts: 3, initialInterval: "10 seconds" },
});

/** EVERY 5 MIN — retry pending webhook deliveries (backoff via SQL),
* dead-letter at attempt 10. First attempt happens in the brain's
* fanOutWebhooks; this sweep owns everything after that. */
export async function webhookRetrySweepWorkflow() {
const due = await act.findRetryableDeliveries();
let delivered = 0, dead = 0;
for (const d of due) {
const r = await act.attemptWebhookDelivery(d.id);
if (r.delivered) delivered++;
if (r.dead) dead++;
}
return { attempted: due.length, delivered, dead };
}
```

```typescript
import { Worker, Runtime, DefaultLogger, LogTimestamp } from "@temporalio/worker";
import * as activities from "./activities";

// Warm the skill + connector registries BEFORE the worker starts —
```

### FILE: apps/workers/src/schedules.ts
```typescript
/**
* Schedule bootstrap. Cron-jitter fix from the load-test round: default
* check crons all land in the 06:00–10:00 wall; each check gets a
* deterministic minute offset (hash of orgId+checkId, 0–39) to flatten
* the spike ~70% before any scaling.
*
* Run once (and after adding orgs/integrations):
* pnpm --filter @workers schedules
*/
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { Client } from "@temporalio/client";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";
import { connectorFor } from "@skills";
import type { SnapshotSpec } from "./workflows/evidenceSync";

function jittered(cron: string, seed: string): string {
const [min, hour, dom, mon, dow] = cron.split(" ");
if (!/^\d+$/.test(min)) return cron; // interval crons untouched
const offset = createHash("sha256").update(seed).digest()[0] % 40;
return `${Math.min(Number(min) + offset, 59)} ${hour} ${dom} ${mon} ${dow}`;
}

async function ensureSchedule(client: Client, scheduleId: string,
spec: { cronExpressions?: string[]; intervals?: string[] },
workflowType: string, args: unknown[]) {
try {
await client.schedule.create({
scheduleId,
spec: spec.intervals
? { intervals: [{ every: spec.intervals[0] }] }
: { cronExpressions: spec.cronExpressions! },
action: { type: "startWorkflow", workflowType, taskQueue: "compliance", args },
state: { paused: false },
policies: { overlap: "SKIP" },
});
console.log(`✓ schedule ${scheduleId}`);
} catch (e: any) {
if (String(e).includes("already") || String(e).includes("Already")) {
console.log(`• schedule ${scheduleId} exists (skipped)`);
} else throw e;
}
}

async function main() {
const client = new Client({ address: process.env.TEMPORAL_ADDRESS ?? "localhost:7233" });

// 1. Per-integration evidence sync (daily) with resolved connector specs
const integrations = await prisma.integration.findMany({
where: { status: { not: "DISCONNECTED" } });
for (const integration of integrations) {
try {
const resolved = (await connectorFor(integration.type).specs())
.map((s) => s); // specs are static; control binding resolved below
const controls = await prisma.control.findMany({
where: { orgId: integration.orgId } });
const bySlug = new Map(controls.map((c) => [c.slug, c.id]));
const snapshots: SnapshotSpec[] = resolved.flatMap((s) => {
const controlId = bySlug.get(s.controlTemplateIds[0] ?? "");
return controlId ? [{ dataType: s.dataType, controlId,
title: s.title, freshnessDays: s.freshnessDays,
expectStable: s.expectStable }] : [];
});
if (snapshots.length === 0) continue;
await ensureSchedule(client, `evidence-sync-${integration.orgId}-${integration.id}`,
{ intervals: ["24 hours"] }, "evidenceSyncWorkflow",
[{ orgId: integration.orgId, integrationId: integration.id, snapshots }]);
} catch (e) { console.warn(`connector ${integration.type}: ${String(e).slice(0, 120)}`); }
}

// 2. Per-check schedules WITH jitter
const tests = await prisma.controlTest.findMany({ where: { enabled: true } });
for (const t of tests) {
const jitteredCron = jittered(t.schedule, `${t.orgId}:${t.id}`);
await ensureSchedule(client, `check-${t.id}`, { cronExpressions: [jitteredCron] },
"checkRunWorkflow", [{ orgId: t.orgId, testId: t.id }]);
}
console.log(`✓ ${tests.length} check schedules (jittered)`);

// 3. Maintenance sweeps
await ensureSchedule(client, "sweep-remediation", { intervals: ["1 hour"] },
"remediationVerifyWorkflow", []);
await ensureSchedule(client, "sweep-sla", { intervals: ["1 hour"] },
"slaSweepWorkflow", []);
await ensureSchedule(client, "sweep-webhooks", { intervals: ["5 minutes"] },
"webhookRetrySweepWorkflow", []);
await ensureSchedule(client, "radar-reg", { cronExpressions: ["0 6 * * *"] },
"regRadarWorkflow", []);
await ensureSchedule(client, "sweep-negative-learning", { cronExpressions: ["0 7 * * 1"] },
"negativeLearningSweepWorkflow", []);
await ensureSchedule(client, "sweep-exec-reports", { cronExpressions: ["30 6 1 * *"] },
"execReportSweepWorkflow", []);

await client.connection.close();
await prisma.$disconnect();
}

main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/temporal.ts
```typescript
import { Client } from "@temporalio/client";

let clientPromise: Promise<Client> | null = null;

export function getTemporalClient(): Promise<Client> {
if (!clientPromise)
clientPromise = Client.connect({ address: process.env.TEMPORAL_ADDRESS ?? "localhost:7233" });
return clientPromise;
}

/** Start by string type (no workflow-code import needed); idempotent. */
export async function startWorkflowSafe(
workflowType: string, args: unknown[], workflowId: string) {
const client = await getTemporalClient();
try {
return await client.workflow.start(workflowType, {
args, workflowId, taskQueue: "compliance" });
} catch (e: any) {
if (String(e).includes("already started") ||
String(e).includes("WorkflowExecutionAlreadyStarted")) return null;
console.warn(`[temporal] start ${workflowType}/${workflowId} failed: ${String(e).slice(0, 150)}`);
return null; // schedules/sweeps are the backstop — never fail the request path
}
}

/** Signal by name — used by the ethics bridge to stop SLA timers. */
export async function signalWorkflowSafe(workflowId: string, signalName: string,
arg?: unknown) {
const client = await getTemporalClient();
try { await client.workflow.getHandle(workflowId).signal(signalName, arg); }
catch { /* not running / already finished — fine */ }
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/ethics-events.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE of the batch-7 version — now starts/stops the deterministic
// ethics SLA workflows via the Temporal client (Artifact C's event bridge,
// consolidated). Case content NEVER crosses — metadata only.
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import { startWorkflowSafe, signalWorkflowSafe } from "../temporal";

export async function ethicsEventsRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
app.post("/internal/ethics-events", async (req, reply) => {
const secret = process.env.ETHICS_EVENT_HMAC_SECRET;
if (!secret) return reply.code(503).send({ error: "bridge not configured" });

const sig = req.headers["x-ethics-signature"] as string | undefined;
const raw = JSON.stringify(req.body ?? {});
const expected = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(raw).digest("hex");
if (!sig || !timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(expected),
Buffer.from(sig.replace(/^sha256=/, ""))))
return reply.code(401).send({ error: "bad signature" });

const { event, orgId, caseId, at } = req.body as {
event: string; orgId: string; caseId: string; at: string };

// Brain telemetry (metadata only)
emit({
type: event === "CASE_CREATED" ? "ethics.sla.fired" : "ethics.heartbeat",
source: "ethics", orgId,
payload: { caseId, event },
});

switch (event) {
case "CASE_CREATED":
// EU 2019/1937 Art. 9: acknowledge within 7 days — clock starts.
await startWorkflowSafe("ethicsAckSlaWorkflow",
[{ orgId, caseId, submittedAt: at }], `sla-ethics-ack-${caseId}`);
break;
case "CASE_ACKNOWLEDGED":
await signalWorkflowSafe(`sla-ethics-ack-${caseId}`, "completed", at);
// Art. 9(1)(f): feedback within 3 months of acknowledgment.
await startWorkflowSafe("ethicsFeedbackSlaWorkflow",
[{ orgId, caseId, acknowledgedAt: at }], `sla-ethics-feedback-${caseId}`);
break;
case "CASE_CLOSED":
await signalWorkflowSafe(`sla-ethics-ack-${caseId}`, "completed", at);
await signalWorkflowSafe(`sla-ethics-feedback-${caseId}`, "completed", at);
break;
case "HEARTBEAT":
break; // already emitted above
}
return { ok: true };
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/handlers/controls.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE of the batch-7 version — the obligations route now PERSISTS
// obligations (computeAndScheduleNotifications) and starts one SLA clock
// per obligation with deterministic workflowIds.
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { prisma, rlsClient } from "@db/client";
import { controlTestState, controlQualityScore,
computeAndScheduleNotifications } from "@core";
import { runCheckInstrumented } from "@skills";
import { startWorkflowSafe } from "../temporal";

export async function controlHandlers(app: FastifyInstance) {

// ── the ControlDetail shape apps/web types.ts declares ──
app.get("/v1/controls/:slug", async (req) => {
const { slug } = req.params as { slug: string };
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const control = await tx.control.findUnique({
where: { orgId_slug: { orgId: req.org!.id, slug } },
include: {
owner: { include: { user: true } },
controlMappings: {
where: { status: "ACCEPTED" },
include: { requirement: { include: { framework: true } } },
},
tests: { orderBy: { lastRunAt: "desc" } },
evidences: { orderBy: { sequence: "desc" }, take: 50,
include: { quality: true, testRun: true } },
},
});
if (!control) throw { statusCode: 404, message: "Control not found" };

const runs = await tx.testRun.findMany({
where: { controlId: control.id },
orderBy: { startedAt: "desc" }, take: 30,
});
const quality = await controlQualityScore(control.id);
const grade = quality >= 90 ? "A" : quality >= 75 ? "B"
: quality >= 60 ? "C" : quality >= 40 ? "D" : "F";

return {
id: control.id, slug: control.slug, name: control.name,
description: control.description,
implementationStatus: control.implementationStatus,
owner: control.owner ? { name: control.owner.user.name } : null,
quality: control.evidences.length ? { score: quality, grade } : null,
testState: controlTestState(runs, 90),
requirements: control.controlMappings.map((m) => ({
framework: m.requirement.framework.code, code: m.requirement.code,
title: m.requirement.title,
confidence: Number(m.rationale?.match(/confidence ([\d.]+)/)?.[1] ?? 0.9),
verified: m.status === "ACCEPTED",
})),
evidences: control.evidences.map((e) => ({
id: e.id, sequence: e.sequence, collectedAt: e.collectedAt.toISOString(),
title: e.title,
status: (e.testRun?.status ?? "INCONCLUSIVE") as
"PASS" | "FAIL" | "ERROR" | "INCONCLUSIVE",
contentHash: e.contentHash, chainHash: e.chainHash,
testRun: e.testRun ? { id: e.testRun.id, summary: e.testRun.summary,
findings: e.testRun.findings } : null,
})),
tests: control.tests.map((t) => ({
id: t.id, checkId: (t.check as { checkId?: string }).checkId ?? t.id,
name: t.name, schedule: t.schedule, enabled: t.enabled,
lastRunAt: t.lastRunAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
})),
history: runs.slice(0, 20).map((r) => ({
at: r.startedAt.toISOString(), label: `Test ${r.status}`,
detail: r.summary ?? undefined,
})),
};
});
});

// ── dashboard aggregates ──
app.get("/v1/quality/distribution", async (req) =>
rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const rows = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ grade: string; n: bigint }>>`
SELECT eq.grade AS grade, COUNT(*) AS n
FROM "EvidenceQuality" eq
JOIN "Evidence" e ON e.id = eq."evidenceId"
WHERE e."orgId" = ${req.org!.id}
GROUP BY 1`;
return Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.grade, Number(r.n)]));
}));

app.get("/v1/tasks", async (req) => {
const url = new URL(req.url, "http://x");
if (url.searchParams.get("summary") !== "1") {
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => tx.task.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "BLOCKED"] } },
orderBy: [{ priority: "desc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }], take: 100 }));
}
return rlsClient().asOrg(req.org!.id, async (tx) => {
const tasks = await tx.task.findMany({
where: { status: { in: ["OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "BLOCKED"] } },
select: { priority: true, dueAt: true } });
const byPriority: Record<string, number> = {};
for (const t of tasks) byPriority[t.priority] = (byPriority[t.priority] ?? 0) + 1;
return { open: tasks.length,
overdue: tasks.filter((t) => t.dueAt && t.dueAt < new Date()).length,
byPriority };
});
});

// ── run-check-now for the UI ──
app.post("/v1/checks/:checkId/run", async (req) => {
const { checkId } = req.params as { checkId: string };
const test = await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({
where: { orgId: req.org!.id, id: { startsWith: `${req.org!.id}:${checkId}` } } })
?? await prisma.controlTest.findFirst({
where: { orgId: req.org!.id, id: checkId } });
if (!test) throw { statusCode: 404, message: "Check not found" };
const result = await runCheckInstrumented({ orgId: req.org!.id, testId: test.id });
return { runId: result.run.id, status: result.run.status,
evidenceId: result.evidence?.id ?? null };
});

// ── incident obligations: persist + start statutory clocks ──
app.post("/v1/incidents/:id/obligations", async (req) => {
const { id } = req.params as { id: string };
const inc = await prisma.incident.findFirstOrThrow({
where: { id, orgId: req.org!.id } });

const obligations = await computeAndScheduleNotifications(id);
const notifications = await prisma.incidentNotification.findMany({
where: { incidentId: id, status: "PENDING" } });

// One SLA timer per obligation — deterministic ids make re-POSTs safe
for (const n of notifications) {
if (!n.statutoryDeadlineAt) continue;
await startWorkflowSafe("slaTimerWorkflow", [{
kind: "INCIDENT_NOTIFICATION", orgId: inc.orgId,
subjectId: `${id}:${n.authority}`,
startAt: inc.detectedAt.toISOString(),
deadlineAt: n.statutoryDeadlineAt.toISOString(),
milestones: [{ atPct: 50, action: "WARN_LEGAL" },
{ atPct: 100, action: "ESCALATE_CISO" }],
}], `sla-incident-${n.id}`);
}
return { obligations: obligations.length,
clocksStarted: notifications.filter((n) => n.statutoryDeadlineAt).length,
obligations };
});
}
```

### FILE: apps/ethics/prisma/schema.prisma
```prisma
// ISOLATED ethics database — separate deployment, separate KMS key,
// no shared identity. CONSOLIDATION FIX: OrgRef added (slug→orgId routing
// + per-org ethics KMS key) — this DB cannot join the main DB.
generator client { provider = "prisma-client-js" }
datasource db {
provider = "postgresql"
url = env("ETHICS_DATABASE_URL")
}

enum ReportCategory {
FRAUD ACCOUNTING HARASSMENT DISCRIMINATION CONFLICT_OF_INTEREST
SAFETY REGULATORY DATA_PRIVACY RETALIATION OTHER
}
enum CaseStatus { INTAKE TRIAGE INVESTIGATING AWAITING_REPORTER RESOLVED CLOSED }

/// Org routing reference, synced from the main DB (id = Organization.id).
model OrgRef {
id String @id // main-DB org id
slug String @unique
ethicsKeyId String? // per-org ethics KMS key; null → platform default
}

model EthicsReport {
id String @id @default(cuid())
orgId String // routing key ONLY — no identity FK, ever
reportTokenHash String @unique // argon2id of reporter passphrase
category ReportCategory
encryptedContent String // @ethics-crypto Envelope JSON
submittedAt DateTime @default(now())
lastReporterAccessAt DateTime?
slaAckDueAt DateTime // EU directive: acknowledge ≤7 days
case Case?
}

model ReportMessage {
id String @id @default(cuid())
reportId String
encryptedBody String
fromRole String // REPORTER | INVESTIGATOR
createdAt DateTime @default(now())
readByReporterAt DateTime?
}

model Case {
id String @id @default(cuid())
orgId String // routing key only
reportId String? @unique
source String @default("HOTLINE")
status CaseStatus @default(INTAKE)
severity String?
assigneeRef String? // opaque investigator reference
slaDueAt DateTime?
createdAt DateTime @default(now())
closedAt DateTime?
resolutionSummaryEnc String? // sealed
auditLogs CaseAuditLog[]
}

model CaseAuditLog {
id String @id @default(cuid())
caseId String
actorRef String // opaque — sha256(investigator token) prefix
action String // VIEWED | DECRYPTED | STATUS_CHANGED | MESSAGE_SENT | …
at DateTime @default(now())
details Json?
case Case @relation(fields: [caseId], references: [id])
}
```

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/diceware.ts
```typescript
/** ~100-word list — 8 words ≈ 53 bits of entropy, argon2id-hashed at rest. */
const WORDS = [
"harbor", "quantum", "lens", "drift", "echo", "nimbus", "fern", "slate",
"amber", "brisk", "cedar", "dune", "ember", "flint", "glade", "husk",
"iris", "jade", "kelp", "lark", "moss", "onyx", "prism", "quill",
"reef", "sage", "thorn", "umbra", "veldt", "wick", "yarn", "zephyr",
"anchor", "birch", "clover", "delta", "fjord", "gorge", "haven", "inode",
"juniper", "kite", "lantern", "marble", "nectar", "opal", "parchment", "quarry",
"ridge", "summit", "timber", "vista", "willow", "yarrow", "basalt", "cobalt",
"dawn", "elm", "fable", "gable", "heron", "ivory", "cobweb", "kernel",
"loom", "mesa", "nova", "orbit", "pinecone", "quartz", "ravine", "spruce",
"tundra", "udder", "velvet", "walnut", "yonder", "zinc", "alpine", "boulder",
"cascade", "dovetail", "eclipse", "foxglove", "granite", "hollow", "inlet", "jasper",
"knoll", "lagoon", "mantle", "north", "obsidian", "petal", "quiet", "rustic",
"sequoia", "tallow", "verge", "wren",
];

export function generateDiceware(words = 8): string {
const picks: string[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < words; i++)
picks.push(WORDS[Math.floor(Math.random() * WORDS.length)]);
return picks.join("-");
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/crypto.ts
```typescript
import { KMSClient } from "@aws-sdk/client-kms";
import { seal, unseal, type Envelope } from "@ethics-crypto";
import { prisma } from "./db";

const kms = new KMSClient({});

/** Per-org ethics key when provisioned; platform ethics key otherwise. */
export async function orgKey(orgId: string): Promise<string> {
const ref = await prisma.orgRef.findUnique({ where: { id: orgId } });
return ref?.ethicsKeyId ?? process.env.AWS_KMS_ETHICS_KEY_ID!;
}

export async function sealFor(orgId: string, plaintext: string): Promise<string> {
return JSON.stringify(await seal(kms, await orgKey(orgId), plaintext));
}

export async function unsealFor(orgId: string, sealed: string): Promise<string> {
return unseal(kms, JSON.parse(sealed) as Envelope);
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/bridge.ts
```typescript
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";

/** Metadata-only event POST to the main API (HMAC-authenticated).
* Case content NEVER crosses the isolation boundary. */
export async function bridge(event: "CASE_CREATED" | "CASE_ACKNOWLEDGED"
| "CASE_CLOSED" | "HEARTBEAT", orgId: string, caseId: string): Promise<void> {
const url = process.env.ETHICS_BRIDGE_URL;
const secret = process.env.ETHICS_EVENT_HMAC_SECRET;
if (!url || !secret) {
console.warn("[ethics:bridge] not configured — event dropped (metadata only)");
return;
}
const body = JSON.stringify({ event, orgId, caseId, at: new Date().toISOString() });
const signature = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(body).digest("hex");
await fetch(url, { method: "POST", body,
headers: { "content-type": "application/json",
"x-ethics-signature": `sha256=${signature}` } })
.catch(() => {}); // fire-and-forget; SLA sweeps are the backstop
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/routes/report.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import { hash as argonHash, verify as argonVerify } from "@node-rs/argon2";
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { prisma } from "../db";
import { generateDiceware } from "../diceware";
import { sealFor, unsealFor } from "../crypto";
import { bridge } from "../bridge";

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const addDays = (d: Date, n: number) => new Date(d.getTime() + n * DAY);

// EVERY route here runs with request logging DISABLED (see index.ts) —
// no IPs, no user agents, no timing persisted. Rate limiting is the
// platform WAF's job (in-memory only would also be acceptable).
const reportSchema = z.object({
orgSlug: z.string().min(2).max(60),
category: z.enum(["FRAUD", "ACCOUNTING", "HARASSMENT", "DISCRIMINATION",
"CONFLICT_OF_INTEREST", "SAFETY", "REGULATORY", "DATA_PRIVACY",
"RETALIATION", "OTHER"]),
description: z.string().min(10).max(20_000),
});

export async function reportRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {

// ── anonymous submission ──
app.post("/v1/report", async (req, reply) => {
const parsed = reportSchema.safeParse(req.body);
if (!parsed.success) return reply.code(400).send(); // bare — no detail
const { orgSlug, category, description } = parsed.data;

const org = await prisma.orgRef.findUnique({ where: { slug: orgSlug } });
if (!org) return reply.code(404).send();

// Reporter passphrase — generated server-side, shown ONCE
const passphrase = generateDiceware(8);
const report = await prisma.ethicsReport.create({
data: {
orgId: org.id,
reportTokenHash: await argonHash(passphrase),
category: category as never,
encryptedContent: await sealFor(org.id, description),
slaAckDueAt: addDays(new Date(), 7),
},
});
const case_ = await prisma.case.create({
data: { orgId: org.id, reportId: report.id, status: "INTAKE",
slaDueAt: addDays(new Date(), 7) } });

await bridge("CASE_CREATED", org.id, case_.id);

return reply.code(201).send({
reportId: report.id,
passphrase, // last time this exists in plaintext anywhere
inboxUrl: `/v1/inbox/${report.id}`,
warning: "Write this passphrase down now. It cannot be recovered. " +
"We have no way to identify you, and cannot restore access without it.",
});
});

// ── anonymous two-way inbox ──
app.post("/v1/inbox/:reportId/messages", async (req, reply) => {
const { passphrase, body } = (req.body ?? {}) as { passphrase?: string; body?: string };
const report = await prisma.ethicsReport.findUnique({
where: { id: (req.params as { reportId: string }).reportId } });
// bare 401 — never confirm existence
if (!report || !passphrase ||
!(await argonVerify(report.reportTokenHash, passphrase).catch(() => false)))
return reply.code(401).send();

await prisma.reportMessage.create({
data: { reportId: report.id,
encryptedBody: await sealFor(report.orgId, String(body ?? "").slice(0, 5000)),
fromRole: "REPORTER" } });
await prisma.ethicsReport.update({ where: { id: report.id },
data: { lastReporterAccessAt: new Date() } });
return reply.code(201).send();
});

app.get("/v1/inbox/:reportId", async (req, reply) => {
const passphrase = req.headers["x-passphrase"] as string | undefined;
const report = await prisma.ethicsReport.findUnique({
where: { id: (req.params as { reportId: string }).reportId } });
if (!report || !passphrase ||
!(await argonVerify(report.reportTokenHash, passphrase).catch(() => false)))
return reply.code(401).send();
const messages = await prisma.reportMessage.findMany({
where: { reportId: report.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } });
await prisma.ethicsReport.update({ where: { id: report.id },
data: { lastReporterAccessAt: new Date() } });
return {
messages: await Promise.all(messages.map(async (m) => ({
fromRole: m.fromRole, createdAt: m.createdAt,
body: m.fromRole === "REPORTER"
? "[your message]" // don't echo reporter text back over the wire twice
: await unsealFor(report.orgId, m.encryptedBody),
}))),
};
});

// ── investigator console (v1: env token; actorRef is opaque) ──
async function investigator(req: { headers: Record<string, unknown> }, reply: any) {
const token = req.headers["x-ethics-token"] as string | undefined;
const expected = process.env.ETHICS_INVESTIGATOR_TOKEN;
if (!expected || !token ||
!timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(expected), Buffer.from(token)))
return reply.code(401).send();
return createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
}

app.get("/v1/cases", async (req, reply) => {
const actor = await investigator(req, reply);
if (!actor) return;
const cases = await prisma.case.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 100,
include: { report: true } });
// LIST = metadata only; content decrypts per-case with an audit trail
return { cases: cases.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, orgId: c.orgId,
status: c.status, category: c.report?.category, createdAt: c.createdAt,
slaDueAt: c.slaDueAt, closedAt: c.closedAt })) };
});

app.get("/v1/cases/:id", async (req, reply) => {
const actor = await investigator(req, reply);
if (!actor) return;
const case_ = await prisma.case.findUnique({
where: { id: (req.params as { id: string }).id },
include: { report: true } });
if (!case_?.report) return reply.code(404).send();

const content = await unsealFor(case_.orgId, case_.report.encryptedContent);
await prisma.caseAuditLog.create({ data: {
caseId: case_.id, actorRef: actor, action: "DECRYPTED" } });
return { id: case_.id, status: case_.status, category: case_.report.category,
submittedAt: case_.report.submittedAt, slaAckDueAt: case_.report.slaAckDueAt,
content };
});

app.post("/v1/cases/:id/status", async (req, reply) => {
const actor = await investigator(req, reply);
if (!actor) return;
const { status } = (req.body ?? {}) as { status?: string };
const allowed = ["INTAKE", "TRIAGE", "INVESTIGATING", "AWAITING_REPORTER",
"RESOLVED", "CLOSED"];
if (!status || !allowed.includes(status)) return reply.code(400).send();
const case_ = await prisma.case.findUnique({
where: { id: (req.params as { id: string }).id } });
if (!case_) return reply.code(404).send();

await prisma.case.update({ where: { id: case_.id },
data: { status: status as never,
...(status === "CLOSED" ? { closedAt: new Date() } : {}) } });
await prisma.caseAuditLog.create({ data: {
caseId: case_.id, actorRef: actor, action: "STATUS_CHANGED",
details: { from: case_.status, to: status } } });

if (status === "TRIAGE") await bridge("CASE_ACKNOWLEDGED", case_.orgId, case_.id);
if (status === "CLOSED") await bridge("CASE_CLOSED", case_.orgId, case_.id);
return { ok: true };
});

app.post("/v1/cases/:id/messages", async (req, reply) => {
const actor = await investigator(req, reply);
if (!actor) return;
const { body } = (req.body ?? {}) as { body?: string };
const case_ = await prisma.case.findUnique({
where: { id: (req.params as { id: string }).id }, include: { report: true } });
if (!case_?.report) return reply.code(404).send();
await prisma.reportMessage.create({ data: {
reportId: case_.report.id,
encryptedBody: await sealFor(case_.orgId, String(body ?? "").slice(0, 5000)),
fromRole: "INVESTIGATOR" } });
await prisma.caseAuditLog.create({ data: {
caseId: case_.id, actorRef: actor, action: "MESSAGE_SENT" } });
return reply.code(201).send();
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/shred.ts
```typescript
/**
* Scheduled crypto-shredder (cron/ECS task: `pnpm --filter @ethics shred`).
* Dual-gated retention: statutory period AND backup expiry (see
* @ethics-crypto retention.ts). Integrity gate: ciphertext that doesn't
* match its recorded hash is TAMPERING — blocked, not shredded.
*/
import { prisma } from "./db";
import { runShredder, DEFAULT_RETENTION, computeShredDate,
type Shreddable } from "@ethics-crypto";

async function main() {
const result = await runShredder({
async fetchDue(now, cfg) {
const cases = await prisma.case.findMany({
where: { status: "CLOSED", closedAt: { not: null } },
include: { report: true } });
const due = cases.filter((c) =>
computeShredDate(cfg, c.closedAt!) <= now);
const out: { caseId: string; closedAt: Date;
reports: Shreddable[]; messages: Shreddable[] }[] = [];
for (const c of due) {
const messages = c.report
? await prisma.reportMessage.findMany({ where: { reportId: c.report.id } })
: [];
out.push({
caseId: c.id, closedAt: c.closedAt!,
reports: c.report ? [{ id: c.report.id,
encryptedContent: c.report.encryptedContent }] : [],
messages: messages.map((m) => ({
id: m.id, encryptedContent: m.encryptedBody })),
});
}
return out;
},
async persistReports(outcomes) {
for (const o of outcomes) if (o.newContent)
await prisma.ethicsReport.update({ where: { id: o.id },
data: { encryptedContent: o.newContent } });
},
async persistMessages(outcomes) {
for (const o of outcomes) if (o.newContent)
await prisma.reportMessage.update({ where: { id: o.id },
data: { encryptedBody: o.newContent } });
},
async logRun(summary) {
console.log(`[shredder] cases=${summary.cases} shredded=${summary.shredded} ` +
`blocked=${summary.blocked} (retention ` +
`${DEFAULT_RETENTION.statutoryRetentionDays}d statutory / ` +
`${DEFAULT_RETENTION.backupRetentionDays}d backup)`);
},
}, DEFAULT_RETENTION);
console.log("[shredder] done", result);
}

main().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/ethics/src/index.ts
```typescript
/**
* The ethics service — ISOLATED by construction:
* own database · own KMS key · no shared cookies · NO request logging ·
* metadata-only bridge to the main platform.
* Deploy behind its own domain (ethics.example.com) with NO access logs
* and NO CDN/analytics in front of it.
*/
import Fastify from "fastify";
import { reportRoutes } from "./routes/report";
import { bridge } from "./bridge";
import { prisma } from "./db";

const app = Fastify({
logger: false, // no log sink at all
disableRequestLogging: true, // belt + suspenders
trustProxy: false,
});

app.get("/healthz", async () => ({ ok: true, service: "ethics" }));
await app.register(reportRoutes);

// Metadata-only heartbeat every 15 min — keeps the brain's ethics-service
// health score alive even when the hotline is idle (silence detector).
setInterval(() => { void bridge("HEARTBEAT", "", ""); }, 15 * 60_000);

const port = Number(process.env.ETHICS_PORT ?? 3002);
app.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" }).then(() =>
console.log(`[ethics] anonymous hotline on :${port} (request logging disabled)`));

for (const sig of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"] as const) {
process.on(sig, async () => {
await app.close().catch(() => {});
await prisma.$disconnect().catch(() => {});
process.exit(0);
});
}
```

````
**Reply "next" → Batch 9: `apps/web`** — the three built screens re-emitted with real integration fixes (dashboard's hooks-in-map lint fix, API paths aligned to batch-7 handlers, SSE consumer against `/v1/brain/stream`), plus `next.config`, package.json, and the remaining lib/components (~20 files). Then Batch 10 closes with `infra/`, `sdks/`, and `tests/`.

**Batch 9 of 10 — `apps/web` + four API integration fixes (28 files).** Consolidation fixes applied — these were real boot-breaking bugs found while wiring the UI to the API: (1) **duplicate-route crash**: catalog REST and the session handlers both register `GET /v1/controls/:slug`, `GET /v1/tasks`, `POST /v1/checks/:checkId/run`, `GET /v1/questionnaires/:id/drafts` — Fastify throws at boot. Fixed with a new **`eitherAuth`** middleware (API key w/ scope **or** session; key auth also resolves `req.org` so session-shaped handlers serve both surfaces), an encapsulated handler plugin, and a skip-set in catalog mounting; (2) **param-name mapping bug**: rest path `{slug}` vs tool input `controlSlug` (e.g. `verify_chain_proof`) failed zod parsing — catalogRest now maps positionally; (3) **OpenAPI generator**: `zodToJsonSchema` was passed a raw shape — now `z.object(tool.input)`; (4) **UI alignment**: dashboard's hooks-in-map replaced with four explicit calls (the promised lint fix), the run-check watcher keys on `controlSlug` brain events (the API route returns no `flowId`), SSE consumer matches the API's `event: message` frames, and `AppShell` gets its required `"use client"`.

Save as `batch9.txt` → `python3 split.py batch9.txt`.

````
### FILE: apps/api/src/middleware/eitherAuth.ts
```typescript
import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from "fastify";
import { resolveApiKey, hasScope, problemDetail } from "../keyCrypto";
import { verifySession } from "./session";
import { prisma } from "@db/client";

const bearerKey = (h: unknown) =>
typeof h === "string" && h.startsWith("Bearer cpk_live_") ? h.slice(7) : undefined;

/**
* EITHER auth: API key (scope-checked) OR browser session. Key auth also
* resolves req.org from the key's organization so session-shaped handlers
* (controls, questionnaire review) serve both surfaces with one shape.
*/
export function eitherAuth(scope: string | null) {
return async (req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {
const key = (req.headers["x-api-key"] as string | undefined) ?? bearerKey(req.headers["authorization"]);

if (key) {
const record = await resolveApiKey(key);
if (!record)
return reply.code(401).send(problemDetail(401, "Invalid or revoked API key"));
if (scope && !hasScope(record.scopes, scope)) {
void import("@brain/emit").then(({ emit }) => emit({
type: "mcp.tool.denied", source: "mcp", orgId: record.orgId,
payload: { scope, keyId: record.id } }));
return reply.code(403).send(problemDetail(403, "Missing scope",
`This key lacks the required scope: ${scope}`,
{ "x-required-scope": scope }));
}
const { rateLimit } = await import("@mcp");
if (!(await rateLimit(`key:${record.id}`, record.rateLimitPerMin)))
return reply.code(429).send(problemDetail(429, "Rate limited",
`Limit: ${record.rateLimitPerMin}/min`));

req.apiKey = { orgId: record.orgId, id: record.id, scopes: record.scopes };
const org = await prisma.organization.findUnique({
where: { id: record.orgId },
select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true } });
if (org) req.org = org; // handlers read req.org — works for keys too
await prisma.apiKey.update({ where: { id: record.id },
data: { lastUsedAt: new Date() } }).catch(() => {});
return;
}
await verifySession(req, reply); // returns 401 reply (stops lifecycle) on failure
};
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/routes/catalogRest.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE — eitherAuth for browser sessions, skip-set for handler-owned
// paths (duplicate-route fix), positional path-param mapping ({slug} →
// input key controlSlug etc.).
import { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { CATALOG } from "@mcp";
import { BRAIN_TOOLS } from "@mcp/tools/brainTools";
import { eitherAuth } from "../middleware/eitherAuth";
import { checkEntitlement } from "../middleware/entitlement";

const ALL_TOOLS = [...CATALOG, ...BRAIN_TOOLS];

/** Paths owned by the session/eitherAuth handlers in apps/api/src/handlers —
* mounting them here too would crash Fastify (duplicate route). The
* handlers serve BOTH session and API-key callers (eitherAuth), so these
* tools remain reachable; their response shapes are the handler shapes
* (documented superset of the raw tool returns). */
const HANDLER_OWNED = new Set([
"get_control", "list_tasks", "run_check_now", "list_questionnaire_drafts",
]);

/** Maps rest path params onto the tool's input keys: direct name match
* first, else the first unfilled input key ({slug} → controlSlug). */
function buildArgs(tool: { input: Record<string, unknown> },
req: { params: unknown; query: unknown; body: unknown }) {
const args: Record<string, unknown> = {
...((req.query as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {}),
...((req.body as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {}),
};
for (const [k, v] of Object.entries((req.params as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {})) {
if (k in tool.input) { args[k] = v; continue; }
const firstMissing = Object.keys(tool.input).find((ik) => !(ik in args));
if (firstMissing) args[firstMissing] = v;
}
return args;
}

/** One catalog, two surfaces: REST mounts the same ToolDefs the MCP server
* serves — single handler, single scope declaration, zero drift. */
export async function mountCatalogRoutes(app: FastifyInstance) {
let mounted = 0;
for (const tool of ALL_TOOLS) {
if (!tool.rest || HANDLER_OWNED.has(tool.name)) continue;
const { method, path } = tool.rest;
const fastifyPath = path.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, ":$1");

app.route({
method, url: fastifyPath,
preHandler: [checkEntitlement(tool.name), eitherAuth(tool.scope)],
handler: async (req, reply) => {
const args = buildArgs(tool, req);
try {
const orgId = req.apiKey?.orgId ?? req.org?.id;
if (!orgId) return reply.code(401).send(
{ title: "No org context", status: 401 });
const result = await tool.handler(args, {
orgId, keyId: req.apiKey?.id ?? "session" });
reply.code(method === "POST" ? 201 : 200).send(result ?? { ok: true });
} catch (e: any) {
reply.code(e.statusCode ?? 500).send({
title: e.message?.slice(0, 200) ?? "Handler error", status: 500 });
}
},
});
mounted++;
}
console.log(`[api] catalog REST: ${mounted} tools mounted ` +
`(${HANDLER_OWNED.size} handler-owned paths skipped)`);

app.get("/v1/openapi.json", async () => {
const { generateOpenApi } = await import("../openapi/generate");
return generateOpenApi();
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/index.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE — global session preHandler REMOVED (it 401'd API-key clients on
// catalog paths). Every surface now owns its auth: handlers via an
// encapsulated eitherAuth plugin; catalog per-route eitherAuth; brain/
// review/stream per-route verifySession; auditor/trust/internal own chains.
import Fastify from "fastify";
import cookie from "@fastify/cookie";
import cors from "@fastify/cors";
import { mountCatalogRoutes } from "./routes/catalogRest";
import { brainRoutes } from "./routes/brain";
import { brainStreamRoutes } from "./routes/brainStream";
import { auditorRoutes, auditorGdprRoutes } from "./routes/auditor";
import { trustRoutes } from "./routes/trust";
import { reviewRoutes } from "./routes/review";
import { ethicsEventsRoutes } from "./routes/ethics-events";
import { controlHandlers } from "./handlers/controls";
import { reviewHandlers } from "./handlers/questionnaireReview";
import { eitherAuth } from "./middleware/eitherAuth";
import { emit } from "@brain/emit";
import "@skills"; // warm skill + connector registries

const app = Fastify({ logger: { level: "warn" }, bodyLimit: 12 * 1024 * 1024 });

await app.register(cookie);
await app.register(cors, {
origin: (origin, cb) =>
cb(null, !origin || origin.includes(process.env.APP_DOMAIN ?? "localhost:3000")),
credentials: true,
});

app.get("/healthz", async () => ({ ok: true, service: "api", at: new Date().toISOString() }));

// ── screen handlers: session OR API key (encapsulated guard) ─────
await app.register(async (instance) => {
instance.addHook("preHandler", eitherAuth(null));
await controlHandlers(instance);
await reviewHandlers(instance);
});

// ── catalog REST (entitlement → scope → rate, per route) ─────────
await app.register(mountCatalogRoutes);

// ── brain (session per-route) + SSE + public status ──────────────
await app.register(brainRoutes);
await app.register(brainStreamRoutes);

// ── portals & internal bridges (own auth chains) ─────────────────
await app.register(auditorRoutes);
await app.register(auditorGdprRoutes);
await app.register(trustRoutes);
await app.register(reviewRoutes);
await app.register(ethicsEventsRoutes);

// request-failure telemetry → brain
app.addHook("onError", async (req, reply, err) => {
emit({ type: "api.request.failed", source: req.url.startsWith("/mcp") ? "mcp" : "api",
orgId: req.org?.id, severityOverride: "WARN",
payload: { url: req.url, status: reply.statusCode,
error: String(err.message).slice(0, 200) } });
});

const port = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);
await app.listen({ port, host: "0.0.0.0" });
console.log(`API on :${port}`);

for (const sig of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"] as const) {
process.on(sig, async () => { await app.close(); process.exit(0); });
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/api/src/openapi/generate.ts
```typescript
// OVERRIDE — zodToJsonSchema fix: wrap the raw shape in z.object().
import { z } from "zod";
import { zodToJsonSchema } from "zod-to-json-schema";
import { CATALOG } from "@mcp";
import { BRAIN_TOOLS } from "@mcp/tools/brainTools";
import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const RATE_BY_TIER: Record<number, string> = { 1: "120/min", 2: "30/min", 3: "6/min" };
const ALL_TOOLS = [...CATALOG, ...BRAIN_TOOLS];

export function generateOpenApi(): object {
const paths: Record<string, any> = {};

for (const tool of ALL_TOOLS) {
if (!tool.rest) continue;
const { method, path, summaryTag } = tool.rest;
const jsonSchema = zodToJsonSchema(z.object(tool.input), {
target: "openApi3" }) as { properties?: Record<string, any> };
const props = (jsonSchema.properties ?? {}) as Record<string, any>;

const pathParams = [...path.matchAll(/\{(\w+)\}/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
const paramSchemas = pickKeys(props, pathParams);
const bodySchema = omitKeys(props, pathParams);

paths[path] ??= {};
paths[path][method.toLowerCase()] = {
operationId: tool.name,
summary: tool.description.slice(0, 120),
tags: [summaryTag],
security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
"x-required-scope": tool.scope,
"x-rate-limit": RATE_BY_TIER[tool.tier],
"x-tier": tool.tier,
parameters: pathParams.map((p) => ({
name: p, in: "path", required: true, schema: paramSchemas[p] })),
...(Object.keys(bodySchema).length && method === "POST"
? { requestBody: { required: true, content: { "application/json":
{ schema: { type: "object", properties: bodySchema } } } } } : {}),
responses: {
...(tool.readOnly ? { 200: success() } : { 201: success() }),
400: problem("Validation error"),
401: problem("Invalid or revoked API key"),
402: problem("Tier or quota gate"),
403: problem("Missing scope", `x-required-scope: ${tool.scope}`),
404: problem("Resource not found"),
429: { description: "Rate limited",
headers: { "Retry-After": { schema: { type: "integer" } } },
content: { "application/problem+json": { schema:
{ $ref: "#/components/schemas/Problem" } } } },
},
};
}

return {
openapi: "3.1.0",
info: { title: "Compliance Platform API", version: "1.0.0",
description: "Generated from the tool catalog — the same handlers serve MCP. " +
"Scopes are identical across surfaces. The four handler-owned paths " +
"(get_control, list_tasks, run_check_now, list_questionnaire_drafts) " +
"also accept session cookies." },
servers: [{ url: process.env.API_PUBLIC_URL ?? "https://api.example.com" }],
security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
paths,
components: {
securitySchemes: { ApiKeyAuth: { type: "apiKey",
in: "header", name: "x-api-key" } },
schemas: { Problem: { type: "object", properties: {
type: { type: "string" }, title: { type: "string" },
status: { type: "integer" }, detail: { type: "string" },
"x-required-scope": { type: "string" } },
description: "RFC 9457 problem details" } },
},
};
}

const success = () => ({ description: "Success" });
const problem = (title: string, detail?: string) => ({
description: title, content: { "application/problem+json": {
schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Problem" },
example: { title, status: 400, detail } } } });

function pickKeys(o: Record<string, any>, keys: string[]) {
return Object.fromEntries(keys.filter((k) => k in o).map((k) => [k, o[k]]));
}
function omitKeys(o: Record<string, any>, keys: string[]) {
return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(o).filter(([k]) => !keys.includes(k)));
}

// CLI: write spec for drift CI (pnpm sdk:generate)
if (require.main === module) {
const spec = generateOpenApi();
const out = join(__dirname, "../../../sdks/openapi.json");
mkdirSync(join(out, ".."), { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2));
console.log(`wrote ${out} (${Object.keys((spec as any).paths).length} paths)`);
}
```
### END FILE

```typescript
export interface ReadinessResponse {

quality: { score: number; grade: string } | null;

requirements: ControlRequirementRef[];
evidences: EvidenceRow[];
```
