### FILE: apps/web/src/lib/api.ts
```typescript
import type { ChainProof, ControlDetail, QuestionnaireReviewData, ReadinessResponse } from "./types";

export const API_BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "";

export class ApiError extends Error {
constructor(public status: number, public body: unknown) { super(`API ${status}`); }
}

async function req<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
method,
headers: body ? { "content-type": "application/json" } : undefined,
body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
credentials: "include",
});
if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(res.status, await res.json().catch(() => ({})));
return (res.status === 204 ? undefined : await res.json()) as T;
}

export const api = {
get: <T>(p: string) => req<T>("GET", p),
post: <T>(p: string, b?: unknown) => req<T>("POST", p, b),
readiness: (fw: string) => req<ReadinessResponse>("GET", `/v1/readiness/${fw}`),
control: (slug: string) => req<ControlDetail>("GET", `/v1/controls/${slug}`),
chainProof: (slug: string) => req<ChainProof>("GET", `/v1/controls/${slug}/chain-proof`),
runCheck: (checkId: string) =>
req<{ runId: string; status: string; evidenceId: string | null }>(
"POST", `/v1/checks/${checkId}/run`),
tasks: () => req<{ open: number; overdue: number; byPriority: Record<string, number> }>(
"GET", "/v1/tasks?summary=1"),
qualityDist: () => req<Record<string, number>>("GET", "/v1/quality/distribution"),
regChanges: () => req<{ id: string; summary: string; effectiveAt: string | null }[]>(
"GET", "/v1/regulatory-changes?limit=3"),
reviewData: (id: string) => req<QuestionnaireReviewData>(
"GET", `/v1/questionnaires/${id}/drafts`),
approve: (id: string, qId: string, answer?: string) =>
req<{ status: string }>("POST",
`/v1/questionnaires/${id}/questions/${qId}/approve`, answer ? { answer } : {}),
reject: (id: string, qId: string, code: string, note?: string) =>
req<{ status: string }>("POST",
`/v1/questionnaires/${id}/questions/${qId}/reject`, { code, note }),
bulkApprove: (id: string, ids: string[]) =>
req<{ approved: number }>("POST", `/v1/questionnaires/${id}/bulk-approve`, { ids }),
};
```

### FILE: apps/web/src/lib/useApi.ts
```typescript
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "./api";

export function useApi<T>(path: string | null) {
const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!!path);
const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);
useEffect(() => {
if (!path) return;
let alive = true;
setLoading(true);
api.get<T>(path)
.then((d) => { if (alive) { setData(d); setError(null); } })
.catch((e) => { if (alive) setError(e); })
.finally(() => { if (alive) setLoading(false); });
return () => { alive = false; };
}, [path, nonce]);
const refresh = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []);
return { data, error, loading, refresh };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/web/src/lib/useBrainStream.ts
```typescript
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { API_BASE } from "./api";
import type { BrainStreamEvent } from "./types";

/** Org-scoped live feed. The API frames events as `event: message`
* (batch-7 brainStream.ts) → onmessage fires. */
export function useBrainStream(max = 60) {
const [events, setEvents] = useState<BrainStreamEvent[]>([]);
const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
useEffect(() => {
const es = new EventSource(`${API_BASE}/v1/brain/stream`, { withCredentials: true });
es.onopen = () => setConnected(true);
es.onerror = () => setConnected(false);
es.onmessage = (m) => {
try { setEvents((p) => [JSON.parse(m.data) as BrainStreamEvent, ...p].slice(0, max)); }
catch { /* ignore malformed frame */ }
};
return () => es.close();
}, [max]);
return { events, connected };
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/web/src/lib/useHotkeys.ts
```typescript
import { useEffect } from "react";

/** Re-registers every render (no dep array) so closures are always fresh.
* Ignores keys typed into inputs/textareas/content-editables. */
export function useHotkeys(map: Record<string, (e: KeyboardEvent) => void>) {
useEffect(() => {
const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
if (t && (t.tagName === "INPUT" || t.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || t.isContentEditable)) return;
if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;
const fn = map[e.key.toLowerCase()];
if (fn) { e.preventDefault(); fn(e); }
};
window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
});
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: apps/web/src/lib/format.ts
```typescript
export const shortHash = (h: string) => `${h.slice(0, 8)}…${h.slice(-4)}`;
export const time = (iso: string) =>
new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
export const date = (iso: string) =>
new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString([], { month: "short", day: "numeric" });
export function relTime(iso: string) {
const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 1000);
if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`;
if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ago`;
if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ago`;
return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}d ago`;
}
```

```tsx
import { ReactNode, ButtonHTMLAttributes, HTMLAttributes } from "react";

const V = {
default: "bg-zinc-900 text-white hover:bg-zinc-700",
outline: "border border-border bg-white hover:bg-muted",
ghost: "hover:bg-muted",
danger: "bg-fail/10 text-fail border border-fail/30 hover:bg-fail/20",
success: "bg-pass/10 text-pass border border-pass/30 hover:bg-pass/20",
};
export function Button({ variant = "default", className = "", ...p }:
ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: keyof typeof V }) {
return <button {...p} className={`inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 text-[13px] font-medium transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none ${V[variant]} ${className}`} />;
}

export function Card({ className = "", ...p }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
return <div {...p} className={`rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface ${className}`} />;
}
export function CardTitle({ children, right }: { children: ReactNode; right?: ReactNode }) {
return <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">{children}{right}</div>;
}

export function Badge({ tone = "zinc", children }:
{ tone?: "pass" | "fail" | "warn" | "info" | "draft" | "zinc"; children: ReactNode }) {
const T = {
pass: "bg-pass/10 text-pass border-pass/30", fail: "bg-fail/10 text-fail border-fail/30",
warn: "bg-warn/10 text-warn border-warn/30", info: "bg-info/10 text-info border-info/30",
draft: "bg-draft/10 text-draft border-draft/30 border-dashed", zinc: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border",
}[tone];
return <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium tabular-nums ${T}`}>{children}</span>;
}

export function Tabs<T extends string>({ tabs, active, onChange }:
{ tabs: { id: T; label: string; count?: number }[]; active: T; onChange: (id: T) => void }) {
return (
<div className="flex gap-1 border-b border-border">
{tabs.map((t) => (
<button key={t.id} onClick={() => onChange(t.id)}
className={`-mb-px border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition ${active === t.id ? "border-zinc-900 text-zinc-900" : "border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-zinc-700"}`}>
{t.label}{t.count !== undefined && <span className="ml-1.5 rounded bg-muted px-1 text-[11px] tabular-nums">{t.count}</span>}
</button>
))}
</div>
);
}

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, width = "max-w-lg" }:
{ open: boolean; onClose: () => void; title: string; children: ReactNode; width?: string }) {
if (!open) return null;
return (
<div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30 p-4" onClick={onClose}>
<div className={`w-full ${width} rounded-lg border border-border bg-white shadow-xl`} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
<div className="border-b border-border px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold">{title}</div>
<div className="p-4">{children}</div>
</div>
</div>
);
}

export function Skeleton({ className = "h-4 w-full" }: { className?: string }) {
return <div className={`animate-pulse rounded bg-muted ${className}`} />;
}

export function Kbd({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
return <kbd className="rounded border border-border bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 font-sans text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">{children}</kbd>;
}
```

```tsx
export function ReadinessRing({ pct, size = 120, label }:
{ pct: number; size?: number; label?: string }) {
const r = size / 2 - 9, c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
const color = pct >= 80 ? "#16a34a" : pct >= 50 ? "#d97706" : "#dc2626";
return (
<div className="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center" style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
<svg width={size} height={size} className="-rotate-90">
<circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={r} stroke="hsl(var(--border))" strokeWidth="9" fill="none" />
<circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={r} stroke={color} strokeWidth="9" fill="none" strokeLinecap="round"
strokeDasharray={c} strokeDashoffset={c * (1 - pct / 100)} />
</svg>
<div className="absolute flex flex-col items-center">
<span className="text-2xl font-semibold tabular-nums">{pct}%</span>
{label && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>}
</div>
</div>
);
}
```

```tsx
import { ReactNode } from "react";
import { shortHash } from "../lib/format";
import type { EvidenceRow } from "../lib/types";

const STATUS_STYLE: Record<string, string> = {
PASS: "border-l-pass", FAIL: "border-l-fail",
ERROR: "border-l-zinc-400", INCONCLUSIVE: "border-l-warn",
};
const STATUS_ICON: Record<string, string> = { PASS: "✓", FAIL: "✗", ERROR: "!", INCONCLUSIVE: "~" };

export function ChainLink({ prevChainHash }: { prevChainHash: string }) {
return (
<div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-0.5 pl-5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
<span className="h-4 w-px bg-border" />
<span className="font-mono">sha256({shortHash(prevChainHash)} ‖ content) ▼</span>
</div>
);
}

export function ChainBlock({ e, expanded, onToggle, children }: {
e: EvidenceRow; expanded: boolean; onToggle: () => void; children?: ReactNode;
}) {
return (
<div className={`rounded-md border border-border border-l-4 bg-white ${STATUS_STYLE[e.status]}`}>
<button onClick={onToggle} className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-muted/50">
<span className={`font-mono text-xs ${e.status === "PASS" ? "text-pass" : e.status === "FAIL" ? "text-fail" : "text-warn"}`}>{STATUS_ICON[e.status]}</span>
<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">#{e.sequence}</span>
<span className="flex-1 truncate text-[13px]">{e.title}</span>
<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(e.collectedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
<span className="hidden font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground md:inline">content {shortHash(e.contentHash)} · chain {shortHash(e.chainHash)}</span>
<span className="text-muted-foreground">{expanded ? "▾" : "▸"}</span>
</button>
{expanded && <div className="border-t border-border px-3 py-2.5">{children}</div>}
</div>
);
}

/** Generic findings renderer — the check DSL's stored findings shape. */
export function Findings({ findings }: {
findings: NonNullable<EvidenceRow["testRun"]>["findings"] }) {
if (!findings) return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No findings recorded.</p>;
const items = findings.items?.slice(0, 10) ?? [];
return (
<div className="space-y-2">
<p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
{findings.eval?.matched ?? 0}/{findings.eval?.total ?? 0} items matched
{findings.failingCount ? <span className="text-fail"> · {findings.failingCount} failing</span> : null}
</p>
<div className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-border">
{items.map((item, i) => (
<div key={i} className={`grid gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 text-xs md:grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(160px,1fr))] ${i % 2 ? "bg-muted/40" : ""}`}>
{Object.entries(item).slice(0, 6).map(([k, v]) => (
<div key={k} className="min-w-0">
<span className="text-muted-foreground">{k}: </span>
<span className="truncate font-medium">{typeof v === "object" ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v)}</span>
</div>
))}
</div>
))}
</div>
{(findings.items?.length ?? 0) > 10 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">+ {findings.items!.length - 10} more…</p>}
</div>
);
}
```

### FILE: apps/web/src/components/CountdownClock.tsx
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function CountdownClock({ deadline }: { deadline: string }) {
const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
useEffect(() => {
const t = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
return () => clearInterval(t);
}, []);
const ms = new Date(deadline).getTime() - now;
if (ms <= 0) return <span className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fail">OVERDUE</span>;
const h = Math.floor(ms / 3.6e6), m = Math.floor((ms % 3.6e6) / 6e4), s = Math.floor((ms % 6e4) / 1000);
const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, 100 - (ms / 8.64e7) * 100)); // vs 24h window
return (
<div>
<div className={`font-mono text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums ${ms < 7.2e6 ? "text-fail animate-pulse" : ms < 1.728e7 ? "text-warn" : "text-zinc-700"}`}>
{h > 24 ? `${Math.floor(h / 24)}d ${h % 24}h` : `${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`}
</div>
<div className="mt-1 h-1 w-24 rounded bg-muted"><div className="h-1 rounded bg-current" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} /></div>
</div>
);
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

// CONSOLIDATION FIX: the hooks-in-map pattern replaced with four explicit
// useApi calls (order-stable, lint-clean — the promised fix).
import Link from "next/link";
import { useApi } from "../lib/useApi";
import { useBrainStream } from "../lib/useBrainStream";
import { time } from "../lib/format";
import { Badge, Card, CardTitle, Skeleton } from "../components/ui";
import { ReadinessRing } from "../components/ReadinessRing";
import type { ReadinessResponse } from "../lib/types";

const FRAMEWORKS = ["SOC2", "ISO27001", "HIPAA", "GDPR"] as const;
const FW_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { SOC2: "SOC 2", ISO27001: "ISO 27001", HIPAA: "HIPAA", GDPR: "GDPR" };

const EVENT_ICON: Record<string, string> = {
"control.test.passed": "✅", "control.test.failed": "❌", "evidence.created": "🧾",
"gap.detected": "⚠️", "gap.remediated": "✅", "policy.published": "📄",
"regchange.applied": "📡", "incident.created": "🚨", "vendor.review.due": "⌂",
};
const SEV_COLOR: Record<string, string> = {
INFO: "text-muted-foreground", WARN: "text-warn", ERROR: "text-fail", CRITICAL: "text-fail font-semibold" };

export default function DashboardPage() {
const soc2 = useApi<ReadinessResponse>("/v1/readiness/SOC2");
const iso = useApi<ReadinessResponse>("/v1/readiness/ISO27001");
const hipaa = useApi<ReadinessResponse>("/v1/readiness/HIPAA");
const gdpr = useApi<ReadinessResponse>("/v1/readiness/GDPR");
const readiness = [soc2, iso, hipaa, gdpr];

const tasks = useApi<{ open: number; overdue: number; byPriority: Record<string, number> }>("/v1/tasks?summary=1");
const quality = useApi<Record<string, number>>("/v1/quality/distribution");
const radar = useApi<{ id: string; summary: string; effectiveAt: string | null }[]>("/v1/regulatory-changes?limit=3");
const { events, connected } = useBrainStream();

const hour = new Date().getHours();
const greeting = hour < 12 ? "Good morning" : hour < 18 ? "Good afternoon" : "Good evening";
const anyData = readiness.some((r) => (r.data?.total ?? 0) > 0);
const allLoaded = readiness.every((r) => !r.loading);

if (!anyData && allLoaded) {
return (
<Card className="mx-auto max-w-lg p-10 text-center">
<h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">Welcome to your compliance program</h1>
<p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">No frameworks set up yet. Connect your stack and we&apos;ll build your controls.</p>
<Link href="/onboarding" className="mt-4 inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-md bg-zinc-900 px-4 text-[13px] font-medium text-white">Start onboarding →</Link>
</Card>
);
}

return (
<div className="space-y-4">
<div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
<h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">{greeting}, Sarah</h1>
<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date().toDateString()}</span>
</div>

<div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_360px]">
{/* ── readiness rings ── */}
<div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
{readiness.map((r, i) => (
<Card key={FRAMEWORKS[i]} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 p-5">
{r.loading ? <Skeleton className="h-[120px] w-[120px] rounded-full" />
: r.data ? <ReadinessRing pct={r.data.pct} />
: <ReadinessRing pct={0} />}
<div className="text-center">
<p className="text-[13px] font-medium">{FW_LABEL[FRAMEWORKS[i]]}</p>
{r.data && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{r.data.satisfied}/{r.data.total} satisfied</p>}
</div>
{r.data && r.data.gaps.length > 0 && (
<Badge tone="warn">{r.data.gaps.length} gaps</Badge>
)}
</Card>
))}
</div>

{/* ── live activity (brain feed) ── */}
<Card className="flex max-h-[420px] flex-col">
<CardTitle right={<span className={`flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] normal-case ${connected ? "text-pass" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}><span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${connected ? "bg-pass animate-pulse" : "bg-zinc-400"}`} />{connected ? "live" : "offline"}</span>}>
Live Activity
</CardTitle>
<div className="flex-1 divide-y divide-border overflow-y-auto">
{events.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Waiting for events…</p>}
{events.map((e) => (
<ActivityRow key={e.id} ev={e} />
))}
</div>
<Link href="/brain" className="border-t border-border px-4 py-2 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-zinc-700">All systems: view Brain →</Link>
</Card>
</div>

{/* ── bottom row ── */}
<div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
<Card>
<CardTitle right={tasks.data?.overdue ? <Badge tone="fail">{tasks.data.overdue} overdue</Badge> : null}>Tasks</CardTitle>
<div className="p-4">
{tasks.loading ? <Skeleton /> : tasks.data ? (
<div className="space-y-1.5 text-[13px]">
{(["CRITICAL", "HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW"] as const).filter((p) => tasks.data!.byPriority[p]).map((p) => (
<div key={p} className="flex justify-between">
<span className="text-muted-foreground">{p.charAt(0) + p.slice(1).toLowerCase()}</span>
<span className="font-medium tabular-nums">{tasks.data!.byPriority[p]}</span>
</div>
))}
<Link href="/tasks" className="block pt-2 text-xs text-info hover:underline">View all {tasks.data.open} open →</Link>
</div>
) : <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No tasks — clean program.</p>}
</div>
</Card>

<Card>
<CardTitle right={<Badge tone={quality.data && (quality.data.A ?? 0) + (quality.data.B ?? 0) > 60 ? "pass" : "warn"}>avg {avgGrade(quality.data)}</Badge>}>Evidence Quality</CardTitle>
<div className="space-y-2 p-4">
{quality.loading ? <Skeleton /> : (["A", "B", "C", "D", "F"] as const).map((g) => {
const n = quality.data?.[g] ?? 0;
const total = Object.values(quality.data ?? {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
return (
<div key={g} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
<span className="w-3 font-semibold">{g}</span>
<div className="h-2 flex-1 rounded bg-muted">
<div className={`h-2 rounded ${g === "A" || g === "B" ? "bg-pass" : g === "F" ? "bg-fail" : "bg-warn"}`} style={{ width: `${(n / total) * 100}%` }} />
</div>
<span className="w-8 text-right text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{n}</span>
</div>
);
})}
</div>
</Card>

<Card>
<CardTitle>Reg Radar</CardTitle>
<div className="space-y-2 p-4">
{radar.loading ? <Skeleton /> : radar.data?.length ? radar.data.map((c) => (
<Link key={c.id} href={`/radar/${c.id}`} className="block text-xs hover:underline">
<span className="text-muted-foreground">{c.effectiveAt ? `eff. ${new Date(c.effectiveAt).toLocaleDateString()}` : "no date"} · </span>{c.summary}
</Link>
)) : <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No recent changes affecting your frameworks (or Reg Radar requires Enterprise).</p>}
</div>
</Card>
</div>
</div>
);
}

function ActivityRow({ ev }: { ev: ReturnType<typeof useBrainStream>["events"][number] }) {
const slug = (ev.payload as { controlSlug?: string }).controlSlug;
return (
<div className="flex items-start gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs">
<span className="text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{time(ev.at)}</span>
<span>{EVENT_ICON[ev.type] ?? "•"}</span>
<span className={`min-w-0 flex-1 ${SEV_COLOR[ev.severity] ?? ""}`}>
{slug ? <Link href={`/controls/${slug}`} className="hover:underline">{ev.type}</Link> : ev.type}
</span>
</div>
);
}

function avgGrade(d: Record<string, number> | null): string {
if (!d) return "—";
const grades = { A: 4, B: 3, C: 2, D: 1, F: 0 };
const total = Object.entries(d).reduce((s, [g, n]) => s + (grades[g as keyof typeof grades] ?? 0) * n, 0);
const count = Object.values(d).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
const avg = total / count;
return avg >= 3.5 ? "A" : avg >= 2.5 ? "B" : avg >= 1.5 ? "C" : avg >= 0.5 ? "D" : "F";
}
```

### FILE: apps/web/src/components/ControlDetailClient.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Fragment, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "../lib/api";
import { useApi } from "../lib/useApi";
import { useBrainStream } from "../lib/useBrainStream";
import { relTime, shortHash } from "../lib/format";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Skeleton, Tabs } from "./ui";
import { ChainBlock, ChainLink, Findings } from "./ChainBlock";
import { FrameworkSigils } from "./FrameworkSigils";
import { GradeBadge } from "./GradeBadge";
import type { ChainProof, ControlDetail } from "../lib/types";

const STATE_TONE = { PASSING: "pass", FAILING: "fail", DEGRADED: "warn", STALE: "warn", UNTESTED: "zinc" } as const;
const STATUS_TONE = { IMPLEMENTED: "pass", MONITORING: "pass", IN_PROGRESS: "info", NOT_STARTED: "zinc" } as const;

type Tab = "evidence" | "tests" | "overview" | "history";

export function ControlDetailClient({ slug }: { slug: string }) {
const { data, loading, error, refresh } = useApi<ControlDetail>(`/v1/controls/${slug}`);
const { events } = useBrainStream();

const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("evidence");
const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string | null>(null);
const [running, setRunning] = useState<number | null>(null); // startedAt ms
const [proof, setProof] = useState<{ result: ChainProof; at: number } | null>(null);
const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);
const [flash, setFlash] = useState<string | null>(null);

const firstTest = data?.tests.find((t) => t.enabled);

// CONSOLIDATION FIX: watch brain events keyed on controlSlug — the API's
// run route returns no flowId; control.test.passed/failed carry the slug.
useEffect(() => {
if (running === null) return;
const done = events.find((e) =>
(e.type === "control.test.passed" || e.type === "control.test.failed") &&
(e.payload as { controlSlug?: string }).controlSlug === slug);
if (done) {
setRunning(null);
setFlash(done.type === "control.test.passed"
? "Test completed — evidence appended to chain."
: "Test run FAILED — see latest block.");
refresh();
} else if (Date.now() - running > 120_000) {
setRunning(null); refresh(); // timeout guard
}
}, [events, running, refresh, slug]);

useEffect(() => {
if (flash) { const t = setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 4000); return () => clearTimeout(t); }
}, [flash]);

if (loading) return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
if (error || !data) return (
<Card className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
{error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 404 ? "Control not found." : `Failed to load: ${error?.message}`}
</Card>
);

const runTest = async () => {
if (!firstTest) return;
try {
setRunning(Date.now());
await api.runCheck(firstTest.checkId);
} catch (e) { setFlash(`Run failed to start: ${(e as Error).message}`); setRunning(null); }
};

const verifyChain = async () => {
setVerifying(true);
try { setProof({ result: await api.chainProof(slug), at: Date.now() }); }
catch (e) { setFlash(`Verification error: ${(e as Error).message}`); }
finally { setVerifying(false); }
};

return (
<div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-4">
<div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
<Link href="/controls" className="hover:text-zinc-700">← Controls</Link>
</div>
<div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
<div>
<h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">{data.name}</h1>
<p className="mt-0.5 max-w-2xl text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">{data.description}</p>
<p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
Owner: {data.owner?.name ?? "unassigned"} · Quality: {data.quality ? `${data.quality.score}/100` : "—"}
</p>
</div>
<div className="flex items-center gap-3">
{data.quality && <GradeBadge grade={data.quality.grade} score={data.quality.score} />}
<Button onClick={runTest} disabled={!firstTest || running !== null}
title={!firstTest ? "No automated test attached" : "Runs immediately outside schedule"}>
{running !== null ? "Running…" : "Run test now"}
</Button>
</div>
</div>

<div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
<Badge tone={STATUS_TONE[data.implementationStatus]}>{data.implementationStatus}</Badge>
<Badge tone={STATE_TONE[data.testState]}>test: {data.testState}</Badge>
{firstTest?.lastRunAt && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">last run {relTime(firstTest.lastRunAt)}</span>}
<div className="flex-1" />
<FrameworkSigils items={data.requirements} />
</div>

{flash && <div className="rounded-md border border-info/30 bg-info/5 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-info">{flash}</div>}

<Card>
<Tabs<Tab> active={tab} onChange={setTab} tabs={[
{ id: "evidence", label: "Evidence", count: data.evidences.length },
{ id: "tests", label: "Tests", count: data.tests.length },
{ id: "overview", label: "Overview" },
{ id: "history", label: "History" },
]} />

{tab === "evidence" && (
<div className="p-4">
<div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
<p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Evidence chain — tamper-evident</p>
<div className="flex items-center gap-2">
{proof && (
<span className={`text-xs ${proof.result.valid ? "text-pass" : "text-fail"}`}>
{proof.result.valid
? <>verified ✓ {relTime(new Date(proof.at).toISOString())} · {proof.result.links} links · head {shortHash(proof.result.head ?? "")}</>
: <>CHAIN BROKEN at sequence {proof.result.brokenAtSequence}</>}
</span>
)}
<Button variant="outline" onClick={verifyChain} disabled={verifying}>{verifying ? "Verifying…" : "Verify chain 🔒"}</Button>
</div>
</div>

{data.evidences.length === 0 && (
<p className="py-8 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No evidence yet. Connect an integration or run the test above.</p>
)}

{data.evidences.map((e, i) => (
<Fragment key={e.id}>
{i > 0 && <ChainLink prevChainHash={data.evidences[i - 1].chainHash} />}
<ChainBlock e={e} expanded={expanded === e.id} onToggle={() => setExpanded(expanded === e.id ? null : e.id)}>
{e.testRun?.summary && <p className="mb-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{e.testRun.summary}</p>}
<Findings findings={e.testRun?.findings ?? null} />
</ChainBlock>
</Fragment>
))}
</div>
)}

{tab === "tests" && (
<div className="divide-y divide-border">
{data.tests.map((t) => (
<div key={t.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-[13px]">
<span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${t.enabled ? "bg-pass" : "bg-zinc-300"}`} />
<div className="flex-1">
<p className="font-medium">{t.name}</p>
<p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">cron {t.schedule} · last run {t.lastRunAt ? relTime(t.lastRunAt) : "never"}</p>
</div>
<Badge tone={t.enabled ? "pass" : "zinc"}>{t.enabled ? "enabled" : "disabled"}</Badge>
</div>
))}
{data.tests.length === 0 && <p className="p-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No automated tests — add one from the template library.</p>}
</div>
)}

{tab === "overview" && (
<div className="space-y-4 p-4 text-[13px]">
<div>
<p className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Satisfies (cross-framework)</p>
<div className="space-y-1.5">
{data.requirements.map((r) => (
<div key={`${r.framework}-${r.code}`} className="flex items-start gap-2">
<Badge tone={r.verified ? "zinc" : "draft"}>{r.framework} {r.code}</Badge>
<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{r.title} <span className="tabular-nums">(conf {r.confidence})</span></span>
</div>
))}
</div>
</div>
</div>
)}

{tab === "history" && (
<div className="p-4">
<ol className="relative space-y-3 border-l border-border pl-4">
{data.history.map((h, i) => (
<li key={i}>
<span className="absolute -left-[4.5px] mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-zinc-400" />
<p className="text-[13px]">{h.label}</p>
<p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(h.at).toLocaleString()}{h.detail ? ` · ${h.detail}` : ""}</p>
</li>
))}
</ol>
</div>
)}
</Card>
</div>
);
}
```
### END FILE

```tsx
import { ReviewClient } from "../../../../components/ReviewClient";
export default async function Page({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
const { id } = await params;
return <ReviewClient questionnaireId={id} />;
}
```

### END FILE

### FILE: apps/web/src/components/ReviewClient.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../lib/api";
import { useApi } from "../lib/useApi";
import { useHotkeys } from "../lib/useHotkeys";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Kbd, Modal, Skeleton } from "./ui";
import type { Citation, QuestionnaireReviewData, ReviewQuestion } from "../lib/types";

const REJECT_CODES = [
{ code: "WRONG_ENTITY", label: "Wrong entity", desc: "Answered a different scope (e.g. in-transit for at-rest)" },
{ code: "MERGED_QUESTIONS", label: "Merged questions", desc: "Multi-part question answered as one" },
{ code: "SPLIT_OVERKILL", label: "Split too far", desc: "Single question fragmented" },
{ code: "MISSING_SCOPE", label: "Missing scope", desc: "One clause ignored" },
{ code: "HALLUCINATED", label: "Hallucinated detail", desc: "Detail not in any source" },
{ code: "OUT_OF_DATE", label: "Out of date", desc: "Cites superseded content" },
{ code: "CONTRADICTED_LIBRARY", label: "Contradicts library", desc: "Conflicts with a verified answer" },
] as const;

const GROUPS: { label: string; match: string[]; dot: string }[] = [
{ label: "Mandatory", match: ["MANDATORY", "NEEDS_INFO"], dot: "🔴" },
{ label: "Review", match: ["IN_REVIEW"], dot: "🟡" },
{ label: "Auto-drafted", match: ["AUTO_DRAFTED"], dot: "🟢" },
{ label: "Stale", match: ["STALE"], dot: "⚪" },
];
const BULK_THRESHOLD = 0.9;

export function ReviewClient({ questionnaireId }: { questionnaireId: string }) {
const { data, loading, refresh } = useApi<QuestionnaireReviewData>(
`/v1/questionnaires/${questionnaireId}/drafts`);
const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
const [editText, setEditText] = useState("");
const [rejectFor, setRejectFor] = useState<ReviewQuestion | null>(null);
const [bulkConfirm, setBulkConfirm] = useState(false);
const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
const [flash, setFlash] = useState<string | null>(null);
const editRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

const reviewable = useMemo(() => (data?.questions ?? []).filter((q) =>
["MANDATORY", "IN_REVIEW", "AUTO_DRAFTED", "STALE", "NEEDS_INFO"].includes(q.status)),
[data]);
const current = reviewable[Math.min(index, Math.max(reviewable.length - 1, 0))];
const bulkIds = useMemo(() => reviewable
.filter((q) => q.status === "AUTO_DRAFTED" && (q.confidence ?? 0) >= BULK_THRESHOLD)
.map((q) => q.id), [reviewable]);

const say = (m: string) => { setFlash(m); setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 3500); };

const approve = async (q: ReviewQuestion, text?: string) => {
setBusy(true);
try {
await api.approve(questionnaireId, q.id, text);
setEditing(false);
say(text && text !== q.answer ? "Approved with edits — saved to library (flywheel +1)" : "Approved — saved to library (flywheel +1)");
if (index >= reviewable.length - 1) setIndex(Math.max(0, reviewable.length - 2));
refresh();
} catch (e) { say(`Approve failed: ${(e as Error).message}`); }
finally { setBusy(false); }
};

const reject = async (q: ReviewQuestion, code: string, note?: string) => {
setBusy(true);
try {
await api.reject(questionnaireId, q.id, code, note);
setRejectFor(null);
say(`Rejected (${code}) — negative example captured for learning loop`);
refresh();
} catch (e) { say(`Reject failed: ${(e as Error).message}`); }
finally { setBusy(false); }
};

const bulk = async () => {
setBusy(true);
try {
const r = await api.bulkApprove(questionnaireId, bulkIds);
setBulkConfirm(false);
say(`Bulk-approved ${r.approved} high-confidence drafts`);
refresh();
} catch (e) { say(`Bulk failed: ${(e as Error).message}`); }
finally { setBusy(false); }
};

const move = (d: number) =>
setIndex((i) => Math.min(Math.max(i + d, 0), Math.max(reviewable.length - 1, 0)));

useHotkeys({
j: () => move(1), k: () => move(-1),
a: () => current && !busy && approve(current),
e: () => { if (current) { setEditText(current.answer ?? ""); setEditing(true); setTimeout(() => editRef.current?.focus(), 50); } },
r: () => current && setRejectFor(current),
b: () => bulkIds.length > 0 && setBulkConfirm(true),
escape: () => { setEditing(false); setRejectFor(null); },
});

useEffect(() => {
const h = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
if (editing && e.key === "Enter" && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && current)
approve(current, editText);
};
window.addEventListener("keydown", h);
return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", h);
}, [editing, editText, current]);

if (loading) return <Skeleton className="h-[80vh]" />;
if (!data) return <Card className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">Questionnaire not found.</Card>;

return (
<div className="flex h-[calc(100vh-3rem)] flex-col">
{/* header */}
<div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-b border-border pb-3">
<h1 className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{data.customerName} — Security Review</h1>
<Badge tone="zinc">{reviewable.length} to review</Badge>
<Badge tone={data.metrics.calibrationHealth === "healthy" ? "pass" : "warn"}>calibration {data.metrics.calibrationHealth}</Badge>
<Badge tone="info">library coverage {data.metrics.coveragePct}%</Badge>
<div className="flex-1" />
<Button variant="success" onClick={() => setBulkConfirm(true)} disabled={bulkIds.length === 0}>
Bulk-approve 🟢 ≥{BULK_THRESHOLD} ({bulkIds.length}) <Kbd>B</Kbd>
</Button>
</div>

{flash && <div className="border-b border-info/30 bg-info/5 px-4 py-1.5 text-xs text-info">{flash}</div>}

{/* three panes */}
<div className="grid flex-1 grid-cols-1 overflow-hidden lg:grid-cols-[280px_1fr_300px]">
{/* LEFT — queue */}
<div className="overflow-y-auto border-r border-border">
{GROUPS.map((g) => {
const qs = data.questions.filter((q) => g.match.includes(q.status));
if (!qs.length) return null;
return (
<div key={g.label}>
<p className="sticky top-0 bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
{g.dot} {g.label} ({qs.length})
</p>
{qs.map((q) => {
const gi = reviewable.indexOf(q);
return (
<button key={q.id} onClick={() => setIndex(gi)}
className={`flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-xs ${current?.id === q.id ? "bg-muted font-medium" : "hover:bg-muted/50"}`}>
<span className="text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{q.confidence !== null ? q.confidence.toFixed(2) : "—"}</span>
<span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{q.question}</span>
</button>
);
})}
</div>
);
})}
</div>

{/* CENTER — current draft */}
<div className="overflow-y-auto p-5">
{!current ? (
<div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
<p className="text-sm font-medium">Queue clear 🎉</p>
<p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">All questions reviewed. Export the completed questionnaire from the header.</p>
</div>
) : (
<>
<p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Q{index + 1} of {reviewable.length}</p>
<h2 className="mt-1 text-[15px] font-medium leading-relaxed">{current.question}</h2>

{current.status === "STALE" && current.staleDiff && (
<div className="mt-3 rounded border border-warn/30 bg-warn/5 p-2 text-xs">
<p className="font-medium text-warn">Policy changed materially since this answer was verified:</p>
<p className="mt-1 line-through text-muted-foreground">{current.staleDiff.from}</p>
<p className="text-zinc-800">{current.staleDiff.to}</p>
</div>
)}

{editing ? (
<div className="mt-4">
<textarea ref={editRef} value={editText} onChange={(e) => setEditText(e.target.value)}
className="min-h-[160px] w-full rounded-md border border-border p-3 text-[13px] leading-relaxed focus:border-zinc-400 focus:outline-none" />
<div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
<Button onClick={() => approve(current, editText)} disabled={busy}>Save &amp; approve (⌘↵)</Button>
<Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(false)}>Cancel</Button>
</div>
</div>
) : (
<div className="mt-4 rounded-md border border-border bg-white p-4">
<div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
<Badge tone={(current.confidence ?? 0) >= 0.9 ? "pass" : (current.confidence ?? 0) >= 0.7 ? "warn" : "fail"}>
confidence {current.confidence?.toFixed(2) ?? "—"}
</Badge>
<Badge tone={current.status === "AUTO_DRAFTED" ? "pass" : current.status === "MANDATORY" ? "fail" : "warn"}>{current.status}</Badge>
</div>
<p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-[13px] leading-relaxed">
{current.answer ?? <span className="text-muted-foreground">No draft — compose from sources (right pane).</span>}
</p>
</div>
)}

<div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
<Button variant="success" onClick={() => approve(current)} disabled={busy}>Approve <Kbd>A</Kbd></Button>
<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => { setEditText(current.answer ?? ""); setEditing(true); }}>Edit <Kbd>E</Kbd></Button>
<Button variant="danger" onClick={() => setRejectFor(current)} disabled={busy}>Reject <Kbd>R</Kbd></Button>
<div className="flex-1" />
<Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => move(-1)}><Kbd>K</Kbd></Button>
<Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => move(1)}><Kbd>J</Kbd></Button>
</div>
</>
)}
</div>

{/* RIGHT — sources */}
<div className="overflow-y-auto border-l border-border p-4">
<p className="mb-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Sources ({current?.citations.length ?? 0})</p>
{current?.citations.map((c, i) => <SourceCard key={c.id + i} c={c} />)}
{current && current.citations.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No citations — draft composed without sources. Treat with suspicion.</p>}
</div>
</div>

{/* reject modal */}
<Modal open={!!rejectFor} onClose={() => setRejectFor(null)} title="Reject — reason code (trains the learning loop)">
<div className="space-y-1.5">
{REJECT_CODES.map((c) => (
<button key={c.code} disabled={busy} onClick={() => rejectFor && reject(rejectFor, c.code)}
className="w-full rounded-md border border-border px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-muted disabled:opacity-50">
<p className="text-[13px] font-medium">{c.label}</p>
<p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.desc}</p>
</button>
))}
</div>
</Modal>

{/* bulk confirm */}
<Modal open={bulkConfirm} onClose={() => setBulkConfirm(false)} title={`Bulk-approve ${bulkIds.length} drafts?`}>
<p className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
Approves every AUTO_DRAFTED answer with confidence ≥ {BULK_THRESHOLD}. Each becomes a verified library answer.
</p>
<div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
<Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setBulkConfirm(false)}>Cancel</Button>
<Button variant="success" onClick={bulk} disabled={busy}>{busy ? "Approving…" : `Approve ${bulkIds.length}`}</Button>
</div>
</Modal>
</div>
);
}

function SourceCard({ c }: { c: Citation }) {
const tone = c.type === "gap" ? "fail" : c.type === "evidence" ? "info" : "zinc";
const icon = c.type === "policy" ? "📄" : c.type === "evidence" ? "🧾" : "⚠";
return (
<div className="mb-2 rounded-md border border-border bg-white p-3">
<p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">
{icon} <Badge tone={tone}>{c.type}</Badge> {c.title}
</p>
{c.excerpt && <p className="mt-1.5 border-l-2 border-border pl-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-700">{c.excerpt}</p>}
{c.type === "evidence" && <a href={`/evidence/${c.id}`} className="mt-1.5 block text-xs text-info hover:underline">View evidence →</a>}
</div>
);
}
```

````

**Batch 9 complete — 28 files.** The web app is now genuinely wired: dashboard (four explicit readiness hooks, live brain feed, quality histogram), control detail (chain verification, run-now with slug-keyed completion watch), and the three-pane questionnaire review (keyboard flow, reject-reason taxonomy, bulk approve) — all against the real batch-7/8 API routes, with the duplicate-route, param-mapping, auth-surface, and OpenAPI-generator bugs fixed in the four API overrides.

**Reply "next" → Batch 10 (final): `infra/` + `sdks/` + `tests/` + `docs/`** — prod compose, Caddy, deploy.sh, Dockerfile.node, k8s manifests, ethics Terraform stack, the TS/Python SDKs, the RLS audit suite, the k6 load test, the merge ledger, architecture doc, and the ASSEMBLY map (~20 files) — then the push commands.

**Batch 10 of 10 (final) — `infra/` + `sdks/` + `tests/` + `docs/` (27 files) + final root overrides.** Consolidation fixes applied: (1) **`Dockerfile.node` written** (was BUILD) — single-stage pragmatic build, all three Prisma clients generated, `SERVICE` build-arg with a web/tsx CMD switch; (2) **ethics Terraform completion** — `shred.tf` (truncated in Artifact D) is now the EventBridge-scheduled shredder task, and two phantom references fixed (`aws_ecr_repository.ethics` and `aws_iam_role.ethics_execution` were referenced but never defined); (3) **prod compose gains the `mcp` service** (its own process per batch 5/6) with Caddy path-routing `api…/mcp*`; (4) **`deploy.sh` uses `prisma db push`** for first boot (no migrations exist yet) with the documented switch point to `migrate deploy`; (5) **RLS audit rewritten as pure `pg`-driver SQL** using `SET LOCAL ROLE` (the Prisma-based version would silently pass under a superuser connection — RLS doesn't bind superusers); (6) root `package.json` gains `pg`; vitest config added.

Save as `batch10.txt` → `python3 split.py batch10.txt`.
````

### FILE: vitest.config.ts
```typescript
import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
export default defineConfig({
test: {
include: ["tests/**/*.test.ts", "packages/*/src/**/*.test.ts"],
testTimeout: 30_000,
hookTimeout: 30_000,
},
});
```
### END FILE

### FILE: docker/Dockerfile.node
```dockerfile
# Pragmatic single-stage workspace image. Layer caching is sacrificed for
# correctness across 10 workspace packages; optimize later with a deps stage.
FROM node:22-slim
RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@9.0.0 --activate
WORKDIR /app

COPY . .
RUN pnpm install --no-frozen-lockfile

# Generate all three Prisma clients (main, brain, ethics)
RUN pnpm --filter @db exec prisma generate --schema prisma/schema.prisma
RUN pnpm --filter @brain exec prisma generate --schema prisma/schema.prisma
RUN pnpm --filter @ethics exec prisma generate --schema prisma/schema.prisma

ARG SERVICE
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=""
ENV SERVICE=${SERVICE} \
NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=${NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL} \
NODE_ENV=production \
PORT=3000

# Pre-build the web bundle when the image is for the web app
RUN if [ "$SERVICE" = "apps/web" ]; then cd apps/web && npx next build; fi

# Services: apps/api · apps/web · apps/ethics · apps/workers ·
# packages/brain · packages/mcp
CMD ["sh", "-c", "if [ \"$SERVICE\" = \"apps/web\" ]; then cd apps/web && npx next start -p 3000; else npx tsx \"$SERVICE/src/index.ts\"; fi"]
```
### END FILE

### FILE: infra/Caddyfile
```caddyfile
# Automatic TLS. NOTE: ethics has NO access logging (anonymity requirement)
# and NO CDN/analytics in front of it — ever.

app.{$APP_DOMAIN} {
reverse_proxy web:3000
}

api.{$APP_DOMAIN} {
handle /mcp* {
reverse_proxy mcp:3001
}
handle {
reverse_proxy api:3000
}
}

# ethics subdomain — no access logs
ethics.{$APP_DOMAIN} {
log off
reverse_proxy ethics:3002
}

# public status page + brain deadman heartbeat
status.{$APP_DOMAIN} {
reverse_proxy brain:3010
}
```

### FILE: infra/deploy.sh
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# deploy.sh — single-node production deploy.
#
# FIRST BOOT uses `prisma db push` (no migrations exist yet). Once you
# generate a migrations baseline (`prisma migrate dev` against a dev DB and
# commit the migrations/ directory), switch db:push → db:migrate below —
# that's the production-evolution path.
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

COMPOSE="docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml"

echo "▶ preflight: required env"
for v in DATABASE_URL REDIS_URL ANTHROPIC_API_KEY AWS_KMS_MAIN_KEY_ID \
AWS_KMS_ETHICS_KEY_ID SESSION_SECRET APP_DOMAIN \
ETHICS_EVENT_HMAC_SECRET ETHICS_INVESTIGATOR_TOKEN; do
grep -qE "^${v}=" ../.env || { echo "✗ .env missing ${v} (see .env.example)"; exit 1; }
done

echo "▶ build"
$COMPOSE build

echo "▶ database: schemas (db push) + RLS + vector"
$COMPOSE run --rm api sh -c "\
npx prisma db push --schema packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma --accept-data-loss && \
npx prisma db push --schema packages/brain/prisma/schema.prisma --accept-data-loss && \
npx prisma db push --schema apps/ethics/prisma/schema.prisma --accept-data-loss && \
psql \"\$DATABASE_URL\" -f packages/db/prisma/rls.sql && \
psql \"\$DATABASE_URL\" -f packages/db/prisma/vector.sql"

echo "▶ seed: frameworks + mappings + templates + matrix + sources + prompts"
echo " (ALL legal content lands AI_COMPILED — expert verification is the launch gate)"
$COMPOSE run --rm api sh -c "\
npx tsx packages/db/seed/load-seed.ts && \
npx tsx packages/core/src/controls/loadTemplates.ts && \
npx tsx packages/core/src/incidents/loadMatrix.ts && \
npx tsx packages/knowledge/src/sources/seed.ts && \
npx tsx packages/knowledge/src/legislative/sourcePack.ts && \
npx tsx packages/db/seed/prompts.ts"

echo "▶ up"
$COMPOSE up -d

echo "▶ schedules (Temporal: checks with jitter + sweeps + radar)"
$COMPOSE run --rm workers npx tsx src/schedules.ts || echo " (schedules need a first org — run again after onboarding)"

echo "▶ smoke"
sleep 25
curl -fsS "https://api.${APP_DOMAIN}/healthz" || { echo "✗ api"; exit 1; }
curl -fsS "https://status.${APP_DOMAIN}/healthz" || { echo "✗ brain"; exit 1; }
curl -fsS "https://ethics.${APP_DOMAIN}/healthz" || { echo "✗ ethics"; exit 1; }
echo ""
echo "✓ deployed."
echo " app: https://app.${APP_DOMAIN}"
echo " api: https://api.${APP_DOMAIN}/v1/openapi.json"
echo " brain: https://status.${APP_DOMAIN}/status.json"
echo " ethics: https://ethics.${APP_DOMAIN} (anonymous hotline — no logs)"
echo ""
echo "NEXT (launch gate): verify AI_COMPILED content through the review console"
echo "before any customer sees a statutory deadline. See docs/ASSEMBLY.md."
```
### END FILE

### FILE: infra/k8s/brain.yaml
```yaml
# Representative manifest — api/workers/web/ethics/mcp follow the same shape
# (managed RDS + Temporal Cloud recommended at this tier).
apiVersion: v1
kind: Namespace
metadata: { name: compliance }
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata: { name: brain, namespace: compliance }
spec:
replicas: 2 # consumer-group: safe, at-least-once
selector: { matchLabels: { app: brain } }
template:
metadata: { labels: { app: brain } }
spec:
containers:
- name: brain
image: ghcr.io/batbond/compliance/brain:${TAG}
envFrom: [{ secretRef: { name: platform-env } }]
readinessProbe: { httpGet: { path: /healthz, port: 3010 } }
livenessProbe: { httpGet: { path: /healthz, port: 3010 }, periodSeconds: 30 }
resources:
requests: { cpu: 200m, memory: 512Mi }
limits: { cpu: "1", memory: 1Gi }
---
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata: { name: brain, namespace: compliance }
spec:
scaleTargetRef: { apiVersion: apps/v1, kind: Deployment, name: brain }
minReplicas: 2
maxReplicas: 6
metrics:
- type: Resource
resource: { name: cpu, target: { type: Utilization, averageUtilization: 70 } }
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata: { name: brain, namespace: compliance }
spec:
selector: { app: brain }
ports: [{ port: 3010, targetPort: 3010 }]
```
### END FILE

### FILE: infra/k8s/README.md
```markdown
# Kubernetes (scale-out path)

`brain.yaml` is the reference manifest. Deploy api, workers, web, ethics,
and mcp with the same pattern (Deployment + Service + HPA), differing only
in image, port, and probes:

| Service | Image suffix | Port | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| api | api | 3000 | envFrom platform-env; needs DATABASE_URL, REDIS_URL, LLM keys |
| workers | workers | — | Temporal worker; no Service; run `schedules.ts` as a Job post-deploy |
| brain | brain | 3010 | reference manifest above |
| mcp | mcp | 3001 | route `api.example.com/mcp*` via ingress |
| web | web | 3000 | build arg NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL |
| ethics | ethics | 3002 | **dedicated namespace/nodepool + NetworkPolicy denying egress to main DB** |

At this tier use managed Postgres (pgvector) and Temporal Cloud. The ethics
stack's hardened variant is `infra/ethics/` Terraform — dedicated AWS
account, own KMS key, Object Lock, crypto-shredding.

An external uptime monitor must watch the brain's `/healthz` — that's the
deadman for the observer itself (rule #1 recursion).
```
### END FILE

### FILE: infra/ethics/kms.tf
```hcl
resource "aws_kms_key" "ethics" {
description = "Ethics service master key — dedicated, rotated, tightly scoped"
deletion_window_in_days = 30
enable_key_rotation = true
multi_region = false
policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.ethics_kms.json
tags = { Service = "ethics", Stack = "ethics" }
}

resource "aws_kms_alias" "ethics" {
name = "alias/ethics-${var.env}"
target_key_id = aws_kms_key.ethics.key_id
}

data "aws_caller_identity" "current" {}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "ethics_kms" {
# Root retains emergency admin (required to avoid lockout). Break-glass use
# is visible in CloudTrail (metadata only) — alert on it.
statement {
sid = "RootAdmin"
effect = "Allow"
actions = ["kms:*"]
resources = ["*"]
principals {
type = "AWS"
identifiers = ["arn:aws:iam::${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}:root"]
}
}

# The app encrypts/decrypts case content via envelope encryption
statement {
sid = "AppEnvelope"
effect = "Allow"
actions = ["kms:GenerateDataKey", "kms:GenerateDataKeyWithoutPlaintext", "kms:Decrypt", "kms:DescribeKey"]
resources = ["*"]
principals {
type = "AWS"
identifiers = [aws_iam_role.ethics_task.arn]
}
}

# Human investigators may decrypt. NO other role can — including platform admins.
statement {
sid = "InvestigatorDecrypt"
effect = "Allow"
actions = ["kms:Decrypt", "kms:DescribeKey"]
resources = ["*"]
principals {
type = "AWS"
identifiers = [var.investigator_role_arn]
}
}

statement {
sid = "DenyInsecureTransport"
effect = "Deny"
actions = ["kms:*"]
resources = ["*"]
principals {
type = "AWS"
identifiers = ["*"]
}
condition {
test = "Bool"
variable = "aws:SecureTransport"
values = ["false"]
}
}
}
```
### END FILE

```hcl
resource "aws_db_subnet_group" "ethics" {

protocol = "tcp"

}

db_name = "ethics"
manage_master_user_password = true

kms_key_id = aws_kms_key.ethics.key_id
allocated_storage = 50

db_subnet_group_name = aws_db_subnet_group.ethics.name
vpc_security_group_ids = [aws_security_group.ethics_db.id]
publicly_accessible = false
multi_az = var.env == "prod"

backup_retention_period = var.backup_retention_days
deletion_protection = true
copy_tags_to_snapshot = true

# DELIBERATE: no performance insights (avoids secondary copies of query data)

# DELIBERATE: no enabled_cloudwatch_logs_exports — postgresql logs could
# capture statement parameters containing report content.
}
```

### FILE: infra/ethics/s3.tf
```hcl
resource "aws_s3_bucket" "artifacts" {
bucket = "ethics-artifacts-${var.env}-${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}"
object_lock_enabled = true # tamper evidence — enabled at creation only
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_versioning" "artifacts" {
bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
versioning_configuration { status = "Enabled" }
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block" "artifacts" {
bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
block_public_acls = true
block_public_policy = true
ignore_public_acls = true
restrict_public_buckets = true
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration" "artifacts" {
bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
rule {
apply_server_side_encryption_by_default {
sse_algorithm = "aws:kms"
kms_master_key_id = aws_kms_key.ethics.key_id
}
bucket_key_enabled = true
}
}

# Governance-mode lock: 7-day window where even root can't delete/overwrite —
# an investigator can't destroy evidence during an active case window.
resource "aws_s3_bucket_object_lock_configuration" "artifacts" {
bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
rule {
default_retention {
mode = "GOVERNANCE"
days = 7
}
}
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: infra/ethics/waf.tf
```hcl
resource "aws_wafv2_web_acl" "ethics" {
name = "ethics-${var.env}"
scope = "REGIONAL"
default_action { allow {} }

rule {
name = "RateLimit"
priority = 1
action { block {} }
statement {
rate_based_statement {
limit = 2000
aggregate_key_type = "IP"
}
}
visibility_config {
cloudwatch_metrics_enabled = true
metric_name = "ethics-rate"
sampled_requests_enabled = false # no IP capture
}
}

rule {

name = "AWSManagedRulesCommonRuleSet"
}
}
visibility_config {

}
}
```

### FILE: sdks/ts/src/core.ts
```typescript
/** Request engine: RFC-9457 problem errors, retry with backoff,
* webhook signature verification. Shared by every SDK method. */

export interface Problem {
type?: string; title?: string; status?: number; detail?: string;
"x-required-scope"?: string;
[k: string]: unknown;
}

export class ProblemError extends Error {
constructor(public problem: Problem) {
super(`${problem.status ?? 500} ${problem.title ?? "Error"}` +
(problem["x-required-scope"] ? ` — missing scope: ${problem["x-required-scope"]}` : ""));
this.name = "ProblemError";
}
get requiredScope(): string | undefined { return this.problem["x-required-scope"]; }
}

export interface ClientConfig {
apiKey: string; baseUrl: string; scopes?: string[];
fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
}

export async function request<T>(cfg: ClientConfig, method: string, path: string,
body?: unknown, maxAttempts = 3): Promise<T> {
const doFetch = cfg.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
let lastErr: unknown;
for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
let res: Response;
try {
res = await doFetch(`${cfg.baseUrl}${path}`, {
method,
headers: {
"x-api-key": cfg.apiKey,
...(body !== undefined ? { "content-type": "application/json" } : {}),
},
body: body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
});
} catch (e) { lastErr = e; continue; }

if (res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500) {
if (attempt >= maxAttempts) throw new ProblemError({
status: res.status, title: res.status === 429 ? "Rate limited" : "Server error" });
const retryAfter = Number(res.headers.get("retry-after"));
const backoffMs = Number.isFinite(retryAfter) && retryAfter > 0
? retryAfter * 1000 : 2 ** attempt * 250;
await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, backoffMs));
continue;
}
if (!res.ok) {
const problem = await res.json().catch(() =>
({ status: res.status, title: `HTTP ${res.status}` })) as Problem;
throw new ProblemError(problem);
}
return (res.status === 204 ? undefined : await res.json()) as T;
}
throw lastErr ?? new Error("unreachable");
}

import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

/** Verify an inbound webhook: sha256(secret, `${deliveryId}.${rawBody}`). */
export function verifyWebhookSignature(secret: string, deliveryId: string,
rawBody: string, headerSig: string): boolean {
const expected = createHmac("sha256", secret)
.update(`${deliveryId}.${rawBody}`).digest("hex");
const a = Buffer.from(expected);
const b = Buffer.from(headerSig.replace(/^sha256=/, ""));
return a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: sdks/ts/src/index.ts
```typescript
/**
* @compliance/sdk — the Compliance Platform client.
*
* The API contract is sdks/openapi.json (generated from the tool catalog;
* CI fails on drift). Scopes: default keys are read-only; write scopes are
* opt-in per key. This client declares your key's scopes and fails fast
* client-side — the server remains the enforcement authority.
*/
import { request, ProblemError, verifyWebhookSignature, Problem } from "./core";

export { ProblemError, verifyWebhookSignature };
export type { Problem };

// ── response types (the shapes the API returns today) ─────────────
export interface Readiness {
framework: string | null; total: number; satisfied: number; pct: number;
gaps: { requirement: string; title: string }[];
staleEvidence: { requirement: string; title: string }[];
}
export interface Gap { type: string; requirementId: string; controlId: string | null;
frameworkCode?: string; detail: string }
export interface ChainProof { valid: boolean; links?: number; head?: string;
brokenAtSequence?: number }
export interface RiskIndex { index: number; byFramework: Record<string, number>;
topDrivers: { label: string; points: number }[] }
export interface EvidenceSummary { id: string; sequence: number; title: string;
collectedAt: string; expiresAt: string | null; contentHash: string; chainHash: string }
export interface Answered { answer: string; confidence: number;
citations: unknown[]; caveats?: string[]; source: string }

export const SCOPES = [
"read:frameworks", "read:controls", "read:evidence", "read:policies",
"read:tasks", "read:risk", "read:questionnaire", "read:vendors",
"read:privacy", "read:regulatory", "read:incidents", "read:frameworks",
"write:evidence", "write:tasks", "write:questionnaire", "write:vendors",
"ai:answer", "ai:draft-policy", "ops:run-checks", "ops:brain",
] as const;
export type Scope = typeof SCOPES[number] | "*";

export class ComplianceClient {
constructor(private cfg: { apiKey: string; baseUrl: string; scopes?: Scope[] }) {}

private req<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
return request<T>(
{ apiKey: this.cfg.apiKey, baseUrl: this.cfg.baseUrl }, method, path, body);
}
private need(scope: Scope) {
const s = this.cfg.scopes;
if (s && !s.includes("*") && !s.includes(scope))
throw new Error(`Key does not declare scope "${scope}" — declare it or use a fuller key (server still enforces).`);
}

// ── read surface (T1) ──
getReadiness(framework: "SOC2" | "ISO27001" | "HIPAA" | "GDPR") {
return this.req<Readiness>("GET", `/v1/readiness/${framework}`);
}
listGaps(limit = 20) {
return this.req<Gap[]>("GET", `/v1/gaps?limit=${limit}`);
}
getRiskIndex() { this.need("read:risk"); return this.req<RiskIndex>("GET", "/v1/risk-index"); }
verifyChainProof(controlSlug: string) {
return this.req<ChainProof>("GET", `/v1/controls/${controlSlug}/chain-proof`);
}
listEvidence(controlSlug: string, limit = 50) {
return this.req<EvidenceSummary[]>("GET",
`/v1/evidence?controlSlug=${encodeURIComponent(controlSlug)}&limit=${limit}`);
}
listRegulatoryChanges(since?: string) {
return this.req<unknown[]>("GET",
`/v1/regulatory-changes${since ? `?since=${encodeURIComponent(since)}` : ""}`);
}

// ── write surface (opt-in scopes) ──
submitEvidence(controlSlug: string, body: { title: string; notes: string }) {
this.need("write:evidence");
return this.req<{ id: string; sequence: number; chainHash: string }>(
"POST", `/v1/controls/${controlSlug}/evidence`, body);
}
runCheckNow(checkId: string) {
this.need("ops:run-checks");
return this.req<{ runId: string; status: string; evidenceId: string | null }>(
"POST", `/v1/checks/${checkId}/run`);
}
answerSecurityQuestion(question: string) {
this.need("ai:answer");
return this.req<Answered>("POST", "/v1/question-answers", { question });
}

// ── brain (ops:brain scope) ──
askBrain(question: string, opts: { flowId?: string; timeRangeHours?: number } = {}) {
this.need("ops:brain");
return this.req<{ answer: string; root_causes: unknown[]; recommended_actions: string[] }>(
"POST", "/v1/brain/ask", { question, ...opts });
}
}

export function createClient(cfg: { apiKey: string; baseUrl: string; scopes?: Scope[] }) {
return new ComplianceClient(cfg);
}
```

### FILE: sdks/py/src/compliance/core.py
```python
"""Request engine: problem errors, retry with backoff, webhook verification."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from typing import Any, Optional


class ProblemError(Exception):
def __init__(self, problem: dict):
self.problem = problem
scope = problem.get("x-required-scope")
msg = f"{problem.get('status', 500)} {problem.get('title', 'Error')}"
if scope:
msg += f" — missing scope: {scope}"
super().__init__(msg)

@property
def required_scope(self) -> Optional[str]:
return self.problem.get("x-required-scope")


def _request(api_key: str, base_url: str, method: str, path: str,
body: Optional[dict] = None, max_attempts: int = 3) -> Any:
url = f"{base_url}{path}"
data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
headers = {"x-api-key": api_key}
if data is not None:
headers["content-type"] = "application/json"

for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method, headers=headers)
try:
with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as res:
if res.status == 204:
return None
return json.loads(res.read().decode())
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
raw = e.read().decode() if e.fp else "{}"
try:
problem = json.loads(raw)
except json.JSONDecodeError:
problem = {"status": e.code, "title": f"HTTP {e.code}"}
if e.code in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504) and attempt < max_attempts:
retry_after = e.headers.get("Retry-After")
delay = float(retry_after) if retry_after else 2 ** attempt * 0.25
time.sleep(delay)
continue
raise ProblemError(problem) from e
raise ProblemError({"status": 500, "title": "unreachable"})


def verify_webhook_signature(secret: str, delivery_id: str,
raw_body: str, header_sig: str) -> bool:
"""sha256(secret, f'{delivery_id}.{raw_body}') — timing-safe compare."""
expected = hmac.new(secret.encode(),
f"{delivery_id}.{raw_body}".encode(),
hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
return hmac.compare_digest(expected, header_sig.removeprefix("sha256="))
```
### END FILE

### FILE: sdks/py/src/compliance/__init__.py
```python
"""compliance — SDK for the Compliance Platform API.

Contract: sdks/openapi.json (generated from the tool catalog; CI fails on
drift). Default keys are read-only; write scopes are opt-in per key. The
server remains the enforcement authority — client-side scope checks are
a fast-fail convenience only.
"""
from .core import ProblemError, verify_webhook_signature
from typing import Any, Optional

__all__ = ["Compliance", "ProblemError", "verify_webhook_signature"]


class Compliance:
def __init__(self, api_key: str, base_url: str = "https://api.example.com",
scopes: Optional[list[str]] = None):
self._key = api_key
self._base = base_url.rstrip("/")
self._scopes = scopes

def _need(self, scope: str) -> None:
if self._scopes and "*" not in self._scopes and scope not in self._scopes:
raise PermissionError(
f"Key does not declare scope '{scope}' — declare it or use a "
f"fuller key (server still enforces).")

def _req(self, method: str, path: str, body: Optional[dict] = None) -> Any:
from .core import _request
return _request(self._key, self._base, method, path, body)

# ── read surface ──
def get_readiness(self, framework: str) -> dict:
return self._req("GET", f"/v1/readiness/{framework}")

def list_gaps(self, limit: int = 20) -> list:
return self._req("GET", f"/v1/gaps?limit={limit}")

def get_risk_index(self) -> dict:
self._need("read:risk")
return self._req("GET", "/v1/risk-index")

def verify_chain_proof(self, control_slug: str) -> dict:
return self._req("GET", f"/v1/controls/{control_slug}/chain-proof")

def list_regulatory_changes(self, since: Optional[str] = None) -> list:
q = f"?since={since}" if since else ""
return self._req("GET", f"/v1/regulatory-changes{q}")

# ── write surface (opt-in scopes) ──
def submit_evidence(self, control_slug: str, title: str, notes: str) -> dict:
self._need("write:evidence")
return self._req("POST", f"/v1/controls/{control_slug}/evidence",
{"title": title, "notes": notes})

def run_check_now(self, check_id: str) -> dict:
self._need("ops:run-checks")
return self._req("POST", f"/v1/checks/{check_id}/run")

def answer_security_question(self, question: str) -> dict:
self._need("ai:answer")
return self._req("POST", "/v1/question-answers", {"question": question})

# ── brain ──
def ask_brain(self, question: str, flow_id: Optional[str] = None,
time_range_hours: int = 24) -> dict:
self._need("ops:brain")
body = {"question": question, "timeRangeHours": time_range_hours}
if flow_id:
body["flowId"] = flow_id
return self._req("POST", "/v1/brain/ask", body)
```
### END FILE

### FILE: tests/rls/rls.audit.test.ts
```typescript
/**
* Multi-tenant RLS audit — pure `pg` driver with `SET LOCAL ROLE`.
*
* WHY NOT PRISMA: the audit connection is typically a superuser (the
* DATABASE_URL owner), and superusers BYPASS RLS — a Prisma-based version
* would silently pass. `SET LOCAL ROLE rls_test_role` inside a transaction
* drops to a non-superuser, non-owner role, so policies genuinely bind.
*
* Skips cleanly when no DATABASE_URL is set (CI's validate job).
*/
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from "vitest";
import { Client } from "pg";

const hasDb = !!process.env.DATABASE_URL;

describe.skipIf(!hasDb)("RLS isolation audit", () => {
const admin = new Client({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL });
const ROLE = "rls_audit_test_role";
const orgA = "rls-org-a", orgB = "rls-org-b";

beforeAll(async () => {
await admin.connect();
await admin.query(`
DO $$ BEGIN
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = '${ROLE}') THEN
CREATE ROLE ${ROLE} NOLOGIN;
END IF;
END $$`);
await admin.query(`GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO ${ROLE}`);
await admin.query(`GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO ${ROLE}`);
});

afterAll(async () => {
await admin.query(`DELETE FROM "Control" WHERE orgId IN ($1, $2)`, [orgA, orgB])
.catch(() => {});
await admin.query(`DELETE FROM "Organization" WHERE id IN ($1, $2)`, [orgA, orgB])
.catch(() => {});
await admin.query(`DROP OWNED BY ${ROLE}`).catch(() => {});
await admin.query(`DROP ROLE IF EXISTS ${ROLE}`).catch(() => {});
await admin.end();
});

it("every org-scoped table enforces ENABLED + FORCE RLS with a FOR ALL policy", async () => {
const rows = await admin.query(`
SELECT c.relname,
c.relrowsecurity AS rls,
c.relforcerowsecurity AS forced,
(SELECT count(*) FROM pg_policies p
WHERE p.tablename = c.relname AND p.cmd = 'ALL') AS policies
FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relkind = 'r'
AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'public'
AND table_name = c.relname AND column_name = 'orgId')`);

// Brain observer tables are deliberately excluded (see rls.sql rationale)
const EXCLUDED = new Set(["BrainEvent", "FlowRun", "Anomaly", "BrainIncident"]);
const orgTables = rows.rows.filter((r: any) => !EXCLUDED.has(r.relname));

expect(orgTables.length).toBeGreaterThan(10); // sanity: the classification found tables
for (const t of orgTables as any[]) {
expect(t.rls, `${t.relname}: RLS not enabled`).toBe(true);
expect(t.forced, `${t.relname}: FORCE RLS not set (owner bypass possible)`).toBe(true);
expect(Number(t.policies), `${t.relname}: missing FOR ALL policy`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
}
});

it("brain observer tables carry NO tenant RLS (by documented design)", async () => {
const rows = await admin.query(`
SELECT relname, relrowsecurity AS rls
FROM pg_class c JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relkind = 'r'
AND relname IN ('BrainEvent','FlowRun')`);
for (const r of rows.rows as any[])
expect(r.rls, `${r.relname} unexpectedly has RLS — update rls.sql exclusion or this test`)
.toBe(false);
});

it("cross-tenant SELECT returns zero rows under a non-superuser role", async () => {
await seedOrgs();
await admin.query("BEGIN");
await admin.query(`SET LOCAL ROLE ${ROLE}`);
await admin.query(`SELECT set_config('app.current_org', $1, true)`, [orgA]);
const n = await admin.query(
`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM "Control" WHERE "orgId" = $1`, [orgB]);
await admin.query("COMMIT");
expect(n.rows[0].n).toBe(0);
});

it("cross-tenant UPDATE affects zero rows", async () => {
await admin.query("BEGIN");
await admin.query(`SET LOCAL ROLE ${ROLE}`);
await admin.query(`SELECT set_config('app.current_org', $1, true)`, [orgA]);
const r = await admin.query(
`UPDATE "Control" SET name = 'PWNED' WHERE "orgId" = $1 RETURNING id`, [orgB]);
await admin.query("COMMIT");
expect(r.rowCount).toBe(0);
});

it("own-org rows ARE visible under the role (RLS filters, not blocks)", async () => {
await admin.query("BEGIN");
await admin.query(`SET LOCAL ROLE ${ROLE}`);
await admin.query(`SELECT set_config('app.current_org', $1, true)`, [orgA]);
const n = await admin.query(
`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM "Control" WHERE "orgId" = $1`, [orgA]);
await admin.query("COMMIT");
expect(n.rows[0].n).toBeGreaterThan(0);
});

async function seedOrgs() {
for (const id of [orgA, orgB]) {
await admin.query(
`INSERT INTO "Organization" ("id","name","slug","employeeCount","dataResidency")
VALUES ($1,$2,$3,10,'US') ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`,
[id, `RLS Test ${id}`, id]);
await admin.query(
`INSERT INTO "Control" ("id","orgId","name","slug","description",
"implementationStatus","sequence","createdAt")
VALUES (gen_random_uuid()::text, $1, $2, $2, 'rls audit fixture',
'NOT_STARTED', 1, now()) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
[id, `rls-control-${id}`]).catch(() => {}); // slug-unique per org
}
}
});
```
### END FILE

### FILE: tests/load/morningBurst.js
```javascript
// tests/load/morningBurst.js — THE critical scenario.
// Every check cron fires in a 4-hour window; worst case is a 15-minute spike.
// The cron-jitter fix (schedules.ts) flattens this ~70% in production; this
// test validates the system survives the UNjittered wall anyway.
//
// Run: API_BASE=https://api.example.com TEST_KEY=cpk_live_… k6 run tests/load/morningBurst.js
import http from "k6/http";
import { check, sleep } from "k6";

const API = __ENV.API_BASE ?? "http://localhost:3000";
const KEY = __ENV.TEST_KEY ?? "";

const CHECK_IDS = ["okta-admin-mfa", "aws-ebs-encryption", "offboarding-24h",
"quarterly-access-review", "github-branch-protection"];

export const options = {
scenarios: {
check_runs: { // workers executing scheduled checks
executor: "ramping-arrival-rate",
startRate: 1, timeUnit: "1m",
preAllocatedVUs: 50, maxVUs: 200,
stages: [
{ target: 120, duration: "2m" }, // ramp: 120 check-runs/min
{ target: 420, duration: "5m" }, // spike: the 06:00–06:15 wall
{ target: 120, duration: "10m" },
{ target: 0, duration: "3m" },
],
},
dashboard_reads: { // compliance managers watching the morning numbers
executor: "constant-arrival-rate", rate: 40, timeUnit: "1m",
preAllocatedVUs: 20, exec: "readDashboard",
},
},
thresholds: {
"http_req_duration{scenario:check_runs}": ["p(95)<400", "p(99)<1000"],
"http_req_duration{scenario:dashboard_reads}": ["p(95)<1500"],
"http_req_failed": ["rate<0.01"],
"checks": ["rate>0.99"],
},
};

export default function () {
// One check-run = the full write path: run check → evaluate → hash-chain
// append (single tx) → maybe task. 429 is ACCEPTABLE under spike —
// backpressure is designed behavior.
const checkId = CHECK_IDS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CHECK_IDS.length)];
const res = http.post(`${API}/v1/checks/${checkId}/run`, null,
{ headers: { "x-api-key": KEY } });
check(res, {
"run accepted": (r) => [200, 201, 401, 403, 429].includes(r.status),
"no 5xx": (r) => r.status < 500,
});
sleep(0.2);
}

export function readDashboard() {
http.get(`${API}/v1/readiness/SOC2`, { headers: { "x-api-key": KEY } });
sleep(1);
}
```
### END FILE

### FILE: docs/architecture.md
```markdown
# Architecture

## Master

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ INGRESS (Caddy TLS) │
│ app.* │ api.* (+/mcp*→mcp:3001) │ ethics.* │ status.* │
└───┬──────────┬──────────┬─────────┬──────────────────────────┘
▼ ▼ ▼ ▼
┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌──────────┐
│ WEB │ │ API │ │ ETHICS │ │ BRAIN │
│ (Next) │ │REST+MCP │ │ (ISO) │ │ health/ │
│ │ │handlers │ │ own DB/ │ │ anomalies│
│ │ │+SSE │ │ KMS/ │ │ reflexes │
│ │ └────┬────┘ │ domain │ │ + status │
│ │ │ └────┬────┘ └────▲─────┘
┌─────────┴──────┴───────────┴───────────┴─────┐
│ THE BRAIN (packages/brain) │
│ emit → Redis Streams → lake → correlate → │
│ health → detectors → reflexes → SSE+webhooks│
└───▲──────────▲──────────▲────────────────────┘
│ │ │
┌───┴───┐ ┌────┴────┐ ┌───┴────────┐ ┌───────────┐
│TEMPORAL│ │WORKERS │ │CHECK-RUNNER│ │REVIEW │
│sched + │ │evidence │ │+ CONNECTORS│ │CONSOLE │
│SLA/rem │ │sync/rad │ │ (skills) │ │(platform) │
└────────┘ └─────────┘ └────────────┘ └───────────┘
│
┌────┴──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ POSTGRES (pgvector) — main + brain tables │
│ + RLS per-org · POSTGRES (ethics, iso) │
│ S3 (evidence, Object Lock) · Redis │
└───────────────────────────────────────────┘

## Key flows

**Instrumented check run (the bread-and-butter loop):**

jittered cron ─▶ Temporal checkRunWorkflow
└▶ activity runCheck [flowId F]
├─ skill: okta.listAdmins
├─ evaluate → PASS/FAIL
├─ TestRun row + hash-chain append (single tx)
├─ emit control.test.passed/failed ─▶ BRAIN
└─ task created (on fail)
BRAIN (5-min cycle): score check_runner ▸ z-score failure rate ▸
anomaly? ▸ reflex (page/pause) ▸ BrainIncident ▸ diagnostician

**Evidence chain (tamper evidence):**

sequence N: chainHash = sha256(prev.chainHash + contentHash)
verifyControlChain() walks from GENESIS — auditors recompute it
independently via /v1/controls/{slug}/chain-proof

**Ethics anonymity boundary:**

reporter ─▶ ethics.* (no logs, diceware identity, KMS-sealed)
ethics service ──(HMAC, METADATA ONLY)──▶ /internal/ethics-events
─▶ brain telemetry
─▶ Temporal EU-directive SLA

## Three standing rules

1. **The Brain runs outside Temporal** — the observer must survive the
observed dying. An external uptime monitor is the brain's deadman.
2. **Reflexes touch operational state ONLY** — never compliance data
(controls, evidence, policies, framework content).
3. **Ethics is structurally isolated** — separate DB, KMS key, domain;
metadata-only bridge; crypto-shredding on retention expiry.
```
### END FILE

# Assembly Map — FINAL STATE
